“icai tirinticaippiṉum” eṉṉum poruḷiyal mutal nūṟpā uraiyil mēṟkōḷākak “kārvirikoṉṟai” eṉṉum akanāṉūṟṟuk kaṭavuḷ vāḻttup pāṭalaik kāṭṭum iḷampūraṇar, ‘civāṉupūtiyiṟ pērulakam taṅkiṟṟu’ eṉṟu uraiyeḻutuvatu koṇṭu ivaraic civaneṟiyar eṉa uṟutippaṭuttuvar.  aḵtāyiṉ, appāṭal toṭaroṭu toṭarpilāta acceṟippum, ‘tāviltāḷ niḻal’ eṉpataṉ poruḷ viṭuppum koṇṭu aiyuṟavu koḷḷaṟku iṭamuṇṭu! pāṭal toṭaraiyē icaittut toṭarpuṟuttum avvuraiyil aḵtoṉṟu maṭṭum oṭṭā oṭṭāka iruttalum, tāviltāḷ niḻal viṭupāṭum piṟitoruvar kaiccarakkō eṉa eṇṇavē vaikkiṉṟatu.
iḷampūraṇar vaḷḷuvar vāymaiyil neñcam paṟikoṭutta tōṉṟal eṉpatu ivar eṭuttukkāṭṭum mēṟkōḷ viḷakkap perukkattāṉē naṉku pulappaṭum.  atikāraṅkaḷ palavaṟṟai aṭukkik kūṟutalālum viḷaṅkum.  ittakaiyar vaḷḷuvaraip pōlac camayak kaṇakkar mativaḻic cellāta camaṉilaic cālpiṉar eṉpatē teḷivām. camayac cārpiṉar veḷippaṭak kāṭṭum valinta poruḷāṭci, mēṟkōḷ āyavai ivariṭattuk kāṇaṟkillāmal eccamayamum oppaniṉaittup pōṟṟum oru peruntakaimaiyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu eṉka.
tolkāppiya uraiyāciriyar iḷampūraṇarum tirukkuṟaḷ uraiyāciriyar maṇakkuṭavarum oruvarē eṉpar. oruvar oru nūṟku oru peyarum, maṟṟoru nūṟku oru peyarum koṇṭu urai varaintaṉar eṉṟal marapu nilaippaṭātu.  oruvar, iruvar, mūvar peyar koṇ ṭeḻutip piḻaikkum ‘vaṇika nōkkar’ accaṭippuk kālattē kāṇa lāmē yaṉṟip payilvār payaṉpāṭu eṉṉum oṉṟē kuṟiyāk koṇṭa ēṭṭukkālattut tūyarai akkūṭṭiṟ cērkka vēṇṭuvatillaiyām.
uraiyāciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar pala nūlkaḷukku urai kaṇṭavar.  avar tam paṉṉūl uraiyoppaic cuṭṭic celkiṟār.  attaku kuṟippoṉṟum iḷampūraṇar uraiyil iṉmai, ikkaruttiṉ akaccāṉṟiṉmaic cāṉṟē.  oru taṉippāṭal ceyti koṇṭu im muṭivukku varutal cālātu.  `maṇak kuṭi puriyāṉ' eṉpatu `maṇakkuṭavar' peyarākalām.  āṉāl `maṇakkuṭi puriyarām avarē iḷampūraṇar' eṉpataṟku appāṭal cāṉṟākātu.  maṟṟum uraiyāciriyarākiya iḷampūraṇa aṭikaḷ eṉak kūṟum aṭiyārkku nallār maṇakkuṭava rākiya iḷampūraṇa aṭikaḷ eṉṟu kūṟat tavaṟār. ēṉeṉil uraiyāciri yar eṉṉum potuppeyariṉum avar kuṭippeyar viḷakkamāṉataṉṟō!
tolkāppiya iḷampūraṇarurai muṟṟākak kiṭaittuḷatu.  atil acciṟappup pāyiram illai.  tirukkuṟaḷ maṇakkuṭavaruraiyum muṟṟāka vāyttuḷatu.  atilum acciṟappup pāyiram illai.  citaivuṟṟa nūlāyiṉ taṉittuk kiṭaikka - piṟar uraikkaṇ kaṇṭeṭukka - muṟai yuṇṭu.  aṉṉavakai etuvum illāc  ciṟappup pāyiraṅ koṇṭu muṭivukku varutal takuvatu aṉṟu.
iḷampūraṇar tirukkuṟaḷ atikārat talaippukkut tarum poruḷurai, maṇakkuṭava ruraiyoṭum porunti niṟpataik kāṭṭi īruraiyum avaruraiyē eṉpar. īruraiyum poruntāvuraiyum uṇmai yāl vēṟuraiyām eṉpārkku maṟumoḻi illāmai kaṇkūṭu.
tuṟavāvatu taṉṉuṭaiya poruḷaip paṟṟaṟat tuṟattal eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (po. 75).
tuṟavāvatu oruvaṉ tavam paṇṇāniṉṟa kālattu yātāyiṉum oru toṭarppāṭu uḷatāyiṉum ataṉaip paṟṟaṟat tuṟattal eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ maṇakkuṭavarurai (ati.tuṟavu).
oppiyavuraiyai appaṭi appaṭiyē ēṟṟukkoṇṭu pōṟṟuva tum, oppā iṭattu maṭṭum tam uraiyum viḷakkamum tarutalum urai marapu ākaliṉ iḷampūraṇattaik kaṟṟa maṇakkuṭavar tam uraiyil avvuraiyaip pōṟṟik koṇṭār eṉpatu poruntuvatām.
iḷampūraṇar uraiyil kāṇum eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷait tokai yiṭṭuk kāṇpavar, tirukkuṟaḷ maṇakkuṭavar uraiyai ivaruraiyeṉak koḷḷār eṉpatu teḷivu.
iḷampūraṇar urai vaḻiyē naṉṉūlār palappala nūṟpākkaḷai iyaṟṟi yuḷḷār. ākupeyar eṉṟa aḷavāṉē kuṟittār tolkāp piyar(co.110).  ataṉai “ākupeyar eṉṟa poruṇmai eṉṉaiyeṉiṉ oṉṟaṉpeyar oṉṟaṟkāy niṟṟal eṉṟavāṟu” eṉṟār iḷampūraṇar.  ataṉaiyē naṉṉūlār “oṉṟaṉ peyarāṉ ataṟkiyai piṟitait toṉmuṟai yuraippaṉa āku peyarē” eṉa nūṟpāvākkik koṇṭār (peyar. 33). ivvāṟu iḷampūraṇak koṭai mikak koṇṭu viḷaṅkiyatu naṉṉūlākaliṉ, annūlār kālattukku muṉṉavar iḷampūraṇarāvar. naṉṉūlāraip puranta cīyakaṅkaṉ kālam ki.pi. 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉpar.  eṉavē akkālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭavar iḷampūraṇar eṉka. puṟapporuḷ veṇpā mālaiyil iruntu iḷampūraṇar mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.  ākaliṉ ammālai tōṉṟiya 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṟpaṭṭavar ākiṟār iḷampūraṇar.  mēlum paraṇiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ, (eḻut. 125, 248) eṉa varuvatu koṇṭu kūṭal caṅkamattup paraṇi koṇṭa vīrarācēn tiraṉ kālattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭavar eṉat tērntu 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉpar.
iḷampūraṇar cōṇāṭṭaic cērntavar eṉṟum, vēḷāṇ kuṭiyiṉar eṉṟum kūṟuvar.  ivar cōṇāṭṭaic colvatuṭaṉ malāṭu maḻanāṭu mutaliya nāṭukaḷaiyum cēramāṉ malaiyamāṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ mutaliya vēntarkaḷaiyum kuṟikkiṟār. uṟaiyūraik  kūṟum ivar karuvūr, maruvūr, kuḻippāṭi, potiyil eṉpavaṟṟaiyum kuṟikkiṟār.  ivar pārvai tamiḻakap pārvaiyāka iruntatu mikat teḷivāka uḷḷatu.
kaivāykkāl eṉpatu cōṇāṭṭu vaḻakku; iṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvatu eṉpar.  atu pāṇṭināṭṭum iṉṟum vaḻakkil uḷḷatē.  ‘kōṭiṉṟu ceviyiṉṟu’ eṉpatu koṇṭu innāḷilum aṅku avvaḻak kuṇmaiyaik kuṟippar.  āṉāl ataṉaik kūṟumiṭattēyē (col. 216)
‘kōṭiṉṟu ceviyiṉṟu’
‘kōṭila ceviyila’
‘kōṭuṭaiya ceviyuṭaiya’
‘kōṭuṭaittu ceviyuṭaittu’
eṉa uṇmaiyum iṉmaiyum aṭukkik kūṟuvar.  ivarai uṇmaip pāṟpaṭuttuvatum, iṉmaippāṟpaṭuttuvatum vēṇṭiṟṟaṉṟu.  tamiḻnāṭṭu vaḻakku muḻutuṟa aṟinta ivarkku evveṭuttukkāṭṭu muntu niṟkiṟatō ataṉaik kūṟuvar eṉak koḷḷalām.
‘num nāṭu yātu eṉṟāl tamiḻnāṭu eṉṟal’ eṉac ceppum viṉāvum vaḻuvātu varuvataṟkuk kūṟuvatum (col. 13) ‘tamiḻnāṭṭu mūvēntarum vantār’ eṉpatum (col. 33) ‘tamiḻnāṭṭu mūvarum vantār’ eṉpatum (co.250) ‘puliviṟ keṇṭai’ eṉpatum(col. 411) iḷam pūraṇariṉ tamiḻ nila muḻutuṟu pārvaiyaiyē cuṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.  mēlum, “nāyai ñamali eṉpa pūḻi nāṭṭār eṉṟakkāl accol ellā nāṭṭārum paṭṭāṅ kuṇarār; nāy eṉpataṉaiyāyiṉ evvet ticai nāṭṭārum uṇarpa eṉpatu”, eṉṟu kūṟum ivar uraiyāl (col. 392) nāṭu taḻuvip paṭṭāṅkuṇarac ceytalē ivar perum pārvai eṉka.
iḷampūraṇar urainayaṅkaḷ
aruñcollukkup poruḷ kūṟutal :
‘kuyiṉ eṉpatu mēkam’ (e. 336), ‘miṉ eṉpatu ōr toḻilumuṇṭu poruḷumuṇṭu’ (e. 346), ‘aḻaṉeṉpatu piṇam’ (e. 355) ‘mūṅkā eṉpatu kīri’ (po. 550) navvi-puḷḷimāṉ (po. 556) ‘karākameṉpatu karaṭi’     (po. 56) ivvāṟu aruñcoṟporuḷ vēṇṭumiṭatturaikkiṉṟār. 
kōyil eṉpatā? kōvil eṉpatā? eṉiṉ iraṇṭum cariyē eṉpār uḷar. avaṟṟuḷ `kōyil' eṉpatē cariyāṉatu eṉpatai ‘illoṭu  kiḷappiṉ iyaṟkaiyākum’ eṉṉum nūṟpāvil (e. 294) teḷivākkukiṟār.
‘kaḵṟu’ eṉpatu uruvu. ‘cuḵṟu’ eṉpatu icai eṉpatait tērntu colkiṟār (e. 40).
‘tapu’ eṉpatu paṭuttuc colla `nīcā' eṉat taṉviṉaiyām eṉavum, ataṉai eṭuttuc colla `nī oṉṟaṉaic cāvi' eṉap piṟaviṉaiyām eṉavum acaiyaḻuttam (yauunavē) kāṭṭi viḷakkukiṟār (e. 76).
aḵṟiṇai eṉpatu altiṇai. allatum atuvē, tiṇaiyum atuvē eṉap pirittuk kāṭṭi viḷakkukiṟār (co. 2).
cēri eṉpatu palar iruppatumaṉ. āyiṉum āṇṭuc cila pārppaṉakkuṭi uḷavēl ataṉaip pārppaṉaccēri eṉpatu; iḵtu uyartiṇaikkaṇ talaimai paṟṟi vantatu eṉkiṟār (co. 49).
“pala pottakam kiṭanta vaḻi oruvaṉ ēvalāḷaṉaip pārttu, pottakaṅ koṇṭu vā’ eṉṟāl, avaṉ oru pottakaṅ koṇṭu vanta viṭattut tāṉ karutiya pottakam aṉṟeṉil ‘maṟṟaiyatu koṇā’ eṉṉum; eṉṟakkāl ik koṇarntataṉai oḻikkum col ik koṇarnta pottakam cuṭṭiṟṟākalāṉ koṇarntataṉai oḻikkuñ cuṭṭu nilai ataṉai oḻittu oḻintateṉṟu avviṉat tallatu piṟitoṉṟu kuṟittatu kollō eṉiṟ kuṟiyā; maṟṟu ap potta kattuḷ oṉṟē piṉṉum kuṟittatu eṉappaṭum” eṉpataṉ vaḻiyāka ōrilakkaṇam kūṟuvatuṭaṉ puttakam eṉpataṉ   cevviya vaṭivattaiyum nilaippaṭuttukiṟār.
caṅkattār nāḷil ‘uḷaṟutal’ eṉpatu kūntalai ularttutal poruḷ tantatu. apporuḷai iḷampūraṇar kālattil ‘ulaṟutal’ eṉpatu taralāyiṟṟu eṉpatai, “ulaṟṟat tiṟamiṉṟip payiṉṟār oru cāṉṟār mayir nīṭṭi ulaṟi niṉṟāraik kaṇṭu oruvaṉ, emperumāṉ ulaṟi niṉṟīrāl eṉṟakkāl vāḷātē ulaṟiṉēṉ eṉṉaṟka, itu kāraṇattāl ulaṟiṉēṉ eṉka. itu taṉakku uṟṟaturaittatu” eṉpataṉ vaḻiyāka aṟiya vaikkiṟār (co. 56).
piṟaroṭu toṭarpu illāṉaik ‘keḻīiyili’ eṉpatum             (co. 57) toḻil ceyyum ēvalāṭṭiyait ‘toḻīi’ eṉpatum          (co. 122) ariya collāṭciyām.
‘aṇṇāttēri’ eṉpatai ivar eṭuttukkāṭṭuvatait tiruvaṇṇā malaiyakattu ēri eṉak karutturaittār uḷar. atu ‘vāṉam pārtta ēri’ eṉpatām. āṟu, kāl āyavaṟṟiṉ nīr varattiṉṟi vāṉam pārttu irukkum ēriyē appeyariyatām (e. 134). ‘aṇṇāttal’  ‘aṇṇāntu nīr kuṭittal’ eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷaik koṇṭu aṟika. “aṇṇāttal ceyyā taḷaṟu” eṉṟār vaḷḷuvar. tiṭṭāttukkuḷam eṉṟu piṟar kūṟuvatu mēṭupaṭṭa kuḷam eṉṟātal karutuka.
“kāmappuṇarcci eṉiṉum, iyaṟkaippuṇarcci eṉiṉum, muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci eṉiṉum, teyvappuṇarcci eṉiṉum okkumeṉa” oru poruṭ palapeyaraic cuṭṭi aiyamakaṟṟukiṟār (kaḷaviyal muṉṉurai).
ceṅkaṭumoḻi eṉpataik “koṭiya kaṭumoḻiyēyaṉṟi maṉattiṉāl iṉiyaḷākik kūṟum kaṭumoḻi” eṉa nayamuṟa viḷakkukiṟār (po. 112).
ottateṉap potuvil tōṉṟum iraṇṭaṉ nuṇṇiya vēṟupāṭṭaiyum aritāka viḷakkic celkiṟār.
“maṭam eṉpataṟkum pētaimai eṉpataṟkum vēṟupāṭu eṉṉai eṉiṉ, maṭam eṉpatu poruṇmai aṟiyātu tiriyakkōṭal; pētaimai eṉpatu kēṭṭataṉai uyttuṇarātu meyyāk kōṭal” (po. 248).
‘aivakai yaṭiyum’ (cey. 48) eṉpatu āciriyappāviṟkuriya ilakkaṇa muṭaittāyiṉum ōcaiyiṉmaiyāṉ āciriyam eṉappaṭātu nūleṉap paṭum eṉṟu koḷka (po. 391).
“kalattalāvatu muttum pavaḷamum nīlamum māṇikkamum viraviṉāṟ pōṟal. mayakkamāvatu poṉṉum veḷḷiyum cempum urukki oṉṟātal pōṟal” eṉpavaṟṟaik kāṇka.
eṭuttukkoṇṭa oṉṟai uvamaiyāl viḷakkum nayattaiyum aritāka mēṟkoḷkiṟār iḷampūraṇar.
ciṟappup pāyirattil “pāyirameṉpatu puṟavurai. aṟanūṟkup puṟavuraiyēl atu kēṭṭu eṉṉai payaṉeṉiṉ kaṟṟu valla kaṇavaṟkuk kaṟpuṭaiyāḷ pōla iṉṟiyamaiyāc ciṟappiṟṟāyum, tiruvamainta mānakarattiṟku uruvamainta vāyiṉ māṭam pōla alaṅkāramātaṟ ciṟappiṟṟāyum varutalāṉum pāyiram kēḷātē nūl kēṭkumēyeṉil kuṟicci pukkamāṉpōla māṇākkaṉ iṭarppaṭumākalāṉum pāyiram kēṭṭal payaṉuṭaittāyiṟṟu” eṉa ōriṭattē mūṉṟuvamaikaḷ vaittu viḷakkukiṟār.
ikara ukaraṅkaḷukkum kuṟṟiyalikara kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷukkum oliyaḷavaiyāl vēṟupāṭu uṇṭāyiṉum uyir eṉṉum peyarīṭṭil vēṟupāṭu illai eṉpārāy, “cantaṉakkōl kuṟukiṉaviṭattup pirappaṅkōl ākātu. atupōla ikara ukaraṅkaḷ kuṟukiṉaviṭattum avai uyir ākaṟpālaṉa” eṉkiṟār (e. 2).
uyirmey olikkum vakaiyai viḷakkumpōtu “meyyum uyirum muṉṉum piṉṉum peṟa niṟkum eṉṟamaiyāl akkūṭṭam pālum nīrum pōla uṭaṉ kalantataṉṟi, viral nuṉikaḷ talaippeytāṟpōla vēṟu niṉṟu kalantaṉa alla eṉpatu peṟutum” eṉkiṟār (e. 18).
‘itaḻ pōṟalāṉ vāy, itaḻ eṉappaṭṭatu’ eṉa vāykku itaḻ eṉap peyar vanta poruttattai uvamaiyāl viḷakkukiṟār (e. 83).
kalippāviṟkuriya tuḷḷalōcai paṟṟik kūṟumpōtu, “tuḷḷuta lāvatu oḻukunaṭaittaṉṟi iṭaiyiṭaiyuyarntu varutal; kaṉṟu tuḷḷiṟ ṟeṉṟāṟpōlak koḷka” eṉkiṟār (po. 387). ivvāṟē piṟavum varum uvamaikaḷum uḷa.
uvamaiyiṉ payaṉ, “pulaṉ allātaṉa pulaṉātalum, alaṅkāra mākik kēṭṭārkkiṉpam payattalum”, eṉṟu uvamaiyiyal mukappil kūṟum avartam uvamaikaḷāl tam karuttai meyppikkiṟār eṉka.
uraivaḷam 
iḷampūraṇar uraivaḷam, vēṇṭumiṭattu vēṇṭumaḷavāṉ viḷaṅki nalam cērkkiṉṟatu.
eḻuttai eṭṭuvakaiyālum eṭṭiṟanta palavakaiyālum uṇartti ṉār eṉa avaṟṟaik kuṟippatum (e. mukappu) ceppuvakai āṟu eṉpatum, viṉāvakai aintu eṉpatum (co. 13) takuti, vaḻakku ākiyavaṟṟaip paṟṟip pakutturaippatum (co. 17) mutaliyavai naṉṉūlār mutaliya piṉṉū lōrkku utaviya uraivaḷaṅkaḷām.
“ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉavē” eṉṉum nūṟpāvukku (co. 152) “peyarccollum viṉaiccollum iṭaiccollum uriccollum ākiya ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟiyātu nillā” eṉa urai kūṟukiṉṟār. ‘poruḷ kuṟittu niṟkum’ eṉṉāmal, poruḷ kuṟiyātu nillā eṉṟu īretir maṟaikaḷāl uṭaṉ pāṭṭuk karuttai valiyuṟuttuvatu, “ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉavē; avvāṟu kuṟiyātatu collaṉṟu” eṉpatai uṟutipaṭak kūṟuvataṟkēyām.
mukkālaṅkaḷaiyum cuṭṭum iḷampūraṇar, “iṟappāvatu toḻilatu kaḻivu; nikaḻvāvatu toḻil toṭaṅkappaṭṭu muṟṟup peṟāta nilaimai; etirvāvatu toḻil piṟavāmai” eṉkiṟār. eḷimaiyum arumaiyum mikka kuṟippukaḷ ivai.
kaikkiḷai ‘ciṟumai uṟavu’ eṉṟu kūṟavēṇṭumeṉak karutukiṟār uraiyāciriyar. ataṉai, “kai eṉpatu ciṟumai paṟṟi varum; atu tattam kuṟippiṟ poruḷ ceyvatōr iṭaiccol; kiḷai eṉpatu uṟavu; perumaiyillāta talaimakkaḷ uṟavu eṉṟavāṟu, kaikkuṭai, kaiyēṭu, kaivāḷ, kaioliyal, kaivāykkāl eṉap perumaiyillātavaṟṟai vaḻaṅkupavākaliṉ” eṉa viḷakkiyamaikiṟār (po. 1). kai ciṟumaip poruṭṭātalai niṟuvutaṟku naṭaimuṟaic cāṉṟukaḷ palavaṟṟai aṭukkukiṟārē! poruḷ viḷakkam ceytalilum avarkkirunta paṟṟutaliṉ viḷaivu tāṉē itu!
pirivu eṉṉum uripporuḷaik kūṟum tolkāppiyar, ‘koṇṭu talaik kaḻitalum, pirintavaṇ iraṅkalum’ eṉa iraṇṭākap pakuttuk kūṟukiṉṟār. itaṉai, “koṇṭutalaik kaḻitalāvatu uṭaṉ koṇṭu peyartal. atu, nilam peyartaliṉ puṇartaliṉ aṭaṅkāmai yāṉum, uṭaṉ koṇṭu peyartaliṉ piritaliṉ aṭaṅkāmaiyāṉum vēṟu ōtappaṭṭatu” eṉa nūlāciriyar karuttait teḷivu ceykiṟār (po. 17).
“iḷamai tīr tiṟam” eṉa āciriyar kūṟiṉārāyiṉum ataṉai, “iḷamai tīr tiṟamāvatu; iḷamai nīṅkiya tiṟattiṉkaṇ nikaḻvatu. atu mūvakaippaṭum: talaimakaṉ mutiyaṉākit talaimakaḷ iḷaiyaḷātalum, talaimakaḷ mutiyaḷākit talaimakaṉ iḷaiyaṉātalum, ivviruvarum iḷamaip paruvam nīṅkiya vaḻi aṟattiṉmēl maṉam nikaḻtaliṉṟik kāmattiṉ mēl maṉam nikaḻtalum eṉa” eṉkiṟār. “eṇṇi uraikārar īvār” eṉpatai meyppippavai ittakaiyavai (po. 54). “koṭuppōr ēttik koṭāarp paḻittal” eṉpataṉ viḷakkamum kāṇka (po. 87).
‘ēṟiya maṭaṟṟiṟam’ mutalāka āciriyar collum uṭaṉpāṭukaḷai ‘ēṟā maṭaṟṟiṟam’ mutalāka etirmaṟaiyākkik koṇṭu iḷampūraṇar kūṟuvatu viyappu mikkatu (po. 55).
“maṟuvil ceyti mūvakaik kālamum neṟiyiṉ āṟṟiya aṟivaṉ tēyamum” eṉpataṟku, “kuṟṟamaṟṟa ceyalaiyuṭaiya maḻaiyum paṉiyum veyilumākiya mūvakaik kālattiṉaiyum neṟiyiṉāṟ poṟutta aṟivaṉ pakkamum” eṉap poruḷ varaintu, “iṟanta kālam mutalākiya mūṉṟu kālattiṉaiyum neṟiyiṉāl tōṟṟiya aṟivaṉ pakkam eṉṟālō eṉiṉ, atu muḻutuṇarntōrkkallatu pulappaṭāmaiyiṉ atu poruḷaṉ ṟeṉka” eṉa viḷakkukiṟār. tām cuṭṭiya poruḷē poruḷeṉap paṉṉirupaṭalac cāṉṟu kāṭṭukiṟār. “aṟivaṉ eṉṟatu kaṇiyaṉai. mūvakaik kālamum neṟiyiṉāl āṟṟutalāvatu pakalum iravum iṭai viṭāmal ākāyattaip pārttu āṇṭu nikaḻum villum miṉṉum ūrkōḷum tūmamum mīṉ vīḻvum kōḷ nilaiyum maḻai nilaiyum piṟavum pārttup payaṉkūṟal” eṉa mēl viḷakkam ceykiṟār (po. 74). ettaku nākarikamāka maṟukkiṟār. avarurai mutaluraiyākaliṉ maṟup purai mikak kūṟavēṇṭum nilaiyillai. eṉiṉum palvēṟu pāṭaṅ kaḷum uraikaḷum paṟpalariṭattuk kēṭṭirukkakkūṭum. avaṟṟai uṭkoṇṭu oru viḷakkam kūṟukiṟār.
“paṉṉiru paṭalattuḷ karantaikkaṇ puṇṇoṭu varutal mutalāka vēṟupaṭac cilatuṟaikaḷ kūṟiṉārātaliṉ, puṇpaṭutal māṟṟōr ceyta maṟattuṟaiyākaliṉ aḵtu ivarkku māṟākak kūṟalum mayaṅkak kūṟalu mām. ēṉaiyavum ivvāṟu mayaṅkak kūṟalum kuṉṟak kūṟalum mikaipaṭak kūṟalum āyavāṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉ perukumātaliṉ uyttuṇarntu kaṇṭu koḷka” eṉa amaiti kāṭṭukiṉṟār. ākaliṉ maṟuppuk kūṟutalil iḷampūraṇar peritum maṉaṅ koṇṭilar eṉpatum, kaṟpār tam ‘nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulattāl’ kaṇṭukoḷvār eṉa amaintār eṉpatum viḷaṅkum.
talaivikkuk kaḷavil kūṟṟu nikaḻumiṭaṅkaḷ eṉpatu kuṟikkum tuṟaikaḷuḷ oṉṟu ‘kaṭṭurai iṉmai’ eṉpatu. ataṟkuc cāṉṟu vēṇṭumō? ‘kaṭṭurai iṉmaikkuk kūṟṟu nikaḻātu’ eṉpatum kūṟukiṉṟār. evarēṉum ataṟkuc cāṉṟu illaiyē eṉa aiyuṟuvarō eṉpatai uṉṉitta kuṟippu itu (po. 109).
‘ciṟṟāṟu pāyntāṭum’ eṉap paṉṉīraṭip paḵṟoṭai veṇpā oṉṟaik kāṭṭukiṟār iḷampūraṇar (po. 123). atil, “itu paṉṉiraṇ ṭaṭiyāṉ peruvallattaik kūṟa vanta iṉṉicaip paḵṟoṭai veṇpā” eṉṟu kuṟippu varaikiṟār. ‘peruvallam’ eṉṟa peyar illākkāl ip pāṭaṟ poruḷ evvāṟṟāṉum kāṇaṟkaritu. ātalāl ‘tiṟavu’ vēṇ ṭuṅkāl tantu cellum `tiṟam' iḷampūraṇar uṭaimaiyāka iruntuḷatu eṉalām.
avarē, ‘cūttirattāṟ poruḷ viḷaṅkum’ eṉavum (po. 585) viṭuttuc celkiṟār.
cila vaḻakkāṟukaḷ 
iḷampūraṇar kālattu vaḻakkāṟukaḷ cila avar urai vaḻiyē aṟiya vāykkiṉṟaṉa.
eṭṭi, kāviti eṉṉum paṭṭaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkuvatupōl ‘nampi’ eṉṉum paṭṭam vaḻaṅkutal (e. 155), makappālukkāka āṭu vaḷarttal          (e. 220), puḷiccōṟu ākkutal (e. 247), pēyōṭṭutal (co. 312), veḷḷāṭai makaḷir uṭuttal (co. 412), tainnīrāṭal (co. 50), kuṟittatoru nāḷil kaṟantapāl muḻuvataiyum aṟattiṟkeṉa ākkutal (co. 50), āṭu māṭukaḷ tiṉavutīrat tēyttuk koḷḷa `ātīṇṭu kuṟṟi' naṭutal (co. 50), cēvaṟ caṇṭai naṭattutal (co. 61), oṟṟikkalam (āvaṇam) eḻututal (co. 76), nellaṭittut tūṟṟum kaḷattiṟkut ‘taṭṭuppuṭai’ eṉappeyar vaḻaṅkutal (co. 77), veṟṟilaiyum pūñceṭiyum naṭutal (co. 110), poḻutiṉ oru pakutiyaik kūṟu eṉak kūṟutal (po. 9) mutaliya vaḻakkāṟukaḷ āṅkāṅku aṟiya varukiṉṟaṉa.
paltuṟaip pulamai
niṟuttaḷattal, peytaḷattal, nīṭṭiyaḷattal, teṟittaḷattal tēṅkamukantaḷattal, cāttiyaḷattal, eṇṇiyaḷattal eṉa eḻuvakai aḷavaikaḷaik kūṟuvatum (e. 7), “kaṭuvum kaipiḻi yeṇṇeyum peṟṟamaiyāṉ mayir nalla āyiṉa” eṉṟum, “eruppeytu iḷaṅkaḷai kaṭṭu nīr kāl yāttamaiyāl paiṅkūḻ nalla” eṉṟu kūṟuvatum         (co. 21, 22) “vilaṅkum maraṉum puḷḷum uḷḷa nōy uṟṟāṟku maṉakkuṟaikku maṟutalai māṟṟam kūṟuvaṉa pōlum kuṟippiṉa” eṉalum (co. 416) vāḷāṉum tōḷāṉum vēṟalaṉṟi, “collāṉ vēṟalum pāṭṭāṉ vēṟalum kūttāṉ vēṟalum cūtāṉ vēṟalum takarppōr pūḻppōr eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ vēṟalum” eṉap pōrvakai aṭukkutalum (po. 74) (aṭiyar) “akattiṇaikku uriyarallarō eṉiṉ, akattiṇai yāvaṉa aṟattiṉ vaḻāmalum poruḷiṉ vaḻāmalum, iṉpattiṉ vaḻāmalum, iyalal vēṇṭum; avaiyellām piṟarkkuk kuṟṟēval ceyvārkkuc ceytal aritākalāṉum, avar nāṇuk kuṟaipāṭuṭaiya rākalāṉum, kuṟippaṟiyātu vēṭkai vaḻiyē cārakkarutuvarākalāṉum, iṉpam iṉitu naṭattuvār piṟarēval ceyyātār” eṉpataṉāṉum  “ivar puṟap poruṭ kuriyarāyiṉār eṉka” eṉpataṉāṉum piṟavaṟṟālum iḷampūraṇar paltuṟaip pulamai naṉkaṟiya varukiṉṟatu.
“cāttaṉ kaiyeḻutumāṟu vallaṉ; ataṉāl taṉ āciriyaṉ uvakkum; tantai uvakkum” eṉpatāl uraiyāciriyar kaiyeḻuttaḻakai evvaḷavu virumpiṉār eṉpatu viḷaṅkum (co. 40).
“maṉaiviyaik kātalikkum; tāyai uvakkum” eṉpavaṟṟāl vāḻviyal nuṇukkattai evvaḷavu tērntiruntār iḷampūraṇar eṉpatu viḷaṅkum (co. 72).
maṇantapiṉṉarum maṉaiviyaik kātalikkalām eṉavum, oruttiyai maṇanta piṉṉarum taṉ  tāyai uvantu pārāṭṭalām eṉavum kūṟal attakaiya tūya tuṟavar illaṟam nallaṟamākat tikaḻa vaḻikāṭṭavum vallār eṉpatai meyppippatām.
- irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ
tamiḻvaḷam -19, tamiḻar vāḻviyal ilakkaṇam 
nūlil iruntu eṭukkappaṭṭatu


pāyiram
teḷivurai
vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari
āyiṭait
tamiḻkūṟu nallulakattu
vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum āyiru mutaliṉ
eḻuttuñ collum poruḷum nāṭic
centamiḻ iyaṟkai civaṇiya nilattoṭu
muntunūl kaṇṭu muṟaippaṭa eṇṇip
pulantokut tōṉē pōkkaṟu paṉuval
nilantaru tiruviṟ pāṇṭiyaṉ avaiyattu
aṟaṅkarai nāviṉ nāṉmaṟai muṟṟiya
ataṅkōṭ ṭācāṟku ariltapat terintu
mayaṅkā marapiṉ eḻuttumuṟai kāṭṭi
malkunīr varaippiṉ aintiram niṟainta
tolkāp piyaṉeṉat taṉpeyar tōṟṟip
palpukaḻ niṟutta paṭimai yōṉē.
teḷivurai:
vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari - vaṭakkil amainta vēṅkaṭa malaikkum, teṟkil amainta kumarimalaikkum.
ā iṭai - iṭaiyē amainta tākiya
tamiḻ kūṟu nal ulakattu - tamiḻ vaḻaṅkum nalla nilapparappiṭattu
vaḻakkum ceyyuḷum ā iru mutaliṉ - makkaḷ vaḻakku nūlvaḻakku ākiya iraṇṭiṉ appaṭaiyilum,
eḻuttum collum poruḷum nāṭi - eḻuttaiyum collaiyum poruḷaiyum nuṭpamāka ārāyntu,
centamiḻ iyaṟkai civaṇiya nilattoṭu - cemmai yāy iyaṟkaiyōṭu porunti amainta nilattiṉ iyalpoṭu
muntu nūl kaṇṭu - tam kālattukku muṉṉai irunta pulamaiyāḷarkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa ilakkiya ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷait tokuttup payiṉṟu,
muṟaippaṭa eṇṇi - mēlum palavakaiyālum nūṉ muṟaiyai ārāyntu,
pulam tokuttōṉē - muṉṉavar pulamai etuvum viṭupāṭu illāmal tokuttu amaittōṉ taṉ,
pōkku aṟu paṉuval - kuṟṟamaṟṟa nūliṉai,
nilantaru tiruviṟpāṇṭiyaṉ avaiyattu - nilantaru tiruviṟ pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉpāṉ peyarāl amainta avaiyiṉ kaṇ,
aṟaṅkarai nāviṉ - aṟaṅkūṟal aṉṟip piṟa kūṟā nāviṉai yuṭaiyavaṉum
nāṉmaṟai muṟṟiya - akkālattil tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiya nāṉku maṟaikaḷaiyum tērntaṟinta mutirpulamaiyuṭaiyavaṉum ākiya
ataṅkōṭṭu ācāṟku - ataṅkōṭṭu ācāṉ eṉpāṉukku,
aril tapa terintu - ekkuṟṟamum illaiyākat teriyac ceytu,
mayaṅkāmarapiṉ - evarkkum enta aiyamum tiripum ēṟpaṭāta vakaiyil,
eḻuttu muṟai kāṭṭi - nūl eḻutappaṭṭa muṟaiyait teḷivuṟa viḷakkik kāṭṭi
malkunīr varaippiṉ - nīr nirampiya kaṭal cūḻnta ulakil
aintiram niṟainta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉa - mēṉmāntarkkuriya aimpula aṭakkamum oruṅkē amainta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉṟu
taṉ peyar tōṟṟi - taṉ peyarai viḷaṅkac ceytu
pal pukaḻ niṟutta paṭimaiyōṉ -palvakaip pukaḻukkum nilaipēṟṟāḷaṉāy viḷaṅkiya tavapperuntaṉmaiyaṉ āvaṉ.
- eṉpatu itaṉ teḷivurai.
viḷakkam:
vaṭavēṅkaṭam eṉṟatu vaṭakkē amainta vēṅkaṭamalaiyai. caṅkac cāṉṟōr vēṅkaṭattu umpar (appāl) moḻi peyar tēyam eṉa 26 iṭaṅkaḷil kuṟittuḷar eṉpatu eṇṇattakkatu.
vaṭavēṅkaṭam eṉṟatu `malai’ ātalāl, teṉkumari eṉpatum malaiyē yām! ēṉeṉil,
“mēlaic cērik kōḻi veṉṟatu eṉṟāl, kīḻaiccērik kōḻi tōṟṟatu” eṉpatu paḻaiya uraikaḷiṉ vaḻiyē aṟivatu. `kōḻi’kkuk kōḻiyaic cuṭṭutalē marapu. mēlūr - kīḻūr; teṟkut teru - vaṭakkutteru; teṉmoḻi - vaṭamoḻi eṉpavai aṟika.
paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmalai aṭukkattuk
kumarikkōṭum koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
vaṭaticaik kaṅkaiyum imayamum koṇṭu
teṉticai yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ
eṉpatu cilappatikāram, kaṭalūḻiyāl kumarimalai, paṉmalaittoṭar paḵṟuḷiyāṟu, aḻintamai teḷivām. iḻanta maṇṇai oppap perukkavē `nilantaru tiru’vuṭaiyāṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉām.
`paḵṟuḷi’ āṟu aḻintupaṭa, appeyar pōṟṟum vakaiyāl nāñcil nāṭṭu vaḷḷuvaṉ poṟṟaip pakutiyil `paṟaḷi’ yāṟu eṉṟa oṉṟuṇmai iṉṟum aṟiyalām.
tamiḻ kūṟu nallulakam eṉpatu tamiḻ vaḻaṅkum nāṭu eṉpatām. “taṇ tamiḻ vēlit tamiḻ nāṭṭakam” eṉṟu paripāṭal itaṉaik kūṟum. oru moḻi, uyirāṟu pōl eṉṟum viḷaṅka vēṇṭumāṉāl, makkaḷ vaḻakku, nūlvaḻakku ākiya iraṇṭu vaḻakkum iṇaintu iyaṅkal vēṇṭum. makkaḷ vaḻakku oḻintāl, ammoḻi uyiruḷḷa moḻiyākak karutappaṭamāṭṭātu. ātalāl, iruvakai vaḻakkukaḷaiyum āyntu nūl ceytār.
eḻuttu, col ākiya iraṇṭum moḻimarapu kāppavai. avviraṇṭaiyē maṟṟai moḻikaḷ koṇṭirukka, tolkāppiyar poruḷ ilakkaṇamum ceytamai, makkaḷ vāḻviyal kāppatām. moḻik kāvalāl makkaḷ vāḻvum, makkaḷ vāḻvāl moḻiyum piṉṉip piṇaiyum ciṟappiṉatu ippakuppām.
`muntu nūl’ eṉpatu tolkāppiyar kālattukku muṟpaṭṭatāy, avar payila vāyttatāy amainta nūlkaḷām!
`akattiyam muntu nūl’ eṉpār kāṭṭum nūṟpākkaḷ eṉa oru `tiraṭṭu nūl’ veḷivantuḷatu. `pērakattiyat tiraṭṭu’ eṉpatu ataṉ peyar. annūṟpākkaḷ aṉaittum uṟaiyūr muttuvīra upāttiyāyar eṉpār iyaṟṟiya `muttu vīriyam’ eṉṉum 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭu nūlai muṉvaittuk koṇṭu, annūliṉum vaṭacoṟkaḷ peruka oruvar uruvākkiya pōlinūl ākum. muttuvīriya nūṟpāvai muṉvaittu, akattiyar peyarāl viḷaṅkum nūṟpāvaip piṉvaittu `pērakattiyap perumpuraṭṭu’ eṉṟoru nūl yāṉ nāṟpatu āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉē eḻutiṉēṉ!
akattiyaṉ eṉṉum peyar pāṭṭu tokai ākiya 2381 pāṭalkaḷil oṉṟil tāṉum illai eṉpatē itaṉai meyppikkum. camatakkiṉiyār, tiraṇatūmākkiṉiyār, pāṇṭiyaṉ mākīrtti, ulōpā muttirai mutaliyavai aṉaittum, mutal tolkāppiya uraiyāciriyar iḷampūraṇa rālō piṟarālō kāṭṭappaṭāmal, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar puṉainta puṉaivām! pāṇṭiyaṉ mākīrtti 24 āyiram āṇṭu vīṟṟiruntāṉ eṉpatoṉṟu pōtātā, iṭṭukkaṭṭal eṉpataṟku! nampi akapporuḷ, puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālai, naṉṉūl mutalāyavai kūṟuvaṉa, muṉṉai nūlām tolkāppiyac cāṉṟu ākiviṭātu.

karakam kaviḻtta kāvirip pāvai mutaliyavai purāṇap puṉaivukaḷām ataṅkōṭu, viḷaṅkōṭu eṉpavai paḻañcēra nāṭṭakattatu! aṅkē kāppiyakkuṭi eṉṉum peyarōṭu ōr ūr uṇmaiyum eṇṇattakkatu. toṉmaiyāṉa marapukaḷaik kākkum nūlō eṉiṉ, tolkāppiya nūliṉ toṭakka iyal nūṉmarapu; niṟaivu iyal marapiyal; mēlum moḻimarapu, tokaimarapu, viḷimarapu eṉa iyaṟpeyarkaḷ uṇmaiyum,
māṟṟarum ciṟappiṉ marapu	(1500)
eṉṟum,
marapunilai tiriyiṉ piṟitupiṟi tākum	(1591)
eṉṟum,
marapunilai tirital ceyyuṭ killai	(1590)
eṉṟum,
moḻipeyarttu atarppaṭa yāttal	(1597)
eṉṟum, marapiṉaik kaṭṭik kāttum, putitu putitāka varum col - ākkum col - ākiyavaṟṟai, `moḻimarapu māṟāmal koḷka’ eṉpataṟku,
kaṭicol illai kālattup paṭiṉē	(935)
eṉṟum kūṟiyatuṭaṉ collākkum vakaiyaik `kiḷaviyākkam’ eṉa ōr iyalē vakuttuk kāṭṭiṉār tolkāppiyar.
collākka aṭiccol kāṭṭuvārāy, uriyiyal eṉṟu ōr iyal virivāka vaittār. uriyāvatu tōl! tol - tōl tōlām paiyuḷ, poṟi, pulaṉ ākiyavai tōṉṟi urukkoṇṭa teṇṇuka! ātalāl, `tol kāppu iyam’ eṉṟu paḻamarapukaḷaik kākkum muṟaiyai nayamākak kūṟum nūl eṉap peyariṭṭamaiyāl, avar tolkāppiyaṉ’ eṉat tam peyar tōṉṟac ceytavar āṉār.
maṟuppu, virippu eṉa amaiyiṉ nūl perukumākaliṉ, ivviḷakkam amaivatām. teḷivurai ātalāl eḷimaiyum curukkamum paḻaiyavuraip pārvaiyum putuppārvaik kuṟippum pōṟṟappaṭuvatu ivvuraiyām.
poruḷ: vaṭakkil amainta vēṅkaṭa malaikkum, teṟkil amainta kumarimalaikkum, iṭaiyē amaintatākiya, tamiḻ vaḻaṅkum nalla parappiṭattu makkaḷ vaḻakku nūlvaḻakku ākiya iraṇṭiṉ aṭippaṭaiyilum, eḻuttaiyum collaiyum poruḷaiyum nuṭpamāka ārāyntu, cemmaiyāy iyaṟkaiyoṭu porunti amainta nilattiṉ amaivoṭu, tam kālattukku muṉṉai irunta pulamaiyāḷarkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa ilakkiya ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷait tokuttup payiṉṟu, mēlum palavakaiyālum nūlmuṟaiyai ārāyntu, muṉṉavar pulamai etuvum viṭupaṭutal illāmal tokuttu amaittōṉ, taṉ kuṟṟamaṟṟa nūliṉai nilantaru tiruviṟ pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉpāṉ peyarāl amainta avaiyiṉ kaṇ, aṟaṅkūṟal aṉṟip piṟakūṟal illāta nāviṉaiyuṭaiyavaṉum akkālattil tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiya nāṉku maṟaikaḷaiyum tērntaṟinta mutirpulamaiyuṭaiyavaṉum ākiya ataṅkōṭṭu ācāṉ eṉpāṉukku ekkuṟṟamum illaiyākat teriyacceytu, evarkkum enta aiyamum tiripum ēṟpaṭāvakaiyil nūl eḻutappaṭṭa muṟaiyait teḷivuṟa viḷakkik kāṭṭi, nīr nirampiya kaṭal cūḻnta ulakil mēṉmāntarkkuriya aimpula aṭakkamum oruṅkē amainta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉṟu taṉ peyarai viḷaṅkac ceytu, palvakaip pukaḻukkum nilaipēṟṟāḷaṉāy viḷaṅkiya tavapperuntaṉmaiyaṉ āvaṉ.
itaṉ vaḻiyē piṉṉavar kaṇṭa pāyirattu ilakkaṇam!
1)	ākkiyōṉ peyar - tolkāppiyaṉ
2)	vaḻi- muṉṉaiyōr ceyta nūlvaḻi
3)	ellai - vaṭavēṅkaṭam, teṉkumari āyiṭait tamiḻ ulakam
4)	nūṟpeyar - tolkāppiyam
5)	yāppu - nūṟpā
6)	nutaliya poruḷ - eḻuttu col poruḷ
7)	kēṭpōr - ataṅkōṭṭācāṉ mutal avaiyōr
8)	payaṉ - iruvakai vaḻakkum aṟital
9) 	kālam - nilantaru tiruviṟpāṇṭiyaṉ kālam
10)	kaḷaṉ - pāṇṭiyaṉ avaiyam
11)	kāraṇam - paḻamarapukaḷaik kāttal
nūl nūṟpā
nūl eṉpatu paḻanāḷil ilakkaṇattaik kuṟippatākavē iruntatu. ceyyuḷai, ēḻuvakaiyākak kūṟuvār tolkāppiyar. aṭivaraiyaṟai yuṭaiyavai `pā’ eṉappaṭum. aṭivaraiyaṟai (aḷavu) illātavai āṟu vakaiyām. avaṟṟuḷ mutalāvatāka niṟpatu `nūliṉāṉa’ eṉpatu (1420, 1421) atu `nūṟpā’ eṉappaṭum.

ceyyuḷiyalil nūliṉ ilakkaṇam, nūṟpā ilakkaṇam mutaliya vaṟṟaik kūṟuvār.
āciriyar ilakkaṇattai nuvalvār (kūṟuvār)! ataṉai māṇavar kēṭpar! `nuval’ eṉpataṉ vaḻiyāka  `nūl’ vantatu.
iraṇṭu kuṟilkaḷ oru neṭilātal colliyal muṟai.
e-ṭu. `akal’ eṉpatu oru marappeyar. atu vaḷarntu kiḷaikaḷ virintu vīḻtukaḷ tōṉṟi maṇṇil ūṉṟi nīṇṭum akaṉṟum celvatāl akal ḻū āl āyatu; pakal ḻū pāl ākum. poḻutu ḻū pōḻtu, pōtu. viḻutu ḻū vīḻtu eṉṟu varuvaṉa mikap palavām.
`nūlē nuval vōṉ nuvalum tiṟaṉē’ eṉpatu naṉṉūl.
tolkāppiyattiṉ piṉ, nēminātam eṉṉum nūl uṇṭāyatu. atu tolkāppiyattai nōkka aḷavil ciṟitākaliṉ, `ciṉṉūl’ eṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉpiṉ tōṉṟiya ilakkaṇa nūl naṉṉūl; ataṉ piṉ toṉṉūl, teṉṉūl, iṉṉūl eṉpaṉa tōṉṟiṉa.
nūṟpāvai, ilakkaṇam kūṟa eṭuttuk koṇṭatu ēṉ eṉiṉ, collum ilakkaṇattukkuttaka irucīr, muccīr, nāṟcīr eṉa aṭikaḷ varalām. poruḷ virivukkuttaka muppataṭi nāṟpataṭi eṉa nīḷavum ākalām (1057, 1060).
cāyal meṉmai  (808)
aiviyap pākum  (868)
eṉa iraṇṭu cīrkaḷilum,
kaṟuppum civappum vekuḷip poruḷa
eṉa nāṉku cīr ōraṭiyākavum (855) nūṟpā varalām. maṟṟaip pāvakaiyil kuṟainta aṭiyaḷavu uṭaiyatu kuṟaṭpāvē. ataṉāl atu mutaṟpā eṉappaṭṭatu. ap pā vakaiyil pāṭiyavar tiruvaḷḷuvar ātalāl, mutaṟpāvalar eṉappaṭṭār.
- irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ,
tolkāppiyam teḷivurai eḻuttatikāram, pak.10-15








eḻuttatikāram - iḷampūraṇam
va.u.citamparampiḷḷai - patavurai
(1928)


tolkāppiyam - mutal patippurai
“tolkāppiyam” eṉṉum innūl tamiḻ moḻikkut talaiyāka viḷaṅkum oppuyarvāṟṟa ōr ilakkaṇam, iḵtu akattiyaṉatu paṉṉiru māṇākkaruḷ “tiraṇa tūmākkiṉi’ eṉṉum iyaṟpeyariṉaiyum, “poruntak kaṟṟup puraitapa vuṇarntōr nallicai niṟutta tolkāppiyaṉ” eṉṉum pukaḻppeyariṉaiyum koṇṭu viḷaṅkiya ōr muṉivaṉāl iyaṟṟappeṟṟatu. itaṟku mutal nūl akattiyaṉāl iyaṟṟappeṟṟa “akattiyam” eṉpa. innūl paṉṉīrāyiram varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ iyaṟṟappeṟṟateṉa vīracōḻiyattiṉ patippuraiyil tirumaṉ. ci.vai. tāmōtarampiḷḷai nilaināṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.
āṇ, peṇ, alikaḷai āṇpāl, peṇpāl, oṉṟaṉpāl vikutikaḷāl uṇarttuvatu tamiḻmoḻi oṉṟē. āṇpāl vikutiyaip porunti āṇaikkuṟiyāmalum, peṇpāl vikutiyaip poruntip peṇṇaik kuṟiyāmalum, oṉṟaṉpāl vikutiyaip porunti aliyaik kuṟiyāmalum niṟkum coṟkaḷ āriyam mutaliya moḻikaḷil eṇṇila uṇṭu. uyartiṇai, aḵṟiṇai eṉṉum tamiḻ ilakkaṇap pākupāṭu āriyam mutaliyavaṟṟil illai. nilattiṉ pākupāṭukaḷaiyum, avaṟṟiṉ uripporuḷ, karupporuḷ kaḷaiyum avaṟṟiṉ makkaḷatu oḻukkaṅkaḷaiyum kūṟuvatu tamiḻ ilakkaṇam oṉṟē.
ivvakait taṉic ciṟappupporuntiya ittamiḻ ilakkaṇat tuḷḷum āriyamoḻikaḷil cilavaṟṟaiyum, āriyar paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟaiyum ,āriyar koḷkaikaḷ cilavaṟṟaiyum, innūlāciriyaṉ nuḻaittiruttalai āṅkāṅkuk kāṇalām. āyiṉum, tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ muṟkālap paḻakka vaḻakka oḻukkaṅkaḷum, tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ nērmaiyum māṇpum ēṟṟamum innūliṉkaṇ teṟṟeṉak kāṇalākum. innūliṉ eḻuttatikārac collatikārac cūttiraṅkaḷaik kaṟpōr naṉṉūlātiya vaṟṟaip puṉaṉūlātiyaṉavākak kāṇpareṉpatu tiṇṇam;
innūṟku urai eḻutiṉōr iḷampūraṇar, kallāṭar, pērāciriyar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, cēṉāvaraiyar eṉṉum aivar. avaruḷ iḷampuraṇarum, pērāciriyarum innūliṉ eḻuttatikāram colla tikāram, poruḷatikāram eṉṉum mūṉṟatikāraṅkaḷukkum urai iyaṟṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. kallāṭar eḻuttatikārattiṟkum collatikārat tiṟku māttiram uraiyeḻutiyiruppatākavum, cēṉāvaraiyar collati kārat tiṟku māttiram uraiyeḻutiyiruppatākavum terikiṟōm. av vainturaikaḷum muṟaiyē iḷampuraṇam, kallāṭam, pērāciriyam, nacciṉārkkiṉiyam, cēṉāvaraiyam eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
ivvuraikaḷil nacciṉārkkiṉiya eḻuttatikāramum collati kāramum taṉittaṉiyē accāki veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. nacciṉārkkiṉiyap poruḷatikāramum, pērāciriyap poruḷati kāramum kalantu accāki veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. pērāciriyac collatikāram karantai tamiḻccaṅkattārāl acciṭappeṟṟu muṭintu viraivil veḷivarum nilaimaiyil irukkiṉṟatu. iḷampūraṇam eḻuttatikāram pala āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarp pūviruntavallik kaṉṉiyappa mutaliyārāl acciṭṭu veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭatu. avveḻut tatikārattaip pala ēṭṭup piratikaḷōṭu oppiṭṭup pārttut tirutti yāṉ ippoḻutu acciṭṭu veḷippaṭuttukiṉṟēṉ. iḷampūraṇam poruḷati kāram akattiṇaiyiyalum puṟattiṇaiyiyalum muṉṉarē acciṭṭu veḷippaṭuttiyuḷḷēṉ. collatikāramum poruḷatikārattiṉ pintiya iyalkaḷum viraivil acciṭṭu veḷiyiṭappeṟum.
iḷampuraṇarē mutal uraiyāciriyar. avar “uraiyāciriyar” eṉavē yāvarālum vaḻaṅkappaṭum perumaivāyntavar. avar mūṉṟu atikāra uraikaḷuḷḷum eḻuttatikāravurai “eḻuttiṟku iḷampūraṇam” eṉṟu yāvarālum pukaḻappeṟṟatu. kaṟpōr eḷitil uṇarumāṟu poruṭṭoṭarpunōkkic cūttirac coṟkaḷaiyum avaṟṟiṉ poruṭ coṟkaḷaiyum pirittum niṟuttip paṭittaṟkuriya aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷiṭṭum patippittuḷḷēṉ. ovvōriṭattil pāṭa vēṟupāṭum, uraivēṟupāṭum cērttuḷḷēṉ. eṉatu cērppiṟku muṉṉum piṉṉum muṟaiyē () ikkuṟikaḷ iṭṭuḷḷēṉ.


ivveḻuttatikārattaiyum poruḷatikārattaiyum 1920ām varuṭattil acciṭat toṭaṅkiṉēṉ. poruḷatikāram akattiṇaiyiyal puṟattiṇaiyiyalkaḷ muṉṉarē accāki veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. iḵtu ippoḻutu veḷivarukiṉṟatu. poruḷatikāra ēṉaiya iyalkaḷum collatikāramum viraivil veḷivarum.
kōviṟpaṭṭi					va.u. citamparampiḷḷai
1.6.1928.



eḻuttatikāram
eḻuttiṉatu atikārattaiyuṭaiyatu eḻuttatikāram eṉak kāraṇappeyarāyiṟṟu. atikāram-muṟaimai. eḻuttukkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇattai muṟaimaippaṭa viritturaikkum paṭalam eḻuttatikāram eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟuvatāyiṟṟu. eḻuttāvatu makkaḷāṟ pēcappaṭum moḻikku mutaṟkāraṇamākiya oliyākum. tolkāppiyaṉārāl eḻutteṉac collappaṭṭavai akara mutal ṉakara mīṟākavuḷḷa muppatum kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukām, āytam eṉṉum mūṉṟum āka muppattu mūṉṟākum. ivaṟṟiṟku eḻutteṉṉum peyar tamakku neṭuṅkālattiṟku muṉṉē tōṉṟi vaḻaṅkiyateṉpataṉai ‘eḻutteṉappaṭupa, akara mutal ṉakara iṟuvāy muppaḵtu eṉpa’ eṉavarum cūttirattāl tolkāppiyaṉār kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.
vilaṅku mutaliya aḵṟiṇaiyuyirkaḷiṉṟum makkaḷai vēṟu pirittu uyartiṇai māntarāka uyarttum aṟitaṟ karuviyāka viḷaṅkuvatu moḻi. attakaiya moḻikaḷuḷ pēccu vaḻakkoṉṟē peṟṟu eḻutturup peṟātaṉavum uḷḷaṉa. pēccu moḻi ōriṭattum orukālattumē payaṉpaṭum. eḻuttumoḻiyō taṉmai, muṉṉilai, paṭarkkaiyākiya mūviṭattum iṟappu, nikaḻvu, etirvu ākiya mukkālattum oppap payaṉ taruvatākum. paṉaiyōlaikaḷilum kallilum piṟa poruḷkaḷilum eṇṇaṅkaḷai eḻuttāṟ poṟittuvaikkum vaḻakkam pallāyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē nam tamiḻ makkaḷāl kaikkoḷḷappeṟṟu varukiṉṟatu. ‘cellun tēettup peyarmaruṅ kaṟimār, kallēṟin teḻutiya nallarai marāatta, kaṭavuḷōṅkiya kāṭēcu kavalai’ (malaipaṭu - 394-395) eṉavum, ‘peyarum piṭum eḻuti yatartoṟum, pīlicūṭṭiya piṟaṅkunilai naṭukal’ (akam - 131) eṉavum ‘peṟikaṇṭaḻikkum āvaṇamākkaḷiṉ (akam - 77) eṉavum varum caṅka ilakkiyat toṭarkaḷāl icceyti pulaṉātal kāṇalām.
makkaḷ tammāṟ pēcappaṭum moḻiyilamainta olikaḷait taṉittaṉiyākap pirittaṟiyum uṇarvupeṟṟa piṉṉarttāṉ karutturuvākiya avvolikaḷaik kaṭpulaṉāka varivaṭivil eḻututalkūṭum. aṟiñarkaḷatu naṉmuyaṟciyāl olikaḷukkuriya varivaṭivaṅka ḷamainta piṉputāṉ avvolikaḷukku eḻut teṉṉuṅ kāraṇappeyar vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum. iv vuṇmai ‘eḻutappaṭutaliṉ eḻuttē’1 eṉavarum paḻaiya cūttirat toṭarāl aṟivuṟuttappaṭṭamai kāṇka.
meyyeḻuttukkaḷ puḷḷipeṟutalum ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷ puḷḷipeṟutalum kuṟṟiyalukaram puḷḷipeṟutalum makarakkuṟukkam meykkuriya mēṟpuḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷēyoru puḷḷipeṟutalum ākiya eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟait tolkāppiyaṉār tam nūlakattu viḷakkiyuḷḷār. ākavē tolkāppiyaṉārkku neṭuṅkālattiṟku muṉpē tamiḻ muṉṉōr akara mutal ṉakara viṟuvāyuḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷukkuriya varivaṭivaṅkaḷai yamaittut tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉai oḻuṅku ceytuḷḷamai naṉku teḷiyappaṭum.
- ka. veḷḷaivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvaricai 10, pak. 163-164


iḷampūraṇarurai
ciṟappup pāyiram
vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari
āyiṭait
tamiḻkūṟu nallulakattu
vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷu māyiru mutaliṉ
eḻuttuñ collum poruḷu nāṭic
centami ḻiyaṟkai civaṇiya nilattoṭu
muntunūl kaṇṭu muṟaippaṭa veṇṇip
pulantokut tōṉē pōkkaṟu paṉuval
nilantaru tiruviṟ pāṇṭiya ṉavaiyat
taṟaṅkarai nāviṉ nāṉmaṟai muṟṟiya
ataṅkōṭ ṭācāṟ kariltapat terintu
mayaṅkā marapi ṉeḻuttumuṟai kāṭṭi
malkunīr varaippi ṉaintiram niṟainta
tolkāp piyaṉeṉat taṉpeyar tōṟṟip
palpukaḻ niṟutta paṭimai yōṉē.
en nūl uraippiṉum an nūlukkup pāyiram uraittu uraikkaveṉpatu marapu. eṉṉai? “āyira mukattāṉ akaṉṟa tāyiṉum, pāyiramillatu paṉuva laṉṟē” eṉpa vākaliṉ. pāyirameṉpatu puṟavurai. atu nūṟkup puṟavuraiyēl atukēṭṭu eṉṉai payaṉeṉiṉ, kaṟṟuvalla kaṇavaṟkuk kaṟpuṭaiyāḷpōla iṉṟiyamaiyāc ciṟappiṟṟāyum, tiruvamainta mānakarattiṟku uruvamainta vāyiṉ māṭampōla alaṅkāramātaṟ ciṟappiṟṟāyum varutalāṉum, pāyiram kēḷātē nūl kēṭkumēyeṉiṉ kuṟicci pukka māṉpōla māṇākkaṉ iṭarppaṭumākalāṉum, pāyiraṅkēṭṭal payaṉuṭait tāyiṟṟu. ap pāyiram potuvum ciṟappum eṉa iruvakaittu. ellā nūṉmukattum potuvāka uraikkap paṭutaliṟ potuveṉappaṭṭatu. ‘īvōṉ ṟaṉmai’ mutaliya nūluṭ collum poruḷallāta puṟap poruḷaik kūṟum potuppāyiram pōlātu, nūlakattellām payattaṉ māttiraiyēyaṉṟi an nūliṟ collappaṭukiṉṟa poruḷ mutaliya uṇarttaliṉ, aṇiyiḻai makaḷirkku av vaṇiyiṟ ciṟanta āṭaipōla nūṟkuc ciṟattalāṟ ciṟappeṉappaṭṭatu.
avaṟṟuḷ potu nālvakaittu: “īvōṉ taṉmai yīta liyaṟkai, koḷvōṉ taṉmai kōṭaṉ marapeṉa, īriraṇ ṭeṉpa potuviṉ tokaiyē.” itaṉāṉ aṟika.
īvōr  kaṟkappaṭuvōrum kaṟkappaṭātōrum eṉa iruvakaiyar. kaṟkappaṭuvōr nāṉku tiṟattāṉ uvamam kūṟappaṭuvar. “malainilam pūvē tulākkōleṉ ṟiṉṉar,  ulaivi luṇarvuṭai yār.” itaṉāṉ aṟika.
iṉik kaṟkappaṭātārkkuk kūṟum uvamamum nālvakaittu:  “kaḻaṟpey kuṭamē maṭaṟpaṉai muṭatteṅku, kuṇṭikaip paruttiyō ṭivaiyeṉa moḻipa.” itaṉāṉ aṟika.
ītaliyaṟkai: “īta liyalpē yiyalpuṟak kiḷappiṉ, poḻippē yakalam nuṭpa meccameṉap, paḻippil pallurai payiṉṟa nāviṉaṉ, pukaḻnta matiyiṟ poruntum ōraiyil, teḷinta aṟiviṉaṉ teyvam vāḻttik, koḷvō ṉuṇarvakai aṟintavaṉ koḷvarak, koṭuttal marapeṉak kūṟiṉar pulavar.” itaṉāṉ aṟika.
koḷvōr kaṟpikkappaṭuvōrum kaṟpikkappaṭātōrum eṉa iruvakaiyar. kaṟpikkappaṭuvōr aṟuvakaiyar. avartām, “taṉmaka ṉācāṉ makaṉē maṉmakaṉ, poruḷnaṉi koṭuppōṉ vaḻipaṭu vōṉē, uraikō ḷāḷaṉō ṭivareṉa moḻipa.” itaṉāṉ aṟika. ivar taṉmai: “aṉṉaṅ kiḷiyē naṉṉiṟam neyyari, yāṉai āṉē ṟeṉṟivai pōlak, kūṟik koḷpa kuṇammāṇ ṭōrē.” itaṉāṉ aṟika. iṉik kaṟpikkap paṭātār eṇvakaiyar: “maṭimāṉi poccāppaṉ kāmukaṉ kaḷvaṉ, aṭunōyp piṇiyāḷaṉ āṟāc ciṉattaṉ, taṭumāṟu neñcat tavaṉuḷḷiṭ ṭeṇmar, neṭunūlaik kaṟkalā tār.” itaṉāṉ aṟika. ivar taṉmai: “kuraṅkeṟi viḷaṅkā yerumai yāṭē, tōṇi yeṉṟāṅ kivaiyeṉa moḻipa.” itaṉāṉ aṟika.
kōṭaṉ marapu: “koḷvōṉ muṟaimai kūṟuṅ kālaip, poḻutoṭu ceṉṟu vaḻipaṭal muṉiyāṉ, muṉṉum piṉṉum iraviṉum pakaliṉum, akalā ṉāki aṉpoṭu puṇarntāṅku, ācaṟa uṇarntāṉ vāveṉa vantāṅku, iruveṉa iruntē ṭaviḻeṉa vaviḻttuc, colleṉac collip pōveṉap pōki, neñcukaḷa ṉākac cevivā yākak, kēṭṭavai kēṭṭavai valla ṉākip, pōṟṟik kōṭa lavaṉatu toḻilē.”, “ettiṟat tācā ṉuvakkum attiṟam, aṟattiṟ ṟiriyāp paṭarccivaḻi pāṭē.” “cevvaṉ terikiṟpāṉ meynnōkkik kāṇkiṟpāṉ, palluraiyuṅ kēṭpāṉ mikapperitum kātalāṉ, teyvattaip pōla matippāṉ tiripillāṉ, ivvāṟu māṇpu muṭaiyāṟ kuraippavē, cevviti ṉūlait terintu.” “vaḻakkiṉ ilakkaṇam iḻukkiṉ ṟaṟital, pāṭam pōṟṟal kēṭṭavai niṉaittal, ācāṟ cārntavai yamaivarak kēṭṭal, ammāṇ puṭaiyōr tammoṭu payiṟal, viṉātal viṉāyavai viṭutta leṉṟivai, kaṭaṉāk koḷiṉē maṭanaṉi yikakkum.” “aṉaiyaṉ nallōṉ koḷkuvaṉāyiṉ, viṉaiyi ṉuḻappoṭu payaṉṟalaip paṭāaṉ.” ivaṟṟāṉ aṟika. ivvāṟu kōṭaṉ marapuṭaiya māṇākkaṉ nūṉmuṟṟa aṟintāṉā māṟu: “ācā ṉuraittavai yamaivarak koḷiṉum, kāṟkū ṟallatu paṟṟala ṉākum.” “avviṉai yāḷaroṭu payilvakai yorupāl, cevvitiṉ uraippa avviru pālum, maiyaṟu pulamai māṇpunaṉi yuṭaittē.” ivaṟṟāṉ aṟika.
ciṟappup patiṉoru vakaittu: “ākkiyōṉ peyarē vaḻiyē yellai, nūṟpeyar yāppē nutaliya poruḷē, kēṭpōr payaṉō ṭāyeṇ poruḷum, vāyppak kāṭṭal pāyirat tiyalpē.” “kālaṅ kaḷaṉē kāraṇa meṉṟim, mūvakai yēṟṟi moḻinaru muḷarē.” ivaṟṟāṉ aṟika. iṉi ac ciṟappilakkaṇam ceppumāṟu: “pāyirat tilakkaṇam pakaruṅ kālai, nūṉutal poruḷait taṉṉakat taṭakki, āciriyat tāṉum veṇpā vāṉum, maruviya vakaiyāṉ nuvaṟal vēṇṭum.” itaṉāṉ aṟika.
nūl ceytāṉ pāyiram ceyvāṉallaṉ: “tōṉṟā tōṟṟit tuṟaipala muṭippiṉum, tāṉtaṟ pukaḻtal takuti yaṉṟē” eṉpavākaliṉ. pāyiram ceyvār taṉ āciriyaṉum, taṉṉōṭu oruṅku kaṟṟa māṇākkaṉum, taṉ māṇākkaṉum eṉa mūvakaiyar. avaruḷ in nūṟkup pāyiram ceytār taṉṉōṭu oruṅku kaṟṟa paṉampāraṉār.
itaṉporuḷ: vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari ā iṭait tamiḻ kūṟum nal ulakattu vaḻakkum ceyyuḷum a iru mutaliṉ - vaṭakkiṉkaṇ ṇuḷatākiya vēṅkaṭamum teṟkiṉkaṇ ṇuḷatākiya kumariyumākiya avaṟṟai ellaiyākavuṭaiya nilattu vaḻaṅkum tamiḻmoḻiyiṉaik kūṟum naṉmakkaḷāṉ vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkum ceyyuḷumākiya iru kāraṇattāṉum, eḻuttum collum poruḷum nāṭi – eḻuttilakkaṇat tiṉaiyum collilakkaṇattiṉaiyum poruḷilakkaṇattiṉaiyum ārāyntu, centamiḻ iyaṟkai civaṇiya nilattoṭu muntu nūl kaṇṭu - (av vārāycciyiṟ kuṟaipāṭuṭaiyavaṟṟiṟkuc) centamiḻiṉatu iyalpu poruntiṉa centamiḻ nilattu vaḻakkoṭu mutalnūlkaḷiṟ coṉṉavaṟṟiṉaik kaṇṭu, muṟaippaṭa eṇṇi - av vilakkaṇam muṟaippaṭa ārāyntu, nilam taru tiruviṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ avaiyattu - māṟṟāratu nilattiṉait taṉkīḻ vāḻvārkkuk koṇṭu koṭukkum pōrt tiruviṉaiyuṭaiya pāṇṭiyaṉ mākīrttiyatu avaik kaṇṇē, aṟam karai nāviṉ nālmaṟai muṟṟiya ataṅkōṭṭu ācāṟku - (av vavaiyuḷḷārk kēṟpat terintē niṉṟa) meycollum nāviṉai yuṭaiya nāṉku vētattiṉaiyum muṟṟavuṇarnta ataṅkōṭu eṉkiṉṟa ūriṉ ācāṉukku, aril tapa terintu - kaṭā aṟat terintukūṟi, mayaṅkā marapiṉ eḻuttu muṟai kāṭṭi - (av veḻuttum collum ceykiṉṟuḻi muṉṉai nūlpōla eḻuttilakkaṇam colluṭ ceṉṟu) mayaṅkāta muṟaimaiyāṉē eḻuttilakkaṇattiṉai vēṟu terivittu, malku nīr varaippiṉ aintiram niṟainta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉa taṉ peyar tōṟṟi - (avvāṟu ceykiṉṟuḻi) mikka nīraiyuṭaiya kaṭalākiya ellaiyai yuṭaiya ulakiṉkaṇṇē intiraṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa aintira viyākaraṇat tiṉai niṟaiya aṟinta paḻaiya kāppiyak kuṭiyiṉuḷḷōṉeṉat taṉ peyarait tōṟṟuvittu, pōkku aṟu paṉuval - nūṟkuc collappaṭṭa kuṟṟaṅkaḷaṟṟa taṉṉūluḷḷē, pulam tokuttōṉ - av vilakkaṇaṅ kaḷait tokuttuk kūṟiṉāṉ, (avaṉ yāreṉiṉ) pal pukaḻ niṟutta paṭimaiyōṉ - (tavattāṉ varum) palpukaḻkaḷai ulakilē niṟuttiṉa tavavoḻukkattiṉai yuṭaiyāṉ.
vaḻakkum ceyyuḷum ā iru mutaliṉ eḻuttum collum poruḷum nāṭi muṟaippaṭa eṇṇi, pāṇṭiyaṉ avaiyattu ataṅ kōṭṭu ācāṟku ariltapat terintu, eḻuttilakkaṇattaic collum muṟaimai mayaṅkā marapiṟ kāṭṭi, tolkāppiyaṉeṉat taṉ peyar tōṟṟi, paṉuvaluḷ pulantokuttōṉ paṭimaiyōṉ eṉak kūṭṭuka.
‘vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari’ yeṉavē, ellai peṟappaṭṭatu. ‘vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum āyiru mutaliṉ’ eṉavē, nūl nutaliyatūum payaṉum peṟappaṭṭaṉa. ‘muntunūl kaṇṭu muṟaippaṭa veṇṇi’ yeṉavē, vaḻiyum yāppum kāraṇamum peṟappaṭṭaṉa. ‘pāṇṭiya ṉavaiyat’ teṉavē, kālamuṅ kaḷaṉum peṟappaṭṭaṉa. ‘ataṅkōṭ ṭācāṟku ariltapat terintu’ eṉavē, kēṭṭōr peṟappaṭṭatu. ‘tolkāp piyaṉeṉat taṉpeyar tōṟṟi’ yeṉavē, ākkiyōṉ peyarum nūṟpeyarum peṟappaṭṭaṉa.
maṅkalatticaiyākaliṉ, vaṭakku muṉ kūṟappaṭṭatu. kaṭal koḷvataṉ muṉpu piṟanāṭum uṇmaiyiṉ, teṟkum ellai kūṟappaṭṭatu. kiḻakkum mēṟkum piṟanāṭu iṉmaiyiṉ, kūṟappaṭāvāyiṉa. piṟa iraṇṭellai kūṟātu im malaiyum āṟum kūṟiyatu, avai tīrttamātalāṉum kēṭilavātalāṉum ellārāṉum aṟiyap paṭutalāṉu meṉpatu. ivai akappāṭṭellai.
āyiṭait tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakat teṉṟatu, av vellai tamiḻ kūṟum nallāciriyaratu eṉṟavāṟu. nallāciriyar - akattiyaṉār  mutalāyiṉōr. ulaka meṉpatu āciriyarai. a eṉṟatu ākupeyarāṉ, avaṟṟai ellaiyākavuṭaiya nilattiṉai. iṭaiyeṉpatu ēḻā murupu. muṟaippaṭa veṇṇi yeṉṟatu, am muntu nūlkaḷil oṉṟaṟkuriya ilakkaṇattiṉai oṉṟaṉ ilakkaṇattōṭu ārāyntāṟpōla ārāyātu muṟaippaṭa ārāyntu eṉṟavāṟu. maṟṟu, nūl ceyyum ilakkaṇamellām in nūluṭpaṭac ceytā ṉeṉpatu, im ‘muṟaippaṭa veṇṇi’ yeṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka. avai yāmāṟu: “ōttē cūttira meṉaviru vakaiya.” “nēriṉa maṇiyai niralpaṭa vaittāṅku, ōriṉap poruḷai yoruvaḻi vaippatu, ōtteṉa moḻipa vuyarmoḻip pulavar.” (cey. 171) “nuṭpa moppan tiṭpañ colliṟ, curukkaṅ karuttup pakutiyoṭu tokaii, varuttamil poruṭpaya ṉikaḻcci cūttiram.” “potuviṉuñ ciṟappiṉum pōṟṟuṅ kālaip, peṟutal peṟṟavai kāttal kāppoṭu, piṟitupeṟa nikaḻtta lataṉkaruttākum.” “atuvē, piṇṭan tokaivakai kuṟiyē ceykai, koṇṭiyal puṟaṉaṭai yeṉṟataṉ vikaṟpamō, ṭoṉṟiya kuṟiyē yoṉṟu meṉpa.” “āṟṟa toḻukkē tēraip pāyvē, cīya nōkkē paruntu vīḻveṉ(ṟu), āvakai nāṉkē kiṭakkaip payaṉē.” “poḻippē yakala nuṭpa meccameṉap, paḻippil cūttirap payaṉāṉ keṉpa.” “pāṭaṅ kaṇṇaḻi vutāraṇa meṉṟivai, nāṭiṟ ṟiripila vākutal poḻippē.” “taṉṉūl maruṅkiṉum piṟaṉūl maruṅkiṉun tuṉṉiya kaṭāviṉ puṟantōṉṟu vikaṟpam, paṉṉiya vakala meṉmaṉār pulavar.” “ētuvi ṉāṅkavai tuṭaitta ṉuṭpam.” “tuṭaittukkoḷ poruḷai yecca meṉpa.” ivaṟṟāṉum piṟavaṟṟāṉum aṟika. iṉi, nūlceytaṟku uriyāṉaiyum, nūlceyyum āṟṟaiyum collutum. “appula mariltapa vaṟintu mutaṉūl, pakkam pōṟṟum payanterin tulakat, tiṭpa muṭaiya teḷivara vuṭaiyōṉ, appulam paṭaittaṟ kamaiyu meṉpa.” “cūttira muraiyeṉ ṟāyiru tiṟaṉum, pāṟpaṭap pōṟṟal paṭaitta leṉpa, nūṟpaya ṉuṇarnta nuṇṇi yōrē.” ivaṟṟāṉ aṟika.
pōkku aṟutal - nūṟkuk kūṟuṅ kuṟṟaṅkaḷaṟṟu naṉmai yuḷavātal. avai: “īraiṅ kuṟṟamum iṉṟi nēritiṉ, eṇvakaip puṇarppiṉa teṉmaṉār pulavar.” itaṉāṉ aṟika.
‘eḻuttum collum poruḷum’ eṉa vaittup piṉṉum ‘mayaṅkā marapiṉ eḻuttumuṟai kāṭṭi’ eṉṟatu, piṟanūl pōlac colluḷ eḻuttiṉai mayakkik kūṟātu, vēṟu cērak kūṟiṉāreṉṟa teṉpatu.
ciṟappup pāyiram muṟṟiṟṟu.



iḷampūraṇam eḻuttatikāram

iv vatikāram eṉ nutali eṭuttukkoḷḷappaṭṭatō veṉiṉ, atikāram nutaliyatūum atikārattiṉatu peyar  uraippavē aṭaṅkum. atikāram eṉṉa peyarttō veṉiṉ, eḻuttatikāram eṉṉum peyarttu. eḻuttuṇarttiṉamai kāraṇattiṟ peṟṟa peyar eṉa uṇarka.
eḻuttu eṉaittu vakaiyāṉ uṇarttiṉārō veṉiṉ, eṭṭu vakaiyāṉum eṭṭiṟanta palavakaiyāṉum uṇarttiṉā reṉpatu. avaṟṟuḷ, eṭṭuvakaiyāvaṉa: eḻuttu iṉaiya eṉṟalum, iṉṉa peyara veṉṟalum, iṉṉa muṟaimaiya veṉṟalum, iṉṉa aḷaviṉa veṉṟalum, iṉṉa piṟappiṉa veṉṟalum, iṉṉa puṇarcciya veṉṟalum, iṉṉa vaṭiviṉa veṉṟalum, iṉṉa taṉmaiya veṉṟalum. eṉavē, avaṟṟuḷ taṉmaiyum vaṭivum āciriyar tām uṇaruvareṉiṉum, namakku uṇarttal arumaiyiṉ oḻinta āṟumē itaṉuḷ uṇarttiṉār eṉa uṇarka. iṉi, eṭṭiṟanta palavakaiyāvaṉa: uṇmait taṉmaiyum, kuṟaivum, kūṭṭamum, pirivum, mayakkamum, moḻiyākkamum, nilaiyum, iṉamum, oṉṟu palavātalum, tirintataṉ tiripatu veṉṟalum, piṟiteṉṟalum, atuvum piṟitum eṉṟalum, nilaiyiṟ ṟeṉṟalum, nilaiyāteṉṟalum, nilaiyiṟṟu nilaiyā teṉṟalum, iṉṉō raṉṉavum eṉa ivai. ivaiyellām āmāṟu mēl vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka.
iv vatikārat tilakkaṇam karuviyum ceykaiyum eṉa iru vakaittu. avaṟṟuḷ, karuvi puṟappuṟakkaruviyum, puṟakkaruviyum, akappuṟakkaruviyum, akakkaruviyum eṉa nāṉku vakaippaṭum. ceykai puṟappuṟacceykaiyum, puṟacceykaiyum, akappuṟac ceykaiyum, akacceykaiyum eṉa nāṉku vakaippaṭum. nūṉ marapum piṟappiyalum puṟappuṟakkaruvi; moḻimarapu puṟakkaruvi; puṇariyal akappuṟakkaruvi. “ekara vokaram peyarkkī ṟākā, muṉṉilai moḻiya veṉmaṉār pulavar” (uyir mayaṅ. 70) eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa akakkaruvi. “ellā moḻikku muyirvarum vaḻiyē, uṭampaṭu meyyi ṉurupukoḷal varaiyār”(puṇar. 38) eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa puṟappuṟacceykai. “laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi muṉṉart, tana veṉavariṟ ṟaṉavā kummē” (tokai. 7) eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa puṟacceykai. “ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷiyiṟuti, yakaramum uyirum varumvaḻi yiyaṟkai” 	(tokai. 21) eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa akappuṟac ceykai. tokaimarapu mutaliya ōttiṉuḷ, iṉṉa īṟu iṉṉavāṟu muṭiyumeṉac ceykai kūṟuvaṉa vellām akac ceykai.
 


nūṉmarapu
‘ivvatikārattāṟ collappaṭum eḻuttilakkaṇattiṉai ōrāṟṟāṟ ṟokuttuṇarttutaliṉ nūṉmarapeṉṉum peyarttu’ eṉa iḷampūraṇarum, ‘ittolkāppiyameṉum nūṟku marapān tuṇaikku vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟait tokut tuṇarttutaliṉ nūṉ marapeṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu’ eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, ‘aḵtāvatu nūliṉatu marapu paṟṟiya peyar kūṟutal’..................... malaikaṭalyāṟu eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattu ulakamarapu paṟṟiya peyar pōlātu īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭum eḻuttu, kuṟil, neṭil, uyir, mey eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattup peyarkaḷ nūliṉkaṇ āḷutaṟporuṭṭu mutaṉūlāciriyaṉāṟ ceytukoḷḷap paṭṭamaiyiṉ ivai nūṉmarapu paṟṟiya peyarāyiṉa’ eṉac civañāṉamuṉivarum ivviyaliṉ peyarkkāraṇam kūṟiṉār. ivviyaluṭ kūṟappaṭum eḻuttukkaḷiṉ peyar mutaliyaṉa aṉaittul tolkāppiyaṉārkku muṟkālattavarāṉa paṇṭait tamiḻc cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷiṟ collappaṭṭa eḻuttiyal marapukaḷāy innūlil āciriyarāl eṭuttāḷap paṭṭaṉavām. eṉpa, pulavar, moḻipa, eṉmaṉār pulavar eṉṟāṅku muṉṉaiyōr karuttāka ivviyalil varuṅ kuṟiyīṭukaḷai āciriyar eṭutturaittalāl ivvuṇmai viḷaṅkum. ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭum ilakkaṇam moḻiyiṭai eḻuttiṟkaṉṟit taṉiniṉṟa eḻuttiṟkuriyatākum.
kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟum yātāyiṉum oru collaic cārntuvariṉallatu taṉiyeḻuttāka olittu niṟkum iyalpuṭaiyaṉa alla. ivaṟṟiṉ iyalpiṉai naṉkaṟinta tamiḻ muṉṉōr immūṉṟiṉaiyum cārpeḻutteṉap peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ. ‘cārntuvaraṉ marapiṉmūṉṟu’ eṉavum, ‘cārntu variṉallatu tamakkiyalpilaveṉat tērntu veḷippaṭutta ēṉai mūṉṟum’ eṉavum varun toṭarkaḷāl tolkāppiyaṉār ivaṟṟiṉ iyalpiṉait teḷivāka viḷakkukiṉṟār.
taṉittiyaṅkum iyalpuṭaiyatu uyir. avviyalpiṉṟi uyiriṉāl iyakkappaṭuvatu mey. akara mutal oḷakāra mīṟāka vuḷḷa paṉṉiraṇ ṭeḻuttum taṉittiyaṅkum āṟṟaluṭaimaiyāl uyireṉappaṭṭaṉa. kakara mutal ṉakara mīṟākavuḷḷa patiṉeṭ ṭeḻuttukkaḷum taṉittiyaṅku miyalpiṉṟi akara mutaliya uyirkaḷāl iyakkappaṭutaliṉ meyyeṉap paṭṭaṉa. uyirvaḻiyāyaṭaṅki ataṉatu viḷakkam peṟṟu niṟkum uṭampaip pōṉṟu meyyeḻuttukkaḷum tammēl ēṟiya uyireḻuttiṉ māttiraikkuḷ aṭaṅki ataṉ oliyōṭu otticaippaṉavākum. iṅṅaṉam meyyum uyirum kūṭiyolikkum eḻuttoliyiṉai uyirmey eṉṟa niṟaiyuvamap peyarāl vaḻaṅkutal paḻaiya tamiḻ marapākum.
ivviyalil 33 cūttiraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. eḻuttiṉṉa teṉpatum, ataṉ vakaiyum, eḻuttukkaḷ peṟum māttiraiyum, avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṉ vaṭivam, kuṟṟeḻuttu, neṭṭeḻuttu, uyir, mey, uyirmey, valleḻuttu, melleḻuttu, iṭaiyeḻuttu, eṉa avai peṟum peyarkaḷum, mey taṉṉoṭum piṟitoṭum kūṭiyolikkum meymayakkamum, kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷuḷ a i u eṉpavaṟṟukkuc cuṭṭeṉṉuṅ kuṟiyum neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷuḷ ā ē ō eṉpavaṟṟukku viṉāveṉṉuṅ kuṟiyum, eḻuttukkaḷ muṟkūṟiya māttiraiyiṉ nīṇṭolikku miṭamum ivviyalil uṇarttappaṭṭaṉa. 
- veḷḷaivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvaricai 10, pak. 16-166



mutalāvatu
nūṉmarapu
iv vōttu eṉ nutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ, atuvum ataṉ peyar uraippavē aṭaṅkum. iv vatikārattāṟ collappaṭum eḻuttilakkaṇattiṉai ōrāṟṟāṉ tokuttu uṇarttutaliṉ,  nūṉmarapu eṉṉum peyarttu. itaṉuṭ kūṟukiṉṟa ilakkaṇam moḻiyiṭai (niṉṟa) eḻuttiṟkaṉṟi, taṉiniṉṟa eḻuttiṟkeṉa uṇarka.
1.	eḻutteṉappaṭupa
	akaramuta ṉakara viṟuvāy
	muppaḵ teṉpa
	cārntuvaraṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē.
it talaic cūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ, eḻuttukaḷatu peyarum muṟaiyum tokaiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉporuḷ: eḻuttu eṉappaṭupa - eḻutteṉṟu ciṟappittuc collappaṭuvaṉa, akaram mutal ṉakara iṟuvāy muppaḵtu eṉpa - akara mākiya mutalaiyuṭaiyaṉavum ṉakaramākiya iṟuvāyiṉai yuṭaiyaṉavu mākiya muppateṉṟu collupa (āciriyar); cārntu varal marapiṉ mūṉṟu alaṅkaṭai - cārntu varutalākiya ilakkaṇattiṉaiyuṭaiya mūṉṟum allāviṭattu.
mūṉṟum āṉaviṭattu muppattu mūṉṟu eṉṟu collupa eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: a, ā, i, ī, u, ū, e, ē,  ai, o, ō, oḷa; 	k, ṅ, c, ñ, ṭ, ṇ, t, n, p, m, y, r, l, v,  ḻ, ḷ, ṟ, ṉ.  eṉavarum.
‘eṉappaṭupa’ eṉṟa ciṟappāṉ, aḷapeṭaiyum uyir meyyum varivaṭivum ciṟappillā eḻuttākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. a ā eṉpaṉa peyar; muṟai, am muṟai; tokai, muppatu. avaṟṟuḷ, akaram tāṉum iyaṅkit taṉi meykaḷaiyum iyakkutaṟ ciṟappāṉ, muṉvaikkappaṭṭatu. ṉakāram vīṭupēṟṟiṟkuriya āṇpālai uṇarttutaṟ ciṟappāṉ piṉvaikkappaṭṭatu. tokai sayeṉpatu] tokaiyuṭ teṭākaiyum, tokaiyuḷ vakaiyum, tokaiyuḷviriyum; vakaiyuḷtokaiyum, vakaiyuḷ vakaiyum, vakaiyuḷviriyum; viriyuṭṭokaiyum, viriyuḷvakaiyum, viriyuḷ viriyum eṉa oṉpatu vakaippaṭum. eḻutteṉpatu tokaiyuḷ tokai. muppateṉpatu ataṉ vakai. muppattu mūṉṟeṉpatu ataṉ viri. muppateṉpatu vakaiyuḷtokai. muppattu mūṉṟeṉpatu ataṉ vakai. aḷapeṭai talaippeytu nāṟpateṉpatu ataṉ viri. muppattu mūṉṟeṉpatu viriyuḷ tokai. nāṟpateṉpatu ataṉ vakai. uyirmey talaippeytu irunūṟṟaimpattāṟeṉpatu ataṉ viri. ceyyuḷiṉpam nōkki vakaram nīkkip pakaram iṭappaṭṭatu. akaramutal ṉakara iṟuvāy eṉpaṉa, irupeyaroṭ ṭākupeyarāṉ muppataṉmēl niṉṟaṉa.	(1)
2.	avaitām
	kuṟṟiyalikaraṅ kuṟṟiyalukaram āyta meṉṟa
	muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meḻuttō raṉṉa.
itu, mēl cārntu varum eṉṉappaṭṭa mūṉṟaṟkum peyarum muṟaiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaitām - mēṟ cārntuvarum eṉappaṭṭavai tām, kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam eṉṟa muppāl puḷḷiyum - kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalukaramum āytam eṉṟu collappaṭṭa mūṉṟu kūṟṟatākiya puḷḷiyum eṉa ivai.eḻuttu ōr aṉṉa - (avai) mēṟcollappaṭṭa muppatu eḻuttōṭu oru taṉmaiya
appeyar, peyar. ammuṟai, muṟai. ‘eḻuttō raṉṉa’ eṉa vēṇṭā kūṟiyavataṉāṉ, muṉ ‘eṉappaṭupa’ eṉṟa ciṟappu, am mūṉṟaṟkum koḷḷakkiṭantamaiyiṉ, atu vilakkutal peṟutu meṉpatu. kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalukaramum eṉṉum eṇṇummai vikārattāṉ tokkaṉa. cantaṉakkōl kuṟukiṉaviṭattup pirappaṅ kōl ākātu; atupōla, ikara ukaraṅkaḷ kuṟukiṉaviṭattum, avai uyir ākaṟpālaṉa. avaṟṟaip puṇarcci vēṟṟumaiyum poruḷ vēṟṟumaiyum nōkki vēṟeḻutteṉṟu vēṇṭiṉāreṉa uṇarka.	(2)
3.	avaṟṟuḷ
	a i u
	e o veṉṉu mappā laintum
	ōraḷa picaikkuṅ kuṟṟeḻut teṉpa.
itu, mēṟkūṟappaṭṭaṉavaṟṟiṟku aḷapum kuṟiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa eḻuttiṉuḷ, a-i-u-e-o eṉṉum appāl aintum  a-i-u-e-o eṉṟu collap paṭukiṉṟa ak kūṟṟu aintum, ōr aḷapu icaikkum - (orō voṉṟu) ōr aḷapāka icaikkum, kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉpa - (avaitām) kuṟṟeḻutteṉṉum kuṟiya eṉṟu colluvar (pulavar).
ivar kāraṇam paṟṟiyaṉṟik kuṟiyiṭār. ākaliṉ, itu taṉ kuṟumaiyāṉ ik kuṟi peṟṟatu. ik kuṟiyai āṇṭavāṟu mēl vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka.	(3)
4.	ā ī ū ē ai
	ō oḷa veṉṉu mappā lēḻum
	īraḷa picaikku neṭṭeḻut teṉpa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ā-ī-ū-ē-ai-ō-oḷa eṉṉum appāl ēḻum - ā-ī-ū-ē-ai-ō-oḷa eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa akkūṟṟu ēḻum, īr aḷapu icaikkum - (orōvoṉṟu) iraṇṭu māttiraiyāka olikkum, neṭṭeḻuttu eṉpa - (avaitām) neṭṭeḻuttu eṉṉum kuṟiya eṉṟu colluvar (pulavar) e-ṟu.
aikāra oḷakāraṅkaḷukku iṉam illaiyeṉiṉum, māttirai yoppumaiyāṉ avai neṭṭeḻuttu eṉappaṭṭaṉa.	(4)

5.	mūvaḷa picaitta lōreḻut tiṉṟē.
iḵtu, uyiraḷapeṭaiyeḻuttiṟku māttirai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mū aḷapu icaittal ōr eḻuttu iṉṟu - mūṉṟu māttiraiyāka olittal iyalpākiya ōr eḻuttiṟku illai (vikāramākiya iraṇṭu kūṭiyataṟku uṇṭu).	(5)
6.	nīṭṭam vēṇṭi ṉavvaḷa puṭaiya
	kūṭṭi yeḻūuta leṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, uyiraḷapeṭai āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ - nīṇṭa māttiraiyaiyuṭaiya aḷapeṭai eḻuttuppeṟa vēṇṭiṉ, a aḷapu uṭaiya kūṭṭi eḻūutal - mēṟkūṟiya iraṇṭaḷapuṭaiya neṭilaiyum ōr aḷapuṭaiya kuṟilaiyum (piḷavupaṭāmaṟ) kūṭṭiyeḻūuka, eṉmaṉār pulavar - eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.	(6)
7.	kaṇṇimai noṭiyeṉa vavvē māttirai
	nuṇṇiti ṉuṇarntōr kaṇṭa vāṟē.
iḵtu, am māttirai yilakkaṇam kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kaṇṇimai eṉa noṭi eṉa av māttirai - kaṇṇimaiyum noṭiyumākiya avai māttiraikku aḷapu, nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarntōr kaṇṭa āṟu - (itu) nuṇṇitāka nūlilakkaṇattiṉai uṇarnta āciriyar kaṇṭa neṟi.
imaiyeṉṟatu, imaittal toḻilai. noṭiyeṉṟatu, noṭiyiṟ piṟanta ōcaiyai. taṉ kuṟippu iṉṟi nikaḻtaliṉ imai muṉ kūṟappaṭṭatu. niṟuttaḷattal, peytaḷattal, nīṭṭiyaḷattal, teṟittaḷattal, tēṅkamukantaḷattal, cārttiyaḷattal, eṇṇiyaḷattal eṉa eḻuvakaiya eṉṉum aḷaviṉuḷ, itu cārttiyaḷattal. ‘nuṇṇiti ṉuṇarntōr kaṇṭavāṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, nāluḻakkuk koṇṭatu nāḻi yeṉṟāṟpōla av vaḷavaikku aḷavai peṟāmai aṟika. 	(7)
8.	oḷakāra viṟuvāyp
	paṉṉī reḻuttu muyireṉa moḻipa.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya kuṟilaiyum neṭilaiyum tokuttu vēṟu oru kuṟiyiṭutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) oḷakāra iṟuvāy paṉṉīr eḻuttum - oḷakāramākiya iṟutiyaiyuṭaiya paṉṉiraṇṭu eḻuttiṉaiyum, uyir eṉa moḻipa - uyireṉṉum kuṟiyiṉaiyuṭaiya eṉṟu colluvar.	(8)
9.	ṉakāra viṟuvāyp
	patiṉeṇ ṇeḻuttu meyyeṉa moḻipa.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya uyirallā eḻuttiṟkum oru kuṟiyiṭutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ṉakāra iṟuvāyp patiṉeṇ eḻuttum - ṉakaramākiya iṟutiyaiyuṭaiya patiṉeṭṭu eḻuttiṉaiyum, mey eṉa moḻipa - meyyeṉṉum kuṟiyiṉaiyuṭaiya eṉṟu colluvar. 	(9)
10.	meyyō ṭiyaiyiṉu muyiriya ṟiriyā.
iḵtu, uyirmeykku aḷapu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) meyyōṭu iyaiyiṉum - (uyirmeyyāvaṉa) meykaḷōṭu uyir iyaiyap piṟanta nilaimaiyavāyiṉum, uyiriyal tiriyā - (av vuyirmeykaḷ av viyaipiṉkaṇṇē vēṟu ōr eḻuttāy niṉṟamaiyiṉ, meyyōṭu iyaipiṉṟi niṉṟa) uyirkaḷatu iyalpiṉ tiriyā.
uyirum meyyum kūṭukiṉṟa uyirmeyk kūṭṭattiṉai,  ‘meyyō ṭiyaiyiṉum’ eṉa uyirmēl vaittuk kūṟiyatu, av vuyiriṉ māttiraiyē itaṟku māttiraiyākak kūṟukiṉṟamai nōkkippōlum. ‘iyal’ eṉṟatu perumpāṉmai māttirai yiṉai. ciṟupāṉmai kuṟiyum eṇṇum koḷka.
ka eṉavum, kā eṉavum avvāṟu niṉṟamai aṟika. 	 (10)
11.	meyyi ṉaḷapē yaraiyeṉa moḻipa.
itu, taṉimeykku aḷapu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) meyyiṉ aḷapu - meyyatu māttiraiyiṉai, arai eṉa moḻipa - (orō voṉṟu) arai māttirai yuṭaiyateṉṟu colluvar.
u-m: kākkai, kōṅku eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka. īṇṭu vēṟṟumai nayamiṉṟi oṟṟumainayam karutappaṭṭatu.	(11)
12.	avviya ṉilaiyu mēṉai mūṉṟē.
itu, cārpiṟṟōṟṟattu eḻuttu mūṉṟaṟkum aḷapu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) a iyal nilaiyum - mēṟkūṟiya arai māttirai yākiya av viyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṟkum, ēṉai mūṉṟu - oḻinta cārpiṟ ṟōṟṟattu mūṉṟum.
 kēṇmiyā, nāku, eḵku. eṉak kaṇṭu koḷka.	 (ēkāram - īṟṟacai  	(12)
13.	araiyaḷapu kuṟukaṉ makara muṭaittē
	icaiyiṭa ṉarukun teriyuṅ kālai.
itu, meykaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟku māttiraic curukkam kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) arai aḷapu kuṟukal makaram uṭaittu - araiyaḷapākiya ellaiyiṟ kuṟukik kāṉmāttiraiyātalai makara mey uṭaittu; (aḵtu yāṇṭōveṉiṉ) icaiyiṭaṉ arukum - vēṟu ōr eḻuttiṉatu oliyiṉkaṇ atu ciṟupāṉmai yāki varum, teriyuṅkālai - ārāyuṅ kālattu.
u-m. pōṉm, varum vaṇṇakkaṉ eṉa varum. kāṉ māttirai yeṉpatu uraiyiṟkōṭal. (ēkāram īṟṟacai)	 (13)

14.	uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē.
itu, pakarattiṉ makarattiṭai varivaṭivu vēṟṟumai ceytal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uḷpeṟu puḷḷi uruvu ākum - puṟattup peṟum puḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷāṟpeṟum puḷḷi makarattiṟku vaṭivām (aḵtiṉmai pakarattiṟku vaṭivām).
u-m. ma, pa - eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka.	(14)
(uḷḷāṟ peṟumpuḷḷi kuṟukiya makarattiṟku vaṭivām eṉpatē iccūttirattiṟku nēr urai. ēkāram - īṟṟacai)
15.	meyyi ṉiyaṟkai puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal.
iḵtu, uyirmeyyoṭu taṉimeyyiṭai vaṭivu vēṟṟumai ceytal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) meyyiṉ iyaṟkai - taṉimeyyiṉatu iyalpu, puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal - puḷḷiyoṭu niṟṟal (uyirmeyyiṉatu iyalpu puḷḷiyiṉṟi niṟṟal).
k ṅ c ñ ṭ ṇ t n p m y r l v ḻ ḷ ṟ ṉ   eṉak kaṇṭu koḷka.	(15)
16.	ekara okarat tiyaṟkaiyu maṟṟē.
iḵtu, ekara okaraṅkaṭku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷoṭu vēṟṟumai ceytal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ekara okarattu iyaṟkaiyum aṟṟu - ekara okaraṅkaḷatu iyalpum avvāṟu puḷḷi peṟum iyalpiṟṟu (ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷatu iyalpu ap puḷḷi peṟā iyalpiṟṟu). (ēkāram - īṟṟacai.)
u-m: e, o.	          (16)
17.	puḷḷi yillā vellā meyyum
	uruvuru vāki yakaramō ṭuyirttalum
	ēṉai yuyirō ṭuruvutirin tuyirttalum
	āyī riyala uyirtta lāṟē.
iḵtu, uyirum meyyum kūṭumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellā meyyum puḷḷi illā - ellā meykaḷum puḷḷi illaiyāmpaṭiyāka, uruvu uruvu āki - tattam muṉṉai vaṭivē iṉṉum vaṭivāka, akaramōṭu uyirttalum - akarattoṭu kūṭi olittalum, ēṉai uyirōṭu uruvu tirintu uyirttalum - oḻinta uyirkaḷoṭu vaṭivu vēṟupaṭṭu olittalumākiya, a īr iyala - avviraṇṭu iyal piṉaiyuṭaiya, uyirttal āṟu - avai olikku muṟaimai.
‘taṉṉiṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, aḷapeṭai uyirōṭum, cārpiṟṟōṟṟattu uyirōṭum kūṭum uyirmeyyum koḷka.
u-m: uruvu uruvāki uyirttal ka ṅa eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka. uruvu tirintu uyirttal kā ṅā eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka.
īṇṭu uyirum meyyum kūṭukiṉṟa uyirmeyk kūṭṭattiṉai ‘ellā meyyu’meṉṟu meymēl vaittuk kūṟiyatu, atu muṉ kūṟik kūṟappaṭutal nōkkip pōlum. uyirmey yeṉpataṉai oṟṟumai koḷvuḻi ummait tokaip puṟattuppiṟanta aṉmoḻittokai yeṉavum, vēṟṟumai koḷvuḻi ummait tokaiyeṉavum koḷka. ‘illāka’ eṉpatu ‘illā’ eṉa niṉṟatu.  uruvu tirintu uyirttal mēlum kīḻum vilaṅkupeṟuvaṉa vilaṅkupeṟṟu uyirttalum, kōṭupeṟuvaṉa kōṭupeṟṟu uyirttalum, puḷḷipeṟuvaṉa puḷḷipeṟṟu uyirttalum, puḷḷiyum kōṭum uṭaṉ peṟṟu uyirttalum eṉak koḷka.	  (17)
18.	meyyiṉ vaḻiya tuyirtōṉṟu nilaiyē.
iḵtu, uyirmeyyuḷ uyirum meyyum niṟkumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir - uyir meyyiṉ vaḻiyatu - meykaḷiṉ piṉṉavām, tōṉṟum nilai - tōṉṟum nilaimaikkaṇ.
‘tōṉṟu nilai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, uyirmeykaḷaip pirikku miṭattum kūṭṭumiṭattum, avvāṟē muṉṉum piṉṉum ātalaik koḷka. meyyum uyirum muṉṉum piṉṉum peṟa niṟku meṉṟamaiyāṉ, ak kūṭṭam pālum nīrumpōla uṭaṉ kalantataṉṟi, viralnuṉikaḷ talaippeytāṟpōla vēṟuniṉṟu kalantaṉavalla eṉpatu peṟutum. īṇṭu vēṟṟumainayam karutappaṭṭatu. (ē - īṟṟacai)	(18)
19.	valleḻut teṉpa kacaṭa tapaṟa.
itu, taṉi meykaḷuḷ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟu ōr kuṟiyiṭutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) valleḻuttu eṉpa - valleḻuttu eṉṉum                 kuṟiya eṉṟu colluvar, ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa - ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa eṉṉum taṉimeykaḷai.
valleṉṟu icaittalāṉum, val eṉṟa talaivaḷiyāṟ piṟattalāṉum valleḻuttu eṉappaṭṭatu. moḻikku mutalā meḻuttu nāṉku uḷavākalāṉum, avaṟṟāṉ vaḻakkuppayiṟci peritākalāṉum valliṉam muṉkūṟappaṭṭatu. (ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa eṉṉum taṉimeykaḷ k c ṭ t p ṟ)	(19)
20.	melleḻut teṉpa ṅañaṇa namaṉa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu eṉpa - melleḻuttu eṉṉum kuṟiya eṉṟu colluvar, ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa - ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa eṉṉum taṉimeykaḷai.
melleṉṟu icaittalāṉum, mel eṉṟa mūkkiṉ vaḷiyāṟ piṟattalāṉum, melleḻuttu eṉappaṭṭatu. moḻikku mutalāmeḻuttu mūṉṟu uḷavākalāṉum, avaṟṟiṉ vaḻakkup payiṟciyāṉum, (melliṉam), mutalāmeḻuttuc ciṟupāṉmai vaḻakkiṉavāy iraṇṭākiya iṭaiyiṉattiṉ muṉ vaikkappaṭṭatu. vaṉmai meṉmai kūṟaliṉ, eḻuttu aruvaṉṟi uruvātal peṟappaṭṭatu. uyirukkum kuṟumai neṭumai kūṟaliṉ, uruveṉpatu peṟutum. (ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa eṉṉum taṉimeykaḷ ṅ ñ ṇ n m ṉ.)	 (20)
21.	iṭaiyeḻut teṉpa yarala vaḻaḷa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) iṭaiyeḻuttu eṉpa - iṭaiyeḻuttu eṉṉum kuṟiya eṉṟu colluvar, ya ra la va ḻa ḷa - ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṉum taṉimeykaḷai.
iṭainikaravāki olittalāṉum, iṭainikarttāya miṭaṟṟu vaḷiyāṟ piṟattalāṉum iṭaiyeḻuttu eṉappaṭṭatu.  (ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṉum taṉimeykaḷ y r l v ḻ ḷ)	(21)
22.	ammū vāṟum vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ
	meymmayak kuṭaṉilai teriyuṅ kālai.
itu, taṉi mey mayakkattiṟkup peyarum muṟaiyum tokaiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) a mū āṟum - mēṟcollappaṭṭa (mūvāṟu) patiṉeṭṭu meyyum, vaḻaṅku iyal maruṅkiṉ - tammai moḻip paṭutti vaḻaṅkum iyalpu uḷatāmiṭattu, mey mayaṅku - meym mayakkam eṉṟum, uṭaṉnilai - uṭaṉnilai mayakkam eṉṟum iru vakaiya, teriyum kālai - (avai mayaṅku muṟaimai) ārāyum kālattu.
uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉaiyum uṟaḻcci vakaiyāṉ uṟaḻa oṉpatu uḷavāmaṉṟē. avaṟṟuḷ taṉi meyyoṭu taṉi meymayakkam oṉṟē kūṟiya teṉṉeṉiṉ, maṟṟavaṟṟiṟku varaiyaṟai yiṉmaiyiṉ varaiyaṟaiyuṭaiya taṉimey mayakkamē kūṟiyoḻintār eṉa uṇarka. ‘mey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, taṉimeyyōṭu uyirmey mayakkamaṉṟi, taṉimeyyoṭu taṉi mey  mayakkamātal koḷka.	 (22)
23.	ṭaṟalaḷa veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉark
	kacapa veṉṉu mūveḻut turiya.
itu, meymayakkam āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu. 
(i-ḷ.) ṭaṟa laḷa eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar - ṭa ṟa la ḷa eṉṟu collappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, ka ca pa eṉṉum mū eḻuttu uriya - ka ca pa eṉṟu collappaṭum mūṉṟeḻuttum mayaṅkutaṟku uriya.
u-m: kaṭka, kaṟka, celka, koḷka eṉavum; kaṭciṟār, kaṟciṟār, celciṟār, koḷciṟār eṉavum; kaṭpa, kaṟpa, celpa, koḷpa eṉavum varum.
mēl ‘teriyuṅ kālai’ (22) eṉṟataṉāṉ, im meymayakkam kūṟukiṉṟa cūttira mellām palapaṭiyāṉ mayakkam koḷḷac col nōkku uṭaiyaveṉiṉum, vaḻakki ṉoṭu porunta oṉṟaṉōṭu oṉṟaṉṟi mayaṅkāteṉpatu koḷka. meymayakkam orumoḻikkum puṇarmoḻikkum potuvāka liṉ, mēṟkūṟum puṇarmoḻic ceykaiyellām talaiyāya aṟiviṉōrai nōkka oruvāṟṟāṟ kūṟiya vāṟāyiṟṟu.	(23)
24.	avaṟṟuḷ
	laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar yavavun tōṉṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟkūṟiya nāṉkaṉuḷḷum, laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar - lakāra ḷakāraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, yavavum tōṉṟum - kacapakkaḷēyaṉṟi yakara vakaraṅkaḷum tōṉṟi mayaṅkum.
u-m: kolyāṉai, veḷyāṉai, kōlvaḷai, veḷvaḷai eṉa varum.	   (24)
25.	ṅañaṇa namaṉa veṉumpuḷḷi muṉṉart
	tatta micaika ḷottaṉa nilaiyē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa eṉum puḷḷimuṉṉar - ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa eṉṟu collappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar; tattam micaikaḷ ottaṉa (neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉuḷ) tattamakku mēl niṟkum eḻuttākiya ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟakkaḷ poruntiṉa, nilai - mayaṅki niṟṟaṟkaṇ. (ēkāram - īṟṟacai)
u-m: teṅku, mañcu, vaṇṭu, pantu, kampu, kaṉṟu eṉa varum. 		(25)
26.	avaṟṟuḷ
	ṇaṉaḵkāṉ muṉṉark
	kacañapa mayavav vēḻu muriya.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa melleḻuttu āṟaṉuḷ, ṇaṉaḵkāṉ muṉṉar- ṇakāra ṉakāraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, ka ca ña pa ma ya va ēḻum uriya - (ṭaṟakkaḷē yaṉṟi) ka ca ña pa ma ya va eṉṟu collappaṭum ēḻum mayaṅkutaṟku uriya e-ṟu.
u-m: veṇkalam, puṉkaṇ; veṇcāntu, puṉcey; veṇ ñāṇ, poṉñāṇ; veṇpali, poṉperitu; veṇ mālai, poṉmālai; maṇyātu, poṉyātu; maṇvalitu, poṉ  valitu eṉa varum.	(26)
27.	ñanamava veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
	yaḵkā ṉiṟṟaṉ meypeṟ ṟaṉṟē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ña na ma va eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar - ña na ma va eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, yaḵkāṉ niṟṟal mey peṟṟaṉṟu - yakaram mayaṅki niṟṟal poruṇmai peṟṟatu.
u-m: uriñyātu, porunyātu, tirumyātu, tevyātu eṉa varum.	(27)
28.	maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ vavvun tōṉṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ - makaramākiya puḷḷi muṉṉar, va um tōṉṟum - (pakara yakaraṅkaḷēyaṉṟi) vakaramum tōṉṟi mayaṅkum. 
u-m: nilam valitu eṉavarum.	 (28)
29.	yaraḻa veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
	mutalā keḻuttu ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) yaraḻa eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar - yaraḻa eṉṟu collap paṭukiṉṟa puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, mutal āku eḻuttu ṅakara moṭu tōṉṟum - moḻikku mutal ām eṉṉappaṭṭa oṉpatu meyyum (mutalākā) ṅakarattoṭu tōṉṟi mayaṅkum e-ṟu.
u-m: vēykaṭitu, vērkaṭitu, vīḻkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu; ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, yātu, valitu eṉa varum. vēyṅaṉam, vērṅaṉam, vīḻṅaṉam eṉavum oṭṭuka. vēyyātu eṉpuḻi, uṭaṉilai yātalāṉ yakaram oḻittu oṭṭuka.	
(`moḻikku mutal ām eṉṉappaṭṭa oṉpatu mey’ eṉpatu moḻikku mutalāy varum oṉpatu uyir meyyeḻuttukaḷil uyiroḻinta meykaḷaik kuṟittu niṉṟatu.)	(29)
30.	meynnilaic cuṭṭi ṉellā veḻuttun
	tammuṟ ṟāmvarūum raḻavalaṅ kaṭaiyē.
itu, niṟutta muṟaiyāṉē uṭaṉilai mayakkam āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) meynnilai cuṭṭiṉ - poruḷ nilaimaik karuttiṉ kaṇ, ellā eḻuttum tammuṉ tām varum - ellā meyyeḻuttum tam muṉṉē tām vantu mayaṅkum, ra ḻa alaṅkaṭai - rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷ allāta iṭattu. (ēkāram īṟṟacai.)
u-m: kākkai, eṅṅaṉam, paccai, maññai, paṭṭai, maṇṇai, tattai, vennōy, appai, ammi, veyyar, elli, evvī, koḷḷi, koṟṟi, kaṉṉi  eṉa varum. ‘meynnilai cuṭṭiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ‘tammuṉ tām varum’ eṉṟatu, meymmuṉṉar meyyeṉṉum māttiraiyaṉṟi, uṭaṉilaimey mēlatām eṉpatu koḷka. ‘ellām’ eṉṟatu, mēl ya ra ḻa (29) eṉṟa atikāram māṟṟivantu niṉṟatu.	 (30)
31.	a i uam mūṉṟuñ cuṭṭu.
(i-ḷ.) a i u am mūṉṟum cuṭṭu - (kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉṉap paṭṭa) a i u eṉṉum am mūṉṟum cuṭṭu eṉṉum kuṟiyavām.
u-m: aṅṅaṉam, iṅṅaṉam, uṅṅaṉam eṉa varum.	(31)
32.	ā ē ōam mūṉṟum viṉā.
(i-ḷ.) ā ē ō am mūṉṟum viṉā - (mēl neṭṭeḻuttu eṉṉappaṭṭa) ā ē ō eṉṉum am mūṉṟum viṉā eṉṉum kuṟiyavām.
u-m: uṇkā, uṇkē, uṇkō cāttā eṉavarum.
‘taṉṉiṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, ekaramum yakara ākāramum viṉāp peṟumeṉak koḷka. ik kuṟikaḷaiyum muṉ kuṟileṉṟum neṭileṉṟum kūṟiya vaḻiyē kūṟuka veṉiṉ, ivai col nilaimaiyiṟ peṟum kuṟiyākaliṉ, āṇṭu vaiyātu moḻimarapiṉaic cāravaittār eṉka. 
ikkuṟi moḻinilaimaikkēl eḻuttiṉmēl vaittuk kūṟiyatu eṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, ivvatikārattup peyar viṉaiyallaṉa vaṟṟiṟkuk karuvi ceyyāmaiyiṉ eṉka.	(32)
33.	aḷapiṟan tuyirttalu moṟṟicai nīṭalum
	uḷaveṉa moḻipa vicaiyoṭu civaṇiya
	narampiṉ maṟaiya veṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, eḻuttukaḷ muṟkūṟiya māttiraiyiṉ nīṇṭu niṟkum iṭam ituveṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷapu iṟantu uyirttalum - (uyireḻuttuk kaḷellām) tamakkuc coṉṉa aḷaviṉaik kaṭantu olit talaiyum, oṟṟu icai nīṭalum - oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ tammoli muṉkūṟiya aḷapiṉ nīṭalaiyum, icaiyoṭu civaṇiya narampiṉ maṟaiya - (in nūluṭ kūṟum viḷiyiṉ kaṇṇēyaṉṟik) kuralmutaliya ēḻicaiyoṭu poruntiya narampiṉai yuṭaiya yāḻiṉatu icainūṟkaṇṇum, uḷa eṉa moḻipa eṉmaṉār pulavar - uḷa eṉac colluvar av vicai nūlāciriyar eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
‘oṟṟicai nīṭalum’ eṉṟaṉar, ‘aḷapiṟan tuyirtta’ leṉṟatu atikārattāl niṉṟa uyirmēṟ cēṟaliṉ. ‘uḷa’ veṉṟatu annīṭṭippu oru talaiyaṉṟeṉpatu viḷakkiṟṟu. icainūlāciriyarum mutaṉūlāciriyar tāmē yeṉiṉum, ‘moḻipa’, eṉa vēṟoruvar pōlak kūṟiyatu, atuvum vēṟu oru nūlākac ceyyappaṭum nilaimai nōkkip pōlum. ‘maṟaiyavum’ eṉpataṉ ummai vikārattāl tokkatu. akaram ceyyuḷ vikāram.	(33)
mutalāvatu - nūṉmarapu muṟṟiṟṟu.


moḻi marapu
‘moḻikaḷukku eḻuttāṉvarum marapuṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ moḻi marapeṉappaṭṭatu’ eṉa iḷampūraṇarum ‘eḻuttāṉām moḻiyatu marapuṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ ivvōttu moḻimarapeṉak kāraṇappeyart tāyiṟṟu’ eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum ivviyaliṉ peyarkkāraṇaṅ kūṟiṉar. ivviyaluṭ kūṟukiṉṟa ilakkaṇam taṉiniṉṟa eḻuttiṟkaṉṟi moḻiyiṭai yeḻuttiṟku’ eṉa iḷampūraṇar kūṟuvar. eṉavē eḻuttukkaḷai moḻippaṭut ticaikkuṅkāl moḻiyil niṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷukku uḷavām iyalpiṉai yuṇarttuvatu ivviyaleṉpatu peṟappaṭum. ivviyaliṉ cūttiraṅkaḷ 49.
moḻiyiṉaic cārntu olittalaiyē iyalpākavuṭaiya cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟiṟkum ‘avaitām kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiya lukaram āytam eṉa muṉṉaruraitta muṟaiyē ivviyalil ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟār. ammūṉṟum oru moḻi puṇarmoḻi ākiya īriṭattum varumeṉakkoṇṭu avaṟṟai orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram, puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram, orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram, puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram, oru moḻiyāytam, puṇar moḻiyāytam eṉa āṟākappirittu muṟaiyē 1-mutal 6-varai yuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷil uṇartti 7-ām cūttirattil avvoru moḻi yāytattiṟku ōr ilakkaṇamuṅ kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivai moḻiyiṭaic cārttiyuṇarappaṭuvaṉa ākalāṉum oruvāṟṟāṉ eḻutteṉak koḷḷappaṭuvaṉa ākalāṉum nūṉ marapaiyaṭuttu moḻimarapiṉ mutaṟkaṇ viḷakkappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟaiyaṭuttu uyiraḷapeṭai, moḻi yākkam, meykaḷiṉ iyakkam, īroṟ ṟuṭaṉilai, makarak kuṟukkam, eḻuttuppōli, aikārak kuṟukkam, moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷ, moḻikkīṟāmeḻuttukkaḷ, eṉpaṉa uṇarttappaṭṭaṉa. ivaiyellām taṉi yeḻuttukkaḷilaṉṟi moḻikaḷil vaittu aṟintukoḷḷutaṟ kuriyaṉavātaliṉ ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
- ka. veḷḷaivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvaricai - 10, pak. 167-168
iraṇṭāvatu
moḻimarapu
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttō veṉiṉ, moḻikaḷukku eḻuttāṉ varum marapu uṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ moḻimarapu eṉappaṭṭatu. itaṉuḷ kūṟukiṉṟatu taṉiniṉṟa eḻuttiṟku aṉṟi moḻiyiṭai (niṉṟa) eḻuttiṟku eṉa vuṇarka.
34.	kuṟṟiya likara niṟṟal vēṇṭum
	yāveṉ ciṉaimicai yuraiyacaik kiḷavik
	kāvayiṉ varūu makara mūrntē.
it talaic cūttiram eṉ  nutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, cārpiṟ ṟōṟṟattu eḻuttukaḷiṟ kuṟṟiyalikarattiṉ orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikarattiṟku iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalikaram - orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram, uraiyacaik kiḷavikku - uraiyacaic collākiya miyā eṉpataṟku, āvayiṉ varūum - (ciṉaiyāka) ac col taṉṉiṭattu varukiṉṟa, yā eṉ ciṉaimicaiyā eṉ ciṉaimicai, makaram ūrntu niṟṟal vēṇṭum - makara oṟṟiṉai ūrntu niṟṟalai vēṇṭum (āciriyaṉ).
u-m: kēṇmiyā eṉa varum. miyā eṉṉum col iṭam. makaram paṟṟukkōṭu. yā eṉṉum ciṉaiyum makarampōlak kuṟukutaṟku oru cārpu.	(1)
35.	puṇariya ṉilaiyiṭaik kuṟukalu murittē
	uṇarak kūṟiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum.
itu, kuṟṟiyalikaram puṇarmoḻiyuḷḷum varumeṉṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) puṇar iyal nilai iṭaiyum - irumoḻi tammiṟ puṇartal iyaṉṟa nilaimaikkaṇṇum, kuṟukal urittu - av vikaram kuṟukutaluṭaittu, uṇarak kūṟiṉ - (āṇṭai iṭattiṉaiyum paṟṟuk kōṭṭiṉaiyum īṇṭu) uṇarak kūṟappukiṉ, muṉṉar tōṉṟum - (atu vēṇṭuvatillai) kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaluḷ (avviṭaṉum paṟṟuk kōṭum) tōṉṟum.
‘puṇariya ṉilai yiṭaiyum’ eṉa moḻimāṟṟi uraikka. muṉṉar tōṉṟumāṟu: “yakaram varumvaḻi yikaraṅ kuṟukum, ukarak kiḷavi tuvarat tōṉṟātu” (kuṟṟiyalukara puṇariyal 5) eṉpataṉuḷ aṟika. yakaram iṭam; ukaram cārnta valleḻuttup paṟṟuk kōṭu.
u-m: nākiyātu, varakiyātu, teḷkiyātu, eḵkiyātu, kokki yātu, kuraṅkiyātu eṉa varum.	(2)
36.	neṭṭeḻut timparun toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟuṅ
	kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntē.
iḵtu, orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟku iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭṭeḻuttu imparum - neṭṭeḻuttiṉatu piṉṉum, toṭarmoḻi īṟṟum - toṭarmoḻiyatu iṟutiyiṉum, kuṟṟiyalukaram vallāṟu ūrntu (niṟṟal vēṇṭum) - kuṟṟiyalukaram valleḻuttu āṟiṉaiyum ūrntu (niṟṟalai vēṇṭum āciriyaṉ).
‘tantu puṇarnturaittal’ (marapu. 110) eṉṉum tantiravuttiyāṉ, muṉṉiṉṟa ‘niṟṟal vēṇṭum’ eṉpatu īṇṭum puṇarkkappaṭṭatu.
u-m:  nāku, varaku eṉa varum.
neṭṭeḻuttimparum toṭarmoḻiyīṟum iṭam. val leḻuttup paṟṟukkōṭu. ivvāṟu iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum kūṟavē, moḻikku īṟātalum kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu.	(3)
37.	iṭaippaṭiṟ kuṟuku miṭaṉu māruṇṭē
	kaṭappā ṭaṟinta puṇariya lāṉa.
itu, kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇarmoḻiyuḷḷum varumeṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṭaippaṭiṉum kuṟukum iṭaṉ uṇṭu - (av vukaram) puṇarmoḻi iṭaippaṭiṉum kuṟukum iṭamuṇṭu, (ataṉ iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum yāṇṭō peṟuvateṉiṉ) kaṭappāṭu aṟinta puṇariyalāṉ - ataṉ puṇarcci muṟaimai aṟiyum kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaliṉ kaṇṇē e-ṟu.
‘iṭaippaṭiṉum kuṟukum’ eṉa moḻimāṟṟi uraikka. ak kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaluḷ, “valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu varuvaḻit, tollai yiyaṟkai nilaiyalu murittē.” (kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal. 4) eṉpataṉuḷ valloṟṟut toṭar moḻiyum valleḻuttu varuvaḻiyum iṭam. av valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟu valleḻuttup paṟṟukkōṭu.
u-m:   cekkukkaṇai, cukkukkoṭu eṉa varum.
‘iṭaṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ik kuṟukkam ciṟupāṉmai eṉṟuṇarka.  ār, ē eṉpaṉa - acaikaḷ. ‘puṇariyalāṉ’ eṉpatu vēṟṟumai mayakkam. akaram - cāriyai)	(4)
38.	kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāytap puḷḷi
	uyiroṭu puṇarnta vallāṟaṉ micaittē.
iḵtu, orumoḻi āytam varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āytap puḷḷi - āytamākiya puḷḷi, kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉar, uyiroṭu puṇarnta val āṟaṉ micaittu - uyiroṭu puṇarnta valleḻuttu āṟaṉ mēlatu e-ṟu.
u-m:  eḵku, kaḵcu eṉavarum.
kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum valleḻuttu micaiyum iṭam. iḵtu uyiraṉmaiyiṉ, itaṟkup paṟṟukkōṭu eṉpatillai.
kaḵṟītu eṉpataṉai meypiṟitākiya puṇarcci (puṇariyal - 7) eṉpa vākaliṉ, ‘puḷḷi’ eṉṟataṉāṉ āytattai meyppāṟ paṭuttuk koḷka. īṇṭu uyireṉṟatu, mēl ‘āytat toṭar moḻi’ (kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal - 1) eṉṟōtaliṉ perum pāṉmaiyum  kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉai. veḵkāmai mutaliya piṟa uyirvaravu ciṟupāṉmai yeṉak koḷka.	(5)
39.	īṟiyaṉ maruṅkiṉu micaimai tōṉṟum.
iḵtu, av vāytam puṇarmoḻiyuḷḷum varumeṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) īṟu iyal maruṅkiṉum - nilaimoḻiyīṟu varumoḻi mutaloṭu puṇarntu naṭakkum iṭattiṉum, icaimai tōṉṟum - āyta oli tōṉṟum.
u-m: 	kaḵṟītu, muḵṭītu eṉavarum. īṇṭum iṭaṅkaḷ avai.		(6)
40.	uruviṉu micaiyiṉu marukit tōṉṟum
	moḻikkuṟip pellā meḻutti ṉiyalā
	āyta maḵkāk kālai yāṉa.
iḵtu, av vorumoḻi āytattiṟku ōr ilakkaṇam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uruviṉum - oru poruḷiṉatu uruvattiṉ kaṇṇum, icaiyiṉum - ōcaiyiṉkaṇṇum, arukit tōṉṟum - ciṟupāṉmaiyāyt tōṉṟum, kuṟippumoḻi yellām - kuṟippu moḻikaḷellām, eḻuttiṉ iyalā - āyta eḻuttāṉiṭṭu eḻutappaṭṭu naṭavā, (aḵtu ekkālattumō veṉiṉ, aṉṟu), āytam aḵkāk kālai yāṉa - av vāytam taṉ arai māttirai aḷapāyc curuṅki nillātu (av vuruvum icaiyatu mikutiyum uṇarttutaṟku) nīṇṭa kālattu an nīṭcikku.
u-m: ‘kaḵṟeṉṟatu’ eṉpatu uruvu. ‘cuḵṟeṉṟatu’ eṉpatu icai.	(7)
41.	kuṉṟicai moḻivayi ṉiṉṟicai niṟaikkum
	neṭṭeḻut timpa rottakuṟ ṟeḻuttē.
itu, ‘nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ’ (nūṉmarapu. 6) eṉpataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṉṟu icai moḻivayiṉ niṉṟu icai niṟaikkum - (aḷapeṭai yōcaiyākac collā toḻiyiṉ) kuṉṟuvatāṉa ōcaiyaiyuṭaiya avvaḷapeṭai eḻuttāṉāya moḻik kaṇṇē niṉṟu avvōcaiyai niṟaikkum, (avai yāvai yeṉṉiṉ,) neṭṭeḻuttu impar otta kuṟṟeḻuttu - neṭṭeḻuttukaḷiṉ piṉṉāka (avaṟṟiṟkup piṟappāṉum puṇarcciyāṉum ōcaiyāṉum) iṉamotta kuṟṟeḻuttukaḷ.
u-m: āa, īi, ūu, ēe, ōo eṉavarum.
īṇṭu ‘moḻi’yeṉṟatu, av vaḷapeṭai eḻuttu oru poruḷ uṇartti ōreḻut torumoḻiyāy niṟkum nilaimaiyiṉai. ivaiyum moḻimēṟ kāṇappaṭutaliṟ cārpiṉ tōṟṟattu eḻuttu eṉappaṭumāleṉiṉ, perumpāṉmaiyum am moḻitāṉē avveḻuttāy varutalāṉum, ammoḻi nilaimai oḻiya vēṟeḻuttākavum collappaṭutalāṉum, avvāṟu ākāteṉpatu.
ciṟupāṉmaiyum ammoḻitāṉē eḻuttāy vārāteṉak koḷka.
u-m: ‘erutu kāluṟāatu’ eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa. 	(8)
42.	aioḷa eṉṉu māyī reḻuttiṟ
	kikara kara micainiṟai vākum.
itu, mēlataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.)  ai oḷa eṉṉum a īr eḻuttiṟku - (otta kuṟṟeḻuttu illā) ai oḷa eṉṟu collappaṭum av viraṇṭeḻuttiṟku muṉ, ikaram ukaram icainiṟai ākum - (īkāra ūkāraṅkaṭku otta kuṟṟeḻut tākiya) ikara ukaraṅkaḷ (ak kuṉṟicai moḻikkaṇ niṉṟu) ōcaiyai niṟaippaṉavākum.

ittuṇaiyum nūṉmarapiṉ oḻipu.	(9)
43.	neṭṭeḻut tēḻē yōreḻut torumoḻi.
iḵtu, eḻuttiṉāṉ moḻiyāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻum - neṭṭeḻuttākiya ēḻum, ōr eḻuttu orumoḻi - ōr eḻuttāṉākum orumoḻiyām.
u-m: ā, ī, ū, ē, ai, ō, oḷa eṉa varum.
ituvum uyirkkum uyirmeykkum potu. oḷakārattil uyirmeyyiṉaiyē koḷka. ‘ēḻum’ eṉpataṉ ummai vikārattāṉ tokkatu.	(10)
44.	kuṟṟeḻut taintu moḻiniṟai pilavē.
itu, kuṟṟeḻuttukaḷ ōreḻuttorumoḻi ākā veṉpatum, avaṟṟuḷ orumoḻiyāvaṉa uḷaveṉpatum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟeḻuttu aintum - kuṟṟeḻuttākiya aintum, moḻi niṟaipu ila - ōreḻuttāy niṉṟu oru moḻiyāy niṟaital ila; avaṟṟuṭ cila niṟaikkum.
okaram oḻinta nāṉkum cuṭṭāyum viṉāvāyum moḻi niṟaikkumaṉṟōveṉiṉ, avai iṭaiccollātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṟkuk karuvi ceyyār eṉka. ituvum uyirkkum uyirmeykkum potu. aintum eṉpataṉ ummai īṇṭu eccappaṭa niṉṟatu.
u-m: tu, no eṉavarum.	(11)
45.	ōreḻut torumoḻi yīreḻut torumoḻi
	iraṇṭiṟan ticaikkun toṭarmoḻi yuḷappaṭa
	mūṉṟē moḻinilai tōṉṟiya neṟiyē.
iḵtu, eḻuttiṉāṉ ākum moḻikaḷiṉ peyarum muṟaiyum tokaiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ōr eḻuttu oru moḻi - ōreḻuttāṉ ākum orumoḻi, īr eḻuttu orumoḻi - iraṇṭeḻuttāṉ ākum orumoḻi, iraṇṭu iṟantu icaikkum toṭarmoḻi - iraṇṭiṟantu palavaṟṟāṉ icaikkum toṭarmoḻi, uḷappaṭa mūṉṟu - uṭpaṭṭa moḻikaḷiṉ nilaimai mūṉṟām, tōṉṟiya neṟi - avai tōṉṟiya vaḻakku neṟikkaṇ. (ēkāram - īṟṟacai)
u-m: ā, maṇi, varaku, koṟṟaṉ eṉavarum.	(12)
46.	meyyi ṉiyakka makaramoṭu civaṇum.
itu, taṉi meykaḷaic collum muṟainilai itu veṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) meyyiṉ iyakkam - taṉi meykaḷiṉatu iyakkam, akaramoṭu civaṇum - akarattoṭu poruntum.
u-m: “ṭa ṟa la ḷa veṉṉum puḷḷi”  (nūṉmarapu 23) eṉavarum.
itu moḻiyiṭai (niṉṟa) eḻuttukaḷ aṉmaiyiṉ nūṉmarapiṉ vaikka veṉil taṉṉai uṇarttātu vēṟu poruḷ uṇarttum col nilaipōla ‘ṭaṟalaḷa’ veṉṟatu uyirmeyyai uṇarttātu taṉi meyyai uṇarttalāṉum, oṟṟiṉai uyirmey pōlac collukiṉṟa vaḻuvamaiti yilakkaṇattāṉum moḻimarapiṉkaṇṇa tāyiṟṟeṉa uṇarka.		(13)
47.	tammiyal kiḷappi ṉellā veḻuttum
	meynnilai mayakka māṉa millai.
itu, meymmayakkattiṟku ōr puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellā eḻuttum tam iyal kiḷappiṉ - ellā mey yeḻuttum moḻiyiṭaiyaṉṟit tam vaṭiviṉ iyalpaic collumiṭattu, meymayakkanilai māṉam illai - mey mayakka nilaiyiṉ mayaṅkivarutal kuṟṟam illai.
u-m: “valleḻut tiyaiyiṉ ṭakāra mākum” (puḷḷimayaṅku -7) eṉa varum.
itaṉai am meymmayakkattu vaikkaveṉiṉ, itu vaḻuvamaiti nōkki moḻimarapiṉkaṇṇa tāyiṟṟu. 	(14)
48.	yaraḻa veṉṉum mūṉṟumuṉ ṉoṟṟak
	kacatapa ṅañanama vīroṟ ṟākum.
iḵtu, īroṟṟu uṭaṉilai āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ya ra ḻa eṉṉum mūṉṟu - yaraḻa eṉṟu collap paṭukiṉṟa mūṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟu, muṉ oṟṟa - (kuṟiṟkīḻum neṭiṟ kīḻum) muṉṉē oṟṟāy niṟpa (avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉē), kacatapa ṅañanama īr oṟṟākum - kacatapakkaḷiṉ oṉṟātal ṅañanamakkaḷiṉ oṉṟātal oṟṟāyvara avai īroṟṟuṭaṉilaiyām.

u-m: vēykkuṟai, vēyṅkuṟai, vērkkuṟai, vērṅkuṟai, vīḻkkuṟai, vīḻṅkuṟai; ciṟai, talai, puṟam eṉa oṭṭuka.
iv vitimēl īṟṟakattu uṇarntukoḷḷappaṭumā leṉiṉ, iḵtu ‘īrkku’, ‘pīrkku’ eṉa oru moḻiyuḷḷum varutalāṉum, iraṇṭumoḻikkaṇ varutal  vikāramātalāṉum īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭṭatu. aḵtēl, itaṉai nūṉmarapiṉakattu meymmayakkattukkaṇ kūṟukaveṉiṉ, āṇṭu vēṟṟumainayam koṇṭatākaliṉ īroṟṟu uṭaṉilaiyātal nōkki oṟṟumainayampaṟṟi īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭṭatu.	(15)
49.	avaṟṟuḷ
	rakāra ḻakāraṅ kuṟṟoṟ ṟākā.
itu, rakāra ḻakāraṅkaṭku eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa mūṉṟaṉuḷḷum, rakāram ḻakāram kuṟṟu oṟṟu ākā - rakāramum ḻakāramum kuṟiṟkīḻ oṟṟākā. avai neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟām; kuṟiṟkīḻ uyirmeyyām.
u-m: tār, tāḻ eṉa neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟāy niṉṟaṉa. karu, maḻu eṉak kuṟiṟkīḻ uyirmeyyāy niṉṟaṉa.
ivvāṟu vilakkiṉamaiyiṉ, yakaram poy eṉavum nōy eṉavum iraṇṭiṭattum oṟṟāyiṟṟu. kuṟṟoṟṟu eṉpatu kuṟitākiya oṟṟu eṉap paṇputtokai. kuṟiṟkīḻ niṟṟalāṉ, kuṟiyatu eṉappaṭṭatu. īṇṭuk kuṟil neṭil eṉkiṉṟatu moḻimutal eḻuttiṉai eṉa uṇarka. itu, mēl varaiyaṟai ila eṉappaṭṭa uyirum meyyum mayaṅkum mayakkattiṟku oru varaiyaṟai kaṇṭu kūṟiṉavāṟu.	(16)
50.	kuṟumaiyu neṭumaiyu maḷaviṟ kōṭaliṉ
	ṟoṭarmoḻi yellā neṭṭeḻut tiyala.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟumaiyum neṭumaiyum aḷaviṉ kōṭaliṉ - uyireḻuttiṟkuk kuṟumaiyum neṭumaiyum aḷaviṟ koḷḷap paṭutaliṉ, toṭarmoḻi ellām neṭṭeḻuttu iyala - toṭarmoḻik kīḻ niṉṟa rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷellām neṭiṟkīḻ niṉṟa rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷiṉ iyalpaiyuṭaiya (eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum).
u-m: akar, pukar, akaḻ, pukaḻ eṉak koḷka.
‘pulavar’ eṉṟāṟpōla iraṇṭu māttiraiyai iṟantataṉ piṉṉum varumāleṉiṉ, avaiyum ‘taṉṉiṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ ‘neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟu’ eṉappaṭum. ‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷēyaṉṟi, piṟa oṟṟukaḷum ‘neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟu’ eṉappaṭum. itaṉāṉē, viral tītu eṉpuḻi lakaram ‘neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟu’ eṉṟu keṭukkappaṭum.	(17)
51.	ceyyu ḷiṟutip pōlumoḻivayiṉ
	ṉakāra makāra mīroṟ ṟākum.
itu, ceyyuṭkaṇ īroṟṟu uṭaṉilai āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ceyyuḷ iṟuti pōlum moḻivayiṉ - ceyyuḷ iṟutik kaṇ ‘pōlum’ eṉṉum moḻikkaṇ, ṉakāram makāram īr oṟṟu ākum - ṉakāramum makāramum vantu īroṟṟu uṭaṉilaiyāy niṟkum (pōli eṉavum pāṭam)
u-m: ‘emmoṭu tammaip porūuṅkāṟ poṉṉoṭu, kūviḷam pūttatu pōṉm’ eṉavarum.	 (18)
52.	ṉakāra muṉṉar makāraṅ kuṟukum.
iḵtu, “araiyaḷapu kuṟukaṉ makara muṭaittu” (nūṉmarapu 13)  eṉpataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ṉakāram muṉṉar makāram kuṟukum - (mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa) ṉakārattu muṉvanta makāram māttirai kuṟuki niṟkum.	 (19)
53.	moḻippaṭut ticaippiṉun terintuvē 		ṟicaippiṉum
	eḻuttiya  ṟiriyā veṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, eḻuttukaṭku moḻikkaṇ māttirai kāraṇamākap piṟappatōr aiyam tīrttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) moḻippaṭuttu icaippiṉum - moḻikkaṇ paṭuttuc colliṉum, terintu vēṟu icaippiṉum - terintukoṇṭu vēṟē colliṉum, eḻuttu iyal tiriyā eṉmaṉār pulavar - uyirum meyyumākiya eḻuttukaḷ (perukkam curukkam uṭaiyaṉapōṉṟu icaippiṉum,) tattam māttirai iyalpiṉ tiriyā eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m:	aḵkal, a eṉavum, āl, ā eṉavum, kaṭal, ka eṉavum, kāl, kā eṉavum kaṇṭukoḷka.
‘vēṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, eṭuttal paṭuttal mutaliya ōcai vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum eḻuttiyal tiriyā veṉpatu koḷka. (20)
54.	akara ikara maikāra mākum.
itu, pōli eḻuttu āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) akaram ikaram - akaramum ikaramum kūṭac colla, aikāramākum - aikārampōla ākum.
u-m:  aiyar, aiyar eṉavarum. atu koḷḷaṟka. 	(21)
55.	akara ukara maukāra mākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) akaram ukaram - akaramum ukaramum kūṭac colla, oḷakāram ākum - oḷakārampōla ākum e-ṟu.
u-m: oḷavai, auvai eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka.  atu koḷḷaṟka. 	(22)
56.	akarat timpar yakarap puḷḷiyum
	aiye ṉeṭuñciṉai meypeṟat tōṉṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) akarattu impar yakarap puḷḷiyum - akarattiṉ piṉṉar ikaramēyaṉṟi yakaramākiya puḷḷiyum, ai eṉ neṭuñciṉai mey peṟat tōṉṟum - ai eṉappaṭṭa neṭṭeḻuttām vaṭivupeṟat tōṉṟum.
u-m: aivaṉam, ayvaṉam eṉavarum.
‘meypeṟat tōṉṟum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, (akarattiṉ piṉṉar ukaramēyaṉṟi,) vakarappuḷḷiyum oḷakārampōla varumeṉak koḷka. 
iccūttiram,
“akarat timpar yavakarap puḷḷiyum
  aiyau neṭuñciṉai meypeṟat tōṉṟum”
eṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum.
i-ḷ: akarattu impar yavakarap puḷḷiyum akarattiṉ piṉṉar yakara voṟṟum vakara voṟṟum, ai oḷa neṭuñciṉai - aikāram oḷakāram eṉṉum neṭṭeḻuttām. meypeṟa tōṉṟum. (avai) vaṭivu peṟattōṉṟum.
u-m: aiyavi, ayyavi; oḷavai, avvai 
‘mey peṟattōṉṟum” eṉṟaṉāl, avaṟṟaik koḷka eṉṟavāṟu’.
kālap paḻaimaiyil ēṭu peyarttu eḻutiṉōr `yavakarappuḷḷi’ eṉpataṉai `yavarappuḷḷi’ eṉavum `aibyḷa neṭuñciṉai’ eṉpataṉai `aiye ṉeṭuñciṉai’ eṉavum piḻaiyāka eḻutiṉar pōlum. appiḻaippāṭattaip piḻaiyaṟṟa pāṭameṉak karuti uraiyāciriyar ataṟkut takkavāṟu uraiyeḻutic ceṉṟaṉar pōlum.) eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka.	(23)
57.	ōraḷa pāku miṭaṉumā ruṇṭē
	tēruṅ kālai moḻivayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, eṉ nutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ, uyirkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟku māttiraic curukkam kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tēruṅkālai moḻivayiṉ ōr aḷapu ākum iṭaṉum uṇṭu - aikāram ārāyuṅkālattu moḻikkaṇ ōr aḷapāy niṟkum iṭamum uṇṭu.
u-m: iṭaiyaṉ, maṭaiyaṉ eṉavarum.
‘tēruṅ kālai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, mutaṟkaṇ curuṅkāteṉpatu koḷka. “iṭaṉ” eṉṟataṉāṉ ik kuṟukkam ciṟupāṉmai yeṉpatu koḷka.	
iccūttirattiṟkup piṉvarumāṟu uraittalē porutta muṭaittu:-
iḵtu, uyirkaḷuḷ iraṇṭaṟku māttiraic curukkam kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
i-ḷ: tērum kālai moḻivayiṉ āṉa- ārāyuṅkālattu moḻikkaṇ niṉṟa ākāra ūkāraṅkaḷ, ōr aḷapu ākum iṭaṉum uṇṭu ōr aḷapāy niṟkum iṭamum uṇṭu.
`tērum `kālai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, taṉiyē  niṉṟa aikāramum oḷakāramum oru māttiraiyāku meṉṟukoḷka.
u-m: pai, mai, kai; kau, cau, veḷa.
`iṭaṉum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ ikkuṟukkam ciṟupāṉmai yeṉpatu koḷka. ār eṉpaṉa acaikaḷ.
mēl “yakarappuḷḷiyum aiya ṉeṭuñciṉai” eṉṟa piḻaip pāṭattaip piḻaiyaṟṟa pāṭameṉak koṇṭatāl urai yāciriyar īṇṭu oḷakārattaik kūṟātu aikāram ōr aḷapāy niṟkum iṭamum uṇṭu eṉṟu uraittuc ceṉṟaṉar pōlum.     		(24)
58.	ikara yakara miṟuti viravum.
ituvum oru pōliyeḻuttu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṟuti ikara yakaram viravum - ikaravīṟṟu moḻikkaṇ yakaramum (atupōla) ikaramum viravivarum e-ṟu.
u-m: nāy, nāi eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka. (viravum eṉṟataṉāl, avaiyiraṇṭum koḷka eṉṟavāṟu.)	  (25)
59.	paṉṉī ruyiru moḻimuta lākum.
iḵtu, uyireḻuttu moḻikku mutalāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉṉīr uyirum moḻimutal ākum - paṉṉiraṇṭu uyireḻuttum moḻikku mutalām e-ṟu.
u-m: aṭai, āṭai, ilai, īyam, ural, ūrti, eḻu, ēṇi, aivaṉam, oḷi, ōkkam, oḷaviyam eṉavarum.	(26)
60.	uyirmey yallaṉa moḻimuta lākā.
itu, meyyeḻuttu moḻikku mutalāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyirmey allaṉa moḻi mutal ākā - uyiroṭu kūṭiya meyyallātaṉavākiya taṉimeykaḷ moḻikku mutalākā; uyiroṭu kūṭiya meykaḷ moḻikku mutalām.
