īṇṭu uyirmey yeṉpatu vēṟṟumainayam karutiyeṉa vuṇarka. īṇṭu oṟṟumai karutil, “katana pamaeṉum āvain teḻuttum, ellā vuyiroṭuñ cellumār mutalē”   (moḻimarapu - 28) eṉac cūttiram curuṅka varuvataṉṟi, itaṉāṟ collappaṭṭa aṟupatu uyirmeyyiṉai eṭuttōta vēṇṭic cūttiram parakka varumeṉpatu.
(`curuṅka ōtumaṉṟi’ eṉavum, `ōtavēṇṭil’ eṉavum muntiya accup piratiyilum ēṭṭup piratikaḷilum kāṇappaṭum (uraip) pāṭam ēṭupeyart teḻutiṉōrāl nērnta piḻai pōlum)	 (27)
61.	katana pamaeṉu māvain teḻuttum
	ellā vuyiroṭuñ cellumār mutalē.
itu, mēl mutalām eṉṉappaṭṭa uyirmeykaṭku varaiyaṟai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ka ta na pa ma eṉum a aintu eḻuttum - ka ta na pa ma eṉṟu collappaṭṭa aintu taṉi meyyeḻuttum, ellā uyiroṭum mutal cellum - paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirōṭum moḻikku mutalātaṟkuc cellum.
u-m: kalai, kāḷi, kiḷi, kīri, kuṭi, kūṭu, keṇṭai, kēḻal, kaitai, koṇṭal, kōṭai, kauvai eṉavum; tantai, tāṭi, tiṟṟi, tīmai, tuṇi, tūṇi, teṟṟi, tēvar, taiyal, toṇṭai, tōṭu, tauvai eṉavum; naṭam, nārai, nilam, nīr, nuḻai, nūl, neytal, nēyam, naikai, noyyaṉ, nōkkam, nauvi eṉavum; paṭai, pāṭi, piṭi, pīṭam, pukaḻ, pūmi, peṭai, pēṭi, paital, poṉ, pōtakam, pauvam eṉavum; maṭam, mālai, miṭaṟu, mīṉam, mukam, mūtūr, melintatu, mēṉi, maiyal, moḻi, mōtakam, mauval eṉavum varum.
‘mutaṟku’ eṉpataṉ nāṉkām urupu vikārattāṉ tokkatu. (ār eṉpatu  acai. ēkāram - īṟṟacai)	(28)
62.	cakarak kiḷaviyu mavaṟṟō raṟṟē
	aai oḷaveṉu mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟu ōr aṟṟu - cakaramākiya eḻuttum mēṟcollappaṭṭavaipōla ellā uyiroṭum moḻikku mutalām, a ai oḷa eṉum mūṉṟu alaṅkaṭai - a ai oḷa eṉṉum mūṉṟum allāviṭattu.
u-m: cālai, cilai, cīṟuka, curumpu, cūḻka, ceykai, cēvaṭi, coṟika, cōṟu eṉa varum.
cakaṭam eṉavum, caiyam eṉavum vilakkiṉavum varumāleṉiṉ, avaṟṟuḷ āriyaccitai vallātaṉa ‘kaṭicol illai’  eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka. (ēkāram iraṇṭum īṟṟacaikaḷ. innūl mūlattil `ōr aṉṉa,’ `ōr aṟṟu’ eṉṟu varum iṭaṅkaḷil `ōr’ eṉpataṉai acaiyeṉṟu koḷḷutal poruttamuṭaiyatākat tōṟṟukiṉṟatu.)	 (29)
63.	uū oō veṉṉu nāṉkuyir
	vaeṉ ṉeḻuttoṭu varuta lillai.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) u ū o ō eṉṉum nāṉku uyir - u ū o ō eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa nāṉku uyirum, va eṉ eḻuttoṭu varutal illai - va eṉṉum meyyeḻuttoṭu moḻi mutalil varutalillai.
piṟa uyirkaḷ varum.
u-m: vaḷai, vāḷi, viḷari, vīṭu, veḷḷi, vēr, vaiyam, veḷavu eṉa varum.	(30)
64.	ā e
	oeṉu mūvuyir ñakārat turiya.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ā e o eṉum mū uyir ñakārattu uriya - ā e o eṉṟu collappaṭum mūvuyirkaḷ ñakāra oṟṟoṭu mutalātaṟku uriya; piṟa uyir uriyavalla.
u-m: ñālam, ñekiḻi, ñoḷkiṟṟu eṉavarum.
ñaḻiyiṟṟu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa vilakkiṉavum varumā leṉiṉ, avai aḻi vaḻakkeṉṟu maṟukka.	  (31)
(pāṭam)  `aḻi vaḻakkeṉṟu maṟukka’ eṉpatu.
65.	āvō ṭallatu yakara mutalātu.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) āvōṭu allatu yakaram mutalātu - ākārattōṭu allatu yakaram mutalākātu.
u-m: yāṉ eṉa varum.
yavaṉar eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa vilakkiṉavum varumāleṉiṉ, avai āriyac citaiveṉṟu maṟukka.	(32)
66.	mutalā vēṉa tampeyar mutalum.
itu, moḻikku mutalākātaṉavum orōvaḻi āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mutalā ēṉa - moḻikku mutalākāta oḻinta meykaḷum, tam peyar mutalum - (av veḻuttukaḷ) tam peyar kūṟutaṟku mutalām.
mutalāyiṉa mey katanapamakkaḷum, vakaramum, cakaramum, ñakaramum, yakaramum eṉa ivai. mutalākāta mey ṅakaramum, ṭakaramum, ṇakaramum, rakaramum, lakaramum, ḻakaramum, ḷakaramum, ṟakaramum, ṉakaramum eṉa ivai.
u-m: avai tam peyarkku mutalāmāṟu: ṅakkaḷaintār, ṭapperitu, ṇannaṉṟu eṉṟāṟpōla oṭṭik koḷka.
iṉi ‘ēṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, mutalām eṉṉappaṭṭa oṉpatu uyir meyyum paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum tam peyar kūṟum vaḻiyum moḻikku mutalām eṉakkoḷka.
u-m: kakkaḷaintār, tapperitu, akkuṟitu, āvalitu eṉṟāṟ pōla oṭṭikkoḷka.	(33)
67.	kuṟṟiya lukara muṟaippeyar maruṅkiṉ
	oṟṟiya nakaramicai nakaramoṭu mutalum.
itu, kuṟṟiyalukaram moḻikku mutalāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟaippeyar maruṅkiṉ - kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟaip peyariṭattu, oṟṟiya nakaramicai nakaramoṭu mutalum - oṟṟāy niṉṟa nakarattiṉ mēlāya nakarattoṭu moḻikku mutalām.
u-m: nuntai eṉa varum.
ivvāṟu mutalākkam kūṟavē, moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiya lukarattiṟku iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. iṭam nuntai eṉṉum muṟaippeyar. paṟṟukkōṭu nakaramicai nakaram. (nakaramicai nakaram - nakara oṟṟiṉ mēluḷḷa nakara oṟṟu)	 (34)
68.	muṟṟiya lukaramoṭu poruḷvēṟu paṭāa
	tappeyar maruṅki ṉilaiyiya lāṉa.
itu, mēlataṟku ōr puṟaṉaṭai.
(i-ḷ.) muṟṟiyalukaramoṭu poruḷ vēṟupaṭātu - (am mutaṟkaṭ kuṟṟiyalukaram āṇṭu itaḻkuvittuk kūṟum vaḻi) muṟṟukarattoṭu piṟa ukarampōlap poruḷ vēṟupaṭātu; (yāṇṭeṉiṉ,) ap peyar maruṅkiṉ nilai iyalāṉ - am muṟaippeyariṭattut tāṉ niṟṟaṟkaṇ e-ṟu.
u-m:  nāku eṉavum, naku eṉavum kuṟukiyum, kuṟukātum niṉṟa ukaraṅkaḷpōla, nuntai eṉṟu kuṟukkamāya vaḻiyum, itaḻ kuvittuk kūṟak kuṟukātavaḻiyum, poruḷ vēṟupaṭātavāṟu aṟika.
iṉi ‘iraṭṭuṟamoḻital’ eṉpataṉāl, poruḷ eṉṟataṉai iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum ākki, piṟa ukaraṅkaḷpōla īṇṭai ukaraṅkaḷ iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum vēṟupaṭātu eṉpatu koḷka. (īṟṟakaram cāriyai)	(35)

69.	uyir oḷa veñciya viṟuti yākum.
iḵtu, uyir moḻikku īṟāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir oḷa eñciya iṟuti ākum - uyireḻuttu kaḷuḷ oḷakāram oḻintavai moḻikku īṟām, oḷakārantāṉ īṟākātu.
iḵtu uyirkkum uyirmeykkum potu.
u-m: āa, ā; īi, ī; ūu, ū;ēe, ē; aii, ai; ōo, ō eṉa uyir īṟāyiṉa. ivaṟṟuṭ kuṟṟeḻuttaintum aḷapeṭai vakaiyāṉ īṟāyiṉa.
uyirmeykaḷum, mēlvaraiyaṟai kūṟātaṉavākiya akara ākāra ikara īkāra aikāraṅkaḷōṭu iyaintaṉa īṇṭē koḷka.
u-m: viḷa, palā,kiḷi, kurī, paṉai eṉa varum.	(36)
70.	kavavō ṭiyaiyi ṉauvu mākum.
iḵtu, īṟākāteṉṟa oḷakāram iṉṉuḻi ām eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ka va ōṭu iyaiyiṉ - kakara vakaramākiya meykaḷoṭu poruntiṉ, oḷaum ākum - muṉ īṟākā teṉṟa oḷakāramum īṟām.
u-m: kau, veḷa eṉa varum.	(37)
71.	e eṉa varumuyir meyyī ṟākātu.
iḵtu, ekaram tāṉē niṉṟavaḻiyaṉṟi meyyōṭukūṭi īṟākāteṉa varaiyaṟai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) e eṉa varum uyir - e eṉṟu colla varum uyir, mey īṟu ākātu - tāṉē īṟāvataṉṟi meyyōṭu iyaintu īṟākātu.	(38)
72.	ovvu maṟṟē navvalaṅ kaṭaiyē.
ituvum varaiyaṟai.
(i-ḷ.) ovvum aṟṟu - okaramum tāṉē niṉṟu īṟāva taṉṟi meyyōṭu iyaintu īṟākātu, na alaṅkaṭai - nakara meyyō ṭallāta viṭattu e-ṟu.
u-m: nau eṉa varum.	(39)
73.	ēō eṉumuyir ñakārat tillai.
ituvum varaiyaṟai.
(i-ḷ.) ē ō eṉum uyir ñakārattu illai - ē ō eṉṟu collappaṭum uyir (tāmē niṉṟum piṟa meykaḷoṭu niṉṟum īṟāvataṉṟi) ñakarattōṭu īṟāvatillai.
tāmē yātal muṉṉē kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. piṟa meykaḷōṭu īṟāvaṉavaṟṟuḷ vaḻakkiṟantaṉa voḻiya iṟavātaṉa vantavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.	(40)
74.	uū kāra navavoṭu navilā.
ituvum varaiyaṟai.
(i-ḷ.) u ūkāram na va oṭu navilā - ukara ūkāraṅkaḷ (tāmē niṉṟum piṟa meykaḷoṭu niṉṟum payilvataṉṟi) nakara vakaraṅkaḷoṭu payilā.
tāmē īṟātal mēlē kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. piṟa meykaḷōṭu īṟāmavaṟṟuḷ vaḻakkiṟantaṉa vallātaṉa vantavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka. ‘navilā’ eṉṟataṉāṟ ciṟupāṉmai novvu kavvu eṉṟu (ukaram) vakārattōṭu īṟātal koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē ciṟupāṉmai nakarattōṭu varavu uṇṭēṉum koḷka. 	(41)
75.	ucca kāra mirumoḻik kurittē.
ituvum atu orōvaḻi varaiyaṟai.
(i-ḷ.) uccakāram irumoḻikku urittu - ukarattoṭu kūṭiya cakaram iru moḻikku īṟākum; (palamoḻikku īṟākātu).
u-m: ucu, mucu eṉa varum. pacuveṉpatu āriyac citaivu.  (42)
76.	uppa kāra moṉṟeṉa moḻipa
	iruvayi ṉilaiyum poruṭṭā kummē.
ituvum moḻivaraiyaṟaiyum moḻiyatu poruḷ pāṭum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uppakāram oṉṟu eṉa moḻipa - ukarattoṭu kūṭiya pakaram oru moḻikku īṟām eṉṟu colluvar, iruvayiṉ nilaiyum poruṭṭu ākum - atutāṉ taṉviṉai piṟaviṉai eṉṉum iraṇṭiṭattum nilaipeṟum poruṇmait tām e-ṟu.
u-m: tapu eṉa varum. itu paṭuttuc colla, nī cā eṉat taṉ viṉaiyām. eṭuttuc colla, nī oṉṟaṉaic cāvippi eṉap piṟa viṉaiyām. (ēkāram - īṟṟacai.)	(43)
77.	eñciya vellā meñcuta lilavē.
itu, moḻikku īṟākā uyirmeyyum orōvaḻi āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) eñciya ellām eñcutal ila - moḻikku īṟākātu niṉṟa uyirmeykaḷellām tam peyar kūṟumvaḻi īṟātaṟku oḻipu ila.
eñciya uyirmeyyāvaṉa: oḷakāram kakāra vakāraṅkaḷai oḻinta meyyōṭu iyainta uyirmeyyum, ekaram ellā meyyōṭum iyainta uyirmeyyum, okaram nakaram oḻinta meyyōṭu iyainta uyirmeyyum, ēkāra ōkāram ñakārattōṭu iyainta uyir meyyum, u ūkāram nakara vakaraṅkaḷōṭu iyainta uyirmeyyum eṉa ivai.
u-m: tam peyarkku (īṟu) āmāṟu: ṅauk kaḷaintār eṉavum, kek kaḷaintār eṉavum, kok kaḷaintār eṉavum, ñēk kaḷaintār eṉavum, ñōk kaḷaintār eṉavum, nuk kaḷaintār eṉavum, nūk kaḷaintār eṉavum, vuk kaḷaintār eṉavum, vūk kaḷaintār eṉavum varum.  ‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, moḻikku īṟāy niṉṟa uyir meykaḷum tam peyar kūṟum vaḻiyum īṟām eṉṟu koḷka. kak kaḷaintār, kā valitu eṉavum, ak kaḷaintār, ā valitu eṉavum ivvāṟē oṭṭik koḷka. taṉṉai uṇara niṉṟavaḻi, moḻikaṭku itu karuviyāka īṟṟakattu muṭipu ‘oṉṟiṉa muṭittalāṟ’ koḷka.	(44)
78.	ñaṇanama ṉayarala vaḻaḷa veṉṉum
	appati ṉoṉṟē puḷḷi yiṟuti.
itu, taṉimeykaḷuḷ moḻikku īṟāvaṉa kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ña ṇa na ma ṉa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṉum ap patiṉoṉṟē - ña ṇa na ma ṉa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa ap patiṉoṉṟumē, puḷḷi iṟuti - puḷḷikaḷil moḻikku īṟāvaṉa.
u-m: uriñ, maṇ, porun, tirum, poṉ, vēy, vēr, vēl, tev, vīḻ, vēḷ eṉa varum. ṉakārattai īṟṟu vaiyātu, makarattoṭu vaittatu ataṉ mayakka iyaipu nōkki eṉṟuṇarka.	 (45)
79.	ucca kāramoṭu nakārañ civaṇum.
ituvum moḻivaraiyaṟai.
(i-ḷ.) uccakāramoṭu nakāram civaṇum - ukarattōṭu kūṭiya cakārattōṭē nakāram porunti aḵtu irumoḻikku īṟāyavāṟu pōlat tāṉum irumoḻikku īṟām. 
u-m: porun, verin eṉa varum.	          (46)
(ukarattōṭu kūṭiya cakaram irumoḻikku īṟāyavāṟu pōla nakaram iru moḻikku īṟām eṉka.)
80.	uppa kāramoṭu ñakāraiyu maṟṟē
	apporu ḷiraṭṭā tivaṇai yāṉa.
ituvum moḻivaraiyaṟai.
(i-ḷ.) uppakāramoṭu ñakāraiyum aṟṟu - ukarattōṭu kūṭiya pakarattoṭu ñakāramum attaṉmaittāy orumoḻikku īṟām, ivaṇaiyāṉa a poruḷ iraṭṭātu - ivviṭattu ataṉ poruḷ av vuppakāram pōla iruporuḷpaṭātu.
u-m: uriñ eṉa varum.
ñakāram orumoḻikku īṟātaliṉ, nakarattiṉpiṉ kūṟappaṭṭatu. (ēkāram - īṟṟacai)	(47)
81.	vakarak kiḷavi nāṉmoḻi yīṟṟatu.
ituvum moḻivaraiyaṟai.
(i-ḷ.) vakarak kiḷavi nāṉmoḻi īṟṟatu - vakaramākiya eḻuttu nāṉkumoḻi īṟṟatām e-ṟu.
u-m: av, iv, uv, tev eṉa varum.	(48)
82.	makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varainta
	ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi yoṉpaḵ teṉpa
	pukaraṟak kiḷanta vaḵṟiṇai mēṉa.
ituvum orōvaḻi moḻivaraiyaṟai.
(i-ḷ.) pukar aṟa kiḷanta aḵṟiṇai mēṉa - kuṟṟam aṟac collappaṭṭa aḵṟiṇaiyiṭattu, makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varainta - makaravīṟṟut toṭarmoḻiyoṭu mayaṅkāteṉṟu varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭa, ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi oṉpaḵtu eṉpa - ṉakaravīṟṟut toṭarmoḻi oṉpateṉṟu colluvar.
u-m: nilaṉ nilam, pilaṉ pilam eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa mayaṅkuvaṉa. iṉi mayaṅkātaṉa: ekiṉ, cekiṉ, viḻaṉ, payiṉ, aḻaṉ, puḻaṉ, kuyiṉ, kaṭāṉ, vayāṉ eṉa varum.
ivaṟṟuḷ tiripuṭaiyaṉa kaḷaika. oṉpaḵtu eṉṉum āytam ceyyuḷ vikāram. aḵṟiṇai yeṉṟatu - īṇṭu aḵṟiṇaip peyariṉai.	 (49)
iraṇṭāvatu - moḻimarapu muṟṟiṟṟu.


piṟappiyal
akara mutal ṉakaraviṟutiyākavuḷḷa mutaleḻuttukkaḷ muppataiyum nūṉmarapilum cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟaiyum moḻi marapilum vaittuṇarttiya āciriyar. ammuppattu mūṉṟeḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappu muṟaiyiṉai ivviyalāl uṇarttukiṉṟār. ataṉāl ivviyal piṟappiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu.
untiyilirunteḻukiṉṟa kāṟṟāṉatu talai, miṭaṟu, neñcu eṉṉum mūṉṟaṭaṅkaḷilum nilaipeṟṟu avaṟṟuṭaṉ pal, utaṭu, nā, mūkku, aṇṇam (mēlvāy) ākiya uṟuppukkaḷ tammiṟ porunti amaitipeṟa vēṟuvēṟuruvākiya eḻuttukkaḷāyp piṟantu pulappaṭa vaḻaṅkutalē eḻuttukkaḷuk kuriya potuvākiya piṟappu muṟaiyākum itaṉai ivviyaliṉ mutaṟ cūttirattu āciriyar viritturaikkiṉṟār. uyireḻuttukkaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭum tattam nilaitiriyātu miṭaṟṟu vaḷiyāṟ piṟakkum. eṉavē uyireḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappiṭam miṭaṟu eṉpatu pulaṉām. ōriṭattē oru muyaṟciyāṟ palaveḻuttukkaḷ piṟakkumeṉap potuvakaiyāṟ kūṟiṉum avveḻuttuk kaḷiṭaiyē nuṇṇiya vēṟupāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉaveṉa āciriyar teḷivupaṭuttukiṉṟār.
ivviyaliṉkaṇ 7-mutal 11-varaiyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷil kaṅa, caña, ṭaṇa, tana, ākiya meyyeḻuttukkaḷukku neṭuṅ kaṇakku muṟai paṟṟip piṟappuk kūṟukiṉṟār. 12 mutal nāvatikāram paṟṟip piṟappukkūṟat toṭaṅki ṟaṉa, raḻa, laḷa, pama, vaya, eṉṉum meyyeḻuttukkaḷukkup piṟappuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. iṅkup piṟappukkūṟiya meyyeḻuttukkaḷuḷ melleḻut tāṟum tattam piṟappiṉatu ākkañ colliya iṭattē nilai peṟṟaṉavāyiṉum avai mūkkiṉkaṇ uḷatākiya kāṟṟōcaiyāl iyaipupeṟat tōṉṟiyolippaṉavām. ivvāṟu melleḻut tāṟukkum mūkkuvaḷiyiṉ toṭarpuṭaimaiyiṉaic collavē iṭaiyeḻut tāṟum miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyaiyum valleḻuttāṟum talaivaḷiyaiyum peṟṟu olippaṉaveṉak koṇṭār iḷampūraṇar.
mutaleḻuttukkaḷaic cārntutōṟṟiṉallatu taṉit tolikkum iyalpillātaṉa eṉa ārāyntu veḷippaṭuttap paṭṭa cārpeḻuttukkaḷ mūṉṟum tattamakkuc cārpākiya muta leḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappiṭattaiyē tamakkuriya piṟappiṭamākap porunti icaippaṉavām. ivvāṟu eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟap puṇarttiya tolkāppiyaṉār avveḻuttukkaḷ peṟum māttiraiyiṉaik kuṟittukkūṟum viḷakkam ivaṇ karutattakuva tākum. ellāveḻuttukkaḷaiyum veḷippaṭac collap paṭṭa iṭattiṉkaṇ eḻuṅkāṟṟiṉālē olikkumiṭattu avveḻuttukkaḷ yāvum koppūḻaṭiyilirunteḻuṅ kāṟṟāṉatu talaiyaḷavuñ ceṉṟu mīṇṭum neñciṉkaṇ nilaipeṟutalākiya tiritaruṅ kūṟu pāṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiyaṉa. ivveḻuttukkaḷukku iṅṅaṉam uṟaḻcci vārattiṉāl uḷatām akatteḻuvaḷiyicaiyiṉai nuṭpamāka ārāyntu māttirai varaiyaṟaiyāl aḷantu koḷḷutal antaṇaratu maṟainūṟ kaṇṇatākiya muṟaiyākum. ammuṟaiyiṉai innūliṟ collātu ellārkkum pulaṉākap puṟattē veḷippaṭṭicaikkum meyterivaḷiyicaiyākiya eḻuttuk kaḷukkē yāṉ iṅku māttirai kūṟiṉēṉ eṉpatu āciriyar kūṟiya viḷakkamākum. untiyil eḻuntakāṟṟu muṉṉart talaikkaṭ ceṉṟu piṉṉar miṭaṟṟilēvantu ataṉpiṉṉar neñcilē niṟṟal uṟaḻccivāram eṉappaṭum. mūlātārattilirun teḻuṅ kāṟṟōcai akatteḻuvaḷiyicai yeṉappaṭṭatu. ellārkkum eḻutturuvam naṉku pulaṉāka vāyiliruntu puṟattē veḷippaṭ ṭicaikkum kāṟṟiṉālākiya eḻuttōcaiyē meyterivaḷi yicai yeṉappaṭum. antaṇar maṟaiyiṟ kūṟumāṟu akat teḻuvaḷiyicaiyākiya eḻuttukkaḷukku aḷapukūṟiṉ aḵtu ellārkkum naṉṟāka viḷaṅkāteṉak karutiya tolkāppiyaṉār puṟatteḻunticaikkum meyterivaḷi yicaiyākiya eḻuttukkaḷukkē māttirai kūṟuvārāyiṉar.
untuyileḻunta kāṟṟiṉaik kūṟupaṭutti māttirai kūṭṭikkoḷḷutalum, mūlātāram mutalākak kāṟṟeḻumāṟu kūṟutalum, antaṇar maṟaikku uḷateṉṟu kūṟiya ivvāciriyar, avar matam paṟṟip peṟuvatōr payaṉiṉṟeṉa ic cūttirattāl uyttuṇara vaittaliṉ, iccūttiram piṟaṉ kōṭkūṟal eṉṉum uttikkiṉam eṉṟār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
- veḷḷaivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvaricai 10, pak. 168-170


mūṉṟāvatu 
piṟappiyal
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttōveṉiṉ, eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟappu uṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ  piṟappiyal  eṉṉum peyarttu. itaṉai nūṉmarapiṉ piṉṉē vaikkaveṉiṉ, cārpiṟṟōṟṟat teḻuttum taṉimeyyum moḻimarapiṭai uṇarttip piṟappu uṇartta vēṇṭutaliṉ, moḻi marapiṉ piṉṉatāyiṟṟu.

83.	unti mutalā muntuvaḷi tōṉṟit
	talaiyiṉu miṭaṟṟiṉu neñciṉu nilaiip
	pallu mitaḻu nāvu mūkkum
	aṇṇamu muḷappaṭa veṇmuṟai nilaiyāṉ
	uṟuppuṟ ṟamaiya neṟippaṭa nāṭi
	ellā veḻuttuñ colluṅ kālaip
	piṟappi ṉākkam vēṟuvē ṟiyala
	tiṟappaṭat teriyuṅ kāṭci yāṉa.
it talaic cūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, eḻuttukaḷatu potup piṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellā eḻuttum neṟippaṭa nāṭi collum kālai - tamiḻeḻuttukaḷellām oruvaṉ muṟaippaṭa ārāyntu tammaic colluṅkālattu, unti mutalā muntuvaḷi tōṉṟi - koppūḻ aṭiyāka mēlē kiḷarkiṉṟa utāṉaṉ eṉṉum peyaraiyuṭaiya vāyut tōṉṟi, talaiyiṉum miṭaṟṟiṉum neñciṉum nilaii - talaiyiṉ kaṇṇum miṭaṟṟiṉ kaṇṇum neñciṉkaṇṇum nilai peṟṟu, pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum aṇṇamum uḷappaṭa eṇmuṟai nilaiyāṉ uṟuppu uṟṟu amaiya - (talaiyum miṭaṟum neñcum eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉōṭum) pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum aṇṇamum uḷappaṭa eṭṭākiya muṟaimaiyaiyuṭaiya iṭaṅkaḷiṉ ōr uṟuppōṭu ōr uṟupput tammiṟ porunti amaitipeṟa, piṟappiṉ ākkam vēṟu vēṟu iyala - piṟappiṉatu ākkam vēṟu vēṟu pulappaṭṭu vaḻaṅkutaluṭaiya, tiṟappaṭat teriyum kāṭciyāṉa - kūṟupāṭuḷatāka ārāyum aṟiviṭattu.
itaḻpōṟalāṉ vāy itaḻeṉappaṭṭatu. ‘ellā eḻuttum’ eṉṉum eḻuvāykkup ‘piṟappi ṉākkam vēṟuvēṟu iyala’ eṉpataṉai orucoṉ ṉīrmaippaṭuttip payaṉilai yākkuka. (`mutalāka’ eṉpatu īṟu keṭṭu niṉṟatu īṟṟakaram cāriyai) 	(1)
84.	avvaḻip
	paṉṉī ruyirun tannilai tiriyā
	miṭaṟṟup piṟanta vaḷiyi ṉicaikkum.
iḵtu, uyireḻuttiṟkup potuppiṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) a vaḻi - avviṭattu, paṉṉīr uyirum tam nilai tiriyā - paṉṉiraṇṭu uyireḻuttum tattam nilaiyiṉ tiriyāvāy, miṭaṟṟu piṟanta vaḷiyiṉ icaikkum - miṭaṟṟiṉkaṭ piṟanta vaḷiyāṉ olikkum.
‘tannilai tiriyā’ eṉṟataṉāl, kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiya lukaram tannilai tiriyumeṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu.	(2)
85.	avaṟṟuḷ
	aā vāyiraṇ ṭaṅkān tiyalum.
iḵtu, uyireḻuttukaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkuc ciṟappup piṟavi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟcollappaṭṭa uyir paṉṉiraṇ ṭaṉuḷ, a ā airaṇṭu aṅkāntu iyalum - akara ākāraṅkaḷākiya av viraṇṭum aṅkāntu colla aḵtu iṭamākap piṟakkum.	(3)
86.	iī eē aiyeṉa vicaikkum
	appā laintu mavaṟṟō raṉṉa
	avaitām
	aṇpaṉ mutaṉā viḷimpuṟa luṭaiya.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) i ī e ē ai eṉa icaikkum a pāl aintum - i ī e ē ai eṉṟu collappaṭum ak kūṟṟu aintum, avaṟṟu ōr aṉṉa - mēṟkūṟiya akara ākāraṅkaḷ pōla aṅkāntu collap piṟakkum, avaitām palaṇ nāmutal viḷimpu uṟal uṭaiya - avaitām (avvāṟu collap piṟakkumiṭattup) palliṉatu aṇiya iṭattiṉai nāviṉatu aṭiyiṉ viḷimpu ceṉṟu uṟutalai yuṭaiya.	(4)
87.	uū oō oḷaeṉa icaikkum
	appā laintum itaḻkuvin tiyalum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) u ū o ō oḷa eṉa icaikkum a pāl aintum - u ū o ō oḷa eṉac colla icaikkum ak kūṟṟu aintum, itaḻ kuvintu iyalum - itaḻ kuvittuc colla naṭakkum.	(5)
88.	tattan tiripē ciṟiya veṉpa.
itu, muṉkūṟiya uyirkkum mēṟkūṟum meykkum ōr puṟaṉaṭai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tamtam tiripu ciṟiya eṉpa - (eḻuttukaḷ oru tāṉattuk kūṭip piṟakku meṉappaṭṭaṉa; avvāṟu kūṭip piṟappiṉum,) tattam vēṟupāṭukaḷaic ciṟiya vēṟupāṭukaḷeṉṟu colluvar. av vēṟupāṭu aṟintukoḷka. (ēkāram - acai).	(6)
89.	kakāra ṅakāram mutaṉā vaṇṇam.
itu, meykaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkup piṟavi āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kakāram ṅakāram nāmutal aṇṇam (mutal) - kakāramum ṅakāramum nāmutalum aṇṇamutalum uṟap 
piṟakkum. 	(7)
90.	cakāra ñakāra miṭainā vaṇṇam.
(i-ḷ.) cakāram ñakāram nāiṭai aṇṇam (iṭai) - cakāramum ñakāramum nāvatu iṭaiyum aṇṇattatu iṭaiyum uṟap piṟakkum.	(8)
91.	ṭakāra ṇakāra nuṉinā vaṇṇam.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ṭakāram ṇakāram nānuṉi aṇṇam (nuṉi) - ṭakāramum ṇakāramum nāvatu nuṉiyum aṇṇattatu nuṉiyum uṟap piṟakkum.	(9)
92.	avvā ṟeḻuttum mūvakaip piṟappiṉa.
itu mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku oru puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) a āṟu eḻuttum mūvakai piṟappiṉa - mēṟ kūṟappaṭṭa āṟu eḻuttum niraṉiṟai vakaiyāṉ (aṟuvakaip piṟappiṉa alla) mūvakaip piṟappiṉa.	(10)
93.	aṇṇaṉ  naṇṇiya paṉmutal maruṅkiṉ
	nānuṉi parantu meyyuṟa voṟṟat
	tāmiṉitu piṟakkun takāra nakāram.
ituvum meykaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟkup piṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aṇṇam naṇṇiya pal mutal maruṅkiṉ - aṇṇattaip poruntiya palliṉatu aṇiya iṭattiṉ kaṇṇē, nānuṉi parantu mey uṟa oṟṟa - nāviṉatu nuṉi parantu vaṭivai uṟumpaṭi oṟṟa, tām iṉitu piṟakkum - tām iṉitākap piṟakkum, takāram nakāram - takāramum nakāramum.
muṉṉē ‘uṟuppuṟ ṟamaiya’ (83) eṉṟu vaittup piṉṉum ‘meyyuṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ellā eḻuttukaḷum meyyuṟṟa pōtē iṉitu piṟappaveṉpatu koḷka.	(11)
94.	aṇari nuṉinā vaṇṇa moṟṟa
	ṟaḵkā ṉaḵkā ṉāyiraṇṭum piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aṇari nuṉinā aṇṇam oṟṟa - aṇarntu nuṉinā aṇṇattaic ceṉṟu oṟṟa, ṟaḵkāṉ ṉaḵkāṉ a iraṇṭum piṟakkum - ṟakāra ṉakāramākiya av viraṇṭum piṟakkum.
iṅkuniṉṟu neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟaimai viṭṭu nā atikāram paṭṭatu kaṇṭu kūṟukiṉṟateṉa vuṇarka.	 (12)
95.	nuṉinā vaṇari  yaṇṇam varuṭa
	rakāra ḻakāra māyiraṇṭum piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nuṉinā aṇari aṇṇam varuṭa - nuṉinā aṇarntu aṇṇattait taṭava, rakāra ḻakāram a iraṇṭum piṟakkum - rakāra ḻakāramākiya avviraṇṭum piṟakkum. 	(13)
96.	nāviḷimpu vīṅki yaṇpaṉ mutaluṟa
	āvayi ṇaṇṇa moṟṟavum varuṭavum
	lakāra ḷakāra māyiraṇṭum piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nā viḷimpu vīṅki pal aṇ mutal uṟa - nāviṉatu viḷimpu taṭittup palliṉatu aṇiya iṭattaip porunta, a vayiṉ aṇṇam oṟṟa lakāramum varuṭa ḷakāramum a iraṇṭum piṟakkum - avviṭattu (mutalnā) av vaṇṇattai oṟṟa lakāramum ataṉait taṭava ḷakāramumāka av viraṇṭum piṟakkum.	 (14)
97.	itaḻiyaintu piṟakkum pakāra makāram.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) itaḻ iyaintu piṟakkum pakāram makāram - kīḻ itaḻum mēl itaḻum tammil iyaiyap piṟakkum pakāramum makāramum.	(15)
98.	pallita ḻiyaiya vakāram piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) pal itaḻ iyaiya vakāram piṟakkum - mēṟpallum kīḻ itaḻum tammil iyaiya vakāram piṟakkum.	(16)
99.	aṇṇañ cērnta miṭaṟṟeḻu vaḷiyicai
	kaṇṇuṟ ṟaṭaiya yakāram piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aṇṇam cērnta miṭaṟṟu eḻu vaḷiicai kaṇ uṟṟu aṭaiya yakāram piṟakkum - aṇṇattai nāc cērnta viṭattu miṭaṟṟiṉiṉṟum eḻum vaḷiyāṉāya icai av vaṇṇattai aṇaintu ceṟiya yakāram piṟakkum. 	(17)
100.	melleḻut tāṟum piṟappi ṉākkañ
	colliya paḷḷi nilaiyiṉa vāyiṉum
	mūkkiṉ vaḷiyicai yāppuṟat tōṉṟum.
itu, melleḻuttiṟku ōr puṟaṉaṭai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu āṟum piṟappiṉ ākkam colliya paḷḷi nilaiyiṉa āyiṉum - melleḻuttukaḷ āṟum tattam piṟappiṉatu ākkam mēṟcollappaṭṭa iṭattē nilai peṟṟaṉa vāyiṉum, mūkkiṉ vaḷi icai yāppuṟa tōṉṟum - avai mūkkiṉkaṇ ṇuḷatākiya vaḷiyatu icaiyāṉ yāppuṟat tōṉṟum.
‘yāppuṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṭaiyeḻuttiṟku miṭaṟṟu vaḷiyum, valleḻuttiṟkut talaivaḷiyum koḷka.	(18)
101.	cārntuvari ṉallatu tamakkiyal pilaveṉat
	tērntuveḷip paṭutta vēṉai mūṉṟun
	tattañ cārpiṟ piṟappoṭu civaṇi
	otta kāṭciyiṉ tammiyal piyalum.
itu, cārpiṉ tōṟṟattu eḻuttiṟkup piṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cārntuvariṉ allatu tamakku iyalpu ila eṉat tērntu veḷippaṭutta ēṉai mūṉṟum - cilavaṟṟaic cārntu variṉ allatu tamakkut tāmē varum iyalpu ilaveṉṟu ārāyntu veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭa oḻinta mūṉṟum, tamtam cārpiṉ piṟappoṭu civaṇi otta kāṭciyiṉ tam iyalpu iyalum - tattamakkuc cārpākiya eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟap piṭattē piṟattaloṭu porunti avviṭattē tamakkuriya iyalpiṉ naṭakkum.
‘otta kāṭci’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, āytattiṟkuk kuṟṟeḻuttuc cārpēyeṉiṉum talaivaḷiyāṟ piṟattaliṉ, uyiroṭu puṇarnta valleḻuttuc cārpākavē piṟakkumeṉpatu koḷka. ‘tammiyal piyalum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, aḷapeṭaiyum uyirmeyyum tamakku aṭiyākiya eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟappiṭamē iṭamāka varumeṉpatu koḷka.	 (19)
102.	ellā veḻuttum veḷippaṭak kiḷantu
	colliya paḷḷi yeḻutaru vaḷiyiṟ
	piṟappoṭu viṭuvaḻi yuṟaḻcci vārat
	takatteḻu vaḷiyicai yariṟapa nāṭi
	yaḷaviṟ kōṭa lantaṇar maṟaittē.
iḵtu, ellā eḻuttiṟkum āvatōr puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellā eḻuttum - ellā eḻuttukkaḷum, veḷippaṭa kiḷantu colliya paḷḷi - veḷippaṭa vitantu collappaṭṭa iṭattiṉ kaṇṇē, eḻutaru vaḷiyiṉ - eḻukiṉṟa vaḷiyāṉē, piṟappoṭu viṭuvaḻi - tām piṟakkun toḻiluṭaiya vātaloṭu tammaic collum iṭattu, uṟaḻcci vārattu akattu eḻuvaḷi icai - tiritaruṅ kūṟṟaiyuṭaiya uḷniṉṟu eḻum vaḷiyāṉāya icaiyai, aril tapa nāṭi - piṇakkamaṟa ārāyntu, aḷapiṉ kōṭal - māttirai varaiyaṟaiyāṟ kōṭal, antaṇar maṟaittu - pārppār vētattuk kaṇṇatu.
‘uṟaḻcci vāram’ eṉṟatu, untimutalā eḻum vaḷi talaikāṟum ceṉṟu mīṇṭum neñciṉkaṇ nilaipeṟutalai eṉak koḷka. ‘vaḷi’ eṉṉātu ‘vaḷiyicai’ eṉṟatu, avvāṟu neñciṉkaṇ nilaipeṟum aḷavē vaḷi eṉappaṭum; piṉṉai neñciṉiṉṟum eḻuvuḻiyellām vaḷittaṉmai tirintu eḻuttām taṉmaiyatām eṉpatu viḷakki niṉṟatu.(ēkāram - īṟṟacai.)	(20)
103.	aḵtiva ṇuvalā teḻuntupuṟat ticaikkum
	meyteri vaḷiyicai yaḷapunuvaṉ ṟiciṉē.
itu, mēṟcūttirattiṟku ōr puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aḵtu ivaṇ nuvalātu - (‘akatteḻu vaḷiyicai aḷapiṟ kōṭalākiya’) ataṉai in nūliṭattuc collātu, eḻuntu puṟattu icaikkum - (akattiṉiṉṟu) eḻuntu puṟattup pōntu icaikkum, mey teri vaḷi icai aḷapu - poruṇmai terikiṉṟa vaḷiyāṉāya icaiyatu māttiraiyiṉai, nuvaṉṟiciṉ - yāṉ īṇṭuk kūṟiṉēṉ.
maṟṟu, iḵtu “aḷapiṟ kōṭa lantaṇar maṟaittu” (piṟappiyal 20) eṉavē peṟappaṭṭataṉṟōveṉiṉ, ‘antaṇar maṟaittu’ eṉpatu piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟutaṟkum, piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟṟu nērntu uṭampaṭutaṟkum oppak kiṭantamaiyiṉ av vaiyam tīrtaṟkuk kūṟiṉāreṉpatu. puṟattu icaippataṉ muṉṉar, akattu icaikkum vaḷiyicaiyai am maṟaikkaṇ ōr eḻuttiṟku mūṉṟu nilai uḷatākak kūṟum (ataṉ āciriyaṉ); aḵtu āmāṟu aṟintu koḷka. ‘meyteri’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, muṟku mutaliyaṉa muyaṟciyāṟ piṟakkumeṉiṉum, poruḷ teriyā nilaimaiya vākaliṉ avaṟṟiṟku aḷapu kūṟārāyiṉāreṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu. ‘nuvaṉṟiciṉ’ eṉpatu īṇṭu iṟantakālat taṉmai viṉai.	  (21)
mūṉṟāvatu - piṟappiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.


puṇariyal
moḻikaḷ tammiṟ puṇartaṟkuriya karuviyiṉ iyalpiṉaik kūṟutaliṉ itu puṇariyal eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭum vitikaḷ piṉvarum iyalkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum ceykaipaṟṟiya vitikaḷukkup payaṉpaṭutaliṉ karuvikaḷeṉap paṭṭaṉa.
tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkiluḷḷa muppattu mūṉṟeḻuttukkaḷuḷ irupattiraṇṭeḻuttukkaḷ moḻikku mutalāmeṉavum, irupattu nāṉkeḻuttukkaḷ moḻikku īṟāmeṉavum, ellā moḻikaḷukkum iṟutiyum mutalumāvaṉa mey, uyir eṉṉum ivviruvakaiyeḻuttukkaḷeṉavum, moḻiyiṟutiyil niṉṟa meyyellām puḷḷipeṟṟu niṟkumeṉavum, moḻiyiṟutiyi luḷḷa kuṟṟiyalukaramum meyyiṉ taṉmaiyaiyuṭaiyatā meṉavum, moḻiyīṟāy niṉṟa uyirmey uyirīṟṟiṉ taṉmaiyaiyuṭaiyatā meṉavum ivviyalil 1-mutal 4-varaiyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
nilaimoḻiyai niṟuttacol eṉṟum varumoḻiyaik kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyeṉṟum tolkāppiyaṉār vaḻaṅkuvar. niṟutta colliṉ īṟṟeḻuttōṭu kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyiṉ mutalaleḻuttup porunta avvirumoḻikaḷum iyaintu varutalē puṇarcciyeṉappaṭum. appuṇarcci uyirīṟṟuc col muṉ uyir varumiṭam, uyirīṟṟuc colmuṉ meyvarumiṭam, meyyīṟṟuc colmuṉ uyir varumiṭam, meyyīṟṟuc colmuṉ mey varumiṭam eṉa eḻuttuvakaiyāl nāṉku vakaippaṭum; peyaroṭu peyar, peyaroṭu toḻil, toḻiloṭu peyar, toḻiloṭu toḻil eṉac col vakaiyāl nāṉkākum. moḻikaḷ puṇaruṅkāl iṭainiṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ oṉṟu maṟṟeṉṟākat tiritalai meypiṟitātal eṉṟum, avviṭattup putiyaveḻuttut toṉṟutalai mikutal eṉṟum, aṅku muṉṉirunta eḻuttuk keṭutalaik kuṉṟal eṉṟum, ivvēṟu pāṭetuvumiṉṟi avvirumoḻikaḷum muṉṉuḷḷavāṟu puṇarntu niṟṟalai iyalpeṉṟum, innāṉkiṉaiyum moḻipuṇariyalpu eṉṟum āciriyar kuṟiyiṭṭu vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār. niṟuttacollum kuṟittu varukiḷaviyum taṉittaṉiyē aṭaimoḻi peṟṟuvariṉum nilaimoḻi varumoḻiyākap puṇartaṟku uriyaṉavām. muṉ piṉṉāka māṟi niṉṟa marūu moḻikaḷum nilaimoḻi varumoḻiyākap puṇarkkappaṭutaluṇṭu.
moḻippuṇarcci vēṟṟumaip puṇarcciyeṉavum alvaḻip puṇarcciyeṉavum poruḷ vakaiyāl irutiṟappaṭum ivaṟṟuḷ vēṟṟumaip puṇarcciyai vēṟṟumai kuṟitta puṇarmoḻi nilaiyeṉṟum alvaḻip puṇarcciyai vēṟṟumaiyalvaḻip puṇarmoḻi nilaiyeṉṟu āciriyar vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār. ivviruvakaip puṇarmoḻikaḷum eḻuttu mikutalum cāriyai mikutalum ākiya irutiṟattālum naṭappaṉa. vēṟṟumai yurupāvaṉa ai, oṭu, ku, iṉ, atu, kaṇ eṉṉum āṟām eṉat tolkāppiyaṉarkku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻiya ṉūlār vakaippaṭuttiyuḷḷārkaḷ. vēṟṟumaiyurupukaḷ puṇarum nilaimaikkaṇ peyariṉ piṉṉiṭattē niṟṟaṟkuriyaṉa. urupēṟkum peyarkaḷ uyartiṇaip peyar, aḵṟiṇaip peyar eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. avai vēṟṟumaiyurupōṭu poruntuṅkāl peyarkkum vēṟṟumaiyurupiṟkumiṭaiyē cāriyai mikum. cāriyaikaḷāvaṉa iṉ, vaṟṟu, attu, am, oṉ, āṉ, akku, ikku, aṉ eṉavarum ivvoṉpatum ivaipōlvaṉa piṟavum ām. iṉ cāriyai ikaraṅkeṭṭu ṉakaramey maṭṭum niṟṟalum, ṉakaraṅ keṭṭu ikaravuyir maṭṭum niṟṟalum, ṉakaram ṟakaramākat tirintu niṟṟalum ākiya mūṉṟu tiripuṭaiyateṉṟum, vaṟṟuc cāriyai mutaṟkaṇuḷḷa vakaramey keṭa aṟṟu eṉattiriyumeṉṟum, iṉ, oṉ, āṉ, aṉ eṉṉum iccāriyaikaḷiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramāyt tiritaluṇṭeṉṟum, akaravīṟṟuc colmuṉ varum attuccāriyai akaraṅkeṭa ‘ttu’ eṉa niṟkumeṉṟum, ikara aikāra vīṟṟuc colmuṉvarum ikkuc cāriyai ikaraṅkeṭa ‘kku’ eṉa niṟkumeṉṟum, valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl akkuc cāriyai iṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaramum ataṉulūrappaṭṭa kakarameyyum akkakara meyyiṉmēl niṉṟa kakarameyyum orucērakkeṭa ‘a’ eṉat tirintu niṟkumeṉṟum, kacata varu moḻiyāy varuṅkālattu am cāriyaiyiṉ makaram muṟaiyē ṅa ña na eṉat tiritalum, melleḻuttum iṭaiyeḻuttum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattuk keṭutalumākiya nilaimait teṉṟum, coṟkaḷ peyarun toḻilumāyp pirintum peyarum peyarumāykkūṭiyum icaippa vēṟṟumaiyurupu virintu niṟku miṭattum maṟaintu niṟkumiṭattum moḻikaḷaip pirittuk kāṇuṅkāl peyarkkum vēṟṟumaiyurupiṟku miṭaiyē vantu niṟṟal cāriyaiyiṉ iyalpeṉṟum cāriyaiyiṉ ilakkaṇat tiṉai āciriyar teḷivāka varaiyaṟuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷar.
coṟkaḷai nilaimoḻi varumoḻiyākap pirittuk kāṇum muṟaiyiṉait ‘coṟcitar maruṅku’ eṉavum peyar cāriyai urupu mutaliya colluṟuppukkaḷ oṉṟaṉpiṉṉoṉṟāka oṭṭi naṭattaṟkuriya moḻivaḻakkiṉai ‘oṭṭutaṟkoḻukiya vaḻakku’ eṉavum āciriyar kuṟiyiṭṭu vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār. niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittu varukiḷaviyumākap pirittaṟkēṟṟavāṟu oṭṭi nillātu, nilākkatir nilāmuṟṟam eṉṟāṟ pōla uṭaṅkiyaintu niṟkum puṇarmoḻikaḷ oṭṭutaṟkoḻukiya vaḻakkiṉa allaveṉavuṅ ākavē avai cāriyai peṟā veṉavum iḷampūraṇar kūṟiya viḷakkam ivaṇ cintittuṇarat takuvatām.
kāram, karam, kāṉ eṉpaṉa eḻuttiṉ cāriyaikaḷām. avaṟṟuḷ karam, kāṉ eṉṉum iru cāriyaikaḷaiyum neṭṭeḻuttup peṟutalillai. kuṟṟeḻuttu mēṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu cāriyaikaḷaiyum peṟum. neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷil ai, oḷa eṉṉumiraṇṭum kāṉ cāriyaiyum peṟum. eṉa ivviyalil iyaipuṭaimai karuti eḻuttuk cāriyaikaḷum uṭaṉ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
meyyīṟṟiṉmuṉ uyirmutaṉ moḻi varuṅkāl varumoḻi mutaliluḷḷa uyir taṉittu nillātu; meykaḷukkuriya puḷḷi peṟutalākiya avviyalpiṉaik keṭuttu nilaimoḻi yīṟṟiluḷḷa ammeyyuṭaṉ kūṭi niṟkum. iṅṅaṉam kūṭiya uyir pirintu nīṅkiyavaḻi nilaimoḻiyīṟṟiluḷḷa mey mīṇṭum taṉ paḻaiya vaṭivākiya puḷḷiyaip peṟum. ivviyalpiṉai ivviyaliṉ 36, 37-ām cūttiraṅkaḷāl  āciriyar kuṟippiṭuvar. kuṟṟiyalukaramum meyyīṟu pōlun taṉmaiyattu eṉa ivviyaliṉ mūṉṟām cūttirattāl āciriyar māṭṭēṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷār. immāṭṭēṟu puḷḷi peṟutalum uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭuttalumākiya meyyiṉ taṉmaikaḷuḷ puḷḷi peṟutalai vilakki uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭuttalākiya avvaḷavukkuc cellutaliṉ “immāṭṭēṟu orupuṭaiccēṟa leṉavuṇarka” eṉa uraiyāciriyar viḷakkaṅ kūṟuvar. ‘kuṟṟiyalukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa’ eṉa muṉṉar māṭṭēṟu kūṟiṉamaiyāl meyyīṟṟiṉmuṉ uyir taṉittu nillātavāṟu pōṉṟu kuṟṟiyalukaravīṟṟiṉ muṉṉum uyir taṉittu nillātu akkuṟṟiyalukarattōṭu porunti niṟkum eṉat tol kāppiyaṉār uyttuṇara vaittamai peṟappaṭum.
uyirīṟṟiṉmuṉ uyirmutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu uyirōṭu uyirkkuk kalantu niṟkum iyalpiṉmaiyāl iru moḻikaḷum oṭṭi nillātu viṭṭicaippaṉavām. nilaimoḻiyīṟṟilum varu moḻi mutalilum niṟkum avviraṇṭuyirkaḷum uṭampaṭutaṟ poruṭṭu avaṟṟiṭaiyē yakara vakaraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu uṭampaṭu meyyāy varum. uṭampaṭāta iraṇṭuyirkaḷum uṭampaṭutaṟ poruṭṭu iṭaiyē tōṉṟum mey uṭampaṭumey eṉa vaḻaṅ kappaṭum. uṭaṉpāṭu, uṭampāṭu eṉa maruvi vaḻaṅkiyatu varumuyirkku uṭampāka aṭukkum mey uṭampaṭumey eṉapporuḷ kōṭalumuṇṭu. ellā moḻikkum iṟutiyum mutalum uyir varumvaḻi avaṟṟiṭaiyē uṭampaṭumey peṟutalai vilakkār eṉat tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟutalāl avar kālattu uyirīṟṟiṉ muṉ uyirmutaṉmoḻi vantu puṇaruṅkāl uṭampaṭumey peṟṟē varavēṇṭumeṉṉum varaiyaṟaiyillai yeṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākum. uṭampaṭumeyyāka varutaṟkuriya eḻuttukkaḷ ivaiyeṉat tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟātu pōyiṉum avariyaṟṟiya iyaṟṟamiḻ nūlākiya it tolkāppiyattilum itaṟkuppiṉ tōṉṟiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷellāvaṟṟilum yakara vakaraṅkaḷē uṭampaṭumeyyāka āḷappeṟṟuḷḷamai kāṇalām. “uṭampaṭu meyyē yakāra vakāram, uyir mutaṉ moḻi varūuṅkālaiyāṉa” eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kāṭṭiya paḻañcūttiram yakara vakaraṅkaḷē uṭampaṭumeyyāy varutaṟkuriyaṉa eṉpatait teḷivāka viḷakkutal kāṇka. uyirkaḷuḷ ikara īkāra aikāra vīṟukaḷ yakara vuṭampaṭumey koḷḷum eṉavum, ēkāravīṟu yakara vakaraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟai uṭampaṭumeyyākap peṟṟuvarum eṉavum, ēṉaiya vuyirīṟukaḷ yāvum vakara vuṭampaṭu meyyē peṟum eṉavum iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum vakaipeṟa viḷakkiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
eḻuttāl oṉṟupōlat tōṉṟic collāl vēṟu paṭṭup poruḷ viḷaṅki niṟkum puṇarmoḻikaḷ ōcai vēṟu pāṭṭaṟ pirintu puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutal colnaṭaiyiṉ nilai peṟṟa paṇpākum. ivvāṟu varum puṇarmoḻikaḷ kuṟippiṉāluṇarum poruḷaiyuṭaiyaṉa. eḻuttu vakaiyāl ittaṉmaiya eṉat teḷivāka vuṇarum muṟaimaiyai yuṭaiyaṉa alla. eṭuttukkāṭṭākac ‘cempoṉ patiṉṟoṭi’ eṉṉum puṇar moḻi poṉṉaippaṟṟip pēcciṉkaṇ eṭuttāḷap paṭṭāl cempoṉ + patiṉṟoṭi eṉap pirintum, cempaik kuṟittu nikaḻum pēccil eṭuttāḷappaṭṭāl cempu + oṉpatiṉṟoṭi eṉap pirintum ōcai vēṟupaṭṭu vēṟuvēṟu poruḷuṇartti niṟṟal kāṇalām. ivaṟṟiṉ iyalpiṉai ivviyaliṉ īṟṟiluḷḷa iraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷālum āciriyar aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟār. eṉavē avar kālattu iṅṅaṉam nuṇṇitākap poruḷuṇarttum puṇarmoḻikaḷ peruka vaḻaṅkiṉamai naṉku pulaṉākum.
- ka. veḷḷaivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvaricai 10, pak. 170-174


nāṉkāvatu
puṇariyal
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttō veṉiṉ, moḻikaḷ, (mēṟ ceykai yōttukkaḷuḷ) puṇartaṟkuriya karuviyiṉ iyalpu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ puṇariyal eṉappaṭṭatu. mēl moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiya moḻikaḷ puṇarumāṟu uṇarttiṉa maiyiṉ moḻimarapiṉōṭu iyaipuṭait tāyiṟṟu.
104.	mūṉṟutalai yiṭṭa muppatiṟ ṟeḻuttiṉ
	iraṇṭutalai yiṭṭa mutalā kirupaḵ
	taṟunāṉ kīṟoṭu neṟiniṉ ṟiyalum
	ellā moḻikku miṟutiyu mutalum
	meyyē yuyireṉ ṟāyī riyala.
it talaic cūttiram eṉnutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ, moḻimarapiṉuḷ mutalum īṟum kūṟiyavaḻi uyirum meyyum uyir meyyum eṉa mūṉṟāy virintu niṉṟataṉai uyirum meyyum eṉa iraṇṭākat tokuttalāṉum, avvaḻi irupattiraṇṭu eḻuttu moḻikku mutal, irupattu nāṉku eḻuttu moḻikku īṟu eṉak kūṟaliṉ, muppattu mūṉṟu eṉappaṭṭa eḻuttu nāṟpattāṟu āvaṉapōl virintataṉai avaiyellām muppattu mūṉṟaṉuḷḷaṉavē eṉat tokuttalāṉum virintatu tokuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟu talaiyiṭṭa muppatiṟṟu eḻuttiṉ - mūṉṟaṉai muṭivilē yiṭappaṭṭa muppatākiya eḻuttiṉuḷ, iraṇṭu talaiyiṭṭa mutal āku irupaḵtu - av viraṇṭaṉai muṭivilē yiṭappaṭṭa moḻikku mutalāya irupatum, aṟunāṉku īṟoṭu neṟi niṉṟu iyalum - irupattu nāṉku īṟṟōṭu vaḻakku neṟikkaṇ niṉṟu naṭakkum, ellā moḻikkum iṟutiyum mutalum - mūvakai moḻikkum īṟum mutalumāvaṉa, mey uyir eṉṟu a īr iyala - meyyum uyirumākiya av viraṇṭu iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiya.
u-m: maram, ilai, āl, viḷa eṉa meyyum uyirum mutalum īṟum āyiṉa.
irupattiraṇṭu eḻuttu mutalāvaṉa  paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum oṉpatu uyirmeyyum moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaramum eṉa ivai. irupattu nāṉku eḻuttu īṟāvaṉa paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum patiṉoru puḷḷiyum īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum eṉa ivai. ‘īṟṟoṭu’ eṉpatu vikārattāṉ tokkatu. mey mutaṟ kūṟiyavataṉāṉ, nālvakaip puṇarcciyum meykkaṇa nikaḻumāṟu uyirkkaṇ nikaḻāveṉpatu koḷka. (`eṉṟu’ eṉpatu eṇṇiṭai c col.) 	 (1)
105.	avaṟṟuḷ
	meyyī ṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal.
itu, mēṟkūṟiyavāṟṟāṉ taṉimeyyum mutalāvāṉ ceṉṟataṉai vilakkaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēl meyyum uyirum eṉṟu kūṟiya iraṇṭaṉuḷ, īṟu meyyellām puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal - moḻikku īṟāya meyyellām puḷḷi peṟutaloṭu niṟkam; mutalāyavai yellām puḷḷiyiḻantu niṟkum.
u-m:  maram eṉa varum.
‘moḻimutaṉ mey puḷḷiyoṭu nillātu’ eṉṉātu, ‘īṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal’ eṉa īṟṟiṉmēl vaittuk kūṟiyavataṉāṉ, av vīṟṟiṉ mey uyir mutaṉmoḻi vanta iṭattu aḵtu ēṟa iṭamkoṭukkumeṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu. (2)
106.	kuṟṟiya lukaramu maṟṟeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, īṟṟiṟ kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟku ōr karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalukaramum aṟṟu eṉa moḻipa - īṟṟiṟ kuṟṟiya lukaramum (puḷḷi yīṟupōla uyirēṟa iṭam koṭukkum) at taṉmaittu eṉṟu colluvar.
immāṭṭēṟu orupuṭaic cēṟal eṉa vuṇarka.	(3)
107.	uyirmey yīṟu muyirīṟ ṟiyaṟṟē.
itu, mēl “meyyē yuyireṉ ṟāyī riyala” (puṇariyal - 1) eṉṟataṟku ōr puṟaṉaṭai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyirmey īṟum uyir īṟṟu iyaṟṟu - uyirmey moḻiyīṟṟil niṉṟatuvum uyirīṟṟiṉ iyalpaiyuṭaittu, iṭaiyil niṉṟatuvum uyiriṉ iyalpai yuṭaittu e-ṟu.
īṟum iṭaiyum uyiruḷ aṭaṅkumeṉavē, mutal meyyuḷ aṭaṅkum eṉpatāyiṟṟu.
itaṉāṉ, viḷa mutaliya uyirmey īṟellām akaravīṟu mutaliya uyirīṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkip puṇarccipeṟuvaṉa vāyiṉa. varaku eṉpuḻi iṭai niṉṟa rakara uyirmey akaramāy uyirt toṭarmoḻi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
īṇṭu uyirmey oṟṟumai nayattāṉ uyirmeyyeṉa vēṟu ōr eḻuttāvataṉṟi, īṟum iṭaiyum uyireṉa ōreḻut tāyum, mutal meyyeṉa ōreḻuttāyum niṉṟatāyiṟṟu.
ittuṇaiyum orumoḻi yilakkaṇaṅ kūṟaliṉ moḻi marapiṉ oḻipāyiṟṟu.	(4)
108.	uyiriṟu coṉmu ṉuyirvaru vaḻiyum
	uyiriṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyum
	meyyiṟu coṉmu ṉuyirvaru vaḻiyum
	meyyiṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyumeṉ
	ṟivveṉa vaṟiyak kiḷakkuṅ kālai
	niṟutta collē kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyeṉ
	ṟāyī riyala puṇarnilaic cuṭṭē.
itu, mēṟkūṟum puṇarcci mummoḻip puṇarcciyākātu, irumoḻip puṇarcciyāmeṉpatūum, avai eḻuttu vakaiyāṉ nāṉkāmeṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir iṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum - uyirīṟṟuc colmuṉ uyirmutalmoḻi varum iṭamum, uyir iṟu colmuṉ mey varu vaḻiyum - uyirīṟṟuc colmuṉ meymmutal moḻi varum iṭamum, mey iṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum - meyyīṟṟuc colmuṉ uyirmutal moḻi varum iṭamum, mey iṟu colmuṉ mey varu vaḻiyum - meyyīṟṟuc colmuṉ meymmutal moḻi varum iṭamum, iv eṉa aṟiya - (eṉṟu ap puṇarccivakai) ivaiyeṉa aṟiya, kiḷakkum kālai - āciriyar colluṅkālattu, niṟutta col kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi eṉṟu a īr iyala - avai niṟutta collum ataṉ poruṇmaiyaik kuṟittuvarum collu mākiya av viraṇṭu iyalpaiyuṭaiya, puṇarnilaic cuṭṭu - puṇarum nilaimaikkaṇ.
u-m: ā ī, ā valitu, āl ilai, āl vīḻntatu eṉak kaṇṭu koḷka.
viḷaviṉaik kuṟaittāṉ eṉpatu av vurupu kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyai nilaimoḻiyuḷ aṭakki irumoḻip puṇarcciyāy niṉṟavāṟu aṟika. (avvurupu - `viḷaviṉai’ eṉpataṉ ai urupu. mutal ēkāram pirinilai. iraṇṭām ēkāram - īṟṟacai. `eṉṟu’ iraṇṭum - eṇṇiṭaiccol.)	(5)
109.	avaṟṟuḷ
	niṟutta colli ṉīṟā keḻuttoṭu
	kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi mutaleḻut tiyaiyap
	peyaroṭu peyaraip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
	peyaroṭu toḻilaip puṇarkkuṅ kālun
	toḻiloṭu peyaraip puṇarkkuṅ kālun
	toḻiloṭu toḻilaip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
	mūṉṟē tiripiṭa ṉoṉṟē yiyalpeṉa
	āṅkan nāṉkē moḻipuṇa riyalpē.
itu, mēṟkūṟum puṇarcci puṇarccivakaiyāṉ nāṉkā meṉpatūum, avai colvakaiyāṉ nāṉkā meṉpatūum, puṇarvatu collum collumēyaṉṟi, eḻuttum eḻuttum eṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - nilaimoḻi varumoḻi yeṉappaṭṭa vaṟṟuḷ, niṟutta colliṉ īṟu āku eḻuttoṭu kuṟittu varu kiḷavi mutal eḻuttu iyaiya - niṟutta colliṉatu īṟākiṉṟa eḻuttiṉōṭu ataṉaik kuṟittu varukiṉṟa colliṉatu mutaleḻuttup porunta, peyaroṭu peyaraip puṇarkkum kālum - peyarccolloṭu peyarc collaip puṇarkkum kālattum, peyaroṭu toḻilai puṇarkkum kālum - peyarccolloṭu viṉaiccollaip puṇarkkum kālattum, toḻiloṭu peyaraip puṇarkkum kālum - viṉaic colloṭu peyarccollaip puṇarkkum kālattum, toḻiloṭu toḻilaip puṇarkkum kālum - viṉaiccolloṭu viṉaiccollaip puṇarkkum kālattum, tiripu mūṉṟu iyalpu oṉṟu eṉa a nāṉkē moḻipuṇar iyalpu - tiriyum iṭam mūṉṟum iyalpu oṉṟum ākiya an nāṉkē moḻikaḷ tammiṟ puṇarum iyalpu e-ṟu.
u-m: cāttaṉ kai, cāttaṉ uṇṭāṉ, vantāṉ cāttaṉ, vantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka.
iṭaiyum uriyum peyar viṉaikaḷai aṭaintallatu tāmāka nillāmaiyiṉ, peyar viṉaikaṭkē puṇarcci kūṟappaṭṭatu.	
(iṭai - iṭaiccol. uri -uriccol. `āṅku’ eṉpatu acai. mutal mūṉṟu ēkāramum tēṟṟapporuḷil vantaṉa. nāṉkām ēkāram - īṟṟacai.)	(6)
110.	avaitām
	meypiṟi tātaṉ mikutal kuṉṟaleṉ
	ṟivveṉa moḻipa tiriyu māṟē.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya tiripu mūṉṟum āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaitām - at tiriputāṉ, meypiṟitu ātal mikutal kuṉṟal eṉṟu iv eṉa moḻipa tiriyum āṟu - mey piṟitātalum mikutalum kuṉṟalumākiya ivai eṉac colluvar tiriyum neṟiyiṉai. im mūṉṟum allātatu iyalpeṉṟu koḷka.
u-m: poṟkuṭam, yāṉaikkōṭu, maravēr eṉavarum. 
kuvaḷai malar eṉpatu iyalpu.
ip puṇarcci nāṉkum oru puṇarccikkaṇṇē nikaḻvu peṟumeṉpatu uraiyiṟkoḷka. mēl ‘iṭam’ (puṇar. 6) eṉṟataṉāṉ, tiripu mūṉṟaṉuḷ, yātāyiṉum oṉṟāyatu, oru puṇarccikku iraṇṭum mūṉṟum oruṅku varappeṟum eṉakkoḷka.
‘makattāṟ koṇṭāṉ’ eṉpatu am mikuti iraṇṭu vantatu; ‘nīyir kuṟiyir’ eṉpatu am mikuti mūṉṟu vantatu. piṟavum aṉṉa. (mutal utāraṇattil `attiripu mūṉṟu vantatu’ eṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. iraṇṭām utāraṇam piḻai. ippiḻaikaḷ ēṭupeyartteḻutiṉōrāl nērntaṉa pōlum. ‘eṉṟu’  eṇṇiṭaiccol.)	(7)
111.	niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyum
	aṭaiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilaik kuriya.
itu, “niṟutta collē kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi” (puṇar. 5) eṉpataṟku ōr puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) niṟutta collum kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyum - nilai moḻiyāka niṟuttiṉa collum ataṉaik kuṟittu varucollum, aṭaiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilaikku uriya- (tāmē vantu puṇarvataṉṟi), avai yiraṇṭiṉum ōrōr collaṭaivantu oṉṟit tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilaikku uriya.
u-m: patiṉāyirattoṉṟu, āyirattorupaḵtu, patiṉāyirat torupaḵtu eṉa varum.
īṇṭu aṭaiyeṉṟatu ummaittokaiyiṉaiyum, iru peyaroṭṭup paṇputtokaiyiṉaiyum eṉa vuṇarka. avaiyallāta tokaikaḷuḷ viṉaittokaiyum, paṇput tokaiyum piḷantu muṭiyāmaiyiṉ, orucol eṉappaṭum. aṉmoḻit tokaiyum taṉakku vēṟu oru muṭipu iṉmaiyiṉ orucol eṉappaṭum. iṉi oḻinta vēṟṟumait tokaiyum uvamait tokaiyum ‘taṉṉiṉa muṭittal’ eṉṟataṉāṉ īṇṭu orucol eṉappaṭum.
uṇṭacāttaṉ vantāṉ, uṇṭuvantāṉ cāttaṉ eṉpaṉavum avvāṟē orucol eṉappaṭum. piṟavum aṉṉa.  (8)
112.	maruviṉ ṟokuti mayaṅkiyaṉ moḻiyum
	uriyavai yuḷavē puṇarnilaic cuṭṭē.
iḵtu, ilakkaṇa vaḻakkēyaṉṟi marūukkaḷum puṇarkkappaṭum eṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) maruviṉ tokuti mayaṅku iyal moḻiyum - (ilakkaṇa vaḻakkēyaṉṟi) marūuttiraḷākiya talaitaṭumāṟāka mayaṅkiṉa iyalpaiyuṭaiya ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiṉa marūu vaḻakkum, uriyavai uḷa puṇarnilaic cuṭṭu - uriyaṉa uḷa puṇarum nilaimaikkaṇ.
u-m: mīkaṇ, muṉṟil eṉa varum.
‘nilai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiya marūu vaḻakkallā marūu vaḻakkum puṇarkkappaṭum eṉakkoḷka.	(9)
113.	vēṟṟumai kuṟitta puṇarmoḻi nilaiyum
	vēṟṟumai yalvaḻip puṇarmoḻi nilaiyum
	eḻuttē cāriyai yāyiru paṇpiṉ
	oḻukkal valiya puṇaruṅ kālai.
itu, nālvakaip puṇarcciyuḷ mikka puṇarcci ittaṉmait teṉpatūum, an nālvakaip puṇarcciyum vēṟu ōr āṟṟāṉ iruvakai yāmeṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumai kuṟitta puṇarmoḻi nilaiyum - vēṟ ṟumaiyatu poruṇmaiyiṉaik kuṟitta puṇarmoḻiyatu nilaimaiyum, vēṟṟumai alvaḻi puṇarmoḻi nilaiyum - vēṟṟumaiyallāta alvaḻiyiṭattup puṇarmoḻiyatu nilaimaiyum, eḻuttu cāriyai a iru paṇpiṉ oḻukkal valiya - (mikka puṇarccikkaṇ) eḻuttu mikutalum cāriyai mikutalu mākiya av viraṇṭu ilakkaṇattāṉum naṭāttutalait tamakku valiyāka uṭaiya, puṇarumkālai - avai puṇaruṅkālattu.
u-m: viḷaṅkōṭu, makaviṉkai, viḷakkuṟitu, paṉaiyiṉ kuṟai eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka.
‘oḻukkal valiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, eḻuttum cāriyaiyum uṭaṉ peṟutal koḷka.
avaṟṟukkōṭu, kalattukkuṟai eṉavarum.
alvaḻi muṟkūṟātatu, vēṟṟumaiyallātatu alvaḻiyeṉa vēṇṭaliṉ eṉpatu. muṉṉē ‘puṇarmoḻi’ eṉṟu vaittu, ‘puṇaruṅ kālai’ eṉṟataṉāṟ puṇarccikkaṇṇē vēṟṟumai alvaḻi eṉa iraṇṭāvatu allākkāl, vēṟṟumaiyeṉavē paṭumeṉpatu koḷka. īṇṭu ‘alvaḻi’ yeṉṟatu perumpāṉmaiyum eḻuvāyiṉai.	(10)
114.	aioṭu kuiṉ atukaṇ ṇeṉṉum
	avvā ṟeṉpa vēṟṟumai yurupē.
itu, mēl vēṟṟumai yeṉṉappaṭṭa avaṟṟatu peyarum, muṟaiyum, tokaiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ eṉṉum - ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ eṉṟu collappaṭum. a āṟu eṉpa vēṟṟumai urupu - av vāṟum eṉṟu colluvar vēṟṟumaiyurupukaḷai e-ṟu.
iv āṟum allaṉa vellām alvaḻi eṉappaṭum. avai eḻuvāyum, viḷiyum, uvamattokaiyum, ummait tokaiyum, paṇput tokaiyum, irupeyaroṭṭup paṇputtokaiyum, muṟṟum, iru vakai eccamum, iṭaiyum, uriyum eṉa ivai.	(11)
115.	valleḻuttu mutaliya vēṟṟumai yurupiṟ
	kolvaḻi yoṟṟiṭai mikutal vēṇṭum.
itu, nāṉkāvataṟkum ēḻāvataṟkum urupiyalai nōkkiṟṟōr karuvi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) valleḻuttu mutaliya vēṟṟumai urupiṟku - valleḻuttu mutalākavuṭaiya vēṟṟumai yurupiṟku, olvaḻi oṟṟu iṭai mikutal vēṇṭum - poruntiṉa iṭattu valloṟṟāyiṉum melloṟṟāyiṉum iṭaikkaṇ mikutal vēṇṭum.
u-m: ūrkku, nīrkku, ūrkkaṇ, nīrkkaṇ eṉa valleḻuttu mikkaṉa. taṅkaṇ, eṅkaṇ eṉa melleḻuttu mikkaṉa.
‘olvaḻi’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, aracarkaṇ, pārppārkaṇ eṉa oṟṟu mikātaṉavum koḷka. ‘meypiṟi tātal’ (puṇar. 7) mutalāya nāṉku puṇarcciyum urupu puṇarccikkaṇṇum eytaliṉ, meypiṟitātalai eṭuttōtātu mikkataṉai eṭuttōtiya vataṉāṉ, mikka puṇarcci yallaṉavum īṇṭē koḷka. poṟku, poṟkaṇ, āṅkaṇ, īṅkaṇ, ūṅkaṇ, avaṟku, ivaṟku; nuṅkaṇ, koṟṟaṟku, cāttaṟku eṉa varum.
avaṉkaṇ, cāttaṉkaṇ eṉpuḻi iyalpum itaṉāṉē koḷka. kuṉṟiya puṇarcci vantavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka. 	(12)
116.	āṟa ṉurupi ṉakarak kiḷavi
	īṟā kakaramuṉaik keṭutal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu, āṟāvataṟkut tokaimarapiṉai nōkkiyatoru karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṟaṉ urupiṉ akarak kiḷavi - āṟām vēṟṟumaiyākiya atu eṉṉum colliṭattu akaramākiya eḻuttu, īṟu āku akaramuṉai keṭutal vēṇṭum - (neṭumutal kuṟukum) moḻiyīṟṟu uḷatākiṉṟa akarattiṉ muṉṉart tāṉ keṭutal vēṇṭum.
u-m: namatu, ematu, taṉatu, eṉatu, niṉatu eṉa varum.
mēl īṟāku akaram itaṟkut tārātu, ivvuru pakaramē ēṟi muṭiya amaitaliṉ, atu kēṭātal vēṇṭā eṉiṉ, neṭumutal kuṟuki vikārappaṭṭu niṉṟa moḻiyākaliṉ ataṉmēl urupakara mēṟi muṭiyal vēṇṭārāyiṉār pōlu meṉpatu. 	(13)


117.	vēṟṟumai vaḻiya peyarpuṇar nilaiyē.
itu, vēṟṟumaiyurupu peyarkkaṇ niṟkumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumai peyar vaḻiya puṇarnilai - vēṟṟumai peyarkaḷiṉ piṉṉiṭattaṉa avaṟṟoṭu puṇarum nilaimaik kaṇ.
u-m: cāttaṉai, cāttaṉoṭu eṉa varum.
mēl “urupunilai tiriyā tīṟupeyark kākum” (vēṟ. 8) eṉṟāraṉṟō veṉiṉ, peyaroṭu peyar mutaliya nālvakaip puṇarcciyaiyum vēṟṟumai alvaḻiyeṉa iraṇṭāka aṭakkaliṉ, viṉaivaḻiyum urupu varumeṉpatupaṭa niṉṟa tākaliṉ īṇṭu itu kūṟappaṭṭatu. (vēṟṟumai - vēṟṟumai yurupukaḷ. ēkāram - īṟṟacai.)	 (14)
118.	uyartiṇaip peyarē yaḵṟiṇaip peyareṉ
	ṟāyiraṇ ṭeṉpa peyarnilaic cuṭṭē.
itu, vēṟṟumaiyoṭu puṇarum peyarkaṭkup peyarum, muṟaiyum, tokaiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyartiṇaip peyarē aḵṟiṇaip peyar eṉṟu a iraṇṭu eṉpa - uyartiṇaip peyarum aḵṟiṇaip peyarumākiya av viraṇṭumeṉṟu collupa, peyar nilaic cuṭṭu - peyarākiya nilaimaiyatu karuttiṉai.
u-m: āṭūu, makaṭūu eṉpaṉa uyartiṇaip peyar. oṉṟu pala eṉpaṉa aḵṟiṇaip peyar.
maṟṟu viravuppeyar kūṟārāyatu eṉṉai yeṉiṉ,  maṟṟatu cāttaṉ vantāṉ; vantatu eṉap puṇarccikkaṇ perumpāṉmai yum oru tiṇaip pāṟpaṭutaliṉ, av viraṇṭāka aṭakkik kūṟiṉā reṉpatu.
(ē, eṉṟu eṉpaṉa - eṇṇiṭaiccol iraṇṭām. ēkāram - īṟṟacai.)	(15)

119.	avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṟ cāriyai varumē.
itu, cāriyai varum iṭam kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṉ cāriyai varum - ap peyarkaḷiṉ piṉṉākiya iṭattiṉkaṇṇē cāriyai varum.
u-m: āṭūuviṉ kai, makaṭūuviṉ kai, palavaṟṟuk kōṭu eṉa varum. (`avaṟṟuḷ vaḻi’ eṉavum pāṭam.)	(16)
120.	avaitām
	iṉṉē vaṟṟē yattē yammē
	oṉṉē yāṉē yakkē yikkē
	aṉṉeṉ kiḷavi yuḷappaṭap piṟavum
	aṉṉa veṉpa cāriyai moḻiyē.
itu, cāriyaikaṭkup peyarum muṟaiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaitām - mēṟcāriyai yeṉṉappaṭṭavai tām, iṉṉē vaṟṟē attē ammē oṉṉē āṉē akkē ikkē aṉ eṉ kiḷavi uḷappaṭa piṟavum - iṉṉum vaṟṟum attum ammum oṉṉum āṉum akkum ikkum aṉ eṉṉum collumākiya av voṉpatum uḷappaṭap piṟavum, aṉṉa eṉpa cāriyai moḻi - ac cāriyaiyām taṉmaiya eṉṟu colluvar cāriyaiyākiya moḻikaḷai.
‘piṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, tam, nam, num, um, keḻu, ē, ai, ñāṉṟu eṉpaṉavuṅ koḷka. ‘eṭutta naṟaviṉ kulaiyalaṅ kāntaḷ’ eṉpuḻi alam eṉpatoru cāriyaiyum uṇṭāleṉiṉ, ataṉai alaṅku kāntaḷ eṉpataṉ vikāra meṉpa. iṉcāriyai vaḻakkup payiṟciyāṉum nūluṭ palakālum eṭut tōtappaṭalāṉum, vāḷā ōtiyavaḻit tāṉē cēṟalāṉum muṉvaikkappaṭṭatu. aṉ cāriyaiyum atupōlac ciṟappuṭai maiyiṉ piṉ vaikkappaṭṭatu. iṭai niṉṟavaṟṟiyalpum aṟintu koḷka. (mutal eṭṭu ēkāramum - eṇṇiṭaiccol. oṉpatām ēkāram - īṟṟacai.)	 (17)
121.	avaṟṟuḷ
	iṉṉi ṉikara māvi ṉiṟuti
	muṉṉark keṭutal urittu mākum.
iḵtu, avaṟṟuḷ iṉcāriyai mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa cāriyaikaḷuḷ, iṉṉiṉ ikaram āviṉ iṟutimuṉṉar - iṉcāriyaiyiṉatu ikaram ā eṉṉum collīṟṟumuṉṉar, keṭutalum urittākum - keṭāmaiyēyaṉṟik keṭutalum urittām e-ṟu.
u-m: āṉai, āviṉai, āṉkōṭu, āviṉkōṭu eṉa varum.
‘muṉṉar’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ‘mā’ eṉṉum colliṉ muṉṉum aviru vitiyum eytum. māṉai, māviṉai, māṉkōṭu, māviṉkōṭu eṉa varum.	 (18)
122.	aḷapāku moḻimuta ṉilaiiya vuyirmicai
	ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkā ṉākiya nilaittē.
iḵtu, iṉ iṟuti tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu. 
(i-ḷ.) aḷavākum moḻi mutal - aḷavup peyarākum moḻi mutaṟkaṇ, nilaiiya uyirmicai ṉaḵkāṉ - nilaipeṟṟa uyirukku mēlāy niṉṟa iṉcāriyaiyatu ṉakāram, ṟaḵkāṉ ākiya nilaittu - ṟakāramākiya nilaimaittu.
u-m: patiṟṟakal, patiṟṟuḻakku eṉpuḻi avvāṟu varutal aṟika.
‘nilaittu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟavaḻiyum iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal koḷka. patiṟṟoṉṟu, patiṟṟēḻu eṉa varum.    (19)
123.	vaḵkāṉ meykeṭac cuṭṭumuta laimmuṉ
	aḵkā ṉiṟṟa lākiya paṇpē.
itu, vaṟṟu mutal tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal aimuṉ - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalāka vuṭaiya aikāra īṟṟuc colmuṉṉar, vaḵkāṉ mey keṭa  aḵkāṉ niṟṟal ākiya paṇpu - vaṟṟuccāriyai taṉ vakaramākiya meykeṭa akaram niṟṟalākiya paṇpiṉaiyuṭaiya.
u-m: avaiyaṟṟai, ivaiyaṟṟai, uvaiyaṟṟai eṉavum, avaiyaṟṟukkōṭu eṉavum varum.
‘ākiya paṇpu’ eṉṟataṉāl, cuṭṭu mutalākiya ai eṉ iṟuti aikārattoṭu nillātavaḻi, vaṟṟiṉ vakaram akaram niṟkak keṭātu vaṟṟāyē niṟṟal koḷka. maṟṟitu, “tirintataṉ tiripu piṟitu” eṉṉum nayattāṟ keṭātē niṟkumākaliṉ, itu kūṟavēṇṭā eṉiṉ, “cuṭṭu mutal ai īṟṟuc colliṉ aimuṉ” eṉa ōtātu, “cuṭṭu mutal aimmuṉ” eṉa ac colmuṉ ellām keṭuvatupōla ōtiṉamaiyiṉ, vēṇṭiṟṟeṉpatu.(20)
124.	ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkā ṉāṉka ṉurupiṟku.
itu, ṉakara īṟṟuc cāriyai nāṉkaṟkum īṟu tiripu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṉkaṉ urupiṟku ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkāṉ - nāṉkām urupiṟku (ṉakara īṟṟuc cāriyaiyellām) ṉakaram ṟakaramām.
u-m: viḷaviṟku, kōoṟku, orupāṟku, ataṟku eṉa varum. 		(21)
125.	āṉi ṉakaramu mataṉō raṟṟē
	nāṇmuṉ varūum vaṉmutaṟ ṟoḻiṟkē.
iḵtu, āṉcāriyai poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ īṟu tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nāḷmuṉ varum val mutal toḻiṟku āṉiṉ ṉakaramum - nāṭpeyar muṉṉar varum valleḻuttai mutalāka vuṭaiya viṉaic coṟkaṇ varum āṉcāriyaiyiṉ ṉakaramum, ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - an nāṉkaṉurupiṉkaṇ varum āṉcāriyai yōṭu oru taṉmaittāy ṉakāram ṟakaramām.
‘toḻiṟku’ eṉpataṉait ‘toḻiṟkaṇ’ eṉa mayakkamākak koḷka.
u-m: paraṇiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.
ummaiyai iraṭṭuṟa moḻitalāṉē etiratu taḻīiya tākki, ataṉāṉ nāḷallavaṟṟumuṉ varum vaṉmutaṟ ṟoḻiṟka ṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaramum ṟakāramāyt tirital koḷka. paṉiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.
toḻiṟkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram tiriyumeṉa, peyarkkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram tiritalum tiriyāmaiyumuṭaiya teṉpatu  ñāpakattāṟ koḷḷap paṭum. paṟampiṟ pāri, vaṇṭiṉ kāl eṉa varum.	 (22)
126.	atti ṉakara makaramuṉai yillai.
iḵtu, attu mutal tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) attiṉ akaram akaramuṉai illai - attuc cāriyaiyiṉ akaram akaravīṟṟuc colmuṉṉar illaiyākum.
u-m: makattukkai eṉa varum.	(23)
127.	ikki ṉikara mikaramuṉai yaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ikkuc cāriyai mutal tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ikkiṉ ikaram ikaramuṉai aṟṟu - ikkuc cāriyaiyiṉatu ikaram ikara īṟṟuccol muṉṉar mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu pōlak keṭum e-ṟu.
u-m: āṭikkuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.	(24)
128.	aiyiṉ muṉṉaru mavviya ṉilaiyum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aiyiṉ muṉṉarum a iyal nilaiyum - (ikkiṉ ikaram) ikaravīṟṟuc col muṉṉaṉṟi aikāravīṟṟuc col muṉṉarum mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa keṭutaliyalpilē niṟkum.
u-m:	cittiraikkuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.         (25)
129.	eppeyar muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi
	akki ṉiṟutimey micaiyoṭuṅ keṭumē
	kuṟṟiya lukara muṟṟat tōṉṟātu.
iḵtu, akkīṟu tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) e peyar muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi akkiṉ iṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟat tōṉṟātu - evvakaippaṭṭa peyar muṉṉum valleḻuttu varumiṭattu akkiṉ iṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaram muṭiyat tōṉṟātu, mey micaiyoṭum keṭum - ataṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa valleḻuttākiya mey taṉakku mēl niṉṟa meyyoṭum keṭum.
u-m:	kuṉṟakkūkai, maṉṟappeṇṇai, īmakkuṭam, aracak kaṉṉi, tamiḻakkūttu eṉavarum.
(‘muṟṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ vaṉkaṇamaṉṟi maṟṟak kaṇaṅkaṭkum koḷka.) tamiḻanūl, tamiḻayāppu, tamiḻavaraiyar eṉa varum. (26)
130.	ammi ṉiṟuti kacatak kālait
	taṉmey tirintu ṅañana vākum.
itu, ammiṉ iṟuti tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ammiṉ iṟuti ka ca tak kālai - ammiṉ iṟutiyākiya makaravoṟṟu ka ca takkaḷ varumoḻiyāka vanta kālattu, taṉ mey tirintu ṅa ña na ākum - taṉ vaṭivutirintu ṅa ña nakkaḷām.
u-m:	puḷiyaṅkōṭu, puḷiyañcetiḷ, puḷiyantōl eṉa varum.
‘taṉmey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ammiṉ iṟuti makaramēyaṉṟit tam nam num um eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ iṟuti makaramum tiriyumeṉpatu koḷka.
ellārtaṅkaiyum, ellānaṅkaiyum, ellīr nuṅkaiyum, ‘vāṉa varivillun tiṅkaḷum’, eṉa varum.	(27)
131.	meṉmaiyu miṭaimaiyum varūuṅ kālai
	iṉmai vēṇṭu meṉmaṉār pulavar.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) meṉmaiyum iṭaimaiyum varum kālai - ammiṉ iṟuti meṉmaiyum iṭaimaiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu; iṉmai vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar - iṉṟimuṭitalai vēṇṭum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m:	puḷiya ñeri; nuṉi, muri, yāḻ, vaṭṭu eṉa  varum.
uraiyiṟ kōṭal eṉpataṉāṉ, puḷiyavilai eṉavum uyir varuvaḻik kēṭum koḷka. puḷiyilai eṉa muḻuvatūum kēṭu kūṟalum koḷka.	(28)
132.	iṉṉeṉa varūum vēṟṟumai yurupiṟ
	kiṉṉeṉ cāriyai iṉmai vēṇṭum.
iḵtu, iṉ cāriyai muḻuvatūum keṭum iṭam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṉ eṉa varum vēṟṟumai urupiṟku - iṉ eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa vēṟṟumai yurupiṟku, iṉ eṉ cāriyai iṉmai vēṇṭum - iṉ eṉṉum cāriyaitāṉ iṉṟi muṭital vēṇṭum e-ṟu.
u-m: viḷaviṉ, palāviṉ eṉa varum.
‘avaṟṟuḷ iṉṉiṉ ikaram’ (puṇar. 18) eṉṟataṉ piṉ vaiyātataṉāl, ciṟupāṉmai iṉ cāriyai keṭātu niṟṟal koḷka. pāmpiṉiṟ kaṭitu tēḷ eṉa varum.	(29)
133.	peyarun toḻilum pirintoruṅ kicaippa
	vēṟṟumai yurupu nilaipeṟu vaḻiyum
	tōṟṟam vēṇṭāt tokutik kaṇṇum
	oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakkoṭu civaṇic
	coṟcitar maruṅkiṉ vaḻivantu viḷaṅkā
	tiṭainiṉ ṟiyaluñ cāriyai yiyaṟkai
	uṭaimaiyu miṉmaiyu moṭuvayi ṉokkum.
itu, cāriyaikaṭkellām potuvāyatōr ilakkaṇam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) peyarum toḻilum pirintu oruṅku icaippa - peyarccollum viṉaiccollum peyarum viṉaiyumāyp pirintum peyarum peyarumāyk kūṭiyum icaippa, vēṟṟumai urupu nilaipeṟu vaḻiyum - vēṟṟumaiyurupu tokātu nilaipeṟṟa iṭattiṉum, tōṟṟam vēṇṭāt tokutik kaṇṇum - avai tōṟṟutal vēṇṭāta tokutikkaṇṇum, oṭṭutaṟku oḻukiya vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi - tām tām poruntutaṟkēṟpa naṭanta vaḻakkoṭu porunti, col citar maruṅkiṉ - cāriyai peṟum puṇarmoḻikaḷaip pirittuk kāṇumiṭattu, vaḻi vantu viḷaṅkātu iṭainiṉṟu iyalum - avaṟṟiṉ piṉ vantu viḷaṅkātu avaṟṟiṭaiyē niṉṟu naṭakkum, cāriyai iyaṟkai - cāriyaiyiṉ iyalpu. uṭaimaiyum iṉmaiyum oṭuvayiṉ okkum - avaitām uṇṭātalum illaiyātalum oṭu urupiṉiṭattu okkum.
u-m:	  viḷaviṉaik kuṟaittāṉ, viḷaviṉaik kuṟaittavaṉ eṉavum, nilāttukkoṇṭāṉ, nilāttuk koṇṭavaṉ eṉavum varum.
oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakkaṉmaiyiṉ, nilākkatir nilāmuṟṟam eṉpaṉa cāriyai peṟāvāyiṉa.
ellā nammaiyum eṉac cāriyai īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum varutaliṉ, iṭainiṉṟiyaṟal perumpāṉmai yeṉak koḷka.
pūviṉoṭu virinta kūntaleṉavum, pūvoṭu virinta kūntaleṉavum, uṇmaiyum iṉmaiyum oṭuvayiṉ ottavāṟu.
‘iyaṟkai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ oṭuvurupiṉkaṇ cāriyai peṟutalum peṟāmaiyum oḻiya, orōvaḻip peṟṟē varumeṉpatu koḷka. palavaṟṟoṭu eṉa varum.	          (30)
134.	attē vaṟṟē āyiru moḻimēl
	oṟṟumey keṭuta ṟeṟṟeṉ ṟaṟṟē
	avaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, attum vaṟṟum varumiṭattu nilaimoḻiyiṉum varumoḻiyiṉum varuñ ceykai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) attē vaṟṟē a irumoḻimēl oṟṟu mey keṭutal teṟṟeṉṟaṟṟu - attum vaṟṟumākiya av virumoḻi mēlniṉṟa oṟṟut taṉvaṭivu keṭutal teḷiyappaṭṭatu, avaṟṟu muṉvarum valleḻuttu mikum - av virucāriyai muṉṉum varum valleḻuttu mikum. (muntiya ēkāram iraṇṭum eṇṇiṭaiccol. pintiya ēkāram iraṇṭum īṟṟacai.)
u-m: kalattukkuṟai, avaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa varum.
kalaṉ eṉa, ṉakāramāka niṟuttik keṭukkavē. ‘oṉṟiṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, puḷḷiyīṟalvaḻi vikāra vakaiyāṉ niṉṟaṉavum avaṟṟiṉmicai yoṟṟeṉṟu keṭukkappaṭumeṉak koḷka.
avaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa varum. iḵtu aikāra īṟu.
īṇṭu ‘valleḻuttu miku’meṉṟatu, īṟṟu valleḻuttiṉṟit tirintu muṭiyum ṉakaramum ṇakaramum lakaramum ḷakaramum eṉa ivaṟṟai nōkkiyeṉa vuṇarka.
attu muṟkūṟiya muṟaiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ, attiṉ micai yoṟṟuk keṭātu niṟkavum peṟum. viṇṇattuk koṭkum, veyilattuc ceṉṟāṉ, iruḷattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.
‘mey’ eṉṟataṉāl, attiṉ akaram piṟavuyiriṉ muṉṉum keṭutal koḷka. aṇṇāttēri eṉavarum.
‘teṟṟu’ eṉṟataṉāl, attiṉ akaram keṭātu niṟṟalum koḷka. viḷavattukkaṇ eṉa varum.	  (31)
135.	kāramuṅ karamuṅ kāṉoṭu civaṇi
	nērat tōṉṟu meḻuttiṉ cāriyai.
iḵtu, eḻuttuc cāriyaikaṭkup peyarum muṟaiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kāramum karamum kāṉoṭu civaṇi - kāramum karamum kāṉoṭu porunti, nērattōṉṟum eḻuttiṉ cāriyai - ellā āciriyarāṉum uṭaṉpaṭat tōṉṟum eḻuttuccāriyai yātaṟku.
‘nērat tōṉṟum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, nērattōṉṟātaṉa āṉam, ōṉam, eṉa ivai.	 (32)
136.	avaṟṟuḷ
	karamuṅ kāṉu neṭṭeḻut tilavē.
iḵtu, avaṟṟuṭ cila cāriyai cila eḻuttōṭu vārā eṉa eytiyatu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ karamum kāṉum neṭṭeḻuttu ila - mēṟ collappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ karamum kāṉum neṭṭeḻuttiṟku ila.
kāram neṭṭeḻuttiṟku uṇṭu eṉṟavāṟu. ākāram, īkāram eṉa varum.	(33)
137.	varaṉmuṟai mūṉṟuṅ kuṟṟeḻut tuṭaiya.
itu, neṭṭeḻuttiṟkuc cila cāriyai vilakkiṉamaiyiṉ kuṟṟeḻuttiṟkum cila vilakkuvatuṇṭukol eṉṉum aiyam tīrttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) varalmuṟai mūṉṟum kuṟṟeḻuttu uṭaiya - varalāṟṟu muṟaimaiyaiyuṭaiya mūṉṟu cāriyaiyum kuṟṟeḻuttu uṭaiya.
u-m: akāram, akaram, aḵkāṉ eṉa varum.
‘varaṉmuṟai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, aḵkāṉ eṉpuḻi āytamikku muṭiyumeṉpatu koḷka.	   (34)
138.	aikāra oḷakāraṅ kāṉoṭun tōṉṟum.
iḵtu, avaṟṟuḷ kāramuṅ kāṉum eṉpataṟku ōr puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aikāram oḷakāram kāṉoṭum tōṉṟum - neṭ ṭeḻuttukkaḷil aikāramum oḷakāramum muṉ vilakkappaṭṭa kāṉoṭum tōṉṟum.
u-m: aikāṉ, oḷakāṉ eṉa varum.	(35)
139.	puḷḷi yīṟṟumu ṉuyirtaṉit tiyalātu
	meyyoṭuñ civaṇu mavviyal keṭuttē.
iḵtu, uyirmutalmoḻi puḷḷiyīṟṟumuṉ varuṅkāṟ piṟappatōr karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) puḷḷi īṟṟumuṉ uyir taṉittu iyalātu - puḷḷiyīṟṟuc colmuṉ uyir taṉittu naṭavātu, meyyoṭum civaṇum a iyal keṭuttu - ap puḷḷiyoṭum kūṭum tāṉ taṉiniṉṟa av viyalpiṉaik keṭuttu e-ṟu.
u-m: āl + aṭai = ālaṭai eṉa varum.
‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, iyalpallāta puḷḷimuṉ uyir vantālum iv viti koḷka. ataṉai eṉavum, nāṭuri eṉavum varum.
puḷḷiyīṟṟu muṉṉum eṉa, tokuttu niṉṟa ummaiyai virittataṉāṉē kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ muṉṉum iv viti koḷka.	nākaritu eṉa varum.	(36)
140.	meyyuyir nīṅkiṟ ṟaṉṉuru vākum.
itu, puṇarcciyiṭattu uyirmey uyir  nīṅkiyavaḻip paṭuvatoru viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mey uyir nīṅkiṉ taṉ uru ākum - mey taṉṉoṭu kūṭiya uyir puṇarcciyiṭattu nīṅkiya vaḻit taṉ puḷḷi vaṭivu peṟum.
u-m: ataṉai - ataṉ + ai eṉa varum. 	(37)
141.	ellā moḻikku muyirvaru vaḻiyē
	uṭampaṭu meyyi ṉurupukoḷal varaiyār.
iḵtu, uyirīṟu uyirmutaṉ moḻiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻi nikaḻva toru karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellā moḻikkum - mūvakaippaṭṭa moḻikkum, uyir varu vaḻi - uyir mutaṉmoḻi varum iṭattu, uṭampaṭu meyyiṉ uruvu koḷal varaiyār - iṭai uṭampaṭumey vaṭivu kōṭalai nīkkār.
u-m: puḷiyaṅkōṭu, eruvaṅkuḻi, viḷavattukkoṭkum eṉa varum.
‘uraiyiṟ kōṭal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, uṭampaṭumeyyāvaṉa yakaramum vakaramum eṉak koḷka. ikaravīṟum īkāravīṟum aikāravīṟum yakaravuṭam paṭumey koḷvaṉa; allaṉa vellām vakaramey koḷvaṉa. ‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, vikārappaṭṭa moḻik kaṇṇum uṭampaṭumey koḷka. maravaṭi, āyirutiṇai eṉa varum.
‘varaiyār’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, uṭampaṭumey kōṭal orutalai aṉṟeṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum. kiḷi aritu, mūṅkā illai eṉa varum.	(38)
142.	eḻuttō raṉṉa poruḷteri puṇarcci
	icaiyiṟ ṟirita ṉilaiiya paṇpē.
iḵtu, eḻuttukkaḷ oṉṟu palavātal kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) eḻuttu ōr aṉṉa poruḷ teri puṇarcci - eḻuttu oru taṉmaiyāṉa poruḷ viḷaṅkiniṟkum puṇar moḻikaḷ, icaiyiṉ tirital nilaiiya paṇpu - ōcai vēṟṟumaiyāṉ puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutal nilaipeṟṟa paṇpu.
u-m:	cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi, kuṉṟēṟāmā eṉa varum. 		          (39)
143.	avaitām
	muṉṉap poruḷa puṇarcci vāyiṉ
	iṉṉa veṉṉum meḻuttukkaṭa ṉilavē.
itu, mēlataṟku ōr puṟaṉaṭai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaitām - mēṟcolliya puṇarmoḻikaḷ tām, muṉṉap poruḷa - muṉṉattiṉāṉ uṇarum poruṇ maiyai yuṭaiya; puṇarcci vāyiṉ iṉṉa eṉṉum eḻuttuk kaṭaṉ ila - avai puṇarcci yiṭattu ittaṉmaiya veṉṉum eḻuttu muṟaimaiyai uṭaiyavalla e-ṟu.
cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi eṉṟavaḻip poṉṉārāycci uḷ vaḻip poṉṉeṉavum, cempārāycci uḷvaḻic cempeṉavum kuṟippāṉ uṇarappaṭṭatu. maṟṟu, itaṉmēl ‘icaiyi ṟirital nilaital’ (eḻut. 142) eṉa aṟiyumāṟu kūṟiṉāraṉ ṟōveṉiṉ, ōcai eṉṟamaiyāṉ aḵtu oliyeḻuttiṟ keṉavum, “iṉṉa veṉṉu meḻuttuk kaṭaṉila” eṉṟataṉāṉ itu varivaṭiviṟkeṉavuṅ koḷka.	(40)
nāṉkāvatu - puṇariyal muṟṟiṟṟu.


tokai marapu
uyiraiyum meyyaiyum iṟutiyākavuṭaiya coṟkaḷaip piṉvarum iyalkaḷil īṟukaḷtōṟum taṉittaṉiyē virittōti muṭikkak karutiya āciriyar, pala vīṟukaḷukkum potuvāṉa vitikaḷai ivviyalil ovvōr cūttiraṅkaḷāl tokuttu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟār. ataṉāl ivviyal tokaimarapu eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. nilaimoḻi varumoḻip puṇarccikaṭpaṭum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷāyt toṉṟutoṭṭu varum ilakkaṇa marapukaḷait tokuttuṇarttutaliṉ tokaimara peṉap paṭṭatu eṉiṉumamaiyum. nilaimoḻiyum varumoḻiyum mēl puṇariyaliṟ kūṟiya karuvikaḷāl tokkuppuṇarum ceykai kūṟuvatu ivviyalātaliṉ itu puṇariyalōṭu iyaipuṭait tāyiṟṟu.
ivviyal muppatu cūttiraṅkaḷaiyuṭaiyatu. itaṉkaṇ alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumaiyumākiya iruvaḻiyilum kacatapa eṉṉum valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl mikutaṟkuriya melleḻuttukkaḷ muṟaiyē ṅa ña na ma eṉpaṉavām eṉṉum varumoḻik karuviyum, irupattunāṉkīṟṟiṉ muṉṉum vaṉkaṇa moḻinta kaṇaṅkaḷukku iruvaḻiyum varumoḻimuṭipum, ṇakara ṉakara vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷukku iruvaḻiyum nilaimoḻi muṭipum, lakara, ṉakara vīṟukaḷiṉ muṉṉum ṇakara ḷakara vīṟukaḷiṉ muṉṉum varum takara nakaraṅkaḷ muṟaiyē ṟakara ṉakaraṅkaḷākavum ṭakara ṇakaraṅkaḷākavum tiriyu meṉṉum varumoḻik karuviyum, uyiraiyum meyyaiyum iṟutiyākavuṭaiya muṉṉilai viṉaic coṟkaḷ valliṉa mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl iyalpum uṟaḻcciyu mākiya irunilaimaiyaiyuṭaiyaṉa veṉpatum, oḷakārattaiyum ñanamava eṉṉum meykaḷaiyum kuṟṟiyalukarattaiyum īṟākavuṭaiya muṉṉilai viṉaiccoṟkaḷ muṟkūṟiya muṭipiṟku muṟṟum poruntuvaṉa alla eṉpatum, uyiraiyum meyyaiyum īṟākavuṭaiya uyar tiṇaip peyarkaḷ nāṉkukaṇattum iruvaḻiyum iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉpatum, avaṟṟuḷ ikaravīṟṟu uyartiṇaip peyar tirintu muṭiyumiṭaṉumuṇ ṭeṉpatum, aḵṟiṇai viravu peyar iyalpāy muṭivaṉavumuḷa veṉpatum, mūṉṟām vēṟṟumaip puṇarcciyilum iraṇṭām vēṟṟumaip puṇarcciyilum uḷavākun tiripukaḷum, ikara aikāravīṟṟup peyarkaḷukku alvaḻi muṭipum, ikara aikāravīṟṟuḷ ēḻām vēṟṟumaiyiṭap poruḷuṇara niṉṟa iṭaiccol muṭipum, neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉiṉṟa meyyīṟu keṭutal kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ piṉṉiṉṟa iṟutimey iraṭṭittal ākiya nilaimoḻikkaru viyum, ivaṟṟiṟku urupiyalai nōkkiyatōr, varumoḻik karuviyum, ukaramoṭu puṇarum meyyīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ yakaramum uyirum varumoḻiyāy variṉ avvukaram peṟātu iyalpām eṉṉum varumoḻic ceykaiyum aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum eṇṇuppeyarum tammiṟ puṇarumāṟum, aḷavuppeyarkkum niṟaippeyarkkum mutalātaṟkuriya veḻuttukkaḷ ka ca ta pa na ma va a u eṉṉumiv voṉpatumē yeṉṉam varaiyaṟaiyum, mēlē kūṟappaṭṭaṉa vaṟṟiṟkup puṟaṉaṭaiyum, yāvar eṉpatu yār eṉavum yātu eṉpatu yāvatu eṉavum varum marūu muṭipum ivviyalil uṇarttappaṭṭaṉa.
- ka. veḷḷaivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvaricai 10, pak. 174-176


aintāvatu
tokaimarapu
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttō veṉiṉ, mēl akattōttiṉuḷ irupattunāṉku īṟṟiṉum virintu muṭivaṉavaṟṟaiyellām tokuttu muṭittaliṉ tokaimarapu eṉappaṭṭatu. mēl puṇariyaluṭ kūṟiya karuvikaḷāṟ ceykai kūṟum vaḻi, tokkup puṇaruñ ceykai kūṟaliṟ puṇariyalōṭu iyaipuṭait tāyiṟṟu.
144.	kacatapa mutaliya moḻimēṟ ṟōṉṟum
	melleḻut tiyaṟkai colliya muṟaiyāṉ
	ṅañanama veṉṉu moṟṟā kummē
	aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻivayi ṉāṉa.
it talaic cūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumaiyumākiya iruvaḻikkaṇṇum, uyirmayaṅkiyalaiyum  puḷḷimayaṅkiyalaiyum nōkkiyatoru varumoḻik karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ka ca ta pa mutaliya moḻimēl tōṉṟum melleḻuttu iyaṟkai - uyirīṟṟiṉum puḷḷiyīṟṟiṉum iru vaḻiyum ka ca ta pakkaḷai mutalākavuṭaiya moḻikaḷiṉmēl tōṉṟum melleḻuttiṉatu iyalpukūṟiṉ, colliya muṟaiyāṉ ṅa ña na ma veṉṉum oṟṟākum - melleḻuttu mēṟcollum muṟaimaiyāṉ ka ca ta pakkaḷukku niraṉiṟai vakaiyāṉē ṅa ña na ma veṉṉum oṟṟākum, aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻivayiṉ āṉa - at taṉmaittākiya muṟaimaiyiṉai yuṭaiya moḻikaḷiṭattu.
u-m: viḷaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
‘tōṉṟumeṉṟataṉāṉ tōṉṟi niṉṟaṉavum avvāṟē tirintu melleḻuttā meṉpatu. maraṅkuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum. aṉṉamarapiṉ moḻiyaṉmaiyiṉ, viḷak kuṟumai eṉpuḻi melleḻuttu mikātāyiṟṟu.
(ēkāram - īṟṟacai. `āṉa’ eṉpataṉ akaram acai.)	(1)
145.	ña na ma ya va veṉu mutalāku moḻiyum
	uyirmuta lākiya moḻiyu muḷappaṭa
	aṉṟi yaṉaittu mellā vaḻiyum
	niṉṟa coṉmu ṉiyalpā kummē.
iḵtu, irupattunāṉku īṟṟiṉ muṉṉum, vaṉkaṇa moḻinta kaṇaṅkaṭku iruvaḻiyum varumoḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ña na ma ya va eṉum mutal āku moḻiyum - ña na ma ya va eṉṟu collappaṭum mutaleḻuttu uḷavākum moḻiyum, uyir mutalākiya moḻiyum uḷappaṭa - uyir mutalākiya moḻiyumāka, aṉṟi aṉaittum ellā vaḻiyum - av vaṉaittu moḻiyum alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumaiyumākiya ellā viṭattum, niṉṟa colmuṉ iyalpākum - irupattu nāṉku īṟṟup peyarākiya nilaimoḻi muṉṉar iyalpāki muṭiyum.
īṇṭu, ‘uḷappaṭa’ veṉpatu, āka veṉṉum poruṇmaittu.
u-m: viḷa, tāḻ eṉa niṟutti - ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, yātu, valitu, aṭaintatu, āṭiṟṟu, iṭintatu, īriṟṟu, uṭaintatu, ūṟiṟṟu, eḻuntatu, ēṟiṟṟu, aitu, oṭintatu, ōṭiṟṟu, oḷaviyattatu, nuntaiyatu eṉavum; ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, yāppu, valimai, aṭaivu, āṭṭam, iṭipu,  īṭṭam, uṭaipu, ūṟṟam, eḻu, ēṇi, aiyam, oṭukkam, ōkkam, oḷaviyam, nuntai eṉavum oṭṭikkoḷka.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, oṟṟiraṭṭalum, uṭampaṭumey kōṭalum, uyirēṟi muṭitalum eṉavarum ikkaruvittiripu mūṉṟum tiripu aṉmaiyiṉ tiripeṉap paṭāveṉpatu koḷka. iḵtu irupattu nāṉku īṟṟiṟkum, alvaḻiyiṉum, vēṟṟumai yiṉum, akattōttiṉuḷ nāṟpatteṭṭuc cūttirattāṉ muṭivataṉai, īṇṭu oru cūttirattāṉ tokuttu muṭitta tāyiṟṟu. mēlum ivvāṟē tokuttu muṭikkiṉṟa vāṟu aṟika.		(2)

146.	avaṟṟuḷ 
	melleḻut tiyaṟkai yuṟaḻiṉum varaiyār
	colliya toṭarmoḻi yiṟuti yāṉa.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya muṭipiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku, am muṭipu vilakkip piṟitu viti eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟcollappaṭṭa mūṉṟu kaṇattiṉum, melleḻuttu iyaṟkai uṟaḻiṉum varaiyār - melleḻuttiṉatu iyalpu iyalpātalēyaṉṟi uṟaḻntu muṭiyiṉum nīkkār, colliya toṭarmoḻi iṟutiyāṉa - collappaṭṭa toṭar moḻi īṟṟukkaṇ e-ṟu.
u-m: katirñeri, katirññeri; nuṉi, muri eṉa varum.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl, ōreḻuttorumoḻi yuḷḷum, īreḻuttorumoḻiyuḷḷum cilavaṟṟiṟku uṟaḻcci muṭipu koḷka.
u-m:	pūñeri, pūññeri; nuṉi, muri eṉavum; kāyñeri, kāyññeri; nuṉi, muri eṉavum varum.
‘colliya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ōreḻuttorumoḻi yuḷḷum īreḻuttorumoḻiyuḷḷum cilavaṟṟiṟku mikku muṭital koḷka. kaiññerittār; nīṭṭiṉār, maṭittār eṉavum, meyñ ñāṉam, meynnūl, meymmaṟantār eṉavum varum.	(3)
147.	ṇaṉaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ yāvum ñāvum
	viṉaiyō raṉaiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.
itu, yakara ñakaraṅkaḷ mutalāmvaḻi nikaḻvatoru karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ṇa ṉa eṉ puḷḷimuṉ - ṇa ṉa eṉṟu collappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉmuṉ, yāvum ñāvum viṉai ōr aṉaiya eṉmaṉār pulavar - yāvum ñāvum viṉaiccoṟkaṇ mutalātaṟku oru taṉmaiya veṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m: maṇyātta eṉavum, poṉyātta eṉavum, maṇñāttu eṉavum,  poṉñāttu eṉavum varum.
ñā muṟkūṟātu yā muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, yā mutaṉ moḻikkaṇ ñā varumeṉṟu koḷka.	(4)
148.	moḻimuta lāku mellā veḻuttum
	varuvaḻi niṉṟa vāyiru puḷḷiyum
	vēṟṟumai yalvaḻit tiripiṭa ṉilavē.
itu, ṇakāravīṟṟiṟkum ṉakāravīṟṟiṟkum alvaḻikkaṇ nilai moḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) moḻimutal ākum ellā eḻuttum varuvaḻi - moḻikku mutalāmeṉṟu collappaṭṭa irupattiraṇ ṭeḻuttum varumiṭattu, niṉṟa a iru puḷḷiyum vēṟṟumai alvaḻi tiripu iṭaṉ ila - peyarc  colliṉiṉṟa ṇa ṉakkaḷākiya avviru puḷḷiyum vēṟṟumaiyallāta alvaḻi yiṭattut tiriyum iṭam ila.
u-m:  maṇ, poṉ eṉa niṟutti, kaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa vaṉkaṇattōṭu oṭṭuka. piṟa kaṇattukkaṇṇum avvāṟē oṭṭuka.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl, ṇakārattiṟkuc ciṟupāṉmai tiripum uṇṭeṉak koḷka. cāṭkōl eṉa varum. (ēkāram īṟṟacai).	(5)
149.	vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum valleḻut talvaḻi
	mēṟkū ṟiyaṟkai yāvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, av viraṇṭu īṟṟiṟkum vēṟṟumaikkaṇ nilaimoḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum - vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarccik kaṇṇum, valleḻuttu alvaḻi - valleḻuttu allāta viṭattu, mēlkūṟu iyaṟkai avayiṉ āṉa - mēṟkūṟiya iyalpu muṭipām av viraṇṭu puḷḷiyiṭattum.
u-m: maṇ, poṉ eṉa niṟutti, ñāṟci, nīṭci eṉa vaṉkaṇam oḻittu ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.	(6)
150.	laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi muṉṉar
	tana veṉavariṟ ṟaṉavā kummē.
itu, puḷḷimayaṅkiyalai nōkkiyatoru varumoḻik karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) la ṉa eṉa varum puḷḷi muṉṉar - la ṉa eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, ta na eṉa variṉ - ta na eṉa varumoḻi variṉ, ṟa ṉa ākum - at takara nakaraṅkaḷ niraṉiṟaiyāṉē ṟakara ṉakaraṅkaḷām.
u-m:	kaḵṟītu, kaṉṉaṉṟu, poṉṟītu, poṉṉaṉṟu eṉa varum.	(7)
151.	ṇaḷaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ ṭaṇaveṉat tōṉṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ṇa ḷa eṉ puḷḷimuṉ - ṇa ḷa eṉṉum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar (atikārattiṉāṉ takāra nakāraṅkaḷ variṉ), ṭa ṇa eṉat tōṉṟum - avai niraṉiṟaiyāṉ ṭakāra ṇakaraṅkaḷāyt tōṉṟum.
u-m: 	maṇṭītu, maṇṇaṉṟu, muḵṭītu, muṇṇaṉṟu eṉa varum.	(8)
152.	uyirī ṟākiya muṉṉilaik kiḷaviyum
	puḷḷi yiṟuti muṉṉilaik kiḷaviyum
	iyalpā kunavum muṟaḻpā kunavumeṉ
	ṟāyī riyala valleḻuttu variṉē.
itu, muṉṉilai viṉaiccol vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir īṟākiya muṉṉilaik kiḷaviyum puḷḷi iṟuti muṉṉilaik kiḷaviyum - uyir īṟākiya muṉṉilaic coṟkaḷum puḷḷi iṟutiyaiyuṭaiya muṉṉilaic coṟkaḷum, valleḻuttuvariṉ - valleḻuttu mutalmoḻi variṉ, iyalpu ākunavum uṟaḻpu ākunavum eṉṟu a īr iyala - iyal pāvaṉavum uṟaḻcciyāvaṉavum eṉa av viraṇṭu iyalpiṉai yuṭaiya.
uyirīṟu puḷḷiyīṟu eṉṟamaiyāṉ, muṉṉilai viṉaic col eṉpatu koḷka.
u-m:	eṟikoṟṟā, koṇākoṟṟā; cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉavum; uṇkoṟṟā, tiṉkoṟṟā eṉavum varum. ivai iyalpu. naṭakoṟṟā, naṭakkoṟṟā eṉavum, īrkoṟṟā, īrkkoṟṟā eṉavum varum. ivai uṟaḻcci.	(9)
153.	oḷaveṉa varūu muyiriṟu collum
	ñanamava veṉṉum puḷḷi yiṟutiṅ
	kuṟṟiya lukarat tiṟutiyu muḷappaṭa
	muṟṟat tōṉṟā muṉṉilai moḻikkē.
itu, mēl muṭipu kūṟiyavaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku am muṭipu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) oḷa eṉa varum uyir iṟu collum - oḷa eṉa varukiṉṟa uyirīṟṟuc collum, ña na ma va eṉṉum puḷḷi iṟutiyum - ña na ma va eṉṟu collappaṭum puḷḷiyīṟṟuc collum, kuṟṟiyalukarattu iṟutiyum uḷappaṭa - kuṟṟiyalukara mākiya iṟutiyaiyuṭaiya collumākiya ivai, muṉṉilai moḻikku muṟṟat tōṉṟā - muṉṉilai moḻikkuk kūṟiya iyalpum uṟaḻvumākiya muṭiviṟku muṟṟattōṉṟā.
‘muṟṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, īṇṭu vilakkappaṭṭavaṟṟuṭ kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu oḻittu, oḻintaṉavellām nilaimoḻi ukaram peṟṟu, varumoḻi valleḻuttu uṟaḻntu muṭitalum, kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttu uṟaḻntu muṭitaluṅ koḷka.
u-m:  kauvukoṟṟā, kauvukkoṟṟā, uriñukoṟṟā, uriñuk koṟṟā, porunukoṟṟā, porunukkoṟṟā, tirumukoṟṟā, tirumukkoṟṟā, tevvukoṟṟā, tevvuk koṟṟā eṉavum; kūṭṭukoṟṟā, kūṭṭuk koṟṟā eṉavum varum. 	(10)
154.	uyirī ṟākiya vuyartiṇaip peyarum
	puḷḷi yiṟuti yuyartiṇaip peyarum
	ellā vaḻiyu miyalpeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, uyartiṇaip peyar nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇum, iru vaḻiyum muṭiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir īṟākiya uyartiṇaip peyarum - uyir īṟākiya uyartiṇaip peyarkaḷum, puḷḷi iṟuti uyartiṇaip peyarum - puḷḷi iṟutiyaiyuṭaiya uyartiṇaip peyarkaḷum, ellā vaḻiyum iyalpu eṉa moḻipa - nāṉku kaṇattilum alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumaiyumākiya ellāviṭattum iyalpām eṉṟu colluvar.
u-m:   nampi eṉavum avaṉ eṉavum niṟutti, alvaḻikkaṇ kuṟiyaṉ, ciṟiyaṉ, tīyaṉ, periyaṉ eṉavum; ñāṉṟāṉ, nīṇṭāṉ, māṇṭāṉ eṉavum; yāvaṉ, valiyaṉ eṉavum; aṭaintāṉ, oḷaviyattāṉ eṉavum; vēṟṟumaikkaṇ kai, cevi, talai, puṟam eṉavum; ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci eṉavum; yāppu, valimai eṉavum; aṭaipu, oḷaviyam eṉavum oṭṭuka. oruveṉ eṉa niṟuttu, kuṟiyeṉ, ciṟiyeṉ eṉavum; kai, cevi, talai, puṟam eṉavum taṉmaip peyarkkaṇṇum oṭṭuka.
‘uyirīṟu’, ‘puḷḷiyiṟuti’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, uyartiṇaip poruḷ iyalpaṉṟi muṭivaṉa vellām koḷka. palcāṉṟār, kapilaparaṇar, iṟaivaneṭuvēṭṭuvar, maruttuva māṇikkar, pallaracar eṉa ivai īṟukeṭṭu muṭintaṉa.
kōlikakkaruvi, vaṇṇārappeṇṭir, ācīvakap paḷḷi eṉa ivai īṟukeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikkaṉa.
kumarakōṭṭam, kumarakkōṭṭam, pirama kōṭṭam, piramak kōṭṭam eṉa ivai īṟukeṭṭu valleḻuttu uṟaḻntu muṭintaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, uyartiṇai(viṉai)ccol iyalpāyum tirintum muṭivaṉa koḷka. uṇpa cāṉṟār, uṇṭār cāṉṟār, uṇpēṉ pārppēṉ eṉa ivai iyalpu. uṇṭēñ cāṉṟēm, uṇṭēnām eṉa ivai tirintaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(11)
155.	avaṟṟuḷ,
	ikaravīṟ ṟuppeyar tiripiṭa ṉuṭaittē.
iḵtu, uyartiṇaip peyaruṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytātatu eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟcollappaṭṭa uyartiṇaip peyaruḷ, ikara īṟṟup peyar tiripu iṭaṉ uṭaittu - ikaramākiya iṟutiyai yuṭaiya peyar tirintu muṭiyum iṭamum uṭaittu.
uraiyiṟkōṭal eṉpataṉāṉ, itu mikka tiripeṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum.
u-m:	eṭṭippū, eṭṭippuravu; kāvitippū, kāvitippuravu; nampippū, nampippēṟu eṉa varum.
‘iṭaṉuṭaittu’ eṉpataṉāṉ, ikara īṟaṉṟip piṟa īṟum īṟu tiriyātu valleḻuttu mikku varuvaṉa īṇṭukkoḷka. tiṉaippū, tiṉaippuravu eṉa varum.	 (12)
156.	aḵṟiṇai viravuppeya riyalpumā ruḷavē.
itu, viravuppeyar muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyar iyalpum uḷa - uyar tiṇaiyōṭu aḵṟiṇai viravum viravuppeyar iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷa; iyalpiṉṟi muṭivaṉavum uḷa. iyalpiṉṟi muṭivaṉa iṉṉavāṟu muṭiyumeṉa, mēl akattōttiṉuṭ kūṟappaṭum.
u-m:   	 cāttaṉkuṟiyaṉ, cāttaṉkuṟitu eṉavum; cāttaṉ
kai eṉavum; ivvāṟu alvaḻiyiṉum vēṟṟumaiyiṉum nāṉku kaṇattōṭum oṭṭikkoḷka.	 (13)
157.	puḷḷi yiṟutiyu muyiriṟu kiḷaviyum
	valleḻuttu mikuti colliya muṟaiyāṟ
	ṟammi ṉākiya toḻiṟcoṉ muṉvariṉ
	meymmai yākalu muṟaḻat tōṉṟalum
	ammuṟai yiraṇṭu muriyavai yuḷavē
	vēṟṟumai maruṅkiṟ pōṟṟal vēṇṭum.
itu, mūṉṟām vēṟṟumait tiripu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu. 
(i-ḷ.) puḷḷi iṟutiyum uyir iṟu kiḷaviyum - puḷḷi yīṟṟuc collum uyirīṟṟuc collum, valleḻuttu mikuti colliya muṟaiyāṉ - valleḻuttiṉatu mikuti mēṟcollum muṟaimaiyāṉ, tammiṉ ākiya toḻiṟcol muṉvariṉ - am mūṉṟāvataṉ poruḷākiya viṉaimutaṟ poruḷkaḷ tammāṉ uḷavākiya viṉaic coṟkaḷ tām avaṟṟumuṉvariṉ, meymmaiyākalum uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum a muṟai iraṇṭum uriyavai uḷa - iyalpākalum uṟaḻat tōṉṟutalumākiya am muṟaimaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya iraṇṭu ceykaiyum uriyaṉa uḷa, vēṟṟumai maruṅkiṉ pōṟṟal vēṇṭum - avaṟṟai mēlē vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarcci muṭipu collumiṭattup pōṟṟiyaṟital vēṇṭum .
u-m: nāykōṭpaṭṭāṉ, pulikōṭpaṭṭāṉ, cārap paṭṭāṉ, tīṇṭappaṭṭāṉ, pāyappaṭṭāṉ eṉa ivai iyalpu. cūrkōṭpaṭṭāṉ, cūrkkōṭpaṭṭāṉ, vaḷikōṭ paṭṭāṉ, vaḷikkōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉa ivai uṟaḻcci.
‘puḷḷiyīṟu uyirīṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, pēeykōṭpaṭṭāṉ pēeyk kōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyuḷ ekarappēṟu koḷka. ‘uriyavai yuḷa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, pāmpukōṭpaṭṭāṉ, pāppukkōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyuḷ nilaimoḻi yoṟṟut tiriyāmaiyum tiritalum koḷka.	(14)
158.	melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi valippoṭu tōṉṟalum
	valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melippoṭu tōṉṟalum
	iyaṟkai maruṅkiṉ mikaṟkai tōṉṟalum
	uyirmika varuvaḻi yuyirkeṭa varutaluñ
	cāriyai yuḷavaḻic cāriyai keṭutaluñ
	cāriyai yuḷavaḻit taṉṉurupu nilaiyaluñ
	cāriyai yiyaṟkai yuṟaḻat tōṉṟalum
	uyartiṇai maruṅki ṉoḻiyātu varutalum
	aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyark kavviya ṉilaiyalum
	meypiṟi tākiṭat tiyaṟkai yākalum
	aṉṉa piṟavun taṉṉiyal maruṅkiṉ
	meypeṟak kiḷantu poruḷvarain ticaikkum
	aikāra vēṟṟumait tiripeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, iraṇṭām vēṟṟumaittiripu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi valippoṭu tōṉṟalum - melleḻuttu mikumiṭattu valleḻuttātaloṭu tōṉṟu talum, valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melippoṭu tōṉṟalum - valleḻuttu mikumiṭattu melleḻuttātaloṭu tōṉṟu talum, iyaṟkai maruṅkiṉ mikaṟkai tōṉṟalum - iyaṟkai yiṭattu mikuti tōṉṟutalum, uyir mika varuvaḻi uyir keṭa varutalum - uyir mika varumiṭattu avvuyir keṭa varutalum, cāriyai uḷavaḻi cāriyai keṭutalum - cāriyaiyuḷḷa iṭattuc cāriyai keṭutalum, cāriyai uḷavaḻi taṉ urupu nilaiyalum - cāriyai uḷḷaviṭattuc cāriyaiyoṭu taṉṉurupu niṟṟalum, cāriyai iyaṟkai uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum - cāriyai peṟuka eṉṟavaḻic cāriyai peṟātu iyalpākiya moḻikaḷ varumoḻiyiṉum nilaimoḻiyiṉum mikkum tirintum varum varumoḻi valleḻuttukkaḷ uṟaḻcciyākat tōṉṟutalum, uyartiṇai maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu varutalum - uyartiṇaip peyariṭattut taṉṉurupu tokātē virintu varutalum, aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyarkku a iyal nilaiyalum - uyartiṇaiyōṭu aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyarkkum av vurupu av viyalpilē niṟṟalum, meypiṟitu āku iṭattu iyaṟkai ākalum - mey piṟitāy muṭiyumiṭattu iyalpāy muṭitalum, aṉṉa piṟavum - attaṉmaiyaṉa piṟa muṭipukaḷum, taṉ iyal maruṅkiṉ meypeṟa kiḷantu poruḷ varaintu icaikkum - taṉakku iyalpākiya kūṟṟāṉ akattōttiṉuṭ poruḷ peṟa eṭuttōtappaṭṭa vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarcciyatu potu muṭiviṉait tāṉ varaintu vēṟu muṭipiṟṟāy niṉṟu olikkum, aikāra vēṟṟumait tiripu eṉa moḻipa - aikāra vēṟṟumai yiṉatu vēṟupāṭṭup puṇarcci eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m:	viḷakkuṟaittāṉ eṉpatu melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi  valleḻuttumikkatu. maraṅkuṟaittāṉ eṉpatu valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melleḻuttu mikkatu. tāyk kolai eṉpatu iyalpāmiṭattu mikkatu. palāk kuṟaittāṉ eṉpatu uyirmika varuvaḻi uyir keṭṭatu. vaṇṭu koṇarntāṉ eṉpatu cāriyai uḷavaḻic  cāriyai keṭṭatu. vaṇṭiṉaik koṇarntāṉ eṉpatu cāriyai uḷavaḻit (421) taṉṉurupu nilaiyiṟṟu. puḷi kuṟaittāṉ, puḷikkuṟaittāṉ, pūlkuṟaittāṉ, pūṟkuṟaittāṉ eṉa ivai  cāriyai yiyaṟkai uṟaḻat tōṉṟiṉa. nampiyaik koṇarntāṉ  eṉpatu uyartiṇai maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu vantatu. koṟṟaṉaik koṇarntāṉ eṉpatu viravuppeyarkku avviyal nilaiyatu. maṇkoṇarntāṉ eṉpatu meypiṟitākiṭattu  iyaṟkaiyāy vantatu.
‘aṉṉa piṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷvaṉa: kaḻikuṟaittāṉ, tiṉaipiḷantāṉ eṉpaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa.
‘oḻiyātu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ orōvaḻi oḻintum varum. ‘avaṟkaṇṭu’  eṉavum, ‘oṉṉārt teṟalum’ eṉavum ivai uyartiṇaiyuḷ oḻintu vantaṉa.  makaṟpeṟṟāṉ, makaṭpeṟṟāṉ eṉa ivai viravup peyaruḷ oḻintu vantaṉa. iv vilēcutaṉṉāṉē, ivaṟṟiṉ muṭipu vēṟṟumaiyum koḷka.
‘meypeṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum koḷka. maikoṇarntāṉ, maikkoṇarntāṉ eṉavum; vilkōḷ, viṟkōḷ eṉavum varum. ivvāṟu tirintu muṭiyātu, akattōttiṉ potumuṭipē muṭipāyk kaṭukkuṟaittāṉ, ceppuk koṇarntāṉ eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa muṭivaṉa aṟintu koḷka. ivvāṟu vēṟupaṭa muṭivatu perumpāṉmaiyum iraṇṭāvatu viṉaiyoṭu muṭiyumvaḻip pōlum. atu “tammiṉākiya toḻiṟ coṉ muṉvariṉ” (tokaimarapu 14) eṉa niṉṟa atikārattāṟ koḷḷa vaittār pōlum. ‘taṉṉiṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, ēḻām vēṟṟumai viṉai yoṭu muṭiyumvaḻi iyalpum koḷka. ‘varaipāy varuṭai’ (malaipaṭu. 503), ‘pulampuk kaṉaṉē pullaṇaṟ kāḷai’ eṉa varum.	 (15)
159.	vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi i ai yeṉṉum
	īṟṟuppeyark kiḷavi mūvakai nilaiiya 
	avaitām
	iyalpā kunavum valleḻuttu mikunavum
	uṟaḻā kunavum eṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, ikaravīṟṟup peyarkkum aikāravīṟṟup peyarkkum alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumai alvaḻi i ai eṉṉum īṟṟup peyark kiḷavi mūvakai nilaiya - vēṟṟumaiyallāta alvaḻiyiṭattu i ai eṉṉum īṟṟaiyuṭaiya peyarccoṟkaḷ mūvakai muṭipu nilaimaiyuṭaiya, avaitām iyalpākunavum valleḻuttu mikunavum uṟaḻākunavum eṉmaṉār pulavar - avaitām iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum val leḻuttu mikuvaṉavum uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum ivaiyeṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m:	parutti kuṟitu, karai kuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa ivai iyalpu. alikkoṟṟaṉ, pulaikkoṟṟaṉ eṉa ivai mikuti. kiḷikuṟitu, kiḷikkuṟitu, tiṉaikuṟitu, tiṉaikkuṟitu eṉa ivai uṟaḻcci.
‘peyarkkiḷavi mūvakai nilaiya’ eṉavē, peyarkkiḷavi yallāta kiḷavi mikutiyum iyalpum eṉa iruvakaiya eṉak koḷka. ollaik koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu aikāravīṟṟu viṉaic col mikuti. ikaravīṟṟu mikuti vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka.
“tillaic collē” (col. 253) “maṉṉaic collē” (col. 252) eṉṟatu aikāravīṟ ṟiṭaiccol mikuti. ivaṟṟiyalpu vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka.
kaṭikā eṉpatu ikaravīṟṟu uriccolliyalpu. iv vīṟṟumikuti vanta vaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka. paṇaittōḷ eṉpatu aikāravīṟṟu uriccol mikuti. ivaṟṟiyalpu vantavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.	(16)
160.	cuṭṭumuta lākiya vikara viṟutiyum
	ekaramutal viṉāvi ṉikara viṟutiyuñ
	cuṭṭucciṉai nīṭiya vaiye ṉiṟutiyum
	yāveṉ viṉāviṉaiye ṉiṟutiyum
	valleḻuttu mikunavu muṟaḻā kunavuñ
	colliya maruṅki ṉuḷaveṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, av vikara aikāra vīṟṟuḷ ēḻām vēṟṟumai iṭap poruḷ uṇaraniṉṟa iṭaiccol muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutalākiya ikara iṟutiyum - cuṭṭeḻut tiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya ikaravīṟṟu iṭaiccollum, ekara mutal viṉāviṉ ikara iṟutiyum - ekaramākiya moḻimutal viṉāviṉuṭaiya ikaravīṟṟu iṭaiccollum, cuṭṭucciṉai nīṭiya aieṉ iṟutiyum - cuṭṭākiya uṟuppeḻuttu nīṇṭa aikāra vīṟṟu iṭaiccollum, yā eṉ viṉāviṉ ai eṉ iṟutiyum - yā eṉṉum viṉāviṉai mutaṟkaṇṇuṭaiya aikāra vīṟṟu iṭaiccollum, valleḻuttu mikunavum uṟaḻākunavum colliya maruṅkiṉ uḷa eṉa moḻipa - valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavum uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum mēṟcollappaṭṭa iṭattiṉ kaṇṇē uḷaveṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m: atōḷikkoṇṭāṉ, itōḷikkoṇṭāṉ,  utōḷik koṇṭāṉ, etōḷikkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; āṇṭaikkoṇṭāṉ, īṇṭaik koṇṭāṉ, ūṇṭaik koṇṭāṉ, yāṇṭaik koṇṭāṉ eṉavum ivai mikku muṭintaṉa.
avvaḻikoṇṭāṉ, avvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ; ivvaḻi koṇṭāṉ, ivvaḻik koṇṭāṉ; uvvaḻi koṇṭāṉ, uvvaḻik koṇṭāṉ; evvaḻikoṇṭāṉ, evvaḻik koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, āṅkavaikoṇṭāṉ, āṅkavaik koṇṭāṉ; īṅkivai koṇṭāṉ, īṅkivaikkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum; ūṅkuvaikoṇṭāṉ, ūṅkuvaik koṇṭāṉ yāṅkavai koṇṭāṉ, yāṅkavaik koṇṭāṉ  eṉavum ivai uṟaḻntu muṭintaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ aikāravīṟṟuḷ uṟaḻntu muṭintaṉa tiripuṭaiyaṉa. tiripillaṉa peṟṟavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.
‘colliya maruṅku’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟa aikāravīṟu mikku muṭivaṉa koḷka. paṇṭaic cāṉṟār, orutiṅkaḷaik kuḻavi eṉa varum.	 (17)
161.	neṭiyataṉ muṉṉa roṟṟumey keṭutaluṅ
	kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉuru piraṭṭalum
	aṟiyat tōṉṟiya neṟiyiya leṉpa.
itu, puḷḷimayaṅkiyalai nōkkiyatōr nilaimoḻik karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟu mey keṭutalum - neṭ ṭeḻuttiṉmuṉ niṉṟa oṟṟut taṉ vaṭivu keṭutalum, kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar taṉ uruvu iraṭṭalum - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa oṟṟut taṉ vaṭivu iraṭṭutalum, aṟiyat tōṉṟiya neṟi iyal eṉpa - ivai aṟiyumpaṭi tōṉṟiya muṟaimaiyāṉa iyal puṭaiyaṉaveṉṟu colluvar.
u-m:	kōṟītu, kōṉaṉṟu eṉa ivai neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟuk keṭṭaṉa. 
	maṇṇakal, poṉṉakal eṉa ivai kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉurupu iraṭṭiṉa.
mēlaiccūttirattu  āṟaṉurupu muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, oṟṟu iraṭṭutal uyirmutaṉmoḻik kaṇṇateṉṟu koḷka. kuṟiyatu piṉkūṟiya muṟaiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ neṭiyaṉa kuṟukiniṉṟa vaḻiyum kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟāy iraṭṭutalum, kuṟiyatu tirintu neṭiyatāyavaḻi ataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟāyk keṭutalum koḷka.
tammai, nammai eṉa ivai neṭiyaṉa kuṟukiniṉṟu oṟṟu iraṭṭiṉa. maṟṟaiyatu vantavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.
‘aṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟukkeṭuvatu takāra nakāraṅkaḷ vantu tirintavaḻi yeṉpatūum, āṇṭu ellām keṭāteṉpatūum koḷka. tēṉṟītu eṉpatu āṇṭuk keṭātatu.
‘neṟiyiyal’ eṉṟataṉāṟ kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa oṟṟiṉṟip puṇarcciyāṟ peṟṟatum iraṭṭumeṉa vuṇarka. avvaṭai eṉavarum.	(18)
162.	āṟa ṉurupiṉu nāṉka ṉurupiṉuṅ
	kūṟiya kuṟṟoṟ ṟiraṭṭa lillai
	īṟāku puḷḷi yakaramoṭu nilaiyum
	neṭumutal kuṟuku moḻimuṉ ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, urupiyalai nōkkiyatōr nilaimoḻik karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṟaṉ urupiṉum nāṉkaṉ urupiṉum īṟu āku puḷḷi akaramoṭu nilaiyum - āṟaṉurupiṉkaṇṇum nāṉka ṉurupiṉkaṇṇum īṟāku puḷḷikaḷ akarattoṭu nilaipeṟum, neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻi muṉ kūṟiya kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭal illai - neṭitākiya mutaleḻuttuk kuṟuki muṭiyum moḻik kaṇ mēṟkūṟiya kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭal (āṟaṉurupiṉkaṇṇum nāṉkaṉurupiṉkaṇṇum) illai.
u-m: tamatu, tamakku; namatu, namakku eṉavarum.
‘kūṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉē neṭumutal kuṟukāta moḻiyum kuṟuku moḻiyum iv viru vitiyum koḷka. ellār tamatum, ellār tamakkum eṉa varum.	
(neṭumutal kuṟukātamoḻikaḷ tam, nam, num eṉṉuñ cāriyai yiṭaiccoṟkaḷ. āṉ eṉpatu iṭaic col. 
a eṉpatu - acai.)	(19)
163.	numme ṉiṟutiyu mannilai tiriyātu.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) num eṉ iṟutiyum a nilai tiriyātu - num eṉṉum makaraviṟuti mēṟkūṟiya īṟāku puḷḷi akaramoṭu nilaiyalum kuṟṟoṟṟiraṭṭāmaiyumākiya annilaimaiyil tiriyātu e-ṟu.
u-m: numakku, numatu eṉa varum.	 (20)


164.	ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti
	yakaramu muyirum varuvaḻi yiyaṟkai.
itu, puḷḷimayaṅkiyalai nōkkiyatoru nilaimoḻic ceykai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi iṟuti - ukarap pēṟṟoṭu puṇarum puḷḷiyiṟutikaḷ, yakaramum uyirum varuvaḻi iyaṟkai - yakaramum uyirum varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu av vukaram peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
u-m: uriñ yāṉā, uriñ aṉantā; porun yāṉā, porun aṉantā; uriñ ātā, porun ātā eṉa oṭṭuka. (21)
165.	uyirum puḷḷiyu miṟuti yāki
	aḷavu niṟaiyu meṇṇuñ cuṭṭi
	uḷaveṉap paṭṭa vellāc collun
	tattaṅ kiḷavi tammakap paṭṭa
	muttai varūuṅ kālan tōṉṟiṉ
	otta teṉpa vēyeṉ cāriyai.
iḵtu, aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum eṇṇup peyarum tammiṟ puṇarumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
