(i-ḷ.) uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyāki aḷavum niṟaiyum eṇṇum cuṭṭi uḷa eṉappaṭṭa ellāc collum - uyirum puḷḷiyum īṟāy aḷavaiyum niṟaiyaiyum eṇṇaiyum karuti uḷaveṉṟu collappaṭṭa ellāc coṟkaḷum, tam tam kiḷavi tam akappaṭṭa muttaivarum kālam tōṉṟiṉ ē eṉ cāriyai ottatu eṉpa - tamakku iṉamākiya coṟkaḷāyt tamakku akappaṭum moḻiyāyuḷḷaṉa tam muṉṉē varum kālam tōṉṟumāyiṉ ē eṉṉuñ cāriyai peṟṟu muṭitalaip poruntiṟṟu eṉpa.
u-m:	uḻakkēyāḻākku, kalaṉēpatakku eṉa ivai aḷavup peyar; toṭiyēkaḵcu, koḷḷēaiyavi eṉa ivai niṟaip peyar; kāṇiyēmuntirikai, kālēkāṇi eṉa ivai eṇṇup peyar.
‘uyirīṟu puḷḷiyīṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, ē eṉ cāriyai peṟātaṉavum uḷaveṉṟu koḷka. kuṟuṇi nāṉāḻi eṉa varum.		  (22)
166.	araiyeṉa varūum pālvarai kiḷavikkup
	puraiva taṉṟāṟ cāriyai yiyaṟkai.
iḵtu, eytiyatu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) arai eṉa varum pālvarai kiḷavikkup puraivatu aṉṟu cāriyai iyaṟkai - arai eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa poruṭkūṟṟai yuṇara niṉṟa colliṟkup poruntuvataṉṟu mēṟkūṟiya ē eṉ cāriyai peṟum iyalpu e-ṟu.
u-m: uḻakkarai, toṭiyarai, oṉṟarai eṉa varum.
iḵtu ‘oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakku’ (puṇar. 30) aṉṟu eṉpataṉāṉ vilakkuṇṇātōveṉil, ‘tammakappaṭṭa’ eṉṟu varumoḻiyaiyum varaintōtiṉamaiyiṉ itaṟku av viti cellā teṉpatu. (āl eṉpatu acai)	  (23)
167.	kuṟaiyeṉ kiḷavi muṉvaru kālai
	niṟaiyat tōṉṟum vēṟṟumai yiyaṟkai.
iḵtu, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟai eṉ kiḷavi muṉ varu kālai - kuṟai eṉṉum col aḷavu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉmuṉ varuṅkālattu, niṟaiyat tōṉṟum vēṟṟumai iyaṟkai - niṟaiyat tōṉṟum mēl (236) vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarcci muṭipiṟkuc collum iyalpu.
u-m: urikkuṟai, kalakkuṟai; toṭikkuṟai, koṭkuṟai; kāṇikkuṟai, kāṟkuṟai eṉa varum.
‘muṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, poruṭpeyaroṭu puṇarumvaḻiyum vēṟṟumai muṭipu eytukaveṉpatu. kalappayaṟu eṉa varum.
‘niṟaiya’ eṉpataṉāṉ, kūṟu eṉpataṉkaṇṇum vēṟṟumai muṭipu eytumeṉpatu. nāḻikkūṟu eṉa varum.	 (24)
168.	kuṟṟiya lukarak kiṉṉē cāriyai.
itu, vēṟṟumai muṭipu vilakki iṉ vakuttamaiyiṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟku - kuṟṟukaravīṟṟu aḷavup peyar mutaliya vaṟṟiṟku, cāriyai iṉ - kuṟaiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻi varum cāriyai iṉ.
u-m: uḻakkiṉkuṟai, kaḻañciṉkuṟai, oṉṟiṉkuṟai eṉa varum.	
(`kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟku’  eṉpatu `kuṟṟiyalukarakku’ eṉa vikārappaṭṭu niṉṟatu. aḵtu ākupeyar, avvīṟṟu aḷavup peyar mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku āyiṉamaiyiṉ. ēkāram - acai.) 	(25)
169.	attiṭai varūuṅ kalameṉ ṉaḷavē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) kalam eṉ aḷavu iṭai attu varum - kalam eṉṉum aḷavup peyar (kuṟaiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻi) iṭaiyil attu varum.
u-m: kalattukkuṟai eṉa varum.
itu, ‘kalaṉ’ eṉṉum ṉakaravīṟēl, nilaimoḻi oṟṟukkēṭum, varumoḻi oṟṟuppēṟum ‘attē vaṟṟē’ (puṇariyal - 31) eṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭum. ‘kalaṉeṉ aḷavu’ eṉa ōtātatu ceyyuḷiṉpam nōkkip pōlum. cāriyai muṟkūṟiya vataṉāṉ, iṉcāriyai peṟṟavaḻi muṉ māṭṭēṟṟāṉ (167) eytiya valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	 (26)
170.	paṉaiye ṉaḷavuṅ kāve ṉiṟaiyum
	niṉaiyuṅ kālai yiṉṉoṭu civaṇum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉai eṉ aḷavum kā eṉ niṟaiyum - paṉai eṉṉum aḷavup peyarum kā eṉṉum niṟaip peyarum, niṉaiyum kālai iṉṉoṭu civaṇum - (kuṟai eṉpataṉoṭu puṇarumiṭattu) ārāyuṅ kālattu iṉcāriyaiyoṭu poruntum.
u-m: paṉaiyiṉ kuṟai, kāviṉkuṟai eṉa varum.
‘niṉaiyuṅ kālai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, valleḻuttup pēṟum ciṟu pāṉmai koḷka. (eḻut. 167) paṉaikkuṟai, kākkuṟai eṉa varum.	(27)
171.	aḷaviṟku niṟaiyiṟku moḻimuta lāki
	uḷaveṉap paṭṭa  voṉpatiṟ ṟeḻuttē
	avaitām
	kacatapa veṉṟā namava veṉṟā
	akara ukaramō ṭavaiyeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, aḷavuppeyarkkum niṟaippeyarkkum mutalām eḻuttukku varaiyaṟai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷaviṟkum niṟaiyiṟkum moḻimutalāki uḷa eṉappaṭṭa oṉpatiṟṟu eḻuttu - aḷavuppeyarkkum niṟaip peyarkkum moḻi mutalāy uḷaveṉṟu collappaṭṭaṉa oṉpateḻuttu, avaitām- avai (yāvaiyōveṉiṉ), ka ca ta pa eṉṟā na ma va eṉṟā akaramoṭu ukaramoṭu avai eṉa moḻipa - ka ca ta pa kkaḷum na ma va kkaḷum akaramum ukaramumākiya avai eṉṟu colluvar.
u-m:	kalam, cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai, nāḻi, maṇṭai, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; kaḻañcu, cīrakam, toṭi, palam, niṟai, mā, varai, antai eṉavum varum.
‘uḷaveṉap paṭṭa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, uḷaveṉap paṭātaṉavum aḷavai uḷaveṉpatu. immi, ōraṭai, ōrāṭai eṉa varum.
maṟṟu iv varaiyaṟai kūṟip payantatu eṉṉai yeṉiṉ, mēl akattōttiṉuḷ (390) avaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟum vaḻi, atikārattāṉ vaṉkaṇattiṉmēṟ cellātu, oḻinta kaṇattiṉum cēṟaṟku eṉpatu. (`akaramoṭu’ eṉpatu oṭu keṭṭu niṉṟatu. `eṉṟā’ eṉpaṉa iraṇṭum, `oṭu’ eṉpaṉa iraṇṭum - eṇṇiṭaiccol. ēkāram - īṟṟacai.)	 (28)
172.	īṟiyaṉ maruṅki ṉivaiyivaṟ ṟiyalpeṉak
	kūṟiya kiḷavip pallā ṟellām
	meyttalaip paṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi
	ottavai yuriya puṇarmoḻi nilaiyē.
iḵtu, iv vōttu, puṟaṉaṭai.
(i-ḷ.) īṟuiyal maruṅkiṉ - uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟuti yāṉavai varumoḻiyoṭu kūṭi naṭakkumiṭattu, ivai ivaṟṟu iyalpu eṉa kūṟiya kiḷavi pal āṟu ellām - im moḻikaḷiṉ muṭipu ivaiyeṉak kūṟi muṭikkappaṭṭa coṟkaḷiṉ (avvāṟṟāṉ muṭiyātu niṉṟa) palavakai muṭipukaḷellām, mey talaippaṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi ottavai uriya puṇar moḻinilai - uṇmaiyait talaippaṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu kūṭip poruntiṉavai uriyavām puṇarum moḻikaḷ nilaimaikkaṇ.
u-m: viḷa ñāṉṟatu eṉpuḻi nilaimoḻip peyaratu iyalpum, ‘ñāṉṟa ñāyiṟu’  eṉa nilai moḻi viṉaiyāya vaḻi irumoḻi iyalpumākiya ‘ña na ma ya va’ (tokai marapu 2) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, maṇ kaṭitu eṉpuḻi varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattu iyalpākiya ‘moḻimutalākum’ (tokai marapu 5) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, naṭañeḷḷā eṉṉum iyalpukaṇattu irumoḻi iyalpum, nilkoṟṟā, niṟkoṟṟā eṉa nilaimoḻi tirinta uṟaḻcciyum, tukkoṟṟā, tuññeḷḷā eṉṉum mikutiyum, uriñuñeḷḷā eṉṉum iyalpukaṇattu ukarap pēṟum, uriñyāṉā, uriñ aṉantā eṉṉum irumoḻi iyalpum, maṇṇukoṟṟā, maṇṇuk koṟṟā, ñeḷḷā eṉavarum ‘oḷaveṉa varūum’ (tokai. 10) eṉpataṉuḷ vilakkappaṭāta ṇakara ṉakara lakara ḷakara mākiya puḷḷiyiṟutikaḷiṉ nilaimoḻi ukarap pēṟumākiya “uyirī ṟākiya muṉṉilaik kiḷavi” (tokai. 9) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, viravuppeyart tiripiṉ mēl eṭuttu ōtappaṭātaṉavāya niṉñāṇ eṉṟāṟpōla varum. ‘aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyar’ (tokai. 13) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, kāvikkaṇ, kuvaḷaikkaṇ eṉṟāṟpōla alvaḻi muṭipākiya ‘vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi’ (tokai. 16) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, kuṟuṇi patakku nāṉāḻi, cīrakarai, orumāvarai eṉṉum “uyirum puḷḷiyu miṟuti yāki” (tokai. 22) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, piṟavaṟṟiṉ oḻipumellām īṇṭē koḷka.	(29)
173.	palaraṟi coṉmuṉ yāva reṉṉum
	peyariṭai vakaraṅ keṭutalu mēṉai
	oṉṟaṟi coṉmuṉ yāteṉ viṉāviṭai
	oṉṟiya vakaram varutalu miraṇṭum
	maruviṉ pāttiyiṟ ṟiriyumaṉ payiṉṟē.
itu, marūu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) palar aṟicolmuṉ yāvar eṉṉum peyariṭai vakaram keṭutalum - palarai aṟiyum colmuṉṉar varukiṉṟa yāvar eṉṉum peyariṭaiyil vakaram keṭutalum, ēṉai oṉṟu aṟi colmuṉ yātu eṉ viṉā iṭai oṉṟiya vakaram varutalum - oḻinta oṉṟaṉai aṟiyum colmuṉṉar varum yātu eṉṉum viṉāmoḻiyiṭai uyiroṭu poruntiya vakaram varutalum, iraṇṭum maruviṉ pāttiyiṉ payiṉṟu tiriyum - iraṇṭum marūukkaḷatu muṭipiṉiṭattup payiṉṟu vaḻaṅkum.
u-m: avar yār eṉavum, atu yāvatu eṉavum varum.
‘oṉṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, vakara uyirmey eṉṟu koḷka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, yāreṉpatum yāvateṉpatum nilai moḻiyāyp piṟa varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻiyum im muṭipu koḷka. ‘yāryārk kaṇṭē yuvappar’ eṉavum, “yāvatu  naṉṟeṉa vuṇarār māṭṭum” (kuṟun. 78) eṉavum varum.
‘payiṉṟu’ eṉṟataṉāl, palaraṟi collum oṉṟaṟi collum varumoḻiyāya vaḻiyum im muṭipu koḷḷappaṭum.  yāravar, yāvatatu eṉa varum.	 (30)
aintāvatu - tokaimarapu muṟṟiṟṟu.


urupiyal
vēṟṟumaiyurupukaḷ peyarkaḷōṭu poruntum muṟaimai yiṉai yuṇarttuvatu urupiyalākum. itaṉkaṇ muppatu cūttiraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. peyarum ataṉāl ēṟkappaṭum vēṟṟumaiyurupum ākiya avviraṇṭiṟkum iṭaiyē varum cāriyaikaḷ ivaiyeṉpatum, vēṟṟumaiyurupiṉai yēṟkuṅkāl peyarkaḷ peṟum iyalpum tiripumāvaṉa ivai yeṉpatum ivviyalil vakuttu viḷakkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
uyirkaḷuḷ a ā u ū ē oḷa eṉṉum āṟiṉaiyum īṟākavuṭaiya peyarkaḷum, meykaḷuḷ ñakara nakaravīṟṟup peyarkaḷum, tev eṉṉum vakaravīṟṟup peyarum, makaravīṟṟup peyarkaḷ cilavum, kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟup peyarkaḷum urupēṟ kuṅkāl iṭaiyē iṉcāriyai peṟuvaṉa.
paṉmaip poruḷaik karutiṉa akara vīṟṟup peyarkaḷum, yā eṉṉum ākāravīṟṟu viṉāppeyarum, avai, ivai, uvai, yāvai, av, iv, uv eṉpaṉavum vaṟṟuccāriyai peṟṟu urupēṟpaṉa. yāvai eṉṉum viṉāppeyar vaṟṟuc cāriyai peṟuṅkāl appeyariṉ īṟṟil niṉṟa aikāramum ataṉālūrappaṭṭa vakarameyyum keṭṭu muṭiyum. ellām eṉṉum potuppeyar aḵṟiṇaikkaṇvariṉ vaṟṟuc cāriyaiyum, uyar tiṇaikkaṇvariṉ nam cāriyaiyum peṟuvatuṭaṉ vēṟṟumai yurupiṉ iṟutiyil um cāriyaiyum peṟṟumuṭiyum.
atu, itu, utu eṉavarum ukaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyarum, ēḻ eṉṉum ḻakaravīṟṟu eṇṇuppeyarum, kuṟṟiyalukaravīṟṟuḷ varum ellā eṇṇuppeyarkaḷum, yātu eṉṉum viṉāp peyarum, aḵtu, iḵtu, uḵtu eṉavarum cuṭṭup peyarkaḷum aṉcāriyai peṟṟu urupēṟpaṉavām. aṉcāriyai peṟuṅkāl atu, itu, utu eṉpaṉa iṟutiyukaraṅ keṭṭu muṭivaṉa; aḵtu, iḵtu, uḵtu eṉpaṉa āytaṅkeṭṭu muṭivaṉa. paḵteṉpataṉai yiṟutiyākavuṭaiya orupatumutal eṇpatu varaiyuḷḷa eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ eṭṭum, aṉcāriyaiyēyaṉṟi āṉcāriyai peṟutalum uṇṭu. āṉcāriyai peṟuṅkāl nilaimoḻiyiluḷḷa paḵteṉpataṉ kaṇ pakarameymaṭṭum niṟka aḵteṉpatu keṭum.
ōkāra vīṟṟuppeyar urupēṟkuṅkāl oṉcāriyai peṟum.
akara ākāra vīṟṟu marappeyarkaḷ ēḻāmurupu peṟumvaḻi attuc cāriyaipeṟum. makaravīṟṟup peyarkaḷ urupēṟkuṅkāl attuccāriyai peṟuvaṉa. aḻaṉ, puḻaṉ eṉpaṉa attum iṉṉum peṟṟu uṟaḻvaṉa.
nī eṉṉum peyar niṉ eṉattirintu urupēṟkum. makara vīṟṟuḷ num eṉpatum tām, nām, yām eṉpaṉavum attum iṉṉum peṟātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉa. yām eṉṉum peyaril yakarattiṉmēlēṟiya ākāram ekaramākat tiriya aṅku niṉṟa yakaram keṭum. tām, nām eṉpaṉa mutalkuṟuki muṟaiyē tam nam eṉa niṟkum. ‘ellārum’ eṉṉum paṭarkkaic colliṭattum ‘ellīrum’ eṉṉum muṉṉilaic colliṭattum iṟutiyil niṉṟa um eṉpatu keṭa, avvirupeyarum muṟaiyē tam cāriyaiyum nam cāriyaiyum peṟṟu urupēṟkum. avaipeṟum urupiṉ piṉṉar um cāriyai vantu poruntum
 ṉakara vīṟṟuḷ tāṉ eṉpatu mutalkuṟukit taṉ eṉṟum yāṉ eṉpatu mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa yakaraṅkeṭṭu ataṉaiyūrntu niṉṟa ākāram ekaramāki eṉ eṉṟum tirintu urupēṟkum.
neṭiṟṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷuḷ (ṭaṟa) oṟṟi raṭṭikkuñ coṟkaḷ urupēṟkuṅkāl iṉcāriyaipeṟātu iyalpāvaṉa.
kuṟṟukaravīṟṟut ticaippeyarkaḷ kaṇṇurupiṉai ēṟkuṅ kāl iṉcāriyai peṟātu iyalpātalumuṇṭu. ivvāṟu iyalpāyavaḻi iṟutiyiluḷḷa kuṟṟiyalukaram tāṉ ūrntu niṉṟa meyyōṭu cērakkeṭum.
iṅku eṭutturaikkappaṭṭa peyarkaḷallāta ēṉaiya peyarkaḷ cāriyai peṟṟē urupēṟṟal vēṇṭumeṉṉum varaiyaṟaiyillātaṉa vām. avai cāriyai peṟṟum peṟātum muṭivaṉavām.
- ka. veḷḷaivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvaricai 10, pak. 176-178

āṟāvatu
urupiyal
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttō veṉiṉ, urupukaḷoṭu peyar puṇarum iyalpu uṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ, urupiyal eṉṉum peyarttu. mēl tokuttup puṇartta ceykai īṇṭu niṉṟum virittup puṇark kiṉṟārākaliṉ, tokaimarapiṉōṭu iyaipu uṭaittāyiṟṟu.
174.	aā uū ē oḷa veṉṉum
	appā lāṟa ṉilaimoḻi muṉṉar
	vēṟṟumai yurupiṟ kiṉṉē cāriyai.
it talaic cūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, akara ākāra ukara ūkāra ēkāra oḷakāra īṟukaḷ urupiṉoṭu puṇarumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) a ā u ū ē oḷa eṉṉum appāl āṟaṉ nilaimoḻi muṉṉar - a ā u ū ē oḷa eṉṟu collap paṭukiṉṟa ak kūṟṟu āṟaṉaiyum īṟākavuṭaiya nilaimoḻi kaḷiṉ muṉṉar, vēṟṟumai urupiṟku cāriyai iṉ - vēṟṟumai yurupukaḷ varumoḻiyāy vanta nilaimaikku iṭaivarum cāriyai iṉ cāriyai.
u-m:	viḷaviṉai, viḷaviṉoṭu, viḷaviṟku, viḷaviṉ, viḷaviṉatu, viḷaviṉkaṇ eṉavum; palāviṉai, palāvi ṉoṭu eṉavum; kaṭuviṉai, kaṭuviṉoṭu eṉavum; kaḻūuviṉai, kaḻūuviṉoṭu eṉavum; cēviṉai, cēviṉoṭu eṉavum; veḷaviṉai veḷaviṉoṭu eṉavum karuvi aṟintu oṭṭuka.	 (1)
175.	pallavai nutaliya vakara viṟupeyar
	vaṟṟoṭu civaṇa lecca miṉṟē.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya īṟṟuḷ akaraīṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) pallavai nutaliya peyar iṟu akaram - paṉmaip poruḷaik karutiṉa peyarkaḷiṉ iṟuti akaram, vaṟṟoṭu civaṇal eccam iṉṟu - vaṟṟuccāriyaiyoṭu poruntutalai oḻital illai.
u-m: pallavaṟṟai, pallavaṟṟoṭu; uḷḷavaṟṟai, uḷḷavaṟ ṟoṭu; illavaṟṟai, illavaṟṟoṭu; cillavaṟṟai, cillavaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘ecca miṉṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ īṇṭu mūṉṟām urupiṉkaṇ cāriyai peṟṟē muṭiyumeṉṟu koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē mēl iṉpeṟṟaṉa piṟacāriyaiyum peṟumeṉak koḷka. makattai, nilattai eṉa varum.	(2)
176.	yāveṉ viṉāvu māyiya ṟiriyātu.
iḵtu, ākāra vīṟṟuḷ orumoḻikku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) yā eṉ viṉāvum a iyal tiriyātu - yā eṉṟu collappaṭum ākāravīṟṟu viṉāp peyarum mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa vaṟṟup peṟum av viyalpiṉ tiriyātu e-ṟu.
u-m: yāvaṟṟai, yāvaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(3)
177.	cuṭṭumuta lukara maṉṉoṭu civaṇi
	oṭṭiya meyyoḻit tukaraṅ keṭumē.
iḵtu, ukaravīṟṟuṭ cila moḻikku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal ukaram aṉṉoṭu civaṇi - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya ukaravīṟṟuc col aṉcāriyaiyoṭu porunti, oṭṭiya mey oḻittu ukaram keṭum - tāṉ poruntiya meyyai oḻittu ukaram keṭum.
u-m:	ataṉai, ataṉoṭu; itaṉai, itaṉoṭu; utaṉai, utaṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(4)

178.	cuṭṭumuta lākiya vaiye ṉiṟuti
	vaṟṟoṭu civaṇi niṟṟalu murittē.
iḵtu, aikāravīṟṟuṭ cila moḻikku muṭipukūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutalākiya ai eṉ iṟuti - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya aikāravīṟṟuc col, vaṟṟoṭu civaṇi niṟṟalum urittu - vaṟṟuccāriyaiyoṭu porunti av vīṟṟu aikāram niṟṟalu murittu; (nillāmaiyu murittu.)
u-m: avaiyaṟṟai, avaiyaṟṟoṭu; ivaiyaṟṟai, ivaiyaṟ ṟoṭu; uvaiyaṟṟai, uvaiyaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
aikāram keṭṭavaḻi, niṉṟa vakarattiṉai vaṟṟiṉmicai oṟṟeṉṟu keṭuttu avaṟṟai, ivaṟṟai, uvaṟṟai eṉa oṭṭuka.
maṟṟu, im muṭipu cuṭṭumutal vakara vīṟṟōṭu ottamaiyiṉ, īṇṭu itu kūṟal mikaipaṭak kūṟalām piṟa eṉiṉ, aḵtu okkum; ivvāṟu kūṟuvaṉa mēlum uḷa; avaṟṟiṟkellām āciriyaṉ karuttu aṟintu koḷḷappaṭu meṉpatu.	(5)
179.	yāveṉ viṉāvi ṉaiye ṉiṟutiyum
	āyiya ṟiriyā teṉmaṉār pulavar
	āvayiṉ vakara maiyoṭuṅ keṭumē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) yā eṉ viṉāviṉ ai eṉ iṟutiyum - yā eṉṉum viṉāviṉaiyuṭaiya aikāravīṟṟuc collum, a iyal tiriyātu eṉmaṉār pulavar - mēṟkūṟiya cuṭṭumutal aikāram pōla vaṟṟup peṟum iyalpiṉ tiriyāteṉṟu colluvar āciriyar; a vayiṉ vakaram aiyoṭum keṭum - avviṭattu vakaram aikārattoṭukūṭak keṭum.
u-m: yāvaṟṟai, yāvaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
vakaram vaṟṟiṉmicai oṟṟeṉṟu (134) keṭuvataṉai, īṇṭuk kēṭōtiyavataṉāl piṟa aikāramum vaṟṟuppeṟutal koḷka.
e-ṭu: kariyavaṟṟai, ceyyavaṟṟai eṉa varum.	(6)
180.	nīye ṉorupeyar neṭumutal kuṟukum
	āvayi ṉakara moṟṟā kummē.
iḵtu, īkāravīṟṟuḷ orumoḻikku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nī eṉ oru peyar neṭumutal kuṟukum - nī eṉṉum oru peyar neṭitākiya mutal kuṟukum; a vayiṉ ṉakaram oṟṟākum - avviṭattu varum ṉakaram oṟṟākum.
u-m: niṉṉai, niṉṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘orupeyar’ eṉṟatu, niṉ eṉpatum vēṟoru peyar pōṟalai vilakkiṟṟu. ‘peyarkuṟukum’ eṉṉātu ‘mutalkuṟukum’ eṉṟatu, ap peyariṉ neṭṭeḻuttu nilaiyatu ak kuṟukka meṉṟaṟ keṉpatu. ‘neṭumutal’ eṉṟatu am moḻimutaliṉ nakaram kuṟukutalai vilakkiṟṟu. īṇṭu, uyirmey yoṟṟumai paṟṟi neṭiyatu mutalāyiṟṟu. cāriyaippēṟṟiṭai eḻuttup pēṟu kūṟiyatu, mūṉṟām urupiṉ kaṇ cāriyai peṟṟē vanta atikāram māṟṟi niṉṟatu.	(7)
181.	ōkāra viṟutik koṉṉē cāriyai.
iḵtu, ōkāra īṟu muṭiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ōkāra iṟutikkuc cāriyai oṉ - ōkāravīṟṟukku iṭaivarum cāriyai oṉcāriyai e-ṟu.
u-m: kōoṉai, kōoṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(8)
182.	a ā veṉṉu marappeyark kiḷavik
	kattoṭuñ civaṇu mēḻa ṉurupē.
iḵtu, akara ākāravīṟṟuṭ cila moḻikku, urupiṉkaṇ eytiyataṉmēl ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) a ā eṉṉum marappeyark kiḷavikku - a ā eṉṟu collappaṭum marattai uṇaraniṉṟa peyarākiya coṟku, attoṭum civaṇum ēḻaṉ urupu - muṉkūṟiya iṉṉōṭaṉṟi (174) attōṭum poruntum ēḻām urupu.
u-m: viḷavattukkaṇ, palāvattukkaṇ eṉa varum.	(9)
183. ña na veṉ puḷḷik kiṉṉē cāriyai.
itu, puḷḷiyīṟṟu ñakāravīṟum nakāravīṟum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ña na eṉ puḷḷikku iṉṉē cāriyai - ña na eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa puḷḷiyīṟukaṭku varum cāriyai iṉcāriyai.
u-m:uriñiṉai, uriñiṉoṭu; poruniṉai, poruni ṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. (ēkāram acai.)	           (10)
184.	cuṭṭumutal vakara maiyu meyyum
	keṭṭa viṟuti yiyaṟṟiri piṉṟē.
itu, vakaravīṟu nāṉkaṉuḷḷum cuṭṭumutal vakaravīṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭumutal vakaram - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalāka vuṭaiya vakaravīṟṟuccol, aiyum meyyum keṭṭa iṟuti iyal tiripu iṉṟu - aikāramum ataṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa meyyum keṭṭu vaṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭinta cuṭṭumutal aikāravīṟṟiyalpiṉ tiripiṉṟi vaṟṟup peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m:	avaṟṟai, avaṟṟoṭu; ivaṟṟai, ivaṟṟoṭu; uvaṟṟai, uvaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(11)
185.	ēṉai vakara miṉṉoṭu civaṇum.
itu, vakaravīṟṟuḷ oḻinta vakaravīṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉai vakaram iṉṉoṭu civaṇum - oḻinta vakara vīṟu iṉcāriyaiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum.
u-m: tevviṉai, tevviṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
maṟṟu itu uriccol laṉṟōveṉiṉ, uriccollē yeṉiṉum paṭuttalōcaiyāṟ peyarāyiṟṟeṉak koḷka.  (12)
186.	maḵkāṉ puḷḷimu ṉattē cāriyai.
itu, makaraīṟu muṭiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ attu cāriyai - makaramākiya puḷḷiyīṟṟuc colmuṉ varum cāriyai attuc cāriyai.
u-m: marattai, marattoṭu eṉa varum.	(13)
187.	iṉṉiṭai varūu moḻiyumā ruḷavē.
itu, makaravīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṉ iṭaivarum moḻiyum uḷa - makaravīṟu attuccāriyai yoḻiya iṉcāriyai iṭaivantu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa.
u-m:  urumiṉai, urumiṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
(ār eṉpatu acai. ēkāram īṟṟacai.)	(14)
188.	numme ṉiṟuti yiyaṟkai yākum.
itu, makaravīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) num eṉ iṟuti iyaṟkai ākum - num eṉṉum makaravīṟu mēṟkūṟiya attum iṉṉum peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum .
u-m: nummai, nummoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(15)
 189.	tāmnā meṉṉu makara viṟutiyum
	yāme ṉiṟutiyu mataṉō raṉṉa
	ā e ākum yāme ṉiṟuti
	āvayiṉ yakaramey keṭutal vēṇṭum
	ēṉai yiraṇṭu neṭumutal kuṟukum.
itu, makaravīṟṟuḷ muṉkūṟiya muṭipu ovvātaṉavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tām nām eṉṉum makara iṟutiyum yām eṉ iṟutiyum ataṉ ōr aṉṉa - tām nām eṉṟu collappaṭum makaravīṟum yām eṉṉum makaravīṟum mēṟkūṟiya num eṉṉum makaravīṟupōla attum iṉṉum peṟātu muṭitalai yuṭaiyavām; yām eṉ iṟuti ā e ākum - yām eṉṉum makaravīṟṟu moḻi ākāram ekāramām; a vayiṉ yakaramey keṭutal vēṇṭum - avviṭattu yakaramākiya mey keṭutal vēṇṭum; ēṉai iraṇṭum neṭumutal kuṟukum - oḻinta iraṇṭum neṭiyavākiya mutal kuṟukiniṉṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: tammai, tammoṭu; nammai, nammoṭu; emmai, emmoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘āvayiṉ mey’ eṉṟataṉāl, piṟavayiṉ meyyum keṭumeṉak koḷka. taṅkaṇ, naṅkaṇ, eṅkaṇ eṉa varum.  (16)
190.	ellā meṉṉu miṟuti muṉṉar
	vaṟṟeṉ cāriyai muṟṟat tōṉṟum
	ummai nilaiyu miṟuti yāṉa.
itu, makaravīṟṟuḷ orumoḻikku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellām eṉṉum iṟutimuṉṉar - ellām eṉṉum makaravīṟṟuc colliṉmuṉpu, vaṟṟu eṉ cāriyai muṟṟat tōṉṟum - mēṟkūṟiya attum (186) iṉṉumiṉṟi (187) vaṟṟu eṉṉum cāriyai muṭiyattōṉṟi muṭiyum, ummai nilaiyum iṟutiyāṉa - um eṉṉum cāriyai nilaipeṟum iṟutikkaṇ.
u-m:	ellāvaṟṟaiyum, ellāvaṟṟoṭum eṉa oṭṭuka. 
‘muṟṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, mūṉṟām urupiṉkaṇṇum nāṉkām urupiṉkaṇṇum āṟām urupiṉkaṇṇum ummiṉ ukarakkēṭu koḷka. iṟutiyiṉ eṉpatu vēṟṟumai mayakkam. akaram cāriyai)	 (17)
191. uyartiṇai yāyi ṉammiṭai varumē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) uyartiṇaiyāyiṉ nam iṭaivarum - ellām eṉṉum viravuppeyar aḵṟiṇaip peyaraṉṟi uyartiṇaip peyarāy niṟkumāyiṉ, nam iṭaivarum - nam eṉṉum cāriyai iṭaivantu puṇarum.
nilaimoḻiyoṟṟum mēl ‘vaṟṟumicai yoṟṟu’ eṉṟu keṭṭuniṉṟa atikārattāṟ keṭukka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē ummup peṟutalum, ataṉkaṇ ukaram cila urupiṉkaṇ keṭutalum koḷka.
u-m: ellānammaiyum, ellānammoṭum eṉa oṭṭuka. (18)
192.	ellāru meṉṉum paṭarkkai yiṟutiyum
	ellīru meṉṉu muṉṉilai yiṟutiyum
	oṟṟu mukaramuṅ keṭumeṉa moḻipa
	niṟṟal vēṇṭum rakarap puḷḷi
	ummai nilaiyu miṟuti yāṉa
	tammiṭai varūum paṭarkkai mēṉa
	nummiṭai varūu muṉṉilai moḻikkē.
itu, makara vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellārum eṉṉum paṭarkkai iṟutiyum ellīrum eṉṉum muṉṉilai iṟutiyum - ellārum eṉṟu collap paṭum paṭarkkaiyiṭattu makaravīṟṟuc collum ellīrum eṉṟu collap paṭum muṉṉilaiyiṭattu makaravīṟṟuc collum, oṟṟum ukaramum keṭum eṉa moḻipa - ivaṟṟiṉ makaravoṟṟum ataṉmuṉ niṉṟa ukaramum keṭṭu muṭiyu meṉṟu colluvar, rakarappuḷḷi niṟṟal vēṇṭum - av vukaram keṭuvaḻi ataṉāl ūrappaṭṭa rakarappuḷḷi keṭātu niṟṟal vēṇṭum, iṟutiyāṉa ummai nilaiyum - av viru moḻikkum iṟutikkaṇ um eṉṉum cāriyai nilaipeṟum, paṭarkkai mēṉa tam iṭai varum - paṭarkkaiyiṭattut tammuc cāriyai iṭai varum, muṉṉilai moḻikku num iṭaivarum - muṉṉilai moḻikku nummuccāriyai iṭaivarum.
u-m:	ellār tammaiyum, ellārtammoṭum; ellīr nummaiyum, ellīrnummoṭum eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘paṭarkkai’, ‘muṉṉilai’ eṉṟa mikutiyāṉ, makaravīṟṟut taṉmaip peyariṭaikkaṇ nammuccāriyaiyum, īṟṟukkaṇ ummuc cāriyaiyum peṟṟumuṭivaṉa koḷka. kariyēnammaiyum, kariyēnammoṭum eṉa oṭṭuka.
paṭarkkaippeyar muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl, rakāravīṟṟup paṭarkkaip peyarum muṉṉilaip peyarum iṟutikkaṇ ummoṭu tammuc cāriyaiyum nummuccāriyaiyum (iṭaiyē) peṟṟu muṭivaṉa koḷka. kariyārtammaiyum, cāṉṟīrnummaiyum eṉa oṭṭuka. ukaramum oṟṟum eṉṉāta muṟaiyaṉṟiya kūṟṟiṉāṉ, am mūṉṟu urupiṉkaṇ (eḻut. 190) ummiṉ ukarakkēṭu eṭuttōtiyavaṟṟiṟkum ilēciṉāṟ koṇṭa vaṟṟiṟkum koḷka. (mēl eṉpatu mēṉ eṉat tirintu akaraccāriyai ēṟṟuniṉṟatu.)	(19)
193.	tāṉyā ṉeṉṉu māyī riṟutiyum
	mēṉmup peyaroṭum vēṟupā ṭilavē.
itu, ṉakara vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tāṉ yāṉ eṉṉum a iru iṟutiyum - tāṉ yāṉ eṉṟu collappaṭum avviraṇṭu ṉakara vīṟum, mēl muppeya roṭum vēṟupāṭu ila  mēl makaravīṟṟuṭ collappaṭṭa (189) mūṉṟu peyaroṭum vēṟupāṭiṉṟit tāṉeṉpatu neṭumutal kuṟukiyum yāṉ eṉpataṉkaṇ ākāram ekāramāy yakaraṅkeṭṭum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
u-m: taṉṉai, taṉṉoṭu; eṉṉai, eṉṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
(`iru’ eṉpatu taṉmuṉṉar uyirvantamaiyāl `īr’ eṉṟāyiṟṟu. akarac cuṭṭu nīṇṭu yakara uṭumpaṭumey peṟṟatu. ēkāram īṟṟacai.)	(20)
194.	aḻaṉē puḻaṉē yāyiru moḻikkum
	attu miṉṉu muṟaḻat tōṉṟal
	otta teṉpa uṇaru mōrē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aḻaṉ puḻaṉ a iru moḻikkum - aḻaṉ puḻaṉākiya av viru moḻikkum, attum iṉṉum uṟaḻat tōṉṟal ottatu eṉpa uṇarumōr - attuccāriyaiyum iṉcāriyaiyum māṟivarat tōṉṟu talaip poruntiṟṟeṉpa uṇaruvōr.
u-m:	aḻattai, aḻattoṭu; aḻaṉiṉai, aḻaṉiṉoṭu;  puḻattai, puḻattoṭu; puḻaṉiṉai, puḻaṉiṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘tōṉṟal’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, evaṉ eṉṟum, eṉ eṉṟum niṟutti, vaṟṟukkoṭuttu vēṇṭum ceykaiceytu evaṟṟai, evaṟṟoṭu eṉavum, eṟṟai, eṟṟoṭu eṉavum muṭikka.
‘ottatu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ekiṉ eṉa niṟutti, attum iṉṉum koṭuttuc ceykai ceytu ekiṉattai, ekiṉattoṭu; ekiṉiṉai, ekiṉiṉoṭu eṉavum muṭikka.
attu muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, attup peṟṟavaḻi iṉitu icaikku meṉak koḷka.	
(aḻaṉ - piṇam. muṉ ēkāram iraṇṭum eṇṇiṭaic col. piṉ ēkāram īṟṟacai. `uṇarumōr’ eṉpatu tollaivaḻakku.)	 (21)
195.	aṉṉeṉ cāriyai yēḻa ṉiṟuti
	muṉṉart tōṉṟum miyaṟkait teṉpa.
itu, ḻakaravīṟṟu orumoḻikku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aṉ eṉ cāriyai ēḻaṉ iṟuti muṉṉart tōṉṟum iyaṟkaittu eṉpa - aṉ eṉṉum cāriyai ēḻeṉṉum colliṟutiyiṉ muṉṉē tōṉṟum iyalpiṉai yuṭaitteṉṟu colluvar e-ṟu.
u-m: ēḻaṉai, ēḻaṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
cāriyai muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, piṟavum aṉ peṟuvaṉa koḷka. pūḻaṉai, pūḻaṉoṭu; yāḻaṉai, yāḻaṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	 (22)
196.	kuṟṟiya lukarat tiṟuti muṉṉar
	muṟṟat tōṉṟu miṉṉeṉ cāriyai.
itu, kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalukarattu iṟutimuṉṉar muṟṟat tōṉṟum iṉ eṉ cāriyai - kuṟṟiyalukaramākiya īṟṟiṉ muṉṉar muṭiyat tōṉṟum iṉ eṉ cāriyai.
u-m: varakiṉai, varakiṉoṭu; nākiṉai, nākiṉoṭu eṉa  oṭṭuka.
‘muṟṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟa cāriyai peṟuvaṉavum koḷka. vaḻakkattāṟ pāṭṭārāyntāṉ, kariyataṉai, kariyataṉoṭu eṉa varum.	(23)
197.	neṭṭeḻut timpa roṟṟumikat tōṉṟum
	appāṉ moḻika ḷalvaḻi yāṉa.
iḵtu, avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭṭeḻuttu impar oṟṟu mikat tōṉṟum - neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉāka (iṭai) iṉavoṟṟu mikat tōṉṟum, appāl moḻikaḷ avvaḻiyāṉa - ak kūṟṟu moḻikaḷ allāta iṭattiṉ kaṇṇē.
av vīṟu iṉcāriyai peṟuvatu; āṇṭāyiṉ peṟātu.
u-m:	yāṭṭai, yāṭṭoṭu, yāṟṟai, yāṟṟoṭu eṉa varum.
appāṉ moḻikaḷāvaṉa: ka ca ta pa kkaḷ. ivai iṉavoṟṟu mikāveṉṟu koḷka.
‘tōṉṟum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, uyirttoṭar moḻiyum iṉpeṟātu iṉavoṟṟu mikutal koḷka. pakaṭṭai, pakaṭṭoṭu; muyiṟṟai, muyiṟṟoṭu eṉa varum.	(24)
198.	avaitām
	iyaṟkaiya vākuñ ceyaṟkaiya veṉpa.
itu, mēṟ cāriyai vilakkappaṭṭavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaitām - mēṟ cāriyai peṟāveṉa vilakkap paṭṭa avaitām, iyaṟkaiya ākum ceyaṟkaiya eṉpa - iyalpāy muṭitalai yuṭaiyavākum ceytiyaiyuṭaiya veṉṟu colluvar.
u-m: yāṭṭai, yāṭṭoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘ceyaṟkaiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉavoṟṟu mikkaṉa ciṟupāṉmai iṉpeṟutalum koḷka. yāṭṭiṉai, yāṭṭi ṉoṭu; muyiṟṟiṉai, muyiṟṟiṉoṭu eṉa varum.	 (25)
199.	eṇṇi ṉiṟuti yaṉṉoṭu civaṇum.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟu eṇṇuppeyar muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) eṇṇiṉ iṟuti aṉṉoṭu civaṇum - eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉatu kuṟṟukara vīṟu aṉcāriyaiyoṭu poruntum.
u-m:	oṉṟaṉai, oṉṟaṉoṭu; iraṇṭaṉai, iraṇṭa ṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(26)
200.	oṉṟu mutalākap pattūrntu varūum
	ellā veṇṇuñ colluṅ kālai
	āṉiṭai variṉu māṉa millai
	aḵteṉ kiḷavi yāvayiṟ keṭumē
	uytal vēṇṭum paḵkāṉ meyyē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉṟu mutalākap pattu ūrntu varum ellā eṇṇum - oṉṟu eṉṉum eṇṇup peyar mutalākap pattu eṉṉum eṇṇup peyarāṉ ūrappaṭṭu varum ellā eṇṇup peyarkaḷum, colluṅ kālai - muṭipu colluṅ kālattu, āṉ iṭaivariṉum māṉam illai - av vīṟṟiṟku mēṟkūṟiya aṉṉēyaṉṟi āṉcāriyai iṭaivariṉum kuṟṟamillai, a vayiṉ aḵtu eṉ kiḷavi keṭum - av vāṉcāriyai peṟṟavaḻi aḵtu eṉṉum col keṭum, paḵkāṉmey uytal vēṇṭum - atu keṭuvaḻi av vakarattāṉ ūrappaṭṭa pakaramākiya mey keṭātu niṟṟalai vēṇṭum.
u-m: orupāṉai, orupāṉoṭu; orupaḵtaṉai, orupaḵtaṉoṭu; irupaḵtaṉai, irupaḵtaṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘colluṅ kālai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, oṉpateṉṉum eṇṇup peyarum āṉ peṟṟu, avvīṟṟiṉ atu eṉṉum coṟkeṭṭu muṭital koḷka. oṉpāṉai, oṉpāṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(27)
201.	yāte ṉiṟutiyuñ cuṭṭumuta lākiya
	āyta viṟutiyu maṉṉoṭu civaṇum
	āytaṅ keṭuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.
ituvum, ak kuṟṟukara vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) yātu eṉ iṟutiyum - yātu eṉa varum kuṟṟukara īṟum, cuṭṭu mutalākiya āyta iṟutiyum - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya āytat toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukara īṟum, aṉṉoṭu civaṇum - aṉcāriyaiyoṭu poruntum, ā vayiṉāṉa āytam keṭutal - avviṭattu āytam keṭuka.
u-m: yātaṉai, yātaṉoṭu; ataṉai, ataṉoṭu; itaṉai, itaṉoṭu; utaṉai, utaṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.  
(akaraccuṭṭu nīṇṭu niṉṟatu. `āṉ’ eṉpatu - vēṟṟumai mayakkam. īṟṟakaram - cāriyai.)	    (28)
202.	ēḻa ṉurupiṟkut ticaippeyar muṉṉarc
	cāriyaik kiḷavi yiyaṟkaiyu mākum
	āvayi ṉiṟuti meyyoṭuṅ keṭumē.
ituvum kuṟṟukaravīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku ēḻaṉ urupoṭu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ēḻaṉ urupiṟku - ēḻām vēṟṟumaikku, ticaip peyar muṉṉar - ticaiyai uṇaraniṉṟa peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, cāriyaikkiḷavi iyaṟkaiyum ākum - iv vīṟṟiṟku muṉkūṟiya iṉcāriyaiyākiya col niṉṟu muṭitalēyaṉṟi nillātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum; a vayiṉ iṟuti meyyoṭum keṭum - iyalpāyavaḻip peyar iṟutik kuṟṟukaram taṉṉāṉ ūrappaṭṭa meyyoṭum keṭum.
u-m: vaṭakkiṉkaṇ, kiḻakkiṉkaṇ, teṟkiṉkaṇ, mēṟkiṉkaṇ eṉavum; vaṭakkaṇ, kiḻakkaṇ, teṟkaṇ, mēṟkaṇ eṉavum varum.
urupu muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, kīḻcār, kīḻpuṭai; mēlcār, mēlpuṭai; teṉcār, teṉpuṭai; vaṭacār, vaṭapuṭai eṉa ivvāṟu cāriyai peṟātu tirintu muṭivaṉavellām koḷka.	 (akaraccuṭṭu nīṇṭu niṉṟatu. ēkāram - īṟṟacai.)	(29)
203.	puḷḷi yiṟutiyu muyiriṟu kiḷaviyum
	colliya valla vēṉaiya vellām
	tēruṅ kālai yurupoṭu civaṇic
	cāriyai nilaiyuṅ kaṭappā ṭilavē.
iḵtu, iv vōttiṟkellām puṟaṉaṭai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) puḷḷi iṟutiyum uyir iṟu kiḷaviyum colliya alla ēṉaiya ellām - puḷḷiyīṟṟuc collum uyirīṟṟuc collumeṉa muṭipu colliyavai allāta oḻintavai yellām, tērum kālai - ārāyuṅkālattu, urupoṭu civaṇi cāriyai nilaiyum kaṭappāṭu ila - urupukaḷoṭu porunti iṉ cāriyai niṉṟu muṭiyum muṟaimaiyai yuṭaiya valla (niṉṟum nillātum muṭiyum).
puḷḷiyīṟṟuḷ oḻintaṉa aintu; uyirīṟṟuḷ oḻintatu oṉṟu. ivaiyum eṭuttōtiya īṟṟuḷ oḻintaṉavumellām īṇṭuk koḷḷappaṭum.
u-m: maṇṇiṉai, maṇṇai; vēyiṉai, vēyai; nāriṉai, nārai; kalliṉai, kallai;  muḷḷiṉai, muḷḷai   eṉavum; kiḷiyiṉai, kiḷiyai eṉavum; poṉṉiṉai, poṉṉai   eṉavum; tāḻiṉai, tāḻai; tīyiṉai, tīyai; kaḻaiyiṉai, kaḻaiyai eṉavum  oṭṭuka.
puḷḷiyīṟṟuḷ oḻintaṉa palavākaliṉ, atu muṟkūṟap paṭṭatu. ‘tēruṅ kālai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, urupukaḷ nilaimoḻi yāka niṉṟu tam poruḷoṭu puṇarumvaḻi muṭiyum muṭipu vēṟṟumai yellām koḷka.
maṇṇiṉaik koṇarntāṉ, nampiyaik koṇarntāṉ, koṟṟaṉaik koṇarntāṉ; malaiyoṭu porutatu mālyāṉai; marattāṟ puṭaittāṉ; marattukkup pōṉāṉ; kākkaiyiṟ karitu; kākkaiyatu pali; marattukkaṇ kaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉa oṭṭuka.
iḵtu urupiyalākaliṉ, ‘urupoṭu civaṇi’ eṉa vēṇṭāvaṉṟē, ataṉāṉ, urupupuṇarccikkaṇ ceṉṟa cāriyaikaḷellām īṟṟup potumuṭipu uḷvaḻip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum cellu meṉpatu koḷka.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, uyirīṟum meyyīṟum cāriyai peṟātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉa koḷka. nampiyai, koṟṟaṉai eṉa varum.	          (30)
āṟāvatu - urupiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.


uyir mayaṅkiyal
uyirīṟu niṉṟu valleḻuttōṭum ciṟupāṉmai ēṉai yeḻuttukkaḷōṭum puṇarumāṟu kūṟuvatu uyir mayaṅkiya lākum. mayaṅkutal - kalattal. uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyākiya uyartiṇaip peyarkaḷ alvaḻi vēṟṟumaiyākiya iru vaḻiyilum iyalpāmeṉavum, oru cila viṭaṅkaḷil aḵṟiṇai viravup peyar iyalpātalu muṇṭeṉavum tokai marapil vitantu kūṟiya āciriyar, avaiyoḻinta uyirīṟṟu aḵṟiṇaip peyarkaḷaiyum ēṉai viṉaimuṟṟu viṉaiyeccam peyarec caṅkaḷaiyum akaravīṟumutal oḷakāravīṟumuṭiya neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟaiyil vaittu uṇarttukiṉṟār. ivviyalil 93-cūttiraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ pala māṭṭēṟṟu muṟaiyil amaintaṉavākum. ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭa vitikaḷ yāvum valleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, iyalpāvaṉa, melleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, uyirmika varuvaṉa, nīṭavaruvaṉa kuṟukivaruvaṉa, cāriyaipeṟuvaṉa, piṟavāṟu tirivaṉa eṉṉum ivvakaiyuḷ aṭaṅkuvaṉavākum.
a, i, u. eṉṉum mūṉṟu cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉum uyirum yakaramumvariṉ vakaravoṟṟum kacatapañanamava eṉpaṉavariṉ vanta oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷum mikumeṉpatum ceyyuḷuḷ cuṭṭu nīṇṭu muṭiyumeṉpatum 3-6, 34, 35, 53-ām cūttiraṅkaḷiṟ collap paṭṭaṉa.
ā, ā, ī, u, ū, ē, ō, ōḷa eṉpavaṟṟai īṟākavuṭaiya peyarkaḷ alvaḻi vēṟṟumaiyākiya īriṭattum valleḻuttu mikappeṟumeṉṟum, ikara aikāra vīṟṟup peyarkaḷ vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ valleḻuttu mikappeṟu meṉṟum 1, 14, 19, 22, 33, 47, 57, 62, 64, 72, 74, 78, 87, 90, 93-ām, cūttiraṅkaḷil āciriyar virittukkūṟiyuḷḷār.
alvaḻikkaṇ akaravīṟṟuḷ aṉṉaveṉṉum uvamavurupum, aṇmaicuṭṭiya uyartiṇai viḷippeyarum, ceymmaṉa eṉṉum viṉaiccollum, ēvalaik karutiya viyaṅkōḷ viṉaiyum, ceyta eṉṉum vāypāṭṭup peyareccamum, ceyyiya eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉaiyeccamum, amma eṉṉum uraiyacaiyum, akara vīṟṟup palavaṟicollum, ākāravīṟṟuḷ ā, mā eṉṉum peyarc collum, uyartiṇai viḷippeyarum, yā viṉāvum muṟṟuviṉaiyum, miyā eṉṉum acaic collum, taṉ toḻilaik kuṟittuvarum ākāra viṉāvaiyuṭaiya viṉaiccollum, īkāra vīṟṟuḷ nī, mī eṉpaṉavum pakaravīkāramum, ukaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭup peyarkaḷum, tēṟṟap poruḷil varum ekaravīṟum, ciṟappup poruḷil varum okaravīṟum, māṟukōḷeccap poruḷilum viṉāp poruṇmaiyilum eṇṇupporuṇmaiyilum aiyap poruṇmaiyilum varum ēkāra ōkāra vīṟṟiṭaic coṟkaḷum valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāvaṉa.
akaravīṟṟu marappeyarum ā, i, u, ē, ai ākiya īṟukaḷil eṭuttōtiya piṭā, taḷā, mā (maram) ā, mā, (vilaṅku) uti, oṭu, cē, vicai, ñemai, namai eṉṉum peyarkaḷum valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻivariṉ melleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavām. yā, piṭā, taḷā, puḷi (cuvai) eṉpaṉa valleḻuttup peṟutalumuṇṭu.
ummaittokaikkaṇvarum ākāravīṟṟuppeyarum vēṟṟumaik kaṇ kuṟiṟkīḻ niṉṟa ākāravīṟṟuppeyarum ōreḻuttoru moḻiyākiya ākāra vīṟṟuppeyarum akaram mikku muṭiyum. irā eṉṉuñ collukku akaramikutal illai. kuṟiṟkīḻ niṉṟa ūkāravīṟum ōreḻuttuttoru moḻiyākiya ūkāra vīṟum vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ukaram mikkumuṭiyum. ē yeṉṉum peyar vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ekaram mikku muṭiyum. ōkāravīṟu vēṟṟumaik kaṇ okaram mikum. oḷakāravīṟṟup peyar iruvaḻiyilum ukarammikum.
ceyyuṭkaṇvarum akaraccuṭṭum, amma eṉṉum iṭaic colliṟutiyum, pala cila eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiṉ iṟuti akaramum, ḻakarameyyaiyūrnta iṟuti ukaramum nīṇṭu muṭivaṉavām.
ā muṉvarum pakara vīkāram kuṟukum. kuṟiṟkīḻ niṉṟa iṟuti ākāram kuṟuki ukaram peṟum.
maka, āṭūu, makaṭūu cē eṉpaṉa iṉcāriyaipeṟum. nilā attuccāriyaipeṟum. paṉi (kālam), vaḷi (pūtam), maḻai eṉpaṉa attuccāriyaiyum iṉcāriyaiyum peṟuvaṉa. puḷi, eru, ceru, paṉai, arai, āvirai eṉpaṉa amcāriyaipeṟuvaṉa. ikara aikāra vīṟṟu nāṭ peyarmuṉ toḻiṟcolvariṉ iṭaiyē āṉcāriyai varum. avviraṇṭīṟṟut tiṅkaṭ peyar muṉ toḻiṟcolvariṉ iṭaiyē ikkuccāriyai varum. ūveṉṉum ōreḻuttorumoḻi ṉakaravoṟṟum akkuc cāriyaiyum peṟum.
a, ī, u, ai, ō eṉṉum īṟukaḷukku urupiyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭa cāriyaikaḷai ivviyaliluḷḷa 18, 51, 61, 79, 92-ām cūttiraṅkaḷāl avvīṟṟup poruṭ puṇarccikkum māṭṭeṟintu viti kūṟuvar āciriyar.
‘cāva’ eṉpataṉ vakaravuyirmeyyum ‘vāḻiya’ eṉpataṉ yakaravuyirmeyyum keṭṭu muṭiyum. nāḻi eṉpataṉmuṉ uri yeṉṉuñcol vantupuṇariṉ nilaimoḻiyīṟṟiṉ ikaram tāṉ ēṟiya ḻakara meyyuṭaṉkeṭa avviṭattu ṭakaramey tōṉṟi muṭiyum. ceru eṉpataṉ muṉvarum amcāriyaiyiṉ makaraṅkeṭa varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum. paṉai, āvirai eṉpaṉa amcāriyai peṟuṅkāl iṟuti aikāraṅkeṭṭu muṭivaṉa. paṉai eṉṉuñcolmuṉ aṭṭu eṉpatu varumoḻiyāyvariṉ nilai moḻiyiṟuti aikāraṅkeṭa avviṭattu ākāram tōṉṟum. pala cila eṉpavai tam muṉṉart tāmvariṉ iṟuti niṉṟa lakara vuyirmey lakaravoṟṟākat tiriyum. iṉṟiyeṉpataṉ iṟutiyikaram ceyyuḷuḷ ukaramākattiriyum. cuṭṭup peyarīṟṟu ukaram aṉṟu eṉpataṉōṭu puṇaruṅkāl ākāramākat tiritalum aiyeṉpataṉōṭu puṇaruṅkāl keṭutalum ceyyuḷiṭat tuṇ ṭākun tiripukaḷām. vēṭkai yeṉṉuñcolmuṉ avā eṉpatu vantu puṇariṉ nilaimoḻiyīṟṟiluḷḷa aikāram tāṉ ūrntu niṉṟa kakarameyyoṭuṅkeṭa aṅkuḷḷa ṭakaram ṇakara māyt tirital ceyyuṭkaṇvarun tiripākum.
- ka.veḷḷaivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvaricai 10, pak. 178-181
ēḻāvatu
uyirmayaṅkiyal
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttōveṉiṉ, uyirīṟu vaṉ kaṇattoṭum ciṟupāṉmai piṟa kaṇattoṭum mayaṅkip puṇarum iyalpu uṇarttaliṉ uyirmayaṅkiyal eṉṉum peyarttu. mēl urupu puṇarcci kūṟi īṇṭup poruṭpuṇarcci kūṟukiṉṟamaiyiṉ, mēlata ṉōṭu iyaipuṭaittāyiṟṟu.
204.	akara viṟutip peyarnilai muṉṉar
	vēṟṟumai yalvaḻik kacatapat tōṉṟiṉ
	tatta motta voṟṟiṭai mikumē.
it talaiccūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, akara vīṟṟup peyarkku vaṉkaṇattōṭu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) akara iṟuti peyarnilai muṉṉar - akaramākiya iṟutiyaiyuṭaiya peyarccol muṉṉar, vēṟṟumai alvaḻi ka ca ta pa tōṉṟiṉ - vēṟṟumaiyallāta alvaḻikkaṇ ka ca ta pa mutalmoḻi varumoḻiyāyt tōṉṟiṉ, tam tam otta oṟṟu iṭaimikum - tattamakkup poruntiṉa ak ka ca ta pakkaḷākiya oṟṟu iṭaikkaṇ mikum.
u-m: viḷakkuṟitu, makakkuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu 
eṉa varum.
‘otta’ oṟṟeṉṉātu ‘tattam’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, akaravīṟṟu uriccol valleḻuttu mikkum melleḻuttu mikkum muṭiyum muṭipum, iṭaiccoṟkaḷuḷ eṭuttōtātavaṟṟiṉ muṭipum, akaram taṉṉai uṇaraniṉṟavaḻi muṭiyum muṭipum koḷka.
taṭakkai, tavakkoṇṭāṉ eṉa ivai uriccol valleḻuttuppēṟu. taṭañcevi, taṭantōḷ eṉa ivai melleḻuttuppēṟu.  “maṭava maṉṟa taṭavu nilaik koṉṟai” eṉpatu iṭaiccol muṭipu. akkuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉpatu taṉṉai uṇara niṉṟavaḻi valleḻuttu mikuti. avyātu eṉpatu iṭaiyeḻuttu mikuti. avvaḻakitu eṉpatu uyirkkaṇattu muṭipu.	 (1)
205.	viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyu muvamak kiḷaviyum
	eṉave ṉeccamuñ cuṭṭi ṉiṟutiyum
	āṅka veṉṉu muraiyacaik kiḷaviyum
	ñāṅkark kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, akaraīṟṟu viṉaiccol muṭipum, iṭaiccol muṭipum kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum uvamak kiḷaviyum - akara vīṟṟu viṉaiyeccamākiya collum uvamac collum, eṉa eṉ eccamum cuṭṭiṉ iṟutiyum - eṉa eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa eccac collum cuṭṭākiya akaravīṟum, āṅka eṉṉum uraiyacaik kiḷaviyum - āṅka eṉṟu collappaṭum uraiyacai yākiya collum, ñāṅkar kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikum - mēlaic cūttirattuc coṉṉa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: uṇakkoṇṭāṉ, tiṉakkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉpaṉa - viṉaiyeccam.  mēlaṉavellām iṭaiccol. pulipōlak koṉṟāṉ eṉpatu - uvamam. koḷḷeṉak koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu - eṉa eṉ eccam. akkoṟṟaṉ, cāttaṉ, tēvaṉ, pūtaṉ eṉpaṉa - cuṭṭiṉ iṟuti. āṅkak koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu - uraiyacaik kiḷavi.	(2)
206.	cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ña na mat tōṉṟiṉ
	oṭṭiya voṟṟiṭai mikutal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu, akaraccuṭṭu meṉkaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭiṉmuṉṉar ña na ma tōṉṟiṉ - akarac cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ñanamakkaḷ mutalākiya moḻi varumoḻiyāyt tōṉṟiṉ, oṭṭiya oṟṟu iṭaimikutal vēṇṭum - tattamakkup poruntiya oṟṟu iṭaikkaṇṇē mikutalai vēṇṭum.
u-m: aññālam, nūl, maṇi eṉa varum.	(3)
207.	yavamuṉ variṉē vakara moṟṟum.
itu, cuṭṭu iṭaikkaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ya va muṉ variṉ vakaram oṟṟum - yakara vakaram mutal moḻiyākac cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉē variṉ iṭaikkaṇ vakaram oṟṟām.
u-m:avyāḻ, avvaḷai eṉa varum.	(4)
208. uyirmuṉ variṉu māyiya ṟiriyātu.
iḵtu, accuṭṭu uyirkkaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir muṉ variṉum a iyal tiriyātu - uyir mutal moḻi akarac cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar variṉum mēṟkūṟiya vakaram mikku muṭiyum av viyalpiṉ tiriyātu e-ṟu.
u-m: avvaṭai, avvāṭai eṉa oṭṭuka.
iṭaimikka vakarattiṉai ‘neṟiyiyal’(161) eṉṟa ilēciṉāṉ iraṭṭutal kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, atu nilaimoḻit toḻileṉpatuvē kūṟappaṭṭatu. ‘tiriyātu’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, mēṟ cuṭṭu nīṇṭavaḻi vakarakkēṭu koḷka.	(5)
209.	nīṭa varutal ceyyuḷu ḷurittē.
iḵtu, accuṭṭuc ceyyuḷuḷ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nīṭavarutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittu - akarac cuṭṭu nīḷumpaṭiyāka varutal ceyyuḷiṭattu urittu e-ṟu.
u-m: “āyiru tiṇaiyi ṉicaikkumaṉa collē”
(kiḷavi. - 1) eṉa varum.
varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, im muṭipu vaṉkaṇa moḻinta kaṇamellāvaṟṟoṭum ceṉṟatu. utāraṇam peṟṟavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka.	(6)
210.	cāva veṉṉuñ ceyave ṉeccat
	tiṟuti vakaraṅ keṭutalu murittē.
itu, mēl ‘viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum’ (uyirmayaṅkiyal 2) eṉṟa muṭipiṟku eytātatu eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cāva eṉṉum ceya eṉ eccattu iṟuti vakaram keṭutalum urittu - cāva eṉṟu collappaṭum ceya eṉ eccattu iṟutikkaṇ akaramum ataṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa vakara voṟṟum keṭātu niṟṟalē yaṉṟik keṭṭu muṭitalum uriya.
u-m:	cākkuttiṉāṉ, cīṟiṉāṉ, takarttāṉ, puṭaittāṉ  eṉa varum.
itaṉai ‘viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum’ (uyirmayaṅkiyal - 2) eṉṟataṉpiṉ vaiyāta muṟaiyaṉṟiya kūṟṟiṉāṉ, iyalpu kaṇattum an nilaimoḻik kēṭu koḷka. cāñāṉṟār eṉa varum.	(7)
211.	aṉṉa veṉṉu muvamak kiḷaviyum
	aṇmai cuṭṭiya viḷinilaik kiḷaviyum
	ceymmaṉa veṉṉun toḻiliṟu collum
	ēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōṭ kiḷaviyum
	ceyta veṉṉum peyareñcu kiḷaviyum
	ceyyiya veṉṉum viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum
	amma veṉṉu muraipporuṭ kiḷaviyum
	palavaṟ ṟiṟutip peyarkkoṭai yuḷappaṭa
	aṉṟi yaṉaittu miyalpeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, akaravīṟṟuḷ orucārp peyarkkum viṉaikkum iṭaikkum muṉ eytiyatu vilakkiyum, eytātatu eytuvittum muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu. aṉṉaveṉpatum ceyyiya veṉpatum palavaṟṟiṟutip peyarkkoṭai yeṉpatum eytiyatu vilakkiṉa. maṟṟaiyaṉa eytātatu eytuvittaṉa.
(i-ḷ.) aṉṉa eṉṉum uvamakkiḷaviyum - aṉṉa eṉṟu collappaṭum uvama urupākiya iṭaiccollum, aṇmai cuṭṭiya viḷinilaik kiḷaviyum - aṇiyāraik karutiya viḷiyākiya nilaimaiyai yuṭaiya uyartiṇaip peyarc collum, ceymmaṉa eṉṉum toḻil iṟu collum - ceymmaṉa eṉṟu collappaṭum viṉaiccollākiya akaravīṟṟuc collum, ēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōḷ kiḷaviyum-  ēvalaik karutiya viyaṅkōḷākiya viṉaiccollum, ceyta eṉṉum peyareñcu kiḷaviyum - ceyta eṉṟu collappaṭum peyareccamākiya viṉaiccollum, ceyyiya eṉṉum viṉai eñcu kiḷaviyum - ceyyiya eṉṟu collap paṭum viṉai yeccamākiya viṉaic collum, amma eṉṉum uraip poruḷ kiḷaviyum - amma eṉṟu collappaṭum urai yacaip poruṇmaiyaiyuṭaiya iṭaiccollum, palavaṟṟu iṟutip peyarkkoṭai uḷappaṭa aṉṟi aṉaittum - paṉmaip poruḷkaḷiṉ akara vīṟṟup palaveṉṉum peyarc collumākiya avvaṉaittuc collum, iyalpu eṉa moḻipa - iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉṟu colluvar (āciriyar).
u-m: poṉ aṉṉa kutirai, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉavum;  ūra koḷ, cel, tā, pō eṉavum; uṇṭaṉa kutirai, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉavum; celka kutirai, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉavum; uṇṭa kutirai, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉavum; itaṉ etirmaṟai uṇṇāta kutirai eṉavum; itaṉ kuṟippu nalla kutirai, cennāy eṉavum; uṇṇiya koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; amma koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉavum;  pala kutirai, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉavum varum.
‘ūra koḷ’ eṉpatu ‘uyirīṟākiya uyartiṇaip peyar’ (154) eṉpataṉuḷ aṭaṅkātōveṉiṉ, ūraṉ eṉpatu ūra eṉa īṟu tirintamaiyiṉ āṇṭu aṭaṅkātāyiṟ ṟeṉpatu.
viḷinilaik kiḷaviyākiya peyarai muṉvaittataṉāl, ceyta veṉpataṉ maṟaiyaṉṟic ceyyum eṉpataṟku maṟaiyākiya ceyyāta eṉpatum av viyalpu uṭaitteṉak koḷka. vārāta koṟṟaṉ eṉa varum.
‘uraiyiṟkōṭal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, viyaṅkōḷ muṉ vaikkaṟ pālataṉai muṉvaiyātu ceymmaṉa eṉpataṉai muṉvaittataṉāṉ, iv viyalpu muṭipiṉkaṇ ceymmaṉa eṉpatu ciṟappuṭaitteṉap peṟappaṭṭatu. ataṉāṉ, ‘ēvalkaṇṇāta viyaṅkōḷum’ iv viyalpu muṭipu uṭaitteṉak koḷka. “maṉṉiya perumanī” (puṟam. 6) eṉa varum.
‘palavaṟṟiṟutip peyarkkoṭai’ eṉpataṉai amma eṉpataṟku muṉ vaiyātataṉāṉ, pala eṉpatu mēṟkūṟum ceyyuḷ muṭipiṉ (214) tirintu muṭital īṇṭai iyalpiṟ ciṟappiṉṟeṉpatūum, akara vīṟṟuḷ muṭipu kūṟāta muṟṟuviṉaiyum viṉaikkuṟippum iv viyalpiṉaveṉpatūum peṟappaṭum. uṇṭaṉa kutirai eṉpatu muṟṟuviṉai. kariya kutirai eṉpatu muṟṟuviṉaik kuṟippu.
‘taṉṉiṉa muṭittal’ (poruḷ.999 urai) eṉpataṉāṉ, pala eṉpataṉ iṉamākiya cila eṉpataṟkum av viyalpu koḷka.
cila kutirai eṉavarum.	(8)
212.	vāḻiya veṉṉuñ cēyeṉ kiḷavi
	iṟuti yakaraṅ keṭutalu murittē.
iḵtu ēval kaṇṇāta viyaṅkōḷkaḷiṉ oṉṟaṟku eytiya iyalpu vilakki vikāram kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vāḻiya eṉṉum cēy eṉ kiḷavi - vāḻiya eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa, av vāḻuṅkālam aṇmaiyataṉṟic cēymaiyatu eṉṟu uṇarttum col, iṟuti yakaram keṭutalum urittu - taṉṉiṟutikkaṇ akaramum ataṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa yakaravoṟṟum keṭātu muṭitalēyaṉṟik keṭṭu muṭitalum urittu.
u-m: vāḻikoṟṟā eṉa varum.
‘cēyeṉ kiḷavi’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, am muṭipu iv ‘vāḻiya’ eṉpataṟku ēval kaṇṇāta nilaiya teṉpatu viḷakkiya niṉṟatu. ‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittal’ (poruḷ - 999 urai) eṉpataṉāṉ, piṟa kaṇattukkaṇṇum iv viti koḷka. vāḻiñeḷḷā eṉa varum.	(9)
213.	uraipporuṭ kiḷavi nīṭṭamum varaiyār.
iḵtu, amma eṉpataṟku eytātatu eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uraipporuḷ kiḷavi nīṭṭamum varaiyār - uraiyacaip poruṇmaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya amma eṉṉum iṭaiccol taṉ īṟṟakaram akaramāy niṟṟalēyaṉṟi ākāramāy nīṇṭu muṭitalaiyum varaiyār.
u-m:ammākoṟṟā eṉa varum.
‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittal’ (poruḷ - 999 urai) eṉpataṉāṉ, an nīṭci iyalpukaṇattum koḷka. ammāñeḷḷā eṉa varum. 	(10)
214.	palavaṟ ṟiṟuti nīṭumoḻi yuḷavē
	ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻi yāṉa.
itu, mēṟ “palavaṟ ṟiṟutip peyarkkoṭai” (uyir mayaṅkiyal 8) eṉṟu ōtiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) palavaṟṟu iṟuti nīṭum moḻi uḷa - pala eṉṉum colliṉ iṟuti akaram nīṇṭu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa; (yāṇṭu uḷaveṉiṉ), ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭar moḻiyāṉa- ceyyuḷātalaik karutiya oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarccippaṭum ceyyuḷ muṭipuṭaiya moḻikaḷiṉ kaṇṇē.
‘ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya moḻi’ eṉṉātu ‘toṭarmoḻi’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ip palaveṉpatu nīḷumvaḻi varumoḻiyāvatu cilaveṉpatē eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum. ‘ceyyuḷāṉa’ eṉṉātu ‘ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻi yāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, pala eṉṉum moḻiyīṟu nīṇṭavaḻi nilaimoḻi akarap pēṟum varumoḻi ñakāra melleḻuttup pēṟum, varumoḻiyīṟu nīṇṭavaḻi akarappēṟum makaramākiya melleḻuttup pēṟum koḷka. ‘uṇṭu’ eṉṉātu ‘uḷa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, cilaveṉṉum varumoḻi yiṟuti nīṭalum koḷka.
u-m:	‘palāañ cilāa meṉmaṉār pulavar’ eṉavarum.  itaṉ colnilai palacila eṉṉum cevveṇ. (akaram cāriyai.)	(11)
215.	toṭara liṟuti tammuṟ ṟāmvariṉ
	lakaram ṟakaravoṟ ṟātalu murittē.
itu, pala cila eṉpavaṟṟiṟku iyalpēyaṉṟit tiripum uṇṭeṉa eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) toṭaral iṟutit tammuṉ tāmvariṉ - toṭar moḻiyallāta īreḻut torumoḻiyākiya pala cila eṉṉum akaravīṟṟuc col tammuṉṉē tāmvariṉ, lakaram ṟakara oṟṟu ātalum urittu - tam īṟṟil niṉṟa lakaravoṟṟu ṟakara voṟṟāyt tirintu muṭitalum urittu.
u-m:	paṟpala koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, ciṟcila vitti eṉavum varum.
‘taṉmuṟṟāṉ’ eṉa orumaiyāṟ kūṟātu ‘tammuṟṟāmvariṉ’ eṉṟa paṉmaiyāṉ, mēṟ pala cila eṉa niṉṟa iraṇṭum taḻuvap paṭṭaṉa. ‘toṭara liṟuti tammuṉ variṉ’ eṉṉātu ‘tām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, im muṭipiṉkaṇ palaveṉpataṉmuṉ pala varuka, cilaveṉpataṉ muṉ cila varuka eṉpatu koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
‘lakaram ṟakaramām’ eṉṉātu ‘lakaram ṟakara voṟṟām’ eṉṟa ñāpakattāṉ, akaram keṭumeṉṟārākak koḷka. aruttāpatti mukattāl, tammuṉ tāmvariṉ lakaram ṟakara voṟṟā meṉavē, tammuṉ piṟa vantaviṭattu lakaram ṟakaravoṟ ṟākātu akaram keṭumeṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum. ‘palpaṭai’ palyāṉai, cilpaṭai, cilvēḷvi eṉa varum.
‘toṭara liṟuti’ eṉpatu cuṭṭallatu ōreḻuttoru moḻi akaramiṉmaiyiṉ, ōreḻut torumoḻimēṟ cellā tāyiṟṟu. uritteṉṟatu, akaravīṟṟorumai paṟṟi. saēkāram īṟṟacai]	(12)
216.	valleḻut tiyaṟkai yuṟaḻat tōṉṟum.
ituvum, mēṟkūṟiya iraṇṭaṟkum iṉṉum oru muṭipu vēṟṟumai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) val eḻuttu iyaṟkai uṟaḻattōṉṟum - mēṟkūṟiya pala cila eṉṉum iraṇṭaṟkum akaravīṟṟup potuvitiyiṟ kūṟiya valleḻuttu mikutiyatu iyalpu, mikutalum mikāmaiyumāki uṟaḻntuvarat tōṉṟum.
u-m: palappala; cilaccila; palapala, cilacila eṉa varum.
īṇṭum tammuṉ tām varutal koḷka. ‘iyaṟkai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ muṉkūṟiya paṟpala, ciṟcila eṉṉum muṭipōṭu palpala, cilcila eṉṉum muṭipupeṟṟu uṟaḻcciyātal koḷka.
‘tōṉṟum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, akaram keṭa lakaram āytamum mel leḻuttumāyt tirintu muṭital koḷka.
u-m: paḵṟāṉai, paṉmaram; ciḵṟāḻicai, ciṉṉūl eṉa varum.	 (13)
217.	vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu, akara īṟṟup peyarkku vaṉkaṇattoṭu vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - akara vīṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum mēṟkūṟiya (204) alvaḻiyōṭu oru taṉmaittāyk ka ca ta pa mutalmoḻi vantavaḻi tattam oṟṟu iṭaimikku muṭiyum.
u-m: iruviḷakkoṟṟaṉ; cāttaṉ, tēvaṉ, pūtaṉ eṉa varum. viḷakkuṟumai eṉak kuṇavēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum koḷka. (iruviḷa - ōlai, vēṇāṭṭakattu ōr ūr)	   (14)
218.	marappeyark kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, akaravīṟṟu marappeyark kiḷavikku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) marappeyark kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikum - akaravīṟṟu marappeyarākiya col melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: viḷaṅkōṭu, viḷañcetiḷ; tōl, pū eṉa varum. (15)
219.	makappeyark kiḷavik kiṉṉē cāriyai.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) makappeyark kiḷavikku iṉcāriyai - akaravīṟṟu maka eṉṉum peyarccolliṟku vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ varum cāriyai iṉ.
u-m: makaviṉkai; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.
cāriyaippēṟu varaiyātu kūṟiyavaḻi nāṉku kaṇattuk kaṇṇum cellumeṉpatākaliṉ, makaviṉñāṇ eṉa iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇṇum koḷka.	  (16)
220.	attavaṇ variṉum varainilai yiṉṟē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) attu avaṇ variṉum varai nilai iṉṟu - mēṟkūṟiya iṉṉēyaṉṟi attu eṉṉum cāriyai iyaipu valleḻuttiṉōṭu am maka eṉṉum colliṭattu vantu muṭiyiṉum nīkkum nilaimai iṉṟu.
u-m:  makattukkai; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉac ceykai aṟintu muṭikka.
‘avaṇ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, makappālyāṭu eṉa valleḻuttup pēṟum, makaviṉkai eṉa mēl iṉcāriyai peṟṟavaḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻvum, urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ vantavaḻi viḷaviṉkōṭu eṉa iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻvum koḷka. ‘nilai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, makampāl yāṭu eṉa melleḻuttup pēṟum koḷka. 	 (17)
221.	palavaṟ ṟiṟuti yurupiya ṉilaiyum.
iḵtu, akaravīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) palavaṟṟu iṟuti urupu iyal nilaiyum - pala eṉṉum akaravīṟṟuc col urupupuṇarccikkaṇ (175) vaṟṟup peṟṟup puṇarnta iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṟkum.
u-m: palavaṟṟukkōṭu; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.   (18)
222.	ākāra viṟuti yakara viyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ākāra īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ākāra iṟuti akara iyaṟṟu - ākāra vīṟṟup peyar (al vaḻikkaṇ) akara vīṟṟu  alvaḻiyatu iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
u-m: tārāk kaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.      (19)
223.	ceyyā veṉṉum viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum
	avviya ṟiriyā teṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟu viṉaiccol muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ceyyā eṉṉum viṉai eñcu kiḷaviyum - (peyarē yaṉṟi) ceyyā eṉṟu collappaṭum ākāravīṟṟu viṉaiyeccac collum, a iyal tiriyātu eṉmaṉār pulavar - valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum avviyalpiṉ tiriyāteṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m:	uṇṇāk koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘tiriyātu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ‘ceyyā’ eṉṉum peyareccamum avvāṟu muṭiyumeṉak koḷka. uṇṇāk koṟṟaṉ eṉa varum.	(20)
224.	ummai yeñciya virupeyart tokaimoḻi
	meymmai yāka vakara mikumē.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟu alvaḻikkaṇ ummaittokai muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ummai eñciya irupeyart tokaimoḻi - ummai tokka irupeyarākiya tokaiccol, meymmai yāka akaram mikum - meyyāka nilaimoḻiyīṟṟu akaram mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: uvāap patiṉāṉku.
‘meymmaiyāka’ eṉṟataṉāṉ valleḻuttuk koṭukka. ‘ummai tokka’ eṉṉātu ‘eñciya’ eṉṟa vāypāṭṭu vēṟṟumaiyāṉ, im muṭipu irupeyaroṭṭup paṇput tokaikkum koḷka. arāap pāmpu eṉavarum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉ, eḻuvāy muṭipiṟkum peyareccat tiṟkum akarappēṟu koḷka. uvāak koṭitu; uvāak kākkai eṉavarum.
nilaimoḻi eḻuttuppēṟu varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟiṉavaḻi nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇum cellumākaliṉ, iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇṇum akarappēṟu koḷka. iṟāa vaḻutuṇaṅ kāy eṉavarum.	 (21)
225.	āvu māvum viḷippeyark kiḷaviyum
	yāveṉ viṉāvum palavaṟ ṟiṟutiyum
	ēval kuṟitta vuraiyacai miyāvum
	taṉṟoḻi luraikkum viṉāviṉ kiḷaviyō
	ṭaṉṟi yaṉaittu miyalpeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku iyalpukūṟi eytiyatu vilakkutalum, eytātatu eytuvittalum nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āvum māvum viḷippeyark kiḷaviyum - ā eṉṉum peyarccollum mā eṉṉum peyarccollum viḷittalaiyuṭaiya peyarākiya uyartiṇaiccollum, yā eṉ viṉāvum palavaṟṟu iṟutiyum - yā eṉṉum viṉāp peyarum aḵṟiṇaip paṉmaip poruḷai uṇarttum ākāravīṟṟu muṟṟuviṉaic collum, ēval kuṟitta uraiyacai miyāvum - muṉṉilaiyil ēval viṉaiccollaik kuṟittu varum uraiyacaiyākiya miyā eṉṉum ākāravīṟṟu iṭaic collum, taṉtoḻil uraikkum viṉāviṉ kiḷaviyōṭu aṉṟi aṉaittum - taṉatu toḻiliṉaic collum ākāra viṉāviṉaiyuṭaiya viṉaic collumākiya avvaṉaittum, iyalpu eṉa moḻipa - iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m:	ā kuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum;  mā kuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; ūrā koḷ, cel, tā, pō eṉavum;  yā kuṟiya, ciṟiya, tīya, periya eṉavum; uṇṇā kutirai, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉavum; kēṇmiyā koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉavum; uṇkā koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉavum varum.
‘viḷippeyark kiḷaviyum’, ‘palavaṟṟiṟutiyum’, ‘ēval kuṟitta uraiyacai miyāvum’, ‘taṉtoḻi luraikkum viṉāvum’ eytātatu eytuvikkappaṭṭaṉa. ūrā koḷ eṉpatu ‘uyirīṟākiya uyartiṇaip peyareṉpataṉuḷ aṭaṅkātō veṉiṉ, muṉ (uyirmayaṅkiyal  8) kūṟiṟṟē kūṟuka.	 (22)
226.	vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟu vēṟṟumai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - (ākāra vīṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇṇēyaṉṟi) vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum akaravīṟṟu alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmaittāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum.
u-m: tārākkāl;  ciṟaku, talai, puṟam eṉavarum.  	(23)

227.	kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉaru mōreḻuttu moḻikkum
	aṟiyat tōṉṟu makarak kiḷavi.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟiṉ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum ōr eḻuttu moḻikkum - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉ niṉṟa ākāravīṟṟiṟkum ōreḻut torumoḻi ākāravīṟṟiṟkum, akarakkiḷavi aṟiyat tōṉṟum - (nilaimoḻikkaṇ) akaramākiya eḻuttu aṟiyat tōṉṟum.
u-m: palāakkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum; kāakkuṟai, ceykai, talai, puṟam eṉavum varum.
ōreḻuttorumoḻi muṟkūṟātavataṉāṉ, ataṉkaṇ akarappēṟu ciṟupāṉmai yeṉak koḷka. ‘aṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, av viruvaḻiyum akaram poruntiya vaḻiyē varutalum, avvīṟṟu vēṟṟumaiyuḷ eṭuttōtātavaṟṟiṉ muṭipum, ivvīṟṟuḷ urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟa vaḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻvum, iv vuyirīṟṟil varum urupīṟṟuc ceykaiyum koḷka.
u-m: aṇṇāattuk kuḷam, uvāattuñāṉṟu koṇṭāṉ, uvāattāṟ koṇṭāṉ, yāvaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa ivai - piṟamuṭipu. mūṅkāviṉṟō eṉpatu - valleḻuttu vīḻvu. iṭāviṉuṭ koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu - urupīṟṟuc ceykai.		(24)
228.	irāveṉ kiḷavik kakara millai.
iḵtu, av akarappēṟṟiṟku oruvaḻi eytiyatu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) irā eṉ kiḷavikku akaram illai - irā eṉṉum ākāravīṟṟuc colliṟku muṉkūṟiya akarappēṟu illai.
u-m: irākkoṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.	(25)
229.	nilāveṉ kiḷavi yattoṭu civaṇum.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟuḷ oruvaḻi akaram vilakki attuvarutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nilā eṉ kiḷavi attoṭu civaṇum - nilā eṉṉum col attuc cāriyaiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum.
u-m: nilāattuk koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
nilaimoḻit toḻil  varumoḻit toḻilai vilakku mākaliṉ, attu vakuppa akaram vīḻntatu.	   (26)
230.	yā marak kiḷaviyum piṭāvun taḷāvum
	āmup peyaru melleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) yāmarak kiḷaviyum piṭāvum taḷāvum a muppeyarum - yā eṉṉum marattai uṇaraniṉṟa collum piṭā eṉṉum collum taḷā eṉṉum collumākiya am mūṉṟu peyarum, melleḻuttu mikum - valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m:	yāaṅkōṭu, piṭāaṅkōṭu, taḷāaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
melleḻuttuppēṟu varumoḻit toḻilātaliṉ, varumoḻi valleḻuttai vilakkiṟṟu.	  (27)
231.	valleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉa millai.
itu, mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku valleḻuttum ciṟupāṉmai mikku muṭiyum eṉa iṟantatu kāttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) valleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉam illai - mēṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu peyarum melleḻuttēyaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyiṉum kuṟṟam illai.
u-m: yāakkōṭu, piṭāakkōṭu, taḷāakkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
‘māṉamillai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, yā mutaliya mūṉṟaṟkum urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi, iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.
u-m: yāaviṉkōṭu, piṭāaviṉkōṭu, taḷāaviṉ kōṭu eṉa varum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, yāattukkōṭu eṉac ciṟupāṉmai attuppēṟuṇṭēṉum koḷka. av vakarap pēṟṟōṭu valleḻuttup peṟutaliṉ. ‘yāmarak kiḷavi’ eṉpataṉaik ‘kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum’ eṉpataṉpiṉ vaiyāta itaṉāṉ, irāviṟ koṇṭāṉ, nilāviṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. nilāviṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉpataṟku nilāattuk koṇ ṭāṉ eṉpatu īṟṟup potu muṭipāyiṉa vāṟu aṟika.	 (28)
232.	māmarak kiḷaviyu māvu māvum
	āmup peyaru mavaṟṟō raṉṉa
	akaram valleḻut tavaiyava ṇilaiyā
	ṉakara moṟṟu māvu māvum.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) māmarakkiḷaviyum āvum māvum a muppeyarum avaṟṟu ōr aṉṉa - māmaramākiya collum ā eṉṉum collum mā eṉṉum collumākiya am mūṉṟu peyarccollum mēṟkūṟiya (230) yā mutaliya mūṉṟaṉoṭum oru taṉmaiyavāy valleḻuttup peṟātu melleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭitalumuṭaiya, āvum māvum akaram valleḻuttu avai avaṇ nilaiyā ṉakaram oṟṟum - avaṟṟuḷ āvum māvum muṉpeṟṟu niṉṟa akaramum (227) valleḻuttumākiya avai avviṭattu nilaipeṟāvāy ṉakaram oṟṟākap peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: māaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum; āṉkōṭu, māṉkōṭu; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉavum varum.
‘aṟiya’ (uyir. 24) eṉṟataṉāṉ, ciṟupāṉmai māṅkōṭu eṉa akaramiṉṟiyum varum. iṉi ‘avaṇ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ kāyāṅkōṭu, āṇāṅkōṭu, nuṇāṅkōṭu eṉṟāṟpōlap piṟavum melleḻuttup peṟutalum; aṅkāk koṇṭāṉ, iṅkāk koṇṭāṉ, uṅkāk koṇṭāṉ, eṅkāk koṇṭāṉ eṉa ivvīṟṟu ēḻām vēṟṟumai iṭapporuḷ uṇaraniṉṟa iṭaiccoṟkaḷ valleḻuttup peṟutalum; āviṉkōṭu, māviṉkōṭu eṉac ciṟupāṉmai iṉpeṟutalum, peṟṟavaḻi valleḻuttu vīḻvum koḷka. ‘avaṟṟōraṉṉa’ eṉṟa māṭṭēṟṟāṉ peṟṟuniṉṟatu mel leḻuttākaliṉ, ‘akaram valleḻut tavaiyava ṇilaiyā’ eṉṟu ōtaṟ pālataṉṟeṉiṉ, mēl “ūveṉ ṉorupeya rāvoṭu civaṇum” (uyirmayaṅkiyal 67) eṉṟavaḻi, am māṭṭēṟṟāṉē ataṉ valleḻuttu vīḻvum koḷal vēṇṭit ‘tirinta taṉṟiripatu’ eṉṉum nayattāṉē mel leḻuttai valleḻuttāka ōtiṉāreṉak koḷka.	(29)
233.	āṉoṟ ṟakaramoṭu nilaiyiṭa ṉuṭaittē.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟuḷ ā eṉṟataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṉ oṟṟu akaramoṭum nilaiiṭaṉ uṭaittu - muṉpu peṟṟuniṉṟa āṉ eṉṉum colliṉatu ṉakaravoṟṟu akarattoṭum niṟkum iṭaṉuṭaittu.
‘iṭaṉuṭaittu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, vaṉkaṇam oḻinta kaṇattu im muṭipeṉak koḷka.
u-m: ‘āṉaney teḷittu nāṉa nīvi’ eṉa varum.
‘akaramoṭum’ eṉṟa ummaiyāṉ, akaramiṉṟi varutalē perumpāṉmai eṉak koḷka.	 (30)
 234.	āṉmuṉ varūu mīkāra pakaram
	tāṉmikat tōṉṟik kuṟukalu murittē.
iḵtu, iṉṉum āṉ eṉpataṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal  nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṉmuṉ varum īkārap pakaram - āṉ eṉṉum colmuṉṉar varumoḻiyāy varukiṉṟa īkārattoṭu kūṭiya pakaramākiya moḻi, tāṉ mikat tōṉṟik kuṟukalum urittu - ap pakaramākiya tāṉ mikat tōṉṟi av vīkāram ikaramākak kuṟuki muṭitalaiyum uṭaittu.
‘tōṉṟi’ eṉṟataṉāl, nilaimoḻip pēṟākiya ṉakara voṟṟiṉ kēṭu koḷka.
u-m: āppi eṉa varum.
ummaiyāṉ, āṉpī eṉpatē perumpāṉmaiyeṉak koḷka.	 (31)
235.	kuṟiyata ṉiṟutic ciṉaikeṭa vukaram
	aṟiya varutal ceyyuḷu ḷurittē.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟkuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟiyataṉ iṟutic ciṉai keṭa ukaram aṟiya varutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittu - kuṟiyataṉ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa ākārattiṉatu ciṉaiyākiya akāram māttirai keṭa āṇṭu ukaram aṟiya varutal ceyyuḷiṭattu urittu.
u-m:	 “iṟavup puṟattaṉṉa piṇarppaṭu taṭavumutal” (naṟ. 19) eṉa varum.
‘aṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ukarampeṟātu ciṉaikeṭutalum koḷka. aravaṇi koṭi eṉa varum.
‘piṇavunāy muṭukkiya’ eṉṟāṟpōla varum alvaḻimuṭipu ‘kiḷanta valla’ (kuṟṟiyalukara puṇariyal - 77) eṉṉum puṟaṉaṭaiya teṉak koḷka.	  (32)
236.	ikara viṟutip peyarnilai muṉṉar
	vēṟṟumai yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, ikaravīṟṟup peyarkku alvaḻi muṭipu tokai marapiṉuṭ kūṟi  niṉṟamaiyiṉ ataṉ vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ikara iṟutip peyarnilai muṉṉar vēṟṟumai yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikum - ikaravīṟṟup peyarccol muṉṉar atikārattāṟ ka ca ta pa mutal moḻi vantavaḻi vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarcciyāyiṉ tamakkup poruntiṉa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: kiḷikkāl; ciṟaku, talai, puṟam eṉa varum. 	(33)
237.	iṉiyaṇi yeṉṉuṅ kālaiyu miṭaṉum
	viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyuñ cuṭṭu maṉṉa.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟuḷ cila iṭaiccollum viṉaic collum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṉi aṇi eṉṉum kālaiyum iṭaṉum viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum cuṭṭum aṉṉa - iṉi eṉṟum aṇi eṉṟum collappaṭukiṉṟa kālattaiyum iṭattaiyum uṇara niṉṟa iṭaiccollum, iv vikaravīṟṟu viṉaiyeccamākiya collum, ivvīṟṟuc cuṭṭākiya iṭaiccollum, mēṟkūṟiya vāṟē valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum taṉmaiya.
u-m: iṉikkoṇṭāṉ, aṇikkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; tēṭikkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; ikkoṟṟaṉ; cāttaṉ, tēvaṉ, pūtaṉ eṉavum varum.	(34)
238.	iṉṟi yeṉṉum viṉaiyeñ ciṟuti
	niṉṟa vikara mukara mātal
	toṉṟiyaṉ maruṅkiṟ ceyyuḷu ḷurittē.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟu viṉaiyeccak kuṟippiṉuḷ oṉṟaṟkuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṉṟi eṉṉum viṉaiyeñcu iṟuti niṉṟa ikaram ukaram ātal - iṉṟi eṉṟu collappaṭum viṉaiyeccattu iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa ikaram ukaramāyt tirintu muṭital, toṉṟu iyal maruṅkiṉ ceyyuḷuḷ urittu - paḻaka naṭanta kūṟṟaiyuṭaiya ceyyuḷkaḷiṭattu urittu.
u-m:	“uppiṉṟu puṟkai yuṇkamā koṟkai yōṉē” eṉa varum.
‘niṉṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, muṉ peṟṟuniṉṟa valleḻuttu vīḻkka. ‘toṉṟiyaṉ maruṅku’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, aṉṟi eṉpatum ceyyuḷuḷ im muṭipiṟṟātal koḷka. ‘nāḷaṉṟu pōkip puḷḷiṭai taṭpa’ (puṟam - 124) eṉa varum.	  (35)
239.	cuṭṭi ṉiyaṟkai muṟkiḷan taṟṟē.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyar iyalpukaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭiṉ iyaṟkai muṉkiḷanta aṟṟu - ikara vīṟṟuc cuṭṭiṉatu iyalpu iyalpukkaṇam varumvaḻiyum uyirkkaṇam varum vaḻiyum muṉ akaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭiṟkuc collappaṭṭa taṉmaittām.
eṉṟatu meṉkaṇam varumvaḻi am melleḻuttu mikkum (uyirmayaṅkiyal 3), iṭaikkaṇam varumvaḻiyum uyirkkaṇam varum vaḻiyum nilaimoḻi vakaram peṟṟum (uyirmayaṅkiyal 4,5), ceyyuṭ kaṇ vakaramkeṭṭuc cuṭṭunīṇṭum (uyirmayaṅkiyal - 6) muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
u-m:	iññāṉam, innūl, immaṇi eṉavum; ivyāḻ, ivvaṭṭu eṉavum; ivvaṭai, ivvāṭai, ivveḷaviyam eṉavum; īvayiṉāṉa eṉavum varum.	(36)
240.	patakkumuṉ variṉē tūṇik kiḷavi
	mutaṟkiḷan teṭutta vēṟṟumai yiyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟu alvaḻikaḷil aḷavuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku mēl tokaimarapiṉuḷ (cūt. 22) eytiya ē eṉ cāriyai vilakki vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) patakku muṉ variṉ tūṇikkiḷavi mutal kiḷantu eṭutta vēṟṟumai iyaṟṟu - patakku eṉṉum col taṉmuṉ variṉ tūṇi eṉṉum col muṉpu  vitanteṭutta vēṟṟumai muṭipiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: tūṇippatakku eṉa varum.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl, irutūṇippatakku eṉa aṭaiyaṭuttu vantavaḻiyum ivviti koḷka. ‘kiḷanteṭutta’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, tūṇimuṉṉarp piṟa poruṭ peyar vantavaḻiyum, āṇṭu nilaimoḻi aṭaiyaṭuttu vantavaḻiyum, taṉ muṉṉart tāṉ vantavaḻiyum im muṭipu koḷka. iṉṉum, ataṉāṉē, taṉmuṉṉart tāṉ vantavaḻi ikkuc cāriyaippēṟum koḷka.
u-m: tūṇikkoḷ, tūṇiccāmai eṉavum; iru tūṇikkoḷ eṉavum; tūṇittūṇi eṉavum; tūṇikkut tūṇi eṉavum varum.	   (37)
241.	urivaru kālai nāḻik kiḷavi
	iṟuti yikara meyyoṭuṅ keṭumē
	ṭakāra moṟṟu māvayi ṉāṉa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) uri varu kālai nāḻikkiḷavi iṟuti ikaram mey yoṭum keṭum - uri eṉṉum col varumoḻiyāy vanta kālattu nāḻi eṉṉum col taṉ īṟṟil niṉṟa ikaram tāṉ ūrntu niṉṟa mey yoṭum keṭum, a vayiṉ ṭakāram oṟṟum - avviṭattu ṭakaram oṟṟāy varum.
 u-m: nāṭuri eṉa varum.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, nilaimoḻi aṭai yaṭuttu ‘iru nāṭuri’ eṉṟa vaḻiyum im muṭipu koḷka. ‘ikaram’ eṉṉātu ‘iṟuyikaram’ eṉṟataṉāṉ īṇṭai nilai moḻiyum varumoḻiyum nilaimoḻikaḷāy niṉṟu piṟa poruṭ peyaroṭu valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum koḷka. 
nāḻikkāyam, urikkāyam eṉa varum.	 (38)
242.	paṉiyeṉa varūuṅ kāla vēṟṟumaik
	kattu miṉṉuñ cāriyai yākum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu vēṟṟumaiyuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻutti ṉoṭu cāriyai peṟumeṉa eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉi eṉa varum kāla vēṟṟumaikku attum iṉṉum cāriyai ākum - paṉi eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa paṉikkālattai uṇara niṉṟa vēṟṟumai muṭipuṭaiya peyarkku varum cāriyai attum iṉṉum ākum.
u-m: paṉiyattuk koṇṭāṉ, paṉiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘vēṟṟumai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉ peṟṟavaḻi iyaipu valleḻuttai vīḻkka.	(39)
243.	vaḷiyeṉa varūum pūtak kiḷaviyum
	avviya ṉilaiyal cevvi teṉpa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) vaḷi eṉavarum pūtak kiḷaviyum a iyal nilaiyal cevvitu eṉpa - vaḷi eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa iṭakkarallāta aimperum pūtaṅkaḷil oṉṟai uṇara niṉṟa collum mēṟkūṟiya attum iṉṉum peṟum av viyalpiṉkaṇ niṟṟal cevviteṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m: vaḷiyattuk koṇṭāṉ, vaḷiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘cevvitu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉpeṟṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	 (40)
244.	utimarak kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu marappeyar oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) utimarak kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikum - uti eṉṉum marattiṉai uṇara niṉṟa col valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m:  utiṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum. 	(41)
245.	puḷimarak kiḷavik kammē cāriyai.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu marappeyar oṉṟaṟku mēl eytiya valleḻuttu vilakkic cāriyai vitittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) puḷimarak kiḷavikku cāriyai am - puḷi eṉṉum marattiṉai uṇara niṉṟa colliṟku varum cāriyai ammuc cāriyai.
u-m: puḷiyaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
cāriyaippēṟṟiṭai eḻuttuppēṟu kūṟiyavataṉāṉ, ammup peṟṟavaḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu (236) vīḻkka.       (42)
246.	ēṉaip puḷippeyar melleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, am marappeyarallāta puḷippeyarkku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉai puḷippeyar melleḻuttu mikum - am marap peyaraṉṟi oḻinta cuvaippuḷi uṇaraniṉṟa peyar valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: puḷiṅkūḻ; cōṟu, tayir, pāḷitam eṉa varum.	(43)
247.	valleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉa millai
	olvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa.
iḵtu, mēlataṟku valleḻuttu mikumeṉa eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) valleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉam illai - ēṉaip puḷippeyarmuṉ eytiya melleḻuttēyaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyiṉum kuṟṟam illai. olvaḻi aṟital vaḻakkattāṉa - poruntum iṭamaṟika vaḻakkiṭattu.
u-m: puḷikkūḻ; cōṟu, tayir, pāḷitam eṉa varum.
‘olvaḻi yaṟital’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, puḷiccōṟu eṉṟatupōla maṟṟaiyavai vaḻakkup payiṟci ilaveṉpatum koḷka. ‘vaḻakkat tāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivvīṟṟuḷ eṭuttōttum ilēcum illātaṉavaṟṟiṉ muṭipu vēṟṟumaiyellām koḷka. kūtāḷaṅkōṭu eṉavum, kaṇaviraṅkōṭu eṉavum, tuḷiyattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, paruttikkuc ceṉṟāṉ eṉavum, kappi tantai kappintai eṉavum, kaṭṭi akal kaṭṭakal eṉavum, kuḷikuṟumai kuḷikkuṟumai eṉavum, iṉṉiṉik koṇṭāṉ aṇṇaṇik koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, ‘puḷiṅkāy’  (aiṅkuṟu. - 51) eṉavum varum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, ivvīṟṟuḷ urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi, iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻtalum koḷka. kiḷiyiṉkāl eṉa varum.	 (44)
248.	nāṇmuṟ ṟōṉṟun toḻiṉilaik kiḷavik
	kāṉiṭai varuta laiya miṉṟē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkuc cāriyai vitittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nāḷ muṉ tōṉṟum toḻilnilaik kiḷavikku - ikaravīṟṟu nāṭpeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum viṉaic colliṟku, āṉiṭai varutal aiyam iṉṟu - āṉcāriyai iṭai vantu muṭital aiyam illai.
u-m: paraṇiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
`aiya miṉṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.			(45)
249.	tiṅkaṇ muṉvari ṉikkē cāriyai.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) tiṅkaḷmuṉ variṉ cāriyai ikku - tiṅkaḷai uṇara niṉṟa ikaravīṟṟup peyarmuṉṉart toḻilnilaik kiḷavi variṉ varum cāriyai ikkuccāriyai.
u-m āṭikkukkoṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.	(46)
250.	īkāra viṟuti yākāra viyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, īkāra vīṟṟuppeyar alvaḻiyiṉkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) īkāra iṟuti ākāra iyaṟṟu - īkāravīṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ ākāravīṟṟu alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu varumvaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: tīkkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavarum. 	(47)
251.	nīyeṉ peyaru miṭakkarp peyarum
	mīyeṉa marīiya viṭamvarai kiḷaviyum
	āvayiṉ valleḻut tiyaṟkai yākum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nī eṉ peyarum iṭakkarp peyarum mī eṉa marīiya iṭamvarai kiḷaviyum - nī eṉṉum peyarum iṭakkarp peyarākiya (pī eṉṉum) īkāravīṟṟup peyarum mī eṉṟu colla varūum maruvāy vaḻaṅkiṉa, ōr iṭattiṉai varaintuṇarttum collum, a vayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟkai ākum - mēl iv vīṟṟuṭ kūṟiya valleḻuttup peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m:	nī kuṟiyai, ciṟiyai, tīyai, periyai eṉavum;  pī kuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; mīkaṇ, cevi, talai, puṟam eṉavum varum.
‘nī kuṟiyai’ eṉpatu mēl “aḵṟiṇai viravuppeya riyalpumāruḷavē” (tokai. 13) eṉṟavaḻi aṭaṅkātō veṉiṉ, mēl (254) vēṟṟumaikkaṇ niṉkai eṉat tirintu muṭitaliṉ aṭaṅkātāyiṟ ṟeṉka. mīkaṇ eṉpatu alvaḻi muṭipaṉ ṟeṉiṉum iyalpātal nōkki uṭaṉkūṟappaṭṭatu. (48)
252.	iṭamvarai kiḷavimuṉ valleḻuttu mikūum
	uṭaṉilai moḻiyu muḷaveṉa moḻipa.
itu, mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ mī eṉpataṟku vēṟoru muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṭamvarai kiḷavimuṉ valleḻuttu mikūum uṭaṉ nilaimoḻiyum uḷa eṉa moḻipa - iṭattiṉai varaintuṇarttum mī eṉṉum colmuṉ iyalpāy muṭitalēyaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum tammil iyaintu niṟṟalaiyuṭaiya moḻikaḷum uḷaveṉṟu colluvar.
u-m: mīkkōḷ,  mīppal eṉa varum.
‘uṭaṉilai’ eṉṟataṉāl, mīṅkuḻi, mīntōl eṉa mel leḻuttup peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum koḷka. 	   (49)
253.	vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumaikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - īkāra vīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum ākāra vīṟṟu  alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmaittāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: īkkāl, ciṟaku, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.	 (50)
254.	nīye ṉorupeya rurupiya ṉilaiyum
	āvayiṉ valleḻut tiyaṟkai yākum.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu vēṟṟumai muṭipiṉuḷ oṉṟaṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nī eṉ oru peyar urupu iyal nilaiyum - nī eṉa niṉṟa orupeyar urupupuṇarccikkaṇ neṭumutal kuṟuki ṉakara voṟṟup peṟṟu muṭinta iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum, a vayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟkai ākum - avvāṟu muṭintaviṭattu iyaipu valleḻuttu mikātu.
u-m: niṉkai; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.	          (51)
255.	ukara viṟuti yakara viyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ukaravīṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ukara iṟuti akara iyaṟṟu - ukara vīṟṟup peyar al vaḻikkaṇ akaraīṟṟu alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: kaṭukkuṟitu;  ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavarum.     	(52)
256.	cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉaru mattoḻiṟ ṟākum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuc cuṭṭu vaṉkaṇattoṭu kūṭi muṭiyu māṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉarum a toḻiṟṟu ākum - ukaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varumvaḻi av akaravīṟṟu al vaḻiyiṉ toḻiṟṟāy (205) valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m:  ukkoṟṟaṉ; cāttaṉ, tēvaṉ, pūtaṉ eṉa varum.  (53)
257.	ēṉavai variṉē mēṉilai yiyalpē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟuc cuṭṭu oḻinta kaṇattoṭu muṭiyu māṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉavai variṉ mēl nilai iyalpu - ukaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭiṉ muṉ oḻinta kaṇam varumoḻiyāka variṉ mēl akaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭu muṭintu niṉṟa nilaimaiyiṉ iyalpai yuṭaiyatāy muṭiyum.
u-m:	uññāṇ, unnūl, ummaṇi eṉavum; uvyāḻ, uv vaṭṭu eṉavum; uvvaṭai, uvvāṭai, uvveḷaviyam eṉavum; ūvayiṉāṉa eṉavum varum.	(54)
258.	cuṭṭumuta liṟuti yiyalpā kummē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku valleḻuttu vilakki iyalpu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal iṟuti iyalpākum - cuṭṭeḻut tiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya ukara vīṟṟuppeyar mēṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m: atukuṟitu, itukuṟitu, utukuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.	 (55)
259.	aṉṟuvaru kālai yāvā kutalum
	aivaru kālai meyvaraintu keṭutalum
	ceyyuṇ maruṅki ṉuritteṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuc cuṭṭumutaṟ peyarkku oru ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aṉṟu varukālai ā ākutalum - atikārattāṉ niṉṟa cuṭṭumutal ukaravīṟṟup peyar aṉṟu eṉṉum col varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu av vukaram ākāramākit tirintu muṭitalum, ai varukālai mey varaintu keṭutalum - ai eṉṉum cāriyai iṭaivantu muṭiyuṅ kālattu avvukaram tāṉ ūrnta meyyai oḻittuk keṭṭu muṭitalum, ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ urittu eṉa moḻipa - av viru muṭipum ceyyuṭkaṇ uritteṉṟu colvar.
u-m: atāaṉṟamma, itāaṉṟamma, utāaṉṟamma eṉavum; ataimaṟṟamma, itaimaṟṟamma, utaimaṟṟamma eṉavum varum.	(56)
260.	vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumaikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - ukaravīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumaipporuṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum av vakara vīṟṟu (204) alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmaittāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: kaṭukkāy; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum. 	(57)
261.	eruvuñ ceruvu mammoṭu civaṇit
	tiripiṭa ṉuṭaiya teriyuṅ kālai
	ammiṉ makarañ ceruvayiṟ keṭumē
	tammoṟṟu mikūum valleḻut tiyaṟkai.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakkic cāriyai vitiyum, oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttiṉoṭu cāriyai vitiyum kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) eruvum ceruvum ammoṭu civaṇi tiripu iṭaṉ uṭaiya teriyum kālai - eru eṉṉum collum ceru eṉṉum collum ammuccāriyaiyoṭu porunti muṉ coṉṉa vēṟṟumaip potu vitiyiṉ vēṟupaṭṭu muṭiyum iṭaṉuṭaiya ārāyuṅ kālattu; ammiṉ makaram ceruvayiṉ keṭum - av vammuc cāriyaiyatu īṟṟiṉ makaram ceru eṉṉum colliṭattuk keṭṭu muṭiyum; valleḻuttu iyaṟkai tam oṟṟu mikūum - avvāṟu keṭṭaviṭattuc ceru eṉpatu valleḻuttākiya iyalpaiyuṭaiya taṉatu oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: eruvaṅkuḻi; cēṟu, tātu, pūḻi eṉavum; ceruvakkaḷam, cēṉai, tāṉai, paṟai eṉavum varum.
‘teriyuṅ kālai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, eru eṉpataṟkuc ciṟupāṉmai melleḻuttuppēṟum, valleḻuttuppēṟum, ceru eṉpataṟkuc ciṟupāṉmai valleḻuttuppēṟum koḷka. eruṅkuḻi, erukkuḻi, cerukkaḷam eṉa varum.
‘valleḻuttiyaṟkai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi av virumoḻikkum atikāra valleḻuttu vīḻvum koḷka. eruviṉ kaṭumai, ceruviṉ kaṭumai eṉa varum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, “ammoṭu civaṇit tiripiṭa ṉuṭaiya” eṉa valleḻuttiṉkaṇṇatāka varaintu kūṟiṉamai yiṉ, ammuc cāriyai iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ peṟuvaṉa koḷka. eruva ñāṟci, ceruva ñāṟci eṉa varum. 	          (58)
262.	ḻakara vukara nīṭiṭa ṉuṭaittē
	ukaram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟiṉ cilavaṟṟiṟkuc ceyyuḷuḷ eytiyataṉ mēl ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ḻakara ukaram nīṭu iṭaṉ uṭaittu - iv vīṟṟu moḻikaḷuḷ ḻakarattoṭu kūṭiya ukaravīṟṟu moḻi av vukaram ūkāramāy nīṇṭu muṭiyum iṭaṉ uṭaittu; a vayiṉ ukaram varutal - avviṭattu ukaram vantu muṭika.
‘iṭaṉuṭaittu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, itu ceyyuḷiṭatteṉak koḷka.
u-m: “paḻūuppal laṉṉa paruvukirp pāvaṭi” eṉa varum.			 (59)
263.	oṭumarak kiḷavi yutimara viyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu marappeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) oṭumarak kiḷavi utimara iyaṟṟu - oṭu eṉṉum marattiṉai uṇaraniṉṟa col uti eṉṉum marattiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy  melleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: oṭuṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum. 	  (60)
264.	cuṭṭumuta liṟuti yurupiya ṉilaiyum
	oṟṟiṭai mikāa valleḻut tiyaṟkai.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku valleḻuttu vilakkic cāriyai vakutl nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal iṟuti urupu iyal nilaiyum - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya ukaravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ urupu puṇarcciyiṟ coṉṉa iyalpilē  niṉṟu aṉcāriyai peṟṟu ukaram keṭṭu muṭiyum; valleḻuttu iyaṟkai oṟṟu iṭaimikā - avviṭattu valleḻuttu iyaṟkaiyākiya oṟṟu iṭaikkaṇ mikā.
u-m: ataṉkōṭu, itaṉkōṭu, utaṉkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
‘oṟṟu iṭai mikā’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, cāriyai vakuppa valleḻuttu mikāteṉpatu peṟṟām. ‘valleḻut tiyaṟkai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iv vīṟṟuḷ urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. kaṭuviṉkuṟai, oṭuviṉpuṟam eṉa varum.	 (61)
265.	ūkāra viṟuti yākāra viyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ūkāravīṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ūkāra iṟuti ākāra iyaṟṟu - ūkāravīṟṟup peyar al vaḻikkaṇ ākāravīṟṟu  alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: koṇmūkkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.(62)
266.	viṉaiyeñcu kiḷavikku muṉṉilai moḻikkum
	niṉaiyuṅ kālai yavvakai varaiyār.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu viṉaiccoṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) viṉaiyeñcu kiḷavikkum muṉṉilai moḻikkum - ūkāravīṟṟu viṉaiyeccamākiya colliṟkum, muṉṉilai moḻi yākiya viṉaiccolliṟkum, niṉaiyumkālai avvakai varaiyār - ārāyuṅ kālattu av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum kūṟṟiṉai nīkkār e-ṟu.
u-m: uṇṇūk koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; kaitūk koṟṟā; cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉavum varum.
‘niṉaiyuṅ kālai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivvīṟṟu uyartiṇaip peyar alvaḻikkaṇ valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉa koḷka. āṭūukkuṟiyaṉ, makaṭūukkuṟiyaṉ eṉa varum.	 (63)
267.	vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu, ūkāravīṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - ūkāra vīṟu vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum av vākāra vīṟṟu al vaḻiyōṭu otta iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: koṇmūkkuḻām; celavu, tōṟṟam, paṟaivu eṉa varum.	 (64)
268.	kuṟṟeḻut timparu mōreḻuttu moḻikkum
	niṟṟal vēṇṭu mukarak kiḷavi.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟeḻuttu imparum ōr eḻuttu moḻikkum ukarak kiḷavi niṟṟal vēṇṭum - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉpiṉ niṉṟa ūkāravīṟṟu moḻikkum ōreḻuttorumoḻiyākiya ūkāra vīṟṟu moḻikkum ukaramākiya eḻuttu niṟṟalai vēṇṭum.
u-m:	uṭūukkuṟai, tūukkuṟai; ceykai, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.
‘niṟṟal’ eṉṟataṉāṉ ivvīṟṟu uyartiṇaippeyar vēṟṟumaik kaṇ valleḻuttu mikuvaṉavum koḷka.  āṭū ukkai, makaṭūukkai; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.  	(65)
269.	pūve ṉorupeya rāyiyal piṉṟē
	āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalu murittē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku ukaramum valleḻuttum vilakkip perumpāṉmai melleḻuttum ciṟupāṉmai valleḻuttum peṟumeṉa eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) pū eṉ oru peyar a iyalpu iṉṟu - pū eṉṉum ūkāra vīṟṟaiyuṭaiya oru peyar mēṟcoṉṉa ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyum anta iyalpillāmaiyai uṭaittu; eṉavē, vēṟu ōr iyalpiṟṟāy melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
a vayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum urittu - avviṭattu ām melleḻuttē yaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum urittu.
melleḻuttup peṟumeṉṟatu, uraiyiṟ kōṭalāṟ koḷḷap paṭṭatu.
u-m: pūṅkoṭi, pūkkoṭi; ceykai, tāmam, pantu eṉa varum.	  (66)
270.	ūve ṉorupeya rāvoṭu civaṇum.
iḵtu, iṉṉum av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku ukaramum valleḻuttum vilakki nilaimoḻi ṉakaram peṟumeṉa eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ū eṉ oru peyar āvoṭu civaṇum - ū eṉṟu collappaṭum ūkāravīṟi yuṭaiya oru peyar ākāra vīṟṟiṉ ā eṉṉum colloṭu porunti, ukaramum  valleḻuttum   peṟātu nilaimoḻi ṉakara voṟṟup peṟṟu  muṭiyum.
u-m: ūṉkuṟai; ceykai, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.  	 (67)

71.	akkeṉ cāriyai peṟutalu murittē
	takkavaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa.
iḵtu, iṉṉum ataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) akku eṉ cāriyai peṟutalum urittu - atikārattāṉ niṉṟa ū eṉṉum peyar mēṟkūṟiya ṉakarattōṭu akku eṉṉum cāriyai peṟṟu muṭitalum urittu; vaḻakkattāṉ  takkavaḻi aṟital - vaḻakkiṭattu am muṭipu takka iṭam aṟika.
‘takkavaḻi yaṟital’ eṉṟataṉāl, cāriyai peṟṟavaḻi nilaimoḻi ṉakaram vilakkuṇṇātu niṟṟalum, muṉ (270) māṭṭēṟṟāṉ vilakkuṇṭa valleḻuttuk keṭātu niṟṟalum koḷka.
u-m: ūṉakkuṟai; ceykai, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.
‘vaḻakkattāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivvīṟṟu urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. koṇmūviṉ kuḻām eṉa varum.	(68)
272.	āṭūu makaṭūu vāyiru peyarkkum
	iṉṉiṭai variṉu māṉa millai.
iḵtu av vīṟṟu uyartiṇaip peyarkku muṉ eytiya valleḻuttē yaṉṟi, cāriyaiyum peṟumeṉa eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṭūu makaṭūu a iru peyarkkum - āṭūu makaṭūu vākiya avviraṇṭu peyarkkum, iṉ iṭaivariṉum māṉam illai - mēl eytiya (“kuṟṟeḻut timparum’ uyirmayaṅkiyal - 65) eṉṉum cūttirattiṉ ‘niṟṟal’ eṉpataṉāṉ vanta valleḻuttēyaṉṟi iṉcāriyai iṭaivariṉum kuṟṟam illai.
u-m:  āṭūuviṉkai, makaṭūuviṉkai; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.
‘māṉa millai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉ peṟṟavaḻi mēl ilēciṉā ṉeytiya valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	  (69)
273.	ekara vokaram peyarkkī ṟākā
	muṉṉilai moḻiya veṉmaṉār pulavar
	tēṟṟamuñ ciṟappu malvaḻi yāṉa.
iḵtu, ekaravīṟṟiṟkum okaravīṟṟiṟkum īṟākāta nilaiyil vēṟupāṭu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ekaram okaram peyarkku īṟu ākā - ekaramum okaramum peyarccoṟku īṟu ākā, muṉṉilai moḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar - viṉaiccolluḷ muṉṉilai moḻiyiṭat taṉaveṉṟu colluvar pulavar, tēṟṟamum ciṟappum alvaḻiyāṉa - tēṟṟap poruṇmaiyiṉ varum iṭaiccol ekāra vīṟum ciṟappup poruṇmaiyiṉ varum iṭaiccol okāra vīṟum allātaviṭattu.
u-m:	ēe eṉavum, ōo eṉavum varum. ivai muṉṉilai viṉai. ēe koṇṭāṉ, ōo koṇṭāṉ. ivai iṭaic col.	  (70)
274.	tēṟṟa vekaramuñ ciṟappi ṉovvum
	mēṟkū ṟiyaṟkai valleḻut tumikā.
itu, muṉ īṟām eṉṉappaṭṭa ekara okara īṟṟu iṭaiccoṟkum, av vīṟṟu muṉṉilai viṉaiccoṟkum muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tēṟṟa ekaramum ciṟappiṉ ovvum - tēṟṟap poruṇ maiyil varum ekaravīṟṟu iṭaiccollum ciṟappup poruṇmaiyil varum okaravīṟṟu iṭaiccollum, mēl kūṟu iyaṟkai valleḻuttu mikā - mēlai muṉṉilai viṉaic coṟkuk kūṟappaṭum iyalpuṭaiya valleḻuttu mikāvāy iyalpāy muṭiyum.
‘mēṟkūṟiyaṟkai valleḻuttu mikā’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ‘vantatu (muṉṉilai viṉai) koṇṭu vārātatu uṇarka’ eṉṉum tantiravutti vakaiyāṉ valleḻuttu mikumeṉpatu kūṟappaṭṭatāyiṟṟu.
u-m: yāṉēe koṇṭēṉ, nīyēe koṇṭāy, avaṉēe koṇṭāṉ eṉavum; ōokoṇṭēṉ, ōo koṇ ṭāy, ōokoṇṭāṉ eṉavum varum. ivai iṭaiccol. ēekkoṟṟā, ōokkoṟṟā; cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉa ivai muṉṉilaiviṉai.
‘iyaṟkai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, am muṉṉilai viṉaikaḷai aḷapeṭaiyāka niṟīik koḷka.	 (71)
275.	ēkāra viṟuti yūkāra viyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ēkāravīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ēkāra iṟuti ūkāra iyaṟṟu - ēkāravīṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ ūkāravīṟṟu alvaḻi iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
u-m: cēkkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.	 (72)
276.	māṟuko ḷeccamum viṉāvu meṇṇum
	kūṟiya valleḻut tiyaṟkai yākum.
iḵtu, avvīṟṟu iṭaiccoṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) māṟukoḷ eccamum viṉāvum eṇṇum - māṟupāṭu kōṭalaiyuṭaiya eccap poruṇmaikkaṇ varum ēkāra vīṟṟu iṭaiccollum, viṉāpporuṇmaikkaṇ varum ēkāra vīṟṟu iṭaiccollum, eṇṇup poruṇmaik kaṇ varum ēkāra vīṟṟu iṭaic collum, kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟkai ākum - mēṟkūṟiya valleḻuttup peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m: yāṉē koṇṭēṉ, ceṉṟēṉ, tantēṉ, pōyiṉēṉ eṉavum; nīyē koṇṭāy, ceṉṟāy, tantāy, pōyiṉāy eṉavum; koṟṟaṉē cāttuṉē tēvaṉē pūtaṉē eṉavum varum.
‘kūṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, pirinilaip poruṇmaikkaṇṇum, īṟṟacaikkaṇṇum varum ēkāraṅkaḷiṉ iyalpu muṭipum koḷka.
avaṉē koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu - pirinilai. ‘kaṭalē pāṭeḻun tolikkum’  eṉpatu - īṟṟacai.          (73)
277.	vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟu vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - ēkāra vīṟu vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum avvūkāra vīṟṟu (265) alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmaittāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m:  vēkkuṭam; cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai eṉa varum.      (74)
278.	ēye ṉiṟutik kekaram varumē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟiṟku eytiyataṉmēl ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ē eṉ iṟutikku ekaram varum - av vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ē eṉṉum iṟutikku ekaram varum.
u-m:	ēekkoṭṭil; cālai, tuḷai, puḻai eṉa varum.
‘uraiyiṟkōṭal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, av vekarappēṟu poruntiṉa vaḻik koḷka.	 (75)
279.	cēveṉ marappeya roṭumara viyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki mel leḻuttu vitittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cē eṉ marappeyar oṭu mara iyaṟṟu - cē eṉṉum marattiṉai uṇaraniṉṟa peyar oṭumarattiṉ (263) iyalpiṟṟāy melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m:	cēṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.     (76)
280.	peṟṟa māyiṉ muṟṟaviṉ vēṇṭum.
iḵtu, am marappeyarallāta cē eṉpataṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) peṟṟam āyiṉ muṟṟa iṉ vēṇṭum - mēṟkūṟiya cē eṉpatu peṟṟattiṉai uṇaraniṉṟa poḻutāyiṉ muṭiya iṉcāriyai peṟṟu muṭiya vēṇṭum.
‘muṟṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ic cē eṉpatu eṭuttōttāṉ iṉ peṟṟavaḻiyum, atuvē marappeyarāy urupiṟku eytiya cāriyai (iṉ) peṟṟavaḻiyum, piṟacol avvāṟu iṉ peṟṟavaḻiyum iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.
u-m: 	cēviṉkōṭu; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉavum; cēviṉ kōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum;  ēviṉ kaṭumai, ciṟumai, tīmai, perumai eṉavum varum.	 (77)
281.	aikāra iṟutip peyarnilai muṉṉar
	vēṟṟumai yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, aikāraīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu tokaimarapiṉuṭ 
(e. 159) kūṟi niṉṟamaiyiṉ, ataṉ vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu. 
(i-ḷ.) aikāra iṟutip peyarnilai muṉṉar - aikāra īṟṟup peyarccol muṉṉar, vēṟṟumai āyiṉ valleḻuttu mikum - atikārattāṟ ka ca ta pa mutalmoḻi vantavaḻi vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarcciyil tamakkup poruntiṉa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: yāṉaikkōṭu; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.     (78)
282.	cuṭṭumutal iṟuti urupiyal nilaiyum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cuṭṭu mutaṟpeyarkku valleḻut toṭu vaṟṟu vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal iṟuti urupu iyal nilaiyum - cuṭṭeḻut tiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya aikāravīṟṟuppeyar urupu puṇarcciyiṟ (178) kūṟiya iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu aikāram keṭātum keṭṭum vaṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: avaiyaṟṟukkōṭu, ivaiyaṟṟukkōṭu, uvai yaṟṟuk kōṭu; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉavum; avaṟṟuk kōṭu, ivaṟṟukkōṭu, uvaṟṟukkōṭu; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉavum varum.	 (79)
283.	vicaimarak kiḷaviyum ñemaiyum namaiyum
	avaimup peyaruñ cēmara iyala.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ marappeyar cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vicaimarakkiḷaviyum ñemaiyum namaiyum avai muppeyarum - vicai eṉṉum marattiṉai uṇaraniṉṟa collum ñemai eṉṉum marattiṉai uṇaraniṉṟa collum namai eṉṉum marattiṉai uṇaraniṉṟa collum ākiya am mūṉṟu peyarum, cēmara iyala - mēṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu mikātu cē eṉṉum marattiṉatu iyalpiṉavāy (279) melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: vicaiṅkōṭu, ñemaiṅkōṭu, namaiṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.	(80)
284.	paṉaiyu maraiyu māviraik kiḷaviyum
	niṉaiyuṅ kālai yammoṭu civaṇum
	aiye ṉiṟuti yaraivaraintu keṭumē
	meyyava ṇoḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉaiyum araiyum āviraik kiḷaviyum - paṉai eṉṉum collum arai eṉṉum collum āvirai eṉṉum collum, niṉaiyuṅkālai ammoṭu civaṇum - ārāyuṅ kālattu mēṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu mikātu, am eṉṉum cāriyaiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum, ai eṉ iṟuti arai varaintu keṭum - avviṭattu ai eṉṉum īṟu arai eṉṉum collai nīkkik (ēṉaiyiraṇṭilum) keṭum, mey avaṇ oḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar - taṉṉāṉūrappaṭṭa mey ac colliṭattē oḻiyaveṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m: paṉaṅkāy, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum; araiyaṅ kōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum; āviraṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum varum.
‘niṉaiyuṅ kālai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ piṟavum tūtuṇai, vaḻutuṇai, tillai, ōlai eṉa varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum ammuk koṭuttu aikāram keṭuttut tūtuṇaṅkāy, vaḻutuṇaṅkāy, tillaṅkāy, ōlampōḻ eṉṟu muṭikka.	     (81)
285.	paṉaiyiṉ muṉṉa raṭṭuvaru kālai
	nilaiyiṉ ṟāku maiye ṉuyirē
	ākāram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉaiyiṉ muṉṉar aṭṭu varukālai - mēṟkūṟiya vaḻiyēyaṉṟip paṉai eṉṉum colliṉ muṉṉar aṭṭu eṉṉum col varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, nilai iṉṟākum ai eṉ uyir - niṟṟal iṉṟām ai eṉṉum uyir; a vayiṉāṉa ākāram varutal - avviṭattu ākāram vantu am meymēl ēṟi muṭika.
u-m: paṉāaṭṭu eṉa varum.
‘āvayiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, viccāvāti eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa vaṟṟatu vēṟṟumai muṭipu koḷka.	(82)
286.	koṭimuṉ variṉē paiyava ṇiṟpak
