	kaṭinilai yiṉṟē valleḻuttu mikuti.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) koṭimuṉ variṉ - koṭi eṉṉum col paṉai eṉṉum colmuṉṉar variṉ, ai avaṇ niṟpa valleḻuttu mikuti kaṭinilai iṉṟu mēṟkeṭum eṉappaṭṭa aikāram āṇṭuk keṭātē niṟpa, valleḻuttu mikuti nīkkum nilaimaiyiṉṟu.
u-m: paṉaikkoṭi eṉa varum.
‘kaṭinilai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iv vīṟṟuḷ eṭuttōttāṉum ilēciṉāṉum ammuccāriyaiyum piṟa cāriyaiyum peṟṟavaḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkuc ceṉṟavaḻiyum iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. u-m: paṉaiyiṉ kāy, araiyiṉkōṭu, āviraiyiṉ kōṭu eṉavum; vicaiyiṉkōṭu, ñemaiyiṉ kōṭu, namaiyiṉ kōṭu eṉavum; tūtuṇaiyiṉkāy, vaḻutuṇaiyiṉkāy eṉavum; vaḻaiyiṉ kōṭu, vaḻaiyiṉ pū eṉavum varum. ‘avaṇ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, paṉaittiraḷ eṉa valleḻuttup pēṟum paṉaintiraḷ eṉṟa melleḻuttup pēṟum koḷka.	 (83)

287.	tiṅkaḷu nāḷu muntukiḷan taṉṉa.
iḵtu, iyaipu valleḻuttiṉoṭu cāriyaippēṟum, valleḻuttu vilakkic cāriyaippēṟum kūṟukiṉṟamaiyiṉ, eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappu vitiyum, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu vitiyum vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tiṅkaḷum nāḷum muntu kiḷantaṉṉa - aikāra vīṟṟut tiṅkaḷai uṇaraniṉṟa peyarum av vīṟṟu nāḷai uṇara niṉṟa peyarum muṉ ikara īṟṟut tiṅkaḷum  nāḷum kiḷanta taṉmaiyavāy ikkum āṉum peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: cittiraikkuk koṇṭāṉ; kēṭṭaiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
nāḷ muṉkūṟātu tiṅkaḷ muṉkūṟiyavataṉāl, kariyavaṟṟuk kōṭu eṉavum; avaiyattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉavum; vaḻaiṅ kōṭu, vaḻaik kōṭu eṉavum; kalaiṅkōṭu, kalaikkōṭu eṉavum ivvīṟṟu muṭiyā taṉavellām koḷka.	(84)
288.	maḻaiyeṉ kiḷavi vaḷiyiya ṉilaiyum.
iḵtu, iyaipu valleḻuttiṉōṭu attuppēṟum, valleḻuttu vilakki iṉṉum vakukkiṉṟamaiyiṉ, eytiya taṉmēṟ ciṟappum, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituvitiyum kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) maḻai eṉ kiḷavi vaḷi iyal nilaiyum - maḻai eṉṉum aikāravīṟṟuccol, ikaravīṟṟu vaḷi eṉṉum col (243) attum iṉṉum peṟṟu muṭinta iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: maḻaiyattuk koṇṭāṉ, maḻaiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.	    (85)
289.	ceyyuṇ maruṅkiṉ vēṭkai yeṉṉum
	aiye ṉiṟuti yavāmuṉ variṉē
	meyyoṭuṅ keṭuta leṉmaṉār pulavar
	ṭakāra ṇakāra mātal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku alvaḻikkaṇ ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ vēṭkai eṉṉum ai eṉ iṟuti - ceyyuḷiṭattu alvaḻikkaṇ vēṭkai eṉṉum aikāra vīṟṟuccol, avā muṉvariṉ - avā eṉṉum col taṉakku muṉvariṉ, meyyoṭum keṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar - av vaikāram tāṉ ūrnta meyyoṭuṅkūṭak keṭukaveṉṟu colluvar pulavar; ṭakāram ṇakāram ātal vēṇṭum - avviṭattu niṉṟa ṭakāravoṟṟu ṇakāra voṟṟāyt tirintu muṭital vēṇṭum.
u-m: “vēṇavā naliya veyya vuyirā” (naṟ. 61) eṉa varum. itaṉai ummaittokaiyākak koḷka. avāveṉpatu av vēṭkaiyiṉ mikuti.
iv valvaḻiyai vēṟṟumai muṭipiṟku muṉ kūṟātataṉāṉ, viccāvāti eṉṟāṟpōla varum ummaittokai alvaḻi muṭipum, pāṟaṅkal eṉa irupeyaroṭṭu alvaḻi muṭipum koḷka.	 (86)
290.	ōkāra viṟuti yēkāra viyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ōkāravīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ōkāra iṟuti ēkāra iyaṟṟu - ōkāravīṟṟu  alvaḻip peyarccol ēkāravīṟṟu (275) alvaḻi iyal piṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum.
u-m: ōkkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum. 	(87)
291.	māṟuko ḷeccamum viṉāvu maiyamum
	kūṟiya valleḻut tiyaṟkai yākum.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟu iṭaiccol muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu. 
(i-ḷ.) māṟukoḷ eccamum viṉāvum aiyamum - māṟu pāṭṭiṉaik koṇṭa eccapporuṇmaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya ōkāramum, viṉāp poruṇmaiyai yuṭaiya ōkāramum, aiyapporuṇmaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya ōkāramum, kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟkai ākum - muṉ peyarkkuk kūṟiya valleḻuttiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum.

u-m: yāṉōkoṇṭēṉ eṉavum, nīyō koṇṭāy eṉavum, pattō patiṉoṉṟō eṉavum varum.
‘kūṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, pirinilaiyum, terinilaiyum, ciṟappum, eṇṇum, īṟṟacaiyum iyalpāy muṭital koḷka. avaṉōkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum, naṉṟō tītōvaṉṟu eṉavum, ōo koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, “kuṉṟuṟaḻnta kaḷiṟeṉkō koyyuḷaiya māveṉkō” (puṟam. 387) eṉavum ‘yāṉō tēṟēṉ’ (kuṟun. 21) eṉavum varum.	 (88)
292.	oḻintata ṉilaiyu moḻintavaṟ ṟiyaṟṟē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) oḻintataṉ nilaiyum oḻintavaṟṟu iyaṟṟu - oḻiyicai ōkārattiṉatu nilaiyum mēṟcolliyoḻinta ōkāraṅkaḷiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m: koḷalō koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.	(89)

293.	vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē
	okaram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - ōkāra vīṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum av vōkāravīṟṟu (290) alvaḻiyō ṭottu valleḻuttup peṟṟup puṇarum; a vayiṉāṉa okaram varutal - avviṭattu okaram varuka. 
u-m: ōokkaṭumai; ciṟumai, tīmai, perumai eṉa varum.  (90)
294.	illoṭu kiḷappi ṉiyaṟkai yākum.
iḵtu, eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) illoṭu kiḷappiṉ iyaṟkai ākum - ōkāra vīṟṟuk kō eṉṉum moḻiyiṉai il eṉṉum varu moḻiyoṭu colliṉ ōkāram mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m: kōyil eṉa varum.
nilaimoḻi okaraveḻuttuppēṟu varaiyātu kūṟiṉavaḻi nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇum cellumeṉpatu itaṉāṟ peṟṟām.
kōveṉṟatu uyartiṇaip peyaraṉṟōveṉiṉ, kōvantatu eṉa aḵṟiṇaiyāy muṭitaliṉ aḵṟiṇaippāṟ paṭṭatu pōlum.	(91)
295.	urupiya ṉilaiyu moḻiyumā ruḷavē
	āvayiṉ valleḻut tiyaṟkai yākum.
iḵtu, avaṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku valleḻuttu vilakkic cāriyai vakuttamaiyiṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) urupuiyal nilaiyum moḻiyumār uḷa - av vīṟṟuṭ cila urupu puṇarcciyatu iyalpilē niṉṟu oṉcāriyai peṟṟu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa; a vayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟkai ākum - avviṭattu valleḻuttiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m: kōoṉkai; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.
itaṉāṉum peṟṟām, cāriyaippēṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttu vilakkāmai.	  (92)
296.	oḷakāra viṟutip peyarnilai muṉṉar
	alvaḻi yāṉum vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum
	valleḻuttu mikutal varainilai yiṉṟē
	avviru vīṟṟum ukaram varutal
	cevvi teṉpa ciṟantici ṉōrē.
iḵtu, oḷakāra īṟu alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) oḷakāra iṟuti peyarnilai muṉṉar alvaḻiyāṉum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum valleḻuttu mikutal varainilai iṉṟu - oḷakāravīṟṟup peyarccol muṉṉar valleḻuttu mutalmoḻi variṉ avai alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum valleḻuttu mikku muṭital nīkkum nilaiyiṉṟām; a iru vīṟṟum ukaram varutal cevvitu eṉpa ciṟanticiṉōr - av virukūṟṟu muṭipiṉkaṇṇum nilai moḻik kaṇ ukaram vantu muṭital cevviteṉṟu colluvar ciṟantōr.
u-m:	kauvukkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; kauvuk kaṭumai; ciṟumai, tīmai, perumai eṉavum varum.
‘cevvitu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, meṉkaṇattukkaṇṇum iṭaik kaṇattukkaṇṇum iruvaḻiyum ukarappēṟu koḷka. u-m: kauvu ñāṉṟatu, kauvuñāṟci eṉavum; kauvu valitu, kauvu valimai eṉavum varum.
‘nilai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, kauviṉ kaṭumai eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi, iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻvu koḷka.	 (93)
ēḻāvatu - uyirmayaṅkiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.



puḷḷi mayaṅkiyal
meyyīṟu vaṉkaṇattōṭum piṟakaṇattōṭum puṇarumāṟu kūṟuvatu puḷḷi mayaṅkiyalākum. meyyīṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu niṟkumātaliṉ puḷḷiyeṉṟār. meyyīṟṟuḷ ukarampeṟuvaṉa, iṟuti keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikuvaṉa melleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, iṟutikeṭātu valleḻuttu mikuvaṉa melleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, valleḻuttum melleḻuttum uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉa, iyalpāy varuvaṉa, cāriyai peṟuvaṉa, tirintu muṭivaṉa eṉṉum ivvakaiyiṉuḷ ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭa vitikaḷyāvum aṭaṅku vaṉavām.
ña, na, ṇa, ma, la, ḷa eṉṉum meykaḷai iṟutiyākavuṭaiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉar valleḻuntu mutaṉmoḻi variṉ iru vaḻiyum varumoḻi valleḻuttu mika nilaimoḻiyīṟu ukaram peṟṟu muṭiyum. nakara vīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ukarampeṟātu akarampeṟṟu muṭiyum, īm, kam, urum, miṉ, piṉ, kaṉ, val, tev, puḷ, vaḷ eṉa eṭuttōtiya peyarkaḷum toḻiṟpeyarpōla iruvaḻiyum ukarampeṟṟu valleḻuttu mikuvaṉavām. avaṟṟuḷ kaṉ eṉṉuñcol vēṟṟumaikkaṇ akaram peṟṟu valleḻuttu mikappeṟum. val eṉṉuñ colliṉmuṉ nāy, palakai eṉpaṉa varumoḻiyāyvariṉ avvaḻi ukaramiṉṟi akarampeṟṟu muṭiyum.
verin eṉṟa col, iṟuti nakaravoṟṟum accol peṟṟa akaramumkeṭa valleḻuttu varumvaḻi avvalleḻut tāyiṉum ataṉ kiḷaiyeḻuttākiya melleḻuttāyiṉum mikku muṭiyum, makaravīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumaikkaṇ iṟuti makaramkeṭa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum. iṅṅaṉam makara vīṟukeṭṭa viṭattu varumoḻi valleḻuttōṭu melleḻuttu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum cila uḷa. āyiram eṉpataṉmuṉ aḷavuppeyar vantu puṇarumiṭattu vēṟṟumaiyiṟpōla iṟuti makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikum. illam eṉṉum marappeyar iṟuti makaraṅkeṭṭu melleḻuttu mikum. aḻaṉ eṉpataṉ iṟutikeṭa varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum.
vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇvarum yaraḻa vīṟṟup peyarkaḷ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu avval leḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉa. tāy eṉṉuñcolmuṉ makaṉ viṉaivariṉ valleḻuttu mikum. ār, vetir, cār, pīr, kumiḻ eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ valleḻuttu varuvaḻi melleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉa. vēṟṟumaikkaṇ yakara vīṟṟuṭ cilavum cār, pāḻ eṉpaṉavum valleḻuttōṭu melleḻuttuppeṟṟu uṟaḻvaṉavām. kīḻ eṉṉuñcol valleḻuttuppeṟṟum peṟātum uṟaḻum.
āṇ, peṇ, umaṇ, muraṇ, kuyiṉ, ekiṉ, tāṉ, pēṉ, kōṉ eṉpaṉavum neṭṭaḻuttiṉ piṉvarum lakāra, ḷakāra vīṟṟuppeyarkaḷ cilavum tiriyātu iyalpāvaṉavām. tāy eṉṉum peyarum alvaḻikkaṇvarum yakāra vīṟṟup peyarkaḷum valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāvaṉa. alvaḻik kaṇvarum ellārum, tām, nām, yām, tāṉ eṉpaṉa kuṟukalun tiritalumiṉṟi iyalpāvaṉa. nūṟāyiram, tāmarai, veḷḷam, āmpal eṉpavaṟṟōṭum ēṉai uyir mutaṉmoḻikaḷōṭum puṇarum ēḻ eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyarum, uyir mutaṉ moḻikaḷōṭum yakara vakara mutaṉmoḻikaḷōṭum puṇarum vakara vīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyarkaḷum kuṟukalun tiritalumiṉṟi iyalpāvaṉavām.
āṇ, ekiṉ, pīr, pūl, vēl, āl, kumiḻ eṉpaṉa amcāriyaiyum, vēṟṟumaikkaṇvarum īm, kam eṉpaṉavum kōl eṉpataṉōṭu puṇarum tāḻ eṉṉuñ collum, tamiḻ eṉṉum collum akkuccāriyaiyum, veyil, iruḷ eṉpaṉa attuccāriyaiyum iṉcāriyaiyum, makaravīṟṟu nāṭpeyar ikara vīṟṟu nāṭpeyarpōla āṉcāriyaiyum ataṉmēl attuc cāriyaiyum, ceyyuḷiṭattuvarum viṇ eṉṉuñcol viṉaic col varumoḻiyāka varumiṭattu attuccāriyaiyum, taṉittum aṭaiyaṭuttum varum āyiram eṉṟa eṇṇuppeyar piṟa eṇkaḷōṭu puṇarumiṭattu attuccāriyaiyum peṟuvaṉa eṉpar āciriyar.
ellārum eṉṉum paṭarkkaippeyarum, ellīrum eṉṉum muṉṉilaippeyarum, neṭumutal kuṟuki muṭiyumiyal puṭaiya tām, nām, yām eṉṉum peyarkaḷum, ṉakara vīṟṟuḷ tāṉ, yāṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum cāriyaipeṟuvaṉa īṟukeṭṭu iṭaiyilum iṟutiyilum cāriyaipeṟṟum, neṭumutal kuṟukuvaṉa neṭumutal kuṟukiyum, urupu puṇarccikkaṇ muṭintavāṟē vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarcciyiṉum muṭiyumeṉa āciriyar māṭṭēṟṟu muṟaiyāṉ vitikūṟiyuḷḷār. ellām eṉṉum viravuppeyar vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ iṭaiyē vaṟṟuccāriyaiyum iṟutiyil umcāriyaiyum peṟum. appeyar alvaḻikkaṇ cāriyai peṟātu. uyartiṇaikkaṇ iṭaiyē nam cāriyaiyum iṟutiyil umcāriyaiyumpeṟum. vakara vīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyar vaṟṟuccāriyaipeṟum. ēḻ eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar aṉcāriyai peṟum.
ṇakara ḷakara vīṟukaḷ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl ṭakaramākavum, ṉakara lakara vīṟukaḷ ṟakaramākavum tirivaṉa. eḷḷai yuṇarttum eṇ eṉṉum peyariṉ ṇakaram alvaḻiyilum ṭakara mākattiriyum. nel, cel, kol, col eṉpavaṟṟiṉ lakaram alvaḻiyilum ṟakaramāyttiriyum. alvaḻikkaṇvarum ḷakaravīṟu tirintum tiriyātum uṟaḻntu muṭiyum. vēṟṟumaiyiṟpōla alvaḻiyilum ṭakaramākattiriyum ḷakara vīṟukaḷ cilavuḷa.
maṉ, ciṉ, āṉ, īṉ, piṉ, muṉ eṉpaṉavum ceyiṉ eṉṉum viṉaiyeccamum avvayiṉ, ivvayiṉ, uvvayiṉ evvayiṉ eṉavarum ēḻām vēṟṟumai yiṭapporuḷuṇarttum iṭaic coṟkaḷum ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramākattiriyum. mīṉ eṉpataṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramākattirintum tiriyātum uṟaḻum.
lakara ḷakara vīṟṟuccoṟkaḷ takaramutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu lakara ḷakaraṅkaḷ āytamākattiriyum. melleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl muṟaiyē lakaram ṉakaramākavum ḷakaram ṇakaramākavum tirivaṉa.
makaravīṟu alvaḻikkaṇ valivariṉ ēṟṟamelleḻuttākat tiriyum. akam eṉṉuñ colliṉmuṉ kai eṉpatu varumoḻiyāyvariṉ nilaimoḻiyiṉ naṭuveḻuttākiya kakaravuyir meykeṭa melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum. num eṉpataṉai alvaḻikkaṇ kūṟuṅkāl nakaravoṟṟiṉmēl niṉṟa ukaram keṭa avvoṟṟiṉmēl īkāram ūrntu nī eṉṟāki ōr ikaram iṭaiyilvara iṟuti makaraṅkeṭṭu rakara voṟṟut tōṉṟi nīyir eṉattiriyumeṉpar tolkāppiyar. eṉavē num eṉpatē tiripil colleṉṟum nīyir eṉpatu ataṉ tiripeṉṟuṅ koḷḷutalvēṇṭum.
tēṉ eṉṉuñcol, valivariṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramākat tirintum tiriyātum uṟaḻtalum, iṟuti ṉakaraṅkeṭṭu valimeli mikutalum, melivariṉ iṟutikeṭṭum keṭātum uṟaḻtalum peṟum. iṟāl eṉpatu varumoḻiyākavariṉ tēṉ eṉpataṉ ṉakaram keṭṭut takaram iraṭṭittu muṭiyum.
cāttaṉ, koṟṟaṉ mutaliya iyaṟpeyar muṉṉart tantaiyeṉpatu varumoḻiyāyvariṉ, tantai yeṉpataṉ mutaliluḷḷa takaraṅkeṭṭu, ataṉmēlēṟiya akaraṅkeṭātu niṟpa, nilaimoḻiyiyaṟpeyariluḷḷa aṉkeṭṭu, aṅku niṉṟa meyyiṉmēl varumoḻi mutaliluḷḷa akaram ēṟimuṭiyum. muṟkūṟiya iyaṟpeyaruḷ ātaṉ, pūtaṉ eṉṉum irupeyarkaḷum varumoḻiyākiya tantaiyeṉṉum muṟaippeyaroṭu puṇaruṅkāl ivviyaṟ peyarkaḷiṉ iṟutiniṉṟa aṉkeṭa eñciya takaravoṟṟum, tantai eṉpatil muṉkūṟiyapaṭi takaraṅkeṭa eñciya akaravuyirum cērakkeṭṭu, āntai, pūntai eṉa muṭivaṉavām. ippeyarkaḷ ciṟappup paṇpaṭuttuvaruṅkāl aṅṅaṉantiritaliṉṟi iyalpāvaṉa eṉpar. mēṟkūṟiya iyaṟpeyarkaḷ iṉṉāṟku makaṉ iṉṉāṉ eṉṉum muṟaiyil oṭṭi niṟkuṅkāl nilaimoḻip peyarīṟṟiṉ aṉkeṭṭu amcāriyai peṟṟup puṇartalumuṇṭu.
muṉ eṉṉum colmuṉ il eṉṉuñcol vantu puṇariṉ iṭaiyē ṟakaravoṟṟut tōṉṟi muṉṟil eṉamuṭital toṉṟu toṭṭu maruvivaḻaṅkum ilakkaṇa muṭipām. poṉ eṉṉuñcol, īṟṟil ṉakaramkeṭa lakaravuyirmeyyum makara meyyumpeṟṟup polam eṉat tirintuvaḻaṅkum. il eṉṉum col, vīṭṭai yuṇarttātu illāmaiyeṉṉum poruḷai yuṇarttuṅkāl, aikāram peṟṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikutalum, mikāmaiyum, iyalpātalum, ākārampeṟṟu valleḻuttu mikutalum ākiya nāṉkumuṭipiṉai yuṭaiyatākum.
- ka. veḷḷaivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvaricai 10, pak. 181-185

eṭṭāvatu
puḷḷimayaṅkiyal
ivvōttu eṉṉa peyarttōveṉiṉ, puḷḷiyīṟu vaṉkaṇat tōṭum ciṟupāṉmai piṟa kaṇattōṭum mayaṅkip puṇarum iyalpu uṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ puḷḷimayaṅkiyal eṉṉum peyarttu.
297.	ñakārai yoṟṟiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉar
	allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum
	valleḻut tiyaiyi ṉavveḻuttu mikumē
	ukaram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.
it talaic cūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, ñakāra īṟu vaṉkaṇattōṭu iruvaḻikkaṇṇum puṇarumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ñakārai oṟṟiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉar - ñakāram īṟṟiṉkaṇ oṟṟāka niṉṟa toḻiṟpeyariṉ muṉṉar, allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum - alvaḻiyaic collu miṭattum vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum, valleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ av veḻuttu mikum - valleḻuttu mutalmoḻi varumoḻiyāy iyaiyiṉ valleḻuttu varumoḻikkaṇ mikku muṭiyum, āvayiṉāṉa ukaram varutal - āṇṭu nilaimoḻik kaṇ ukaram varuka.
u-m:	uriñuk kaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; uriñuk kaṭumai; ciṟumai, tīmai, perumai eṉavum varum.	 (1)
298.	ñanamava viyaiyiṉu mukara nilaiyum.
iḵtu, av vīṟu meṉkaṇattōṭum iṭaikkaṇattu vakarat toṭum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ña na ma va iyaiyiṉum ukaram nilaiyum - anta ñakāravīru vīṉkaṇamaṉṟi ña na ma va mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻi yāy iyaiyiṉum nilaimoḻikkaṇ ukaram nilaipeṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m:	 uriñu ñāṉṟatu; nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu eṉavum; uriñu ñāṟci;  nīṭci, māṭci eṉavum; uriñuvalitu, valimai eṉavum varum.
iṭaikkaṇattu yakarattōṭum uyirōṭum puṇarumāṟu tokaimarapiṉuḷ “ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti” (cūt. 21) eṉpataṉuṭ kūṟappaṭṭatu.	(2)
299.	nakara viṟutiyu mataṉō raṟṟē.
itu, nakaravīṟu mēṟkūṟiya kaṇaṅkaḷōṭu oruvaḻi muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nakara iṟutiyum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - nakaraīṟṟup peyarum mēṟkūṟiya kaṇaṅkaḷoṭu puṇarumvaḻi añ ñakāra īṟṟōṭu ōr iyalpiṟṟāy muṭiyum.
u-m: porunuk kaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; porunu ñāṉṟatu; nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu eṉavum; porunu valitu eṉavum oṭṭuka.	(3)
300.	vēṟṟumaik kukkeṭa vakara nilaiyum.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ nilaimoḻi muṭipu vēṟāy muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumaikku u keṭa akaram nilaiyum - an nakara vīṟu vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ eytiya nilaimoḻi ukaram keṭa akaram nilaipeṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m:	porunak kaṭumai; ciṟumai, tīmai, perumai eṉavum; poruna ñāṟci; nīṭci, māṭci eṉavum; poruna valimai eṉavum varum.
‘akaram nilaiyum’ eṉṉātu ‘ukaraṅ keṭa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, av vīr īṟṟiṉ urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻvum ciṟupāṉmai ukarappēṟum koḷka. poruniṉ kuṟai, uriñiṉ kuṟai eṉavum, “uyaval yāṉai porunucceṉ ṟaṉṉa” (akam. 65) eṉavum varum.	(4)
301.	verine ṉiṟuti muḻutuṅ keṭuvaḻi
	varumiṭa ṉuṭaittē melleḻut tiyaṟkai.
iḵtu, an nakaravīṟṟu orumoḻikku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) verin eṉ iṟuti muḻutum keṭuvaḻi - verin eṉṟu collappaṭum nakaravīṟṟumoḻi taṉ īṟṟu nakaram muṉ peṟṟa akarattōṭu eñcāmaṟ keṭṭaviṭattu, melleḻuttu iyaṟkai varum iṭaṉ uṭaittu - melleḻuttup peṟum iyalpu vantu muṭiyum iṭaṉuṭaittu.
u-m: veriṅ kuṟai; ceykai, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.	(5)
302.	āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalu murittē.
iḵtu, iṉṉum am moḻikku eytiyatu oru muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) a vayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum urittu - av verin eṉṉum col avvāṟu īṟu keṭṭu niṉṟaviṭattu melleḻuttē yaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭitalum urittu.
u-m: verik kuṟai; ceykai, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.
ñakāravīṟṟoṭu nakāravīṟu otta muṭipiṟṟātaliṉ uṭaṉ kūṟappaṭṭatu.	(6)
303.	ṇakāra viṟuti valleḻut tiyaiyiṉ
	ṭakāra mākum vēṟṟumaip poruṭkē.
itu, ṇakāravīṟu vēṟṟumaipporuṭkaṇ puṇarumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ṇakāra iṟuti valleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ - ṇakāra vīṟṟuppeyar valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi iyaiyiṉ, ṭakāram ākum vēṟṟumaip poruṭku - ṭakāramāy muṭiyum vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ.
u-m: maṭkuṭam; cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai eṉa varum.	(7)
304.	āṇum peṇṇu maḵṟiṇai yiyaṟkai.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu viravuppeyaruṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṇum peṇṇum aḵṟiṇai iyaṟkai - āṇ eṉṉum peyarum peṇ eṉṉum peyarum mēl tokai marapiṉuḷ ‘moḻimuta lākum’ eṉpataṉkaṇ aḵṟiṇaip peyar muṭinta iyalpupōlavē vēṟṟumaikkaṇ iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
u-m:	āṇkai, peṇkai; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.
maṟṟitu tokaimarapiṉuḷ “aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyar” (cūttiram 13) eṉpataṉuḷ iyalpāy muṭintataṉṟōveṉiṉ, ivai āṇṭu muṭintaṉapōlat tattam marapiṉ viṉaiyāṟ pālaṟiyappaṭuvaṉa vaṉṟi, irutiṇaikkaṇṇum aḵṟiṇaiyāy muṭitaliṉ av vaḵṟiṇaip peyaratu iyalpoṭu māṭṭeṟintu muṭittār eṉak koḷka. ivvāṟātaliṉ, āṇkaṭitu peṇ kaṭitu eṉṉum alvaḻiyum ‘moḻimuta lākum’ (tokai. 5) eṉpataṉuṭ koḷḷappaṭum. 			(8)
305.	āṇmarak kiḷavi yaraimara viyaṟṟē.
itu, tiripu vilakkic cāriyai vakuttamaiyiṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṇmarak kiḷavi araimara iyaṟṟu - āṇ eṉṉum marattai uṇaraniṉṟa peyarccol araimaram ammuppeṟum iyalpiṟṟāyt (284) tāṉum ammuppeṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: āṇaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.	(9)

306.	viṇṇeṉa varūuṅ kāyap peyarvayiṉ
	uṇmaiyu murittē yatteṉ cāriyai
	ceyyuṇ maruṅkiṟ ṟoḻilvaru kālai.
itu, ceyyuḷuḷ tiripu vilakkic cāriyai vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) viṇ eṉa varum kāyap peyarvayiṉ - viṇ eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa ākāyattai uṇaraniṉṟa peyarkkaṇ, attu eṉ cāriyai uṇmaiyum urittu - attu eṉṉum cāriyai uṇṭātalum urittu illaiyātalum urittu, ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ toḻil varu kālai - ceyyuḷiṭattu viṉaivarum kālattu.
u-m: viṇṇattuk koṭkum eṉavum, ‘viṇkuttu nīḷvarai’ (nālaṭi. 226) eṉavum varum.	(10)
307.	toḻiṟpeya rellān toḻiṟpeya riyala.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkut tiripu vilakki ukaramum valleḻuttum vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) toḻiṟpeyarellām toḻiṟpeyar iyala - ṇakāra vīṟṟut toḻiṟ peyarellām alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum ñakāravīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyaratu iyalpāy vaṉkaṇam vantavaḻi valleḻuttum ukarappēṟum, meṉkaṇamum iṭaikkaṇattu vakaramum vantavaḻi ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m:	maṇṇukkaṭitu eṉavum, maṇṇukkaṭumai eṉavum; maṇṇu ñāṉṟatu; ñāṟci eṉavum; maṇṇuvalitu; valimai eṉavum iruvaḻiyum oṭṭuka.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, toḻiṟpeyarallaṉavum ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟuvaṉa koḷka. veṇṇukkarai, eṇṇuppāṟu, maṇṇuccōṟu eṉa varum.	(11)
308.	kiḷaippeya rellāṅ koḷattiri pilavē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkut tiripu vilakki iyalpu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kiḷaippeyar ellām koḷat tiripu ila - ṇakāra vīṟṟuḷ ōr iṉattai uṇaraniṉṟa peyarellām tiripuṭaiya veṉṟu karutum paṭiyākat tiritaluṭaiyavaṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m: umaṇkuṭi; cēri, tōṭṭam, pāṭi eṉa varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iv vīṟṟuc cāriyai peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum koḷka.
u-m:  maṇṇakkaṭi, eṇṇanōlai eṉavum; paraṇkāl, kavaṇkāl eṉavum varum.
`koḷa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivvīṟṟu ēḻām vēṟṟumaip poruṇmai uṇara niṉṟa iṭaiccol tirintu muṭivaṉa koḷka.
u-m: aṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ, iṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ, uṅkaṭ koṇṭāṉ eṉavum; āṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ, īṅkaṭ koṇṭāṉ, ūṅkaṭ koṇṭāṉ eṉavum; avaṭ koṇṭāṉ, ivaṭ koṇṭāṉ, uvaṭ koṇṭāṉ eṉavum oṭṭuka.
(`maṇṇakkaṭi’ eṉpatil akkuccāriyaiyum `vaṇṇa nōlai’ eṉpatil amccāriyaiyum vantaṉa.)	(12)
309.	vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi yeṇṇe ṉuṇavuppeyar
	vēṟṟumai yiyaṟkai nilaiyalu murittē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟu alvaḻiyuḷ vēṟṟumai muṭipu pōlaṭ tirintu muṭivatu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumai alvaḻi - vēṟṟumai yallāta viṭattu, eṇ eṉ uṇavuppeyar - eṇ eṉṟu collap paṭukiṉṟa uṇaviṉai yuṇarttum peyar, vēṟṟumai iyaṟkai nilaiyalum urittu - vēṟṟumaiyatu tirintu muṭiyum iyalpu niṟṟalum urittu.
u-m: eṭkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavarum.
ummaiyāṉ, eṇkaṭitu eṉṟu iyalpātalē (148) perum pāṉmai.	(13)
310.	muraṇeṉ ṟoḻiṟpeyar mutaliya ṉilaiyum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar oṉṟaṟkut toḻiṟ peyar muṭipu vilakki, iv vīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipum vēṟṟumai muṭipum kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) muraṇ eṉ toḻiṟpeyar mutal iyal nilaiyum - muraṇ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum toḻiṟpeyar iv vīṟṟiṟku iruvaḻiyum muṉkūṟiya iyalpum (148), tiripumākiya (303) iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum.
u-m:	muraṇkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; muraṭ kaṭumai, cēṉai, tāṉai, paṟai eṉavum varum.
itaṉai ‘toḻiṟpeya rellām’ (cūttiram 11) eṉpataṉpiṉ vaiyāta muṟaiyaṉṟiya kūṟṟiṉāṉ, muraṇkaṭumai eṉṉum iyalpum; araṇ kaṭumai, araṭkaṭumai eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyum koḷka.	 (14)
311.	makara viṟuti vēṟṟumai yāyiṉ
	tuvarak keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikumē.
itu, makaravīṟṟiṟku mēṟkūṟiya ṇakaravīṟṟu vēṟṟumai muṭipōṭu iyaiya vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) makara iṟuti vēṟṟumaiyāyiṉ - makaravīṟṟup peyarc col vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇāyiṉ, tuvarak keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikum - am makaram muṟṟak keṭṭu varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: marakkōṭum; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
‘tuvara’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum uyar tiṇaip peyarkkaṇṇum viravuppeyarkkaṇṇum makarakkēṭu koḷka. 
u-m: marañāṇ, nūl, maṇi, yāḻ, vaṭṭu, aṭai, āṭai eṉavum; naṅkai, eṅkai; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉavum; nuṅkai, taṅkai eṉavum varum.	  (15)
312.	akara ākāram varūuṅ kālai
	īṟṟumicai yakara nīṭalu murittē.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu muṭipu vēṟṟumaiyuṭaiyaṉa kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) akaram ākāram varuṅkālai - akara mutal moḻiyum ākāra mutalmoḻiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālai, īṟṟumicai akaram nīṭalum urittu - nilaimoḻikkaṇ īṟṟiṉmēl niṉṟa akaram nīḷātu niṟpatēyaṉṟi nīṇṭu muṭitalum urittu.
u-m: marāaṭi, kuḷāampal eṉavum; maravaṭi, kuḷavāmpal eṉavum varum.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, ivvīṟṟuṭ piṟavum vēṟupaṭa muṭivaṉa koḷka. kōṇākōṇam, kōṇāvaṭṭam eṉa varum.
muṉṉarc ‘celvaḻi yaṟital’ (cūt-17) eṉpataṉāl, kuḷā ampal eṉpuḻi varumoḻi ākārakkuṟukkamum, kōṇā kōṇam eṉpuḻi varumoḻi valleḻuttukkēṭum koḷka.
(kōṇā kōṇam, kōṇāvaṭṭam eṉpaṉavaṟṟuḷ kōṇattuṭ kōṇam kōṇattuḷ vaṭṭam eṉa ēḻaṉurupu virikka.)	 (16)
313.	melleḻut tuṟaḻu moḻiyumā ruḷavē
	celvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu uṟaḻum moḻiyum uḷa - melleḻuttōṭu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa; vaḻakkattāṉa celvaḻi aṟital - vaḻakkiṉkaṇ avai vaḻaṅkum iṭam aṟika.
u-m:	kuḷaṅkarai, kuḷakkarai; cēṟu, tātu, pūḻi eṉa varum.
‘celvaḻi yaṟital’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, kuḷaṅkarai, kuḷakkarai eṉṟatu pōla, allaṉa otta uṟaḻcciyalla veṉpatu koḷka. ‘vaḻakkat tāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iv vīṟṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ muṭiyātaṉa vellām muṭittuk koḷka. ilavaṅkōṭu eṉavum, ‘pulampuk kaṉaṉē’ eṉavum, nilattuk kiṭantāṉ eṉavum varum.	  (17)
314.	illa marappeyar vicaimara viyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) illam marappeyar vicai mara iyaṟṟu - illam eṉṉum marattiṉai uṇaraniṉṟa peyar vicaiyeṉṉum marattiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: illaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
itaṉkaṇ makarakkēṭu muṉṉar ‘ellām’ (cūttiram 19) eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka.	   (18)
315.	alvaḻi yellā melleḻut tākum.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) alvaḻi ellām melleḻuttu ākum - makaravīṟu alvaḻikkaṇellām melleḻuttāyt tirintu muṭiyum.
u-m: maraṅkuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iv vīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipiṉ muṭiyātaṉa vellām koḷka. 
u-m: marañāṉṟatu; nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu eṉavum; vaṭṭat taḻai, vaṭṭappalakai eṉavum; kalakkoḷ, kalanel eṉavum; nīlakkaṇ, pavaḷavāy eṉavum; nilanīr eṉavum; kolluṅ koṟṟaṉ, paṟakkunārai eṉavum varum.	     (19)

316.	akameṉ kiḷavikkuk kaimuṉ variṉē
	mutaṉilai yoḻiya muṉṉavai keṭutalum
	varainilai yiṉṟē yāciri yarkka
	melleḻuttu mikuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ marūu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) akam eṉ kiḷavikku kai muṉvariṉ - akam eṉṉum colliṟku kai eṉṉum col muṉvariṉ, mutal nilai oḻiya muṉṉavai keṭutalum - muṉ ‘makaraviṟuti’ (cūttiram -15) eṉṟataṉāṉ makaram keṭṭu niṉṟa nilaimoḻi mutal niṉṟa akaram oḻiya ataṉmuṉ niṉṟa akaramum akarattāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa kakarameyyum keṭṭu muṭitalum avai keṭātu niṉṟu muṭitalum, varainilai iṉṟu āciriyarkku - nīkkum nilaimaiyiṉṟu āciriyarkku; a vayiṉṉāṉa melleḻuttu mikutal - av viraṇṭiṭattum melleḻuttu mikku muṭika.
u-m: aṅkai, akaṅkai eṉa varum. (oḻiya-tavira)	(20)
317.	ilameṉ kiḷavikkup paṭuvaru kālai
	nilaiyalu murittē ceyyu ḷāṉa.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu uriccol oṉṟaṟkuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ilam eṉ kiḷavikkup paṭu varukālai - ilam eṉṉum colliṟkup paṭu eṉṉum col varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, nilaiyalum urittuc ceyyuḷāṉa - muṉ ‘makaraviṟuti’ (cūttiram -15) eṉpataṉāṟ keṭṭa īṟu keṭātu niṉṟu muṭitalum urittuc ceyyuṭkaṇ.
u-m:	“ilampaṭu pulava rēṟṟakai niṟaiya” (malaipaṭu. 576) eṉa varum.
uriccollākalāṉ urupu viriyāteṉiṉum ilattāṟ paṟṟappaṭum pulavareṉṉum poruḷ uṇara niṟṟaliṉ, vēṟṟumai muṭipāyiṟṟu. ummai makaravīṟu eṉṉum cātiyorumaipaṟṟi vanta etirmaṟai.	(21)
318.	attoṭu civaṇu māyirat tiṟuti
	otta veṇṇu muṉvaru kālai.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu eṇṇuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟkut tokai marapiṉuḷ eytiya ē eṉ cāriyai vilakki, attu vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āyirattu iṟuti - āyiram eṉṉum eṇṇup peyariṉ makaramey, otta eṇṇu muṉ varukālai - taṉakku akappaṭumoḻiyāyp poruntiṉa eṇṇuppeyar taṉmuṉ varuṅkālattu, attoṭu civaṇum - tokaimarapiṟ kūṟiya ē eṉ cāriyai oḻittu attuccāriyai porunti muṭiyum.
u-m:	āyirattoṉṟu, āyirattiraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku eṉa oṭṭuka.
nilaimoḻi muṟkūṟātu cāriyai muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, itaṉ muṉṉark kuṟai, kūṟu, mutal eṉpaṉa vantavaḻiyum im muṭipu koḷka. āyirattukkuṟai; kūṟu, mutal eṉa oṭṭuka. 	(22)
319.	aṭaiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉu mataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu, av veṇṇuppeyar aṭaiyaṭuttavaḻi muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aṭaiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - av vāyiram eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar aṭaiyaṭutta moḻiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum mēṟcoṉṉataṉōṭu orutaṉmaittāy attup peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: patiṉāyirattoṉṟu; iraṇṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
mēl ilēciṉāṉ vantaṉavum aṭaiyaṭuttu oṭṭuka. patiṉāyirattukkuṟai; kūṟu, mutal eṉa varum.	 (23)
320.	aḷavu niṟaiyum vēṟṟumai yiyala.
iḵtu, av veṇṇiṉ muṉṉar aḷavuppeyar niṟaippeyar vantāl muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷavum niṟaiyum vēṟṟumai iyala - āyirattu muṉ aḷavum niṟaiyum vantāl iv vīṟṟu vēṟṟumai iyalpāy (311) makaramkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum e-ṟu.
u-m:	āyirakkalam, cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai eṉavum; āyirak kaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum oṭṭuka.
immāṭṭēṟṟāṉē, mēl ‘tuvara’ (cū. 15) eṉṟa ilēciṉāṉ, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ kēṭu eytiya makaram īṇṭum keṭuttuk koḷka. 
u-m: āyiranāḻi; vaṭṭi, akal eṉa varum.
patiṉāyirakkalam eṉṟāṟpōla aṭaiyaṭuttu vantavaḻiyum oṭṭuka.	(24)
321.	paṭarkkaip peyaru muṉṉilaip peyarum
	toṭakkaṅ kuṟukum peyarnilaik kiḷaviyum
	vēṟṟumai yāyi ṉurupiya ṉilaiyum
	melleḻuttu mikuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cila uyartiṇaippeyarum viravup peyarum urupipuṇarcci muṭintavāṟē īṇṭup poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum muṭiyumeṉa uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) paṭarkkaippeyarum muṉṉilaip peyarum toṭakkam kuṟukum peyarnilaikkiḷaviyum - ellārum eṉṉum paṭarkkaip peyarum ellīrum eṉṉum muṉṉilaip peyarum (kiḷait toṭarccip poruḷavāy) neṭumutal kuṟuki muṭiyum tām nām yām eṉṉum peyarum, vēṟṟumai yāyiṉ urupu iyal nilaiyum - vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarccik kaṇṇāyiṉ urupupuṇarccikkaṇ coṉṉa iyalpiṉ kaṇṇē niṉṟu, cāriyai peṟuvaṉa īṟukeṭṭu iṭaiyum īṟum cāriyai peṟṟum (192), neṭumutal kuṟukuvaṉa neṭumutal kuṟukiyum (189) muṭiyum, avayiṉāṉ melleḻuttu mikutal āṉa - an neṭumutal kuṟukumoḻikkaṇ melleḻuttu mikum.
u-m: ellārtaṅkaiyum, ellīrnuṅkaiyum; ceviyum, talaiyum, puṟamum eṉavum; taṅkai, naṅkai, eṅkai; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉavum oṭṭuka.
‘vēṟṟumaiyāyiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, paṭarkkaippeyarkkum muṉṉilaippeyarkkum iyalpukaṇattu ñakaramum nakaramum vantavaḻi tammuccāriyaiyum nummuccāriyaiyum īṟu keṭutal koḷka.
‘āvayiṉāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, paṭarkkaippeyarkkum muṉṉilaip peyarkkum ñakaramum nakaramum vantuḻi avai mikutalum, toṭakkam kuṟukum peyarkkum añ ñakaramum nakaramum vantuḻi makaraṅkeṭṭu avai mikutaluṅ koḷka. ellār taññāṇum, ellīr nuññāṇum, nūlum eṉavum; taññāṇ, naññāṇ, eññāṇ, nūl eṉavum varum.
iṉṉum ‘āvayiṉāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, paṭarkkaippeyarum muṉṉilaippeyarum cāriyai peṟātu iṟuti ummup peṟutalum koḷka. 
u-m: ellārkaiyum, ellīrkaiyum; ceviyum, talaiyum, puṟamum eṉavarum. 
u-m: iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, urupīṟṟuc ceykai yellām koḷka. tamakāṇam eṉavarum. (iḵtu akara urupukoṭuttu muṭittavāṟu kāṇka).	  (25)
322.	allatu kiḷappi ṉiyaṟkai yākum.
itu, mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) allatu kiḷappiṉ iyaṟkai ākum - mēṟkūṟiya paṭarkkaip peyarum muṉṉilaip peyarum toṭakkam kuṟukum peyar nilaikkiḷaviyum alvaḻiyaic collumiṭattu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
īṇṭu iyalpeṉpatu cāriyai peṟāmai nōkki. ivaṟṟiṉ īṟutirital ‘alvaḻi yellām’ (cūttiram 19) eṉṟataṉuḷ ‘ellām’ eṉṉum ilēciṉāṟ koḷka.
u-m:	ellāruṅ kuṟiyar; ciṟiyar, tīyar, periyar eṉavum; ellīruṅ kuṟiyīr, ciṟiyīr, tīyīr, periyīr eṉavum; tāṅ kuṟiyar;ciṟiyar, tīyar, periyar eṉavum; nāṅ kuṟiyam; ciṟiyam, tīyam, periyam eṉavum; yāṅ kuṟiyēm; ciṟiyēm, tīyēm,  periyēm eṉavum varum.
iṉṉum ‘ellām’ eṉṉum ilēciṉāṉē, iv vīṟṟukkaṇ meṉkaṇattu makaram oḻintaṉa vantavaḻi makaram avvoṟṟāyt tiritalum koḷka. 
u-m: ellāruñ ñāṉṟār, nīṇṭār eṉavum; ellīruñ ñāṉṟīr, nīṇṭīr eṉavum; tāññāṉṟār, nīṇṭār eṉavum; nāññāṉṟām, nīṇṭām eṉavum; yāññāṉṟēm, nīṇṭēm eṉavum koḷka.        	  (26)
323.	allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum
	ellā meṉumpeya rurupiya ṉilaiyum
	vēṟṟumai yalvaḻic cāriyai nilaiyātu.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu viravuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku alvaḻik kaṇṇum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum urupiyaloṭu māṭṭeṟintu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum - al vaḻiyaic collumiṭattum vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarcci yiṭattum, ellām eṉum peyar urupu iyal nilaiyum - ellām eṉṉum viravup peyar urupupuṇarcciyiṉ iyalpilē niṉṟu (190) vaṟṟuccāriyaiyum iṟuti ummuccāriyaiyum peṟṟu muṭiyum, vēṟṟumai alvaḻi cāriyai nilaiyātu - ap peyar vēṟṟumaiyallāta iṭattuc cāriyai peṟutal nilaiyātāyē muṭiyum.
māṭṭēṟu ēlāta alvaḻiyiṉaiyum urupiyaloṭu māṭṭeṟintu vilakkiya mikutiyāṉ, alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇat tiṟuti ummuppēṟum, nilaimoḻi makarakkēṭum, varumoḻi valleḻuttuppēṟum, meṉ kaṇattu makarakkēṭum, paṇput tokaikkaṇ makarakkēṭṭōṭu iṟuti ummuppēṟum koḷka.
u-m: ellākkuṟiyavum; ciṟiyavum, tīyavum, periyavum eṉavum; ellāvaṟṟukkōṭum; ceviyum, talaiyum, puṟamum eṉavum; ellāñāṉṟaṉa; nīṇṭaṉa, māṇṭaṉa eṉavum; ellāñāṇum; nūlum, maṇiyum, yāppum, valimaiyum, aṭaivum, āṭṭamum eṉavum; ellāvaṟṟuñāṇum; nūlum, maṇiyum, yāppum, valimaiyum, aṭaivum, āṭṭamum eṉavum varum.
ellāk kuṟiyarum; ciṟiyarum, tīyarum, periyarum eṉa uyar tiṇaikkaṇṇum oṭṭuka.
īṇṭuc cāriyai peṟṟavaḻi, makarakkēṭu vaṟṟiṉmicai oṟṟāyk keṭṭatu. itu viravuppeyarākaliṉ, īṟṟup potu muṭipiṟku ēlāteṉṟu cāriyai valleḻuttuk koḷḷap paṭṭatu.	 (27)
324.	melleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉa millai.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya ellām eṉpataṟku alvaḻikkaṇ eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉam illai - av vellāmeṉpatu alvaḻikkaṇ mēl ilēciṉāṟ kūṟiya valleḻuttē yaṉṟi melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyiṉum kuṟṟam illai e-ṟu.
mēṟkūṟiya ceykaimēlē itu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, makarak kēṭum iṟuti ummuppēṟuṅ koḷka.
u-m: ellāṅkuṟiyavum; ciṟiyavum, tīyavum, periyavum eṉavum; ellāṅkuṟiyarum; ciṟiyarum, tīyarum, periyarum eṉavum oṭṭuka.
iṉi, uraiyiṟkōṭal eṉpataṉāṉ, iṟuti ummiṉṟi ellāṅ kuṟiya, ellāruṅkuṟiyar eṉavum varum.
mēl ilēciṉāṟ kūṟiniṉṟa valleḻuttiṉoṭu mel leḻuttu vakuttamaiyiṉ, iḵtu alvaḻiyāyiṟṟu.
325.	uyartiṇai yāyi ṉurupiya ṉilaiyum.
itu, mēlataṟku uyartiṇai muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyartiṇaiyāyiṉ urupu iyal nilaiyum - av vellāmeṉpatu aḵṟiṇaippeyarāyaṉṟi uyartiṇaip peyarāy niṉṟa nilaimaiyāyiṉ urupupuṇarcciyiṉ iyalpilē niṉṟu āṇṭuk kūṟiya nammuccāriyai peṟṟu muṭiyum.
vaṟṟu vakutta ceykai mēl vakuttamaiyiṉ, makarakkēṭu koḷka. iṟuti ummaiyum ac ceykai mēlē vakuttamaiyiṟ koḷka.
u-m: ellānaṅkaiyum; ceviyum, talaiyum, puṟamum eṉa varum.
mēl māṉamillai (cūt. 28) eṉṟataṉāṉ, alvaḻik kaṇ vaṉkaṇattu makaramkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikku iṟuti ummuppeṟṟu muṭitalum, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ makaram keṭṭu iṟuti ummuppeṟṟu muṭitalum koḷka. 
u-m: ellāk kollarum, cēvakarum, taccarum, pulavarum eṉavum; ellā ñāyiṟum, nāyakarum, maṇiyakārarum vaṇikarum, aracarum eṉavum varum. 	(29)
326.	numme ṉorupeyar melleḻuttu mikumē.
ituvum, av vīṟṟu viravuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) num eṉ oru peyar melleḻuttu mikum - num eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa oru viravuppeyar vēṟṟumaikkaṇ melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: nuṅkai; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.
makaram ‘tuvara’ (cūt. 15) eṉṟa ilēciṉāṟ keṭṭatu. ‘orupeyar’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ñakara nakaraṅkaḷ vanta iṭattum avvoṟṟu mikutal koḷka. nuññāṇ, nunnūl eṉa varum.
‘oṉṟiṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, uṅkai eṉa um eṉpataṉ muṭipum iv vīṟṟatākak koḷka.	 (30)
327.	allataṉ maruṅkiṟ colluṅ kālai
	ukkeṭa niṉṟa meyvayi ṉīvara
	iiṭai nilaii yīṟukeṭa rakaram
	niṟṟal vēṇṭum puḷḷiyoṭu puṇarntē
	appāṉ moḻivayi ṉiyaṟkai yākum.
itu, mēlataṟku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) allataṉ maruṅkiṉ collum kālai - an num eṉ oru peyar taṉṉai alvaḻiyiṭattuc colluṅkālai, u keṭa niṉṟa meyvayiṉ ī vara i iṭainilaii īṟu keṭa rakaram puḷḷiyoṭu puṇarntu niṟṟal vēṇṭum - nakarattu ukaraṅkeṭa an niṉṟa meyyiṭattu īkāram vara, ōr ikaram iṭaiyilē peṟa makaram keṭa avviṭattu oru rakaram puḷḷiyoṭu porunti niṟṟal vēṇṭum; appāl moḻivayiṉ iyaṟkai yākum - varumoḻiyiṭattu am moḻitāṉ ivvāṟu tiriyātu iyalpātal vēṇṭum.
u-m: nīyīrkuṟiyīr, ciṟiyīr, tīyīr, periyīr eṉa varum. 
ñāṉṟīr, nīṇṭīr, māṇṭīr eṉa iyalpu kaṇattōṭum oṭṭuka.	 (31)
328.	toḻiṟpeya rellān toḻiṟpeya riyala.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyarkaḷ alvaḻik kaṇṇum vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum muṭipu vēṟṟumai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) toḻiṟpeyarellām toḻiṟpeyar iyala - makaravīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyarellām alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum ñakāravīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar iyalpiṉavāy  vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum, iyalpukaṇattu ukaram peṟṟum varum.
u-m:	cemmukkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; cemmu ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu eṉavum; cemmuk kaṭumai, ciṟumai, tīmai, perumai eṉavum; cemmu ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, valimai eṉavum varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ukarampeṟātu alvaḻikkaṇ nāṭṭaṅ kaṭitu eṉa melleḻuttāyt tirivaṉavum, vēṟṟumaik kaṇ nāṭṭak kaṭumai eṉa makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikku varuvaṉavuṅ koḷka.	          (32)
329.	īmuṅ kammu murumeṉ kiḷaviyum
	āmup peyaru mavaṟṟō raṉṉa.
itu, poruṭpeyaruṭ cila alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum toḻiṟpeyarōṭu ottu muṭiyumeṉak kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) īmum kammum urum eṉ kiḷaviyum a muppeyarum- īm eṉṉum collum kam eṉṉum collum urum eṉṉum collumākiya am mūṉṟu peyarum, avaṟṟu ōr aṉṉa - alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum at toḻiṟ peyarōṭu (297, 298) orutaṉmaiyavāy vaṉkaṇam vantavaḻi ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum, iyalpukaṇattu ukaram peṟṟum muṭiyum.
u-m:	īmukkaṭitu, kammukkaṭitu, urumukkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, eṉavum; īmuñāṉṟatu, kammuñāṉṟatu, urumuñāṉṟatu; nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu eṉavum; īmuk kaṭumai, kammukkaṭumai, urumukkaṭumai; ciṟumai, tīmai, perumai eṉavum; īmuñāṟci, kammu ñāṟci, urumuñāṟci; -nīṭci, māṭci, valimai eṉavum varum.
(īm-iṭukāṭu, kam-kammiyaratutoḻil, urum-iṭi.)     (33)
330.	vēṟṟumai yāyi ṉēṉai yiraṇṭum
	tōṟṟam vēṇṭu makkeṉ cāriyai.
itu, mēl muṭipu kūṟiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ iraṇṭaṟku vēṟṟumaikkaṇ vēṟu oru muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumai āyiṉ - vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarcci yāyiṉ, ēṉai iraṇṭum akku eṉ cāriyai tōṟṟam vēṇṭum - iṟuti urum oḻinta iraṇṭum `akku’ eṉṉum cāriyai tōṟṟi muṭital vēṇṭum.
‘tōṟṟam’ eṉṟataṉāṉ ukaram nīkkutal vēṇṭumeṉka.
u-m: īmakkuṭam, kammakkuṭam; cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai 
eṉa varum.
mēl vēṟṟumai kūṟiya muṭipu kuṇavēṟṟumaik kaṇṇa teṉṟum, īṇṭuk kūṟiya muṭipu poruṭpeyarkkaṇṇa teṉṟum koḷka.	(34)
331.	vakāra micaiyu makāraṅ kuṟukum.
itu, paruntuviḻukkāṭāy ‘araiyaḷapu kuṟukal’ (nūṉmarapu 13) eṉpataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭai kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vakāram micaiyum makāram kuṟukum - mēl oru moḻikkaṇ kūṟiya ‘ṉakārai muṉṉar’ (moḻimarapu. cūttiram. 19) aṉṟi, īṇṭup puṇarmoḻikkaṇ vakarattiṉmēlum makaraṅ kuṟukum.
u-m: nilam valitu eṉa varum.	(35)
332.	nāṭpeyark kiḷavi mēṟkiḷan taṉṉa
	attu māṉmicai varainilai yiṉṟē
	oṟṟumey keṭuta leṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟu nāṭpeyarkku vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nāḷpeyark kiḷavi mēl kiḷanta aṉṉa - makaravīṟṟu nāṭpeyarccol mēl (248) ikaravīṟṟu nāṭpeyariṟ kiḷanta taṉmaiya vāy āṉ peṟṟu muṭiyum; attu āṉ micaiyum varainilai iṉṟu - attuccāriyai av vāṉcāriyaimēlum piṟa cāriyaimēlum nīkkum nilaimai yiṉṟām; oṟṟumey keṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar - avviṭattu makaravoṟṟut taṉ vaṭivu keṭuka veṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m: makattāṟ koṇṭāṉ; makattuñāṉṟu koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘oṟṟu’ eṉṉātu ‘mey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, nāṭpeyarallāta poruṭpeyarkkaṇṇum am muṭipu koḷka.
u-m: marattāṟ koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum. (ñāṉṟu eṉpatu -  cāriyai.)	(36)
333.	ṉakāra viṟuti valleḻut tiyaiyiṉ
	ṟakāra mākum vēṟṟumaip poruṭkē.
itu, ṉakāra vīṟṟu vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ṉakāra iṟuti valleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ - ṉakāra vīṟṟuppeyar valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy vantu iyaiyiṉ, ṟakāram ākum vēṟṟumaip poruṭku - ṟakāram ākum vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ.
u-m: poṟkuṭam; cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai eṉa varum.     (37)
334.	maṉṉuñ ciṉṉu māṉu mīṉum
	piṉṉu muṉṉum viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum
	aṉṉa viyala veṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟu acainilai iṭaiccollum, ēḻām vēṟṟumai iṭapporuḷ uṇaraniṉṟa iṭaiccoṟkaḷum, viṉaiyeccamum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) maṉṉum ciṉṉum āṉum īṉum piṉṉum muṉṉum viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum - maṉ eṉṉum collum ciṉ eṉṉum collum āṉ eṉṉum collum īṉ eṉṉum collum piṉ eṉṉum collum muṉ eṉṉum collum viṉaiyeccamākiya collum, aṉṉa iyala eṉmaṉār pulavar- mēṟkūṟiya iyalpiṉavāy ṉakaram ṟakaramāy muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m:	“atumaṟ koṇkaṉ ṟērē,” “kāppum pūṇṭiciṟ kaṭaiyum pōkalai” eṉavum; āṟkoṇṭāṉ, īṟkoṇṭāṉ,  piṟkoṇṭāṉ, muṟkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; variṟkoḷ ḷum; cellum, tarum, pōm eṉavum varum.
peyarān taṉmaiyavākiya āṉ, īṉ eṉpaṉavaṟṟai muṟ kūṟātataṉāṉ, āṉkoṇṭāṉ, īṉkoṇṭāṉ eṉat tiriyātu muṭitalum koḷka.
piṉ, muṉ eṉpaṉa peyarnilaiyum viṉaiyeccanilaiyum urupu nilaiyum paṭum. avaṟṟuḷ viṉaiyeccanilai īṇṭu ‘viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum’ eṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyum. urupunilai urupiyaluḷ muṭiyum. īṇṭup peyar kūṟukiṉṟatu. ap peyarai muṉ kūṟātataṉāṉ, piṉ koṇṭāṉ, muṉkoṇṭāṉ eṉat tiriyāmaiyum koḷka.
‘iyala’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ūṉ eṉṉum cuṭṭu ūṉ koṇṭāṉ eṉa iyalpāy muṭital koḷka.	          (38)
335.	cuṭṭumutal vayiṉu mekaramutal vayiṉum
	appaṇpu nilaiyu miyaṟkaiya veṉpa.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ orucār ēḻām vēṟṟumai iṭapporuḷ uṇartti niṉṟa iṭaiccolliṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭumutal vayiṉum ekaramutal vayiṉum - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya vayiṉ eṉṉum collum ekaramākiya mutalaiyuṭaiya vayiṉ eṉṉum collum, a paṇpu nilaiyum iyaṟkaiya eṉpa - mēl ṟakaramāy muṭiyumeṉṟa ap paṇpu nilai peṟṟu muṭiyum iyaṟkaiyaiyuṭaiya eṉṟu colluvar.
u-m: avvayiṟkoṇṭāṉ, ivvayiṟkoṇṭāṉ, uvvayiṟ koṇṭāṉ, evvayiṟkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘iyaṟkaiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum koḷka. kāṉkōḻi eṉa varum.	(39)
336.	kuyiṉeṉ kiḷavi yiyaṟkai yākum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuyiṉ eṉ kiḷavi iyaṟkai yākum - kuyiṉ eṉṉum col tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m:	kuyiṉkuḻām;  celavu, tōṟṟam, maṟaivu eṉa varum. kuyiṉ eṉpatu mēkam.	 (40)
337.	ekiṉmara māyi ṉāṇmara viyaṟṟē.
itu, tiripu vilakki ammu vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ekiṉ maram āyiṉ āṇmara iyaṟṟu - ekiṉ eṉṉum collum marappeyarāyiṉ āṇmarattiṉatu iyalpiṟṟāy  ammup peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: ekiṉaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.       (41)
338.	ēṉai yekiṉē yakaram varumē
	valleḻut tiyaṟkai mikutal vēṇṭum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉai ekiṉ akaram varum - oḻinta maramallā ekiṉ nilaimoḻikkaṇ akaram vantu muṭiyum; valleḻuttu iyaṟkai mikutal vēṇṭum - avviṭattu varumoḻi valleḻuttu iyalpu mikku muṭital vēṇṭum.
u-m: ekiṉakkāl, cevi, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.
mēlaic cūttirattōṭu itaṉai oṉṟāka ōtātataṉāṉ, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum akarappēṟu koḷka. ekiṉañāṟci, yāppu, aṭaivu eṉa varum.
`iyaṟkai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ akarappēṟṟōṭu melleḻuttup pēṟum koḷka. 
um:ekiṉaṅkāl, cevi, talai, puṟam eṉavarum. 	(42)
339.	kiḷaippeya rellāṅ kiḷaippeya riyala.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kiḷaippeyar ellām kiḷaippeyar iyala - ṉakāra īṟṟuk kiḷaippeyarellām ṇakāra īṟṟuk kiḷaippeyar pōlat (308) tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m: eyiṉkuṭi; cēri, tōṭṭam, pāṭi eṉa varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, akkuccāriyaiyum valleḻuttum peṟṟu eyiṉakkaṉṉi eṉa muṭitalum; pārppaṉakkaṉṉi eṉa nilaimoḻi tirintu akkum valleḻuttum peṟutalum; iṉic cāriyai peṟātu īṟu tirintu vēḷāṇkumari, vēḷāṇ vāḻkkai eṉa muṭitalum koḷka. 	    (43)
340.	mīṉeṉ kiḷavi valleḻut tuṟaḻvē.
ituvum, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mīṉ eṉ kiḷavi valleḻuttu uṟaḻvu - mīṉ eṉṉum col taṉ tiripu valleḻuttiṉōṭu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum.
u-m: mīṉkaṇ, mīṟkaṇ; ciṉai, talai, puṟam eṉa varum. (44)
341.	tēṉeṉ kiḷavi valleḻut tiyaiyiṉ
	mēṉilai yottalum valleḻuttu mikutalum
	āmuṟai yiraṇṭu murimaiyu muṭaittē
	valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi yiṟuti yillai.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) tēṉ eṉ kiḷavi valleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ - tēṉ eṉṉum col valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻiyāy vantu poruntiṉ, mēlnilai ottalum valleḻuttu mikutalum ā muṟai iraṇṭum urimaiyum uṭaittu - mēl mīṉ eṉṉum colliṟkuc coṉṉa tiri puṟaḻcciyiṉ nilaimaiyaiyottu muṭitalum varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalumākiya am muṟaiyaiyuṭaiya iraṇṭaṉaiyum urittātalaiyum uṭaittu, valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi iṟuti illai - valleḻuttu mikumiṭattu nilaimoḻiyiṟuti ṉakaravoṟṟu nilaiyiṉṟik keṭum.
‘urimaiyum’ eṉṟa vummai ‘melleḻuttu mikiṉum’ (342) eṉa mēlvarukiṉṟa muṭiviṉai nōkki niṉṟatu.
u-m: tēṉkuṭam, tēṟkuṭam; cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai eṉavum; tēkkuṭam; cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai eṉavum varum.	          (45)
342.	melleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉa millai.
itu, mēlataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti yuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉam illai - tēṉ eṉ kiḷavi valleḻuttu vantāl valleḻuttu mikutalē yaṉṟi melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyiṉum kuṟṟam illai.
ṉakarakkēṭu atikārattāṟ koḷka.
u-m: tēṅkuṭam; cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai eṉa varum.        (46)
343.	melleḻut tiyaiyi ṉiṟutiyō ṭuṟaḻum.
itu, mēlataṟku meṉkaṇattukkaṇ tokaimarapiṟ kūṟiya muṭipu oḻiya vēṟumuṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ iṟutiyōṭu uṟaḻum - at tēṉ eṉ kiḷavi melleḻuttu mutalmoḻi vantu iyaiyiṉ nilaimoḻiyiṟuti ṉakāravoṟṟuk keṭutalum keṭāmaiyumākiya uṟaḻcciyāy muṭiyum.
u-m:	tēṉñeri, tēñeri; nuṉi, muri eṉak koḷka.
mēl ‘māṉa millai’ (cūttiram 49) eṉṟataṉāṉ, ‘iṟuti yōṭu uṟaḻum’ eṉṟatu īṟukeṭṭu varumoḻi melleḻuttu mikkum mikātum uṟaḻtaṟkum, varumoḻi mikātu iṟuti keṭṭum keṭātum uṟaḻtaṟkum, av viraṇṭaṟkum urittāyc ceṉṟataṉai vilakki varumoḻi mikātē niṟpa avvīṟē keṭṭum keṭātum niṉṟu uṟaḻumeṉpatu koḷḷap paṭṭatu.
ataṉmēl ‘āmuṟai’ (cūttiram 45) eṉṟataṉāṉ, ciṟupāṉmai īṟukeṭṭut tēññeri; nuṉi, muri eṉa melleḻuttu mikutalum koḷka.	         (47)
344.	iṟāaṟ ṟōṟṟa miyaṟkai yākum.
iḵtu, iṉṉum ataṟku uyirkkaṇattu orumoḻikkaṇ muṭiyum vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṟāal tōṟṟam - tēṉ eṉṉum col iṟāl eṉṉum varumoḻiyatu tōṟṟattukkaṇ, iyaṟkai ākum - nilaimoḻi ṉakāraṅkeṭātē niṉṟu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m: tēṉiṟāl eṉa varum.	(48)
345.	oṟṟumiku takaramoṭu niṟṟalu murittē.
iḵtu, ataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) oṟṟumiku takaramoṭu niṟṟalum urittu - attēṉ eṉpatu iṟāl eṉṉum varumoḻikkaṇ piṟitum oru takaravoṟṟu uṭaṉmiku takarattoṭu niṉṟu muṭitalum urittu.
mēl “valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi yiṟutiyillai” (puḷḷi. 45) eṉṟataṉāṉ nilaimoḻi yīṟu keṭukka, ‘takara mikum’ eṉṉātu, ‘oṟṟumiku takaram’ eṉṟataṉāṉ īroṟṟākkuka.
u-m: tēttiṟāl eṉa varum.
mēlaic cūttirattōṭu itaṉai oṉṟāka ōtātataṉāl, piṟa varumoḻikkaṇṇum im muṭipukoḷka. tēttaṭai, tēttī eṉa varum.
‘tōṟṟam’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, tēṉaṭai, tēṉī eṉṉum iyalpum koḷka.	 (49)

346.	miṉṉum piṉṉum paṉṉuṅ kaṉṉum
	annāṟ collun toḻiṟpeya riyala.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟumuṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) miṉṉum piṉṉum paṉṉum kaṉṉum a nāl collum - miṉ eṉṉum collum piṉ eṉṉum collum paṉ eṉṉum collum kaṉ eṉṉum collumākiya an nāṉku collum, toḻiṟ peyar iyala - vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum alvaḻikkaṇṇum ñakaravīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar pōla (297, 298) vaṉkaṇam vantavaḻi ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum, meṉkaṇattukkaṇṇum iṭaik kaṇattukkaṇ ṇum ukaram peṟṟum muṭiyum.
u-m: miṉṉukkaṭitu, piṉṉukkaṭitu, paṉṉukkaṭitu, 
kaṉṉuk kaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu, yātu eṉavum; miṉṉukkaṭumai, piṉṉukkaṭumai, paṉṉuk kaṭumai, kaṉṉuk kaṭumai; ciṟumai, tīmai, perumai, ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, valimai, yāppu eṉavum varum.
“toḻiṟpeyarellān toḻiṟpeyariyala” eṉṟu ōtātu ivvāṟu eṭuttōtiyavataṉāṉ, im muṭipiṉait toḻiṟpeyarkkum poruṭpeyarkkum uṭaṉ koḷka. miṉ eṉpatu oru toḻilumuṇṭu; poruḷumuṇṭu. piṟavum aṉṉa.		  (50)
347.	vēṟṟumai yāyi ṉēṉai yekiṉoṭu
	tōṟṟa mokkuṅ kaṉṉeṉ kiḷavi.
itu, mēl muṭipu kūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku vēṟu oru muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kaṉ eṉ kiḷavi vēṟṟumaiyāyiṉ - kaṉ eṉṉum col vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarcciyāyiṉ, ēṉai ekiṉoṭu tōṟṟam okkum - oḻinta maramallā ekiṉoṭu tōṟṟam ottu (338) akaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m:	kaṉṉakkuṭam; cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai, ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, valimai eṉa varum.
ciṟupāṉmai kaṉṉakkaṭumai eṉak kuṇavēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum im muṭipu koḷka.
‘tōṟṟam’ eṉṟataṉāl, alvaḻikkaṇ akaramum, vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ melleḻuttum koḷka. 
u-m kaṉṉaṅkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, yātu, valitu eṉa varum.
iṉṉum ‘tōṟṟam’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ciṟupāṉmai kaṉṉaṅ kaṭumai; ciṟumai, tīmai, perumai eṉak kuṇavēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum akaramum melleḻuttum koḷka.
ik ‘kaṉ’ eṉpataṉ vēṟṟumai muṭipiṟku, mēṟkūṟiyatu (346) kuṇavēṟṟumaikku eṉavum, īṇṭuk kūṟiyatu poruṭpeyarkku eṉavum koḷka.	(51)
348.	iyaṟpeyar muṉṉart tantai muṟaivariṉ
	mutaṟkaṇ meykeṭa vakara nilaiyum
	meyyoḻit taṉkeṭu mavviyaṟ peyarē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu viravuppeyaruḷ iyaṟpeyarkkut tokai marapiṉuḷ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iyaṟpeyar muṉṉart tantai muṟaivariṉ - ṉakāra vīṟṟu iyaṟpeyar muṉṉart tantai eṉṉum muṟaippeyar varumoḻiyāy variṉ, mutaṟkaṇ meykeṭa akaram nilaiyum - mutaṟkaṇ meykeṭa ataṉmēl ēṟiniṉṟa akaram keṭātu nilaipeṟum, a iyaṟpeyar mey oḻittu aṉkeṭum - nilaimoḻiyākiya iyaṟpeyar avvaṉ eṉṉum colliṉ akaram ēṟiniṉṟa meyyai oḻittu avvaṉtāṉ keṭṭu muṭiyum.
u-m: cāttantai, koṟṟantai eṉa varum.
‘mutaṟkaṇ mey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, cāttaṉṟantai, koṟṟaṉ ṟantai eṉṉum iyalpu muṭipum koḷka.	(52)
349.	ātaṉum pūtaṉuṅ kūṟiya viyalpoṭu
	peyaroṟ ṟakaran tuvarak keṭumē.
itu, mēlataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ātaṉum pūtaṉum - mēṟkūṟiya iyaṟpeyaruḷ ātaṉum pūtaṉum eṉṉum iyaṟpeyarkaḷ, kūṟiya iyalpoṭu peyar oṟṟu akaram tuvarakkeṭum - mēṟkūṟiya ceykaiyoṭu nilaimoḻip peyaruḷ aṉ keṭaniṉṟa oṟṟum varumoḻiyuḷ oṟṟukkeṭa niṉṟa akaramum muṟṟakkeṭṭu muṭiyum.
u-m: āntai, pūntai eṉa varum.
‘iyalpu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, peyaroṟṟum akaramum keṭātē niṉṟu  muṭintavāṟē muṭitalum koḷka. ātantai, pūtantai eṉavarum.
‘tuvara’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, aḻāṉ, puḻāṉ eṉa niṟuttip poruntiṉa ceykai ceytu aḻāntai, puḻāntai eṉa muṭikka.		  (53)
350.	ciṟappoṭu varuvaḻi yiyaṟkai yākum.
iḵtu, eytiyatu oruvaḻi vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ciṟappoṭu varuvaḻi iyaṟkai ākum - av viyaṟpeyar paṇpaṭuttu varuvaḻi muṉkūṟiya irumoḻic ceykaiyum tavirttu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m:	peruñcāttaṉṟantai, peruṅkoṟṟaṉṟantai eṉa varum.	   (54)
351.	appeyar meyyoḻit taṉkeṭu vaḻiyē
	niṟṟalu murittē yammeṉ cāriyai
	makkaṇ muṟaitokūu maruṅki ṉāṉa.
itu, mēlataṟku vēṟu oru varumoḻikkaṇ eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) a peyar makkaḷ muṟai tokum maruṅkiṉ āṉa mey oḻittu aṉkeṭu vaḻi - avviyaṟpeyar makkaḷ eṉṉum muṟaippeyar vantukūṭum iṭattiṉkaṇṇum piṟitiṭattum, tāṉ ēṟiya meyyai oḻittu aṉkeṭum avvaḻi, am eṉ cāriyai niṟṟalum urittu - am eṉṉum cāriyai niṟṟalum urittu.
u-m:cāttaṅkoṟṟaṉ, koṟṟaṅkoṟṟaṉ eṉavum; cāttaṅkuṭi, koṟṟaṅkuṭi eṉavum varum.	(55)
352.	tāṉum pēṉuṅ kōṉu meṉṉum
	āmuṟai yiyaṟpeyar tiripiṭa ṉilavē.
itu, mēlataṟku oruvaḻi eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tāṉum pēṉum kōṉum eṉṉum a muṟai iyaṟpeyar - av viyaṟpeyaruḷ tāṉum pēṉum kōṉum eṉṉum muṟaimai yiṉaiyuṭaiya iyaṟpeyarkaḷ tantai makkaḷ eṉṉum muṟaippeyaroṭu puṇarumvaḻi, tiripu iṭaṉ ila - mēṟkūṟiya tiripukaḷ iṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum .
u-m: tāṉṟantai, pēṉṟantai, kōṉṟantai eṉavum; 
tāṉ koṟṟaṉ, pēṉkoṟṟaṉ, kōṉkoṟṟaṉ eṉavum varum.  		(56)
353.	tāṉyā ṉeṉumpeya rurupiya ṉilaiyum.
itu, viravuppeyaruḷ tāṉ eṉpataṟkum, uyartiṇaip peyaruḷ yāṉ eṉpataṟkum, vēṟṟumaikkaṇ tokaimarapiṉuḷ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tāṉ yāṉ eṉum peyar urupu iyal nilaiyum - tāṉ eṉṉum viravuppeyarum yāṉ eṉṉum uyartiṇaip peyarum mēl tokaimarapiṉuṭ kūṟiya iyalpukaḷ oḻittu urupu puṇarcciyiṟ kūṟiya iyalpiṉkaṇṇē  nilaipeṟṟut tāṉ eṉpatu neṭumutal kuṟukit taṉ eṉṟāyum, yāṉ eṉpatu yakaramkeṭṭu ākāram ekāramāy eṉ eṉṟāyum muṭiyum.
u-m: taṉkai, eṉkai; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉavum; 
taṉ ñāṇ, eṉñāṇ; nūl, maṇi, yāḻ, vaṭṭu, aṭai, āṭai eṉavum varum.	(57)
354.	vēṟṟumai yalvaḻik kuṟukalun tiritalum
	tōṟṟa millai yeṉmaṉār pulavar.
itu, mēlavaṟṟiṟku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumai alvaḻi - mēṟkūṟiya tāṉ yāṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ vēṟṟumaippuṇarcciyallātaviṭattu, kuṟukalum tiritalum tōṟṟam illai eṉmaṉār pulavar - tāṉ eṉpatu neṭumutal kuṟukalum yāṉ eṉpatu avvāṟu tiritalum tōṟṟamiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m: tāṉkuṟiyaṉ; ciṟiyaṉ, tīyaṉ, periyaṉ, ñāṉṟāṉ, nīṇṭāṉ, māṇṭāṉ eṉavum; yāṉkuṟiyēṉ, ciṟiyēṉ, tīyēṉ, periyēṉ, ñāṉṟēṉ, nīṇṭēṉ, māṇṭēṉ eṉavum varum.
‘tōṟṟam’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, vēṟṟumaikkaṇ avvāṟaṉṟit tiritalum koḷka.
u-m: 	 taṟpukaḻ, taṟpakai eṉavum; eṟpakai, eṟpāṭi eṉavum varum.	   (58)
355.	aḻaṉeṉ ṉiṟutikeṭa valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aḻaṉ eṉ iṟutikeṭa valleḻuttu mikum - aḻaṉ eṉṉum col taṉ ṉakaravīṟu keṭa varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
u-m: aḻakkuṭam; cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai eṉa varum. 
aḻaṉ eṉpatu piṇam.	 (59)
356.	muṉṉeṉ kiḷavi muṉṉart tōṉṟum
	illeṉ kiḷavimicai ṟakara moṟṟal
	tolliyaṉ maruṅkiṉ marīiya marapē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiya marūukkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) muṉ eṉ kiḷavi muṉṉart tōṉṟum il eṉ kiḷavi micai ṟakaram oṟṟal - muṉ eṉṉum colliṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum il eṉṉum colliṉmēl ṟakara voṟṟu vantu muṭital, tol iyal maruṅkiṉ marīiya marapu - paḻaiyatākiya iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiya viṭattu maruvi vanta ilakkaṇa muṭipu.
u-m: muṉṟil eṉa varum.
itu kaṭaikkaṇ eṉṟāṟpōla varūum marūu muṭipu pōlaṉṟi, muṉṉil eṉa oṟṟiraṭṭi muṭiyaṟpālatu, iru moḻikkum iyalpilatōr oṟṟu mikku muṭinta marūu muṭipu.		   	   (60)
357.	poṉṉeṉ kiḷavi yīṟukeṭa muṟaiyiṉ
	muṉṉart tōṉṟum lakāra makāram
	ceyyuṇ maruṅkiṟ ṟoṭariya lāṉa.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟkuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) poṉ eṉ kiḷavi īṟu keṭa muṉṉar muṟaiyiṉ lakāram makāram tōṉṟum - poṉ eṉṉum col pakara mutaṉmoḻi vanta viṭattut taṉ īṟṟiṉ ṉakaramkeṭa ataṉ muṉṉar muṟaiyāṉēlakaramum makaramum tōṉṟi muṭiyum; ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ toṭar iyalāṉa - (yāṇṭeṉil,) ceyyu ḷiṭattuc coṟkaḷ tammil toṭarccippaṭum iyalpiṉkaṇ.
‘muṟaiyiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, lakaram uyirmeyyākavum makaram taṉimeyyākavum koḷka.
u-m: “polampaṭaip polinta koycuvaṟ puravi” (malaipaṭu. 574) eṉa varum.
‘toṭariyalāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, pakaram allāta vaṉkaṇattuk kaṇṇum ciṟupāṉmai īṟukeṭṭu lakaramum valleḻuttiṟkēṟṟa melleḻuttum mikkumuṭital koḷka. polaṅkalam, polañcuṭar, polantēr eṉa varum.
‘oṉṟiṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ ‘polanaṟun teriyal’ (puṟam. 29) ‘polamala rāvirai’ (kali. 138) eṉṟāṟpōla varum piṟa kaṇattu muṭipum koḷka.	(61)
358.	yakara viṟuti vēṟṟumaip poruḷvayiṉ
	valleḻut tiyaiyi ṉavveḻuttu mikumē.
itu, yakāravīṟṟiṟku vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) yakara iṟuti vēṟṟumaip poruḷvayiṉ - yakāra vīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ, valleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ a eḻuttu mikum - valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi vantu iyaiyiṉ av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m:	nāykkāl; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.       (62)
359.	tāyeṉ kiḷavi yiyaṟkai yākum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu viravuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tāy eṉ kiḷavi iyaṟkai yākum - tāy eṉṉum col valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m: tāy kai; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.
ivviyalpu mēl iṉṉavaḻi mikum eṉkiṉṟamai yiṉ, ‘aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyar’ eṉpataṉuḷ aṭaṅkātāyiṟṟu. (63)
360.	makaṉviṉai kiḷappiṉ mutaṉilai yiyaṟṟē.
itu, mēlataṟku aṭaiyaṭuttu vantavaḻi iṉṉavāṟu muṭiyumeṉa eytātatu eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) makaṉ viṉai kiḷappiṉ mutalnilai iyaṟṟu - at tāy eṉṉum col makaṉatu viṉaiyaik kiḷantu collu miṭattu, iv vīṟṟu mutaṟkaṇ (358) kūṟiya nilaimaiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: makaṉṟāyk kalām; ceru, tār, paṭai eṉa varum.
‘makaṉviṉai’ eṉṟatu, makaṟkut tāyāṟ payaṉpaṭum nilaimaiyiṉṟi, avaḷoṭu pakaitta nilaimaiyai.	64)
361.	melleḻut tuṟaḻu moḻiyumā ruḷavē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytātatu eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu uṟaḻum moḻiyumār uḷa - mēṟkūṟiya valleḻuttiṉoṭu melleḻuttu mikkum uṟaḻntum muṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa.
u-m:	vēyṅkuṟai; vēykkuṟai; ciṟai, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.	(65)
362.	alvaḻi yellā miyalpeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) alvaḻi ellām iyalpu eṉa moḻipa - yakara vīṟṟu alvaḻiyellām iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m:  nāykaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāl, iv vīṟṟu urupu vārātu, urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vanta iṭaiccol muṭipum, viṉaiyecca muṭipum, irupeyaroṭṭup paṇputtokai muṭipum, alvaḻi yuṟaḻcci muṭipum koḷka. 
u-m: avvāyk koṇṭāṉ, ivvāyk koṇṭāṉ, uvvāyk koṇṭāṉ, evvāyk koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; tāyk koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; poyccol, meyccol eṉavum; vēykaṭitu, vēykkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum varum.	 (66)
363.	rakāra viṟuti yakāra viyaṟṟē.
itu, rakāra vīṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) rakāra iṟuti yakāra iyaṟṟu - rakāra vīṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ yakāra vīṟṟu iyal piṟṟāy (358) valleḻuttu vantavaḻi mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: tērkkāl; ceykai, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.       (67)
364.	ārum vetiruñ cārum pīrum
	melleḻuttu mikutaṉ meypeṟat tōṉṟum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ārum vetirum cārum pīrum - ār eṉṉum collum vetir eṉṉum collum cār eṉṉuñ collum pīr eṉṉum collum, melleḻuttu mikutal meypeṟa tōṉṟum - melleḻuttumikku muṭital meymmaipeṟat tōṉṟum.
u-m: ārṅkōṭu, vetirṅkōṭu, cārṅkōṭu, pīrṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
‘meypeṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟavum melleḻuttu mikutal koḷka. 
u-m: kutirṅkōṭu, tuvarṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, pīr eṉpatu mēl ammup peṟṟa vaḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻvum koḷka.	(68)
365.	cāreṉ kiḷavi kāḻvayiṉ valikkum.
itu, mēlaṉavaṟṟuṭ cār eṉpataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cār eṉ kiḷavi kāḻvayiṉ valikkum - cār eṉṉum col kāḻ eṉṉum colloṭu puṇarumiṭattu valleḻuttu mikkup puṇarum.
u-m: cārkkāḻ eṉa varum.	(69)
366.	pīreṉ kiḷavi yammoṭuñ civaṇum.
iḵtu, avaṟṟuṭ pīr eṉpataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) pīr eṉ kiḷavi ammoṭum civaṇum- pīr eṉṉum col melleḻuttēyaṉṟi ammuccāriyaiyum peṟṟu vantu muṭiyum.
u-m: pīraṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.  	 (70)
367.	lakāra iṟuti ṉakāra viyaṟṟē.
itu, lakāra īṟṟiṟku ṉakāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumaiyōṭu iyaiya vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) lakāra iṟuti ṉakāra iyaṟṟu - lakāra vīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉ ṉakāravīṟṟiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy (333) lakāram ṟakāramāyt tirintu muṭiyum.
u-m: kaṟkuṟai; ciṟai, talai, puṟam eṉa varum. 	 (71)
368.	melleḻut tiyaiyi ṉakāra mākum.
iḵtu, av vīṟu meṉkaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ ṉakāram ākum - avvīṟu meṉkaṇam vantu iyaiyiṉ lakāram ṉakāramāyt tirintu muṭiyum.
u-m: kaṉñeri; nuṉi, muṟi eṉa varum.
ic cūttirattiṉai vēṟṟumaiyatu īṟṟukkaṇ alvaḻiyatu eṭuttukkōṭaṟkaṇ  ciṅkanōkkāka  vaittamaiyāṉ, alvaḻikkum im muṭipu koḷka. kaṉñerintatu; nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu eṉa varum.	(72)
369.	alvaḻi yellā muṟaḻeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, av vīṟṟiṟku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) alvaḻi ellām uṟaḻ eṉa moḻipa - av vīṟu alvaḻikkaṇellām tantiripu valleḻuttiṉōṭu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m: kalkuṟitu, kaṟkuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, kalkuṟumai, kaṟkuṟumai eṉak kuṇam paṟṟi vanta vēṟṟumaikkum ivvuṟaḻcci koḷka.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, ivvīṟṟu viṉaiccollīṟu tirintaṉavum koḷka. vantāṉāṟ koṟṟaṉ eṉa varum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, akkāṟkoṇṭāṉ, ikkāṟ koṇṭāṉ, ukkāṟ koṇṭāṉ, ekkāṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa urupu vārātu urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantavaṟṟiṉ muṭipum īṇṭē koḷka.
itaṉāṉ, piṟavum uḷḷavāṟu aṟintu oṭṭik koḷka.	(73)
370.	takaram varumvaḻi yāyta nilaiyalum
	pukariṉ ṟeṉmaṉār pulamai yōrē.
itu, mēlataṉuḷ oru kūṟṟiṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) takaram varumvaḻi - avvāṟu ṟakāramāyt tirinta lakāram takara mutalmoḻi varumoḻiyāy vantavaḻi, āytam nilaiyalum pukar iṉṟu eṉmaṉār pulamaiyōr - avvāṟu ṟakāramāyt tiritalēyaṉṟi āytamāyt tirintu niṟṟalum kuṟṟamiṉṟeṉṟu colluvar āciriyar.
u-m: kaḵṟītu, kaṟṟītu eṉa varum.
‘pukariṉṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ‘neṭiyata ṉiṟuti’ (puḷḷi. 75) eṉpataṉuḷ, vēṟītu, vēṟṟītu eṉṉum uṟaḻcci muṭipuṅ koḷka.	  (74)
371.	neṭiyata ṉiṟuti yiyalpumā ruḷavē.
itu, mēlataṟku oruvaḻi eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭiyataṉ iṟuti iyalpumār uḷa - neṭiyataṉ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa lakāravīṟu kuṟiyataṉ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa lakāram pōlat tirintu uṟaḻtalēyaṉṟi iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷa.
u-m: pālkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.
iyalpākātu tirintaṉa vēlkaṭitu, vēṟkaṭitu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa.	  (75)
372.	nelluñ celluṅ kolluñ collum
	allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumai yiyala.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu alvaḻiyuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nellum cellum kollum collum - nel eṉṉum collum cel eṉṉum collum kol eṉṉum collum col eṉṉum collum ākiya ivai nāṉku collum, allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumai iyala - avvaḻiyaic collumiṭattum  tam vēṟṟumai muṭipiṉ iyalpiṉavāy lakāram ṟakāramāyt tirintu muṭiyum.
u-m: neṟkaṭitu, ceṟkaṭitu, koṟkaṭitu, coṟkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavarum.	(76)
373.	illeṉ kiḷavi yiṉmai ceppiṉ 
	valleḻuttu mikutalu maiyiṭai varutalum
	iyaṟkai yātalu mākāram varutalum
	koḷattaku marapi ṉākiṭa ṉuṭaittē.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟu viṉaikkuṟippuc col oṉṟaṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) il eṉ kiḷavi iṉmai ceppiṉ - il eṉṉum col pukkuṟaiyum illai uṇarttātu oru poruḷatu illāmaiyai uṇarttumiṭattu, valleḻuttu mikutalum ai iṭai varutalum iyaṟkai ātalum ākāram varutalum - valleḻuttu mikutalum ai iṭai varutalum iyalpātalum ākāram mikku muṭitalum ākiya im muṭipu nāṉkum, koḷattakum marapiṉ āku iṭaṉ uṭaittu - ic colliṟku muṭipākak koḷattakum marapāṉē itaṉ muṭipu ākum iṭaṉ uṭaittu.
‘koḷattaku marapu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, valleḻuttu mikkavaḻi aikāram varutalum, ākāram mikkavaḻi valleḻuttu mikutalum koḷka. iyalpu muṟkūṟātu oḻintataṉāṉ, valleḻuttu mikuti ākāram vantavaḻi mikkē muṭitaliṉ ciṟappuṭaittātalum, aikāram vantavaḻi mikutalum mikāmaiyu muṭaimaiyiṉ ciṟappiṉṟātalum koḷka.
u-m:	illaikkal, illaikal; ilkal; illākkal; cuṉai, tuṭi, paṟai eṉa varum.
illaikkal, illaikal eṉpaṉa illai eṉṉum aikāra vīṟṟuc col muṭipaṉṟō veṉṟum, illākkal eṉpatu uḷḷa eṉṉum peyareccattu etirmaṟaiyākiya ākāravīṟṟuc collatu muṭipaṉṟō veṉṟum, ilkal eṉpatu paṇput tokai muṭipaṉṟō veṉṟum kūṟiṉ, am muṭipukaḷōṭu im muṭipukaḷ eḻuttoppumaiyaṉṟi, ivai ōcai vēṟṟumai yuṭaiya veṉpatu pōlum karuttu. avvōcai vēṟṟumai yāvaṉa, aikāra vīṟāyavaḻi av vaikārattu mēl oliyūṉṟiyum, lakāravīṟu iyalpu muṭipāyavaḻip paṇputtokai pōla oru tiraṇmaiyāka oliyātu iṭaiyaṟṟu lakārattumēl oli yūṉṟiyum, ākāravīṟāyavaḻi, oru tiraṇmaiyāl av ākārattumēl oliyūṉṟiyum, āṇṭu lakāra vīṟāya vaḻi oru tiraṇmaiyāka oliyātu anta ākārattumēl oliyūṉṟiyum varum vēṟupāṭukaḷ pōlum.
il eṉpatu etirmaṟai viṉaikkuṟippumuṟṟu viravuviṉai.		 (77)
374.	valleṉ kiḷavi toḻiṟpeya riyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) val eṉ kiḷavi toḻiṟpeyar iyaṟṟu - val eṉṉum col alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ñakāravīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar iyalpiṟṟāy (297, 298) vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum, meṉkaṇattum iṭaikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaram peṟṟum muṭiyum.
u-m: vallukkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu eṉavum; vallukkaṭumai; ciṟumai, tīmai, perumai, ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, valimai eṉavum varum.      			(78)
375.	nāyum palakaiyum varūuṅ kālai
	āvayi ṉukaraṅ keṭutalu murittē
	ukaraṅ keṭuvaḻi yakara nilaiyum.
itu, mēlataṟku eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟuttup piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nāyum palakaiyum varum kālai - mēlniṉṟa val eṉpataṉmuṉ nāy eṉṉum collum palakai eṉṉum collum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, a vayiṉ ukaram keṭutalum urittu - avviṭattu ukaram keṭātē niṉṟu muṭitalēyaṉṟik keṭṭu muṭiyavum peṟum, ukaram keṭuvaḻi akaram nilaiyum - av vukaram keṭumiṭattu akaram nilaipeṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: vallanāy, vallappalakai eṉa varum.
‘akaram nilaiyum’ eṉṉātu ‘ukaram keṭum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟa varumoḻikkaṇṇum av vakarappēṟu koḷka.
u-m: vallakkaṭumai; ciṟumai, tīmai, perumai eṉa  varum.		(79)
376.	pūlvē leṉṟā varaleṉ kiḷaviyō
	ṭāmup peyarkku mammiṭai varumē.
ituvum, avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) pūl vēl āl eṉ kiḷaviyōṭu a 
muppeyarkkum - pūl eṉṉum collum vēl eṉṉum collum āl eṉṉum collum ākiya am mūṉṟu peyarkkum, am iṭai varum - vēṟṟumaikkaṇ tiripiṉṟi ammuccāriyai iṭai vantu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
u-m:	pūlaṅkōṭu, vēlaṅkōṭu, ālaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum. (eṉṟā eṉpatu - eṇṇiṭaiccol) (80)
377.	toḻiṟpeya rellān toḻiṟpeya riyala.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyarkku alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) toḻiṟpeyar ellām toḻiṟpeyar iyala - lakāravīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyarellām ñakāravīṟṟut toḻiṟ peyariṉ iyalpiṉavāy alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum, meṉkaṇattum iṭaikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaram peṟṟum muṭiyum.
u-m: kallukkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu eṉavum; kallukkaṭumai; ciṟumai, tīmai, perumai, ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, valimai eṉavum varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivvāṟu muṭiyātu piṟavāṟu muṭivaṉavum koḷka. 
u-m: piṉṉalkaṭitu, tuṉṉalkaṭitu; piṉṉaṟ kaṭumai, tuṉṉaṟ kaṭumai eṉa varum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, meṉkaṇam vantavaḻi, piṉṉaṉ ñāṉṟatu; piṉṉaṉ ñāṟci eṉa melleḻuttāyt tirintu muṭivaṉavum koḷka.	(81)
378.	veyileṉ kiḷavi maḻaiyiya ṉilaiyum.
ituvum, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) veyil eṉ kiḷavi maḻai iyal nilaiyum - veyil eṉṉum kiḷavi maḻai eṉṉum colliyalpiṉkaṇṇē (288) nilaipeṟṟu attum iṉṉum peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: veyilattuk koṇṭāṉ; veyiliṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.	(82)
379.	cuṭṭumuta lākiya vakara viṟuti
	muṟpaṭak kiḷanta vurupiya ṉilaiyum.
itu, vakāravīṟṟup peyar nāṉkiṉuḷ cuṭṭumutal vakaram mūṉṟaṟkum vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutalākiya vakara iṟuti - cuṭṭeḻuttai mutalākavuṭaiya vakara vīṟṟuccol, muṟpaṭak kiḷanta urupu iyal nilaiyum - vēṟṟumaikkaṇ muṟpaṭac coṉṉa urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpu nilaipeṟṟu vaṟṟup peṟṟup puṇarum.
u-m:	avaṟṟukkōṭu, ivaṟṟukkōṭu, uvaṟṟuk kōṭu; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.
‘muṟpaṭak kiḷanta’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, vaṟṟiṉōṭu iṉṉum peṟutal koḷka. 
u-m: avaṟṟiṉkōṭu, ivaṟṟiṉkōṭu, uvaṟṟiṉ kōṭu; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.	        (83)
380.	vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi yāyta mākum.
itu, mēlavaṟṟiṟku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumai alvaḻi āytam ākum - ac cuṭṭu mutalākiya vakaravīṟu vaṉkaṇam vantāl vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarcciyallāta alvaḻikkaṇ avvakaram āytamāyt tirintu muṭiyum.
u-m: aḵkaṭiya, iḵkaṭiya, uḵkaṭiya; ciṟiya, tīya, periya eṉa varum.	   (84)
381.	melleḻut tiyaiyi ṉavveḻut tākum.
itu, mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku meṉkaṇattu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ a eḻuttu ākum - accuṭṭu mutalākiya vakaravīṟu meṉkaṇam vantu iyainta viṭattu avvakaram avvam melleḻuttāyt tirintu muṭiyum.
u-m:	aññāṇ, iññāṇ, uññāṇ; nūl, maṇi eṉa varum.	 (85)
382.	ēṉavai puṇari ṉiyalpeṉa moḻipa.
itu, mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku iṭaikkaṇattum uyirkkaṇattum muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉavai puṇariṉ iyalpu eṉa moḻipa - ac cuṭṭu mutalākiya vakaravīṟu oḻinta iṭaikkaṇamum uyirkkaṇamum vantu puṇariṉ av vakaram tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭiyum.
u-m: avyāḻ, ivyāḻ, uvyāḻ; vaṭṭu, aṭai, āṭai eṉa oṭṭuka.	(86)
383.	ēṉai vakaran toḻiṟpeya riyaṟṟē.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oḻinta oṉṟaṟkum alvaḻik kaṇṇum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉai vakaram toḻiṟpeyar iyaṟṟu - oḻinta vakaravīṟu ñakara vīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar iyalpiṟṟāy vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum, allaṉa vaṟṟukkaṇ ukaramē peṟṟum muṭiyum.
u-m: tevvukkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu eṉavum; tevvukkaṭumai; ciṟumai, tīmai, perumai, ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, valimai eṉavum varum.        			(87)
384.	ḻakāra viṟuti rakāra viyaṟṟē.
itu, ḻakāravīṟṟu vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ḻakāra iṟuti rakāra iyaṟṟu - ḻakāra īṟṟup peyar vaṉkaṇam vantāl vēṟṟumaikkaṇ rakāra īṟṟu iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.
u-m: pūḻkkāl; ciṟaku, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.	 (88)
385.	tāḻeṉ kiḷavi kōloṭu puṇariṉ
	akkiṭai varuta lurittu mākum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tāḻ eṉ kiḷavi kōloṭu puṇariṉ - tāḻ eṉṉum col kōl eṉṉum colloṭu puṇarum iṭattu, akkuiṭai varutalum urittu - valleḻuttu mikutalēyaṉṟi akkuccāriyai iṭai vantu muṭitalum urittu. 
u-m: tāḻakkōl eṉa varum.	(89)
386.	tamiḻeṉ kiḷaviyu mataṉō raṟṟē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) tamiḻ eṉ kiḷaviyum ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - tamiḻ eṉṉum collum ataṉōṭu oru taṉmaittāy valleḻuttu mikutalēyaṉṟi akkum peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: tamiḻakkūttu; cēri, tōṭṭam, paḷḷi eṉa varum.(90)
387.	kumiḻeṉ kiḷavi marappeya rāyiṉ
	pīreṉ kiḷaviyō ṭōriyaṟ ṟākum.
ituvum, av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kumiḻ eṉ kiḷavi marappeyar āyiṉ - kumiḻ eṉṉum col kumiḻttal eṉṉum toḻilaṉṟi marappeya rāyiṉ, pīr eṉ kiḷaviyōṭu ōr iyaṟṟu ākum - pīr eṉ kiḷaviyōṭu ōr iyalpiṟṟāy melleḻuttum ammuc cāriyaiyum peṟṟu muṭiyum.
u-m: kumiḻṅkōṭu; kumiḻaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū
eṉavarum.
‘ōr iyaṟṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟavaṟṟiṟkum im muṭipu koḷka. makiḻṅkōṭu eṉa varum.	(91)
388.	pāḻeṉ kiḷavi melleḻut tuṟaḻvē.
ituvum, av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēl ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) pāḻ eṉ kiḷavi melleḻuttu uṟaḻvu - pāḻ eṉṉum col valleḻuttoṭu melleḻuttu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum.
u-m:	pāḻkkiṇaṟu; pāḻṅkiṇaṟu; cēri, tōṭṭam, pāṭi eṉa varum. (`ēkāram’ - īṟṟacai.)	  (92)
389.	ēḻeṉ kiḷavi yurupiya ṉilaiyum.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟu eṇṇuppeyarkku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ēḻ eṉ kiḷavi - ēḻ eṉṉum eṇṇup peyaratu iṟuti, urupu iyal nilaiyum - urupu puṇarccikkaṇ coṉṉa iyalpiṉ kaṇṇē (195) nilaipeṟṟu (aṉpeṟṟu) muṭiyum.
u-m: ēḻaṉ kāyam; cukku, tōrai, payaṟu eṉa varum.
iyalpu valleḻuttu (384) iv vōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭaiyāṉ (406) vīḻkka. (`kiḷavi’ āku peyar.)	 (93)
390. 	aḷavu niṟaiyu meṇṇum varuvaḻi
	neṭumutal kuṟukalu mukaram varutalum
	kaṭinilai yiṉṟē yāciri yarkka.
itu, mēlataṟku eytātatu eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷavum niṟaiyum eṇṇum varuvaḻi - (av ēḻ eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar) aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum eṇṇup peyarum (varumoḻiyāy) varumiṭattu, neṭumutal kuṟukalum ukaram varutalum āciriyarkku kaṭinilai iṉṟu - neṭumutal kuṟukutalum (āṇṭu) ukaram varutalum āciriyarkku nīkkum nilaimai iṉṟu.
u-m: eḻukalam; cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai, nāḻi, maṇṭai, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; eḻukaḻañcu; toṭi, palam eṉavum; eḻumūṉṟu, eḻunāṉku eṉavum varum.
‘nilaiyiṉṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, vaṉkaṇattup poruṭ peyarkkum im muṭipu koḷka.
u-m: eḻukaṭal; cilai, ticai, piṟappu eṉa varum.
(`ēkāram’ - acai. `āciriyarkku’ eṉpatu `āciriyarkka’ eṉṟāyiṟṟu.)	 (94)
391.	patteṉ kiḷavi yoṟṟiṭai keṭuvaḻi 
	niṟṟal vēṇṭu māytap puḷḷi.
itu, mēlataṟku oruvaḻi eytiyataṉmēl ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) pattu eṉ kiḷavi iṭai oṟṟu keṭuvaḻi āytap puḷḷi niṟṟal vēṇṭum - (av) ēḻ eṉpataṉoṭu pattu eṉpatu (puṇarum iṭattu appattu eṉ(ṉum) kiḷavi(yiṉ) iṭai oṟṟuk keṭuvaḻi āytamākiya puḷḷi niṟṟal vēṇṭum.)
u-m: eḻupaḵtu eṉa varum.	(95)
392.	āyiram varuvaḻi yukaraṅ keṭumē.
itu, mēlataṟku eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āyiram varuvaḻi ukaram keṭum - (av ēḻ) eṉpatu āyiram eṉpatu varumiṭattu (muṉpeṟṟa) ukaram peṟātu muṭiyum.
u-m: eḻāyiram eṉa varum. (ēkāram - īṟṟacai)	(96)
393. 	nūṟūrntu varūu māyirak kiḷavikkuk
	kūṟiya neṭumutal kuṟukka miṉṟē.
ituvum, mēlataṟku oruvaḻi eytiyatu muḻuvatum vilakku kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nūṟu ūrntu varum āyirak kiḷavikku - (av ēḻ) eṉpatu nūṟu eṉṉum col ūrntu varukiṉṟa āyirak kiḷaviyākiya nūṟāyiram eṉpataṟku, kūṟiya neṭumutal kuṟukkam iṉṟu - (muṉ) kūṟiya neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram peṟutal iṉṟu.
u-m: ēḻ nūṟāyiram eṉa varum.
‘kūṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, neṭumutal kuṟuki, ukaram peṟṟu eḻu nūṟāyiram eṉṟum ām. iv vilēciṉāṉē ēḻāyiram eṉṟum ām.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, iyalpukaṇattu muṭipu koḷka. eḻuñāyiṟu, eḻunāḷ eṉa varum. (`ēkāram’ - īṟṟacai)  	(97)
394.	aiyam palleṉa varūu miṟuti
	alpeya reṇṇu māyiya ṉilaiyum.
ituvum, mēlataṟku oruvaḻi eytiyatu muḻuvatum vilakku kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ai am pal eṉa varūum iṟuti alpeyar eṇṇum  -  (avvēḻ eṉpataṉmuṉ)  ai eṉṟum, am eṉṟum, pal eṉṟum varukiṉṟa iṟutikaḷaiyuṭaiya (poruṭpeyar) allāta eṇṇup peyarākiya tāmarai veḷḷam āmpal eṉpaṉavum (vantāl); a iyal nilaiyum -  (neṭumutal kuṟukkam iṉṟi ukaram peṟātu) avviyalpiṉ kaṇṇē niṉṟu  muṭiyum.
u-m:	ēḻtāmarai, ēḻveḷḷam, ēḻāmpal eṉa varum.	 (`ākāram’ ceyyuḷ vikāram.)	(98)
395. 	uyirmuṉ variṉu māyiya ṟiriyātu.
ituvum, mēlataṟku eytiyatu oruvaḻi vilakkip piṟitu viti vakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir muṉ variṉum - (av vēḻ eṉpatu aḷavu mutaliya peyarkaḷuḷ) uyir mutalmoḻi (muṉ) variṉum, a iyal tiriyātu - (neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram varātu) av viyalpil tiriyātu muṭiyum.
u-m:	ēḻakal, ēḻuḻakku, ēḻoṉṟu, ēḻiraṇṭu eṉa varum. (`ākāram’ ceyyuḷ vikāram)	(99)
396. 	kīḻeṉ kiḷavi yuṟaḻat tōṉṟum.
iḵtu, iv īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku vēṟṟumaikkaṇ vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kīḻ eṉ kiḷavi uṟaḻa tōṉṟum - kīḻ eṉṉum col uṟaḻcciyākat tōṉṟi muṭiyum.
u-m: kīḻkuḷam, kīḻkkuḷam eṉa varum.
‘tōṉṟum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, neṭumutal kuṟukātu ukaram varutalum koḷka. (āṅku) iyaipu valleḻuttu iv vōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭaiyāṉ (406) vīḻkka.
u-m: kīḻukuḷam; cēri, tōṭṭam, pāṭi eṉa varum.      (100)
397.	ḷakāra viṟuti ṇakāra viyaṟṟē.
itu, ḷakāra īṟṟiṟku ṇakāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumai muṭipōṭu iyaiya vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ḷakāra iṟuti ṇakāra iyaṟṟu - ḷakāra īṟṟup peyar (vēṟṟumaikkaṇ) ṇakāra īṟṟu iyalpiṟṟāy (303) (vaṉkaṇam vantāl ḷakāram ṭakāramāyt tirintu) muṭiyum.
u-m: muṭkuṟai; ciṟai, talai, puṟam eṉa varum. 	(101)
398. 	melleḻut tiyaiyiṉ ṇakāra mākum.
itu, mēlataṟku meṉkaṇattu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ ṇakāram ākum - (ḷakāra īṟu) melleḻuttu (varumoḻiyāy vantu) iyaiyiṉ ṇakāramāy(t tirintu) muṭiyum.
u-m: muṇñeri; nuṉi, muri eṉa varum.
itaṉai vēṟṟumai iṟutikkaṇ alvaḻiyatu eṭuttuk kōṭaṟ kaṇ ciṅkanōkkāka vaittaliṉ, alvaḻikkum im muṭipu koḷka.
u-m: muṇñerintatu; nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu eṉa varum. (102)
399. 	alvaḻi yellā muṟaḻeṉa moḻipa.
itu, mēlataṟku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) alvaḻi ellām uṟaḻ eṉa moḻipa - (ḷakāra īṟṟu) alvaḻikaḷellām (tirintum tiriyātum) uṟaḻntu muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
u-m: muḷkaṭitu, muṭkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, kuṇavēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum iv vuṟaḻcci koḷka.
u-m: muḷkuṟumai, muṭkuṟumai; ciṟumai, tīmai, perumai eṉavum; kōḷkaṭumai, kōṭkaṭumai eṉavum varum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, urupu vārātu urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantavaṟṟiṉ muṭipum koḷka.
u-m: atōṭkoṇṭāṉ, itōṭkoṇṭāṉ, utōṭ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.	   (103)
400.	āyta nilaiyalum varainilai yiṉṟē
	takaram varūuṅ kālai yāṉa.
itu, mēlataṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āytam nilaiyalum varainilai iṉṟu - (ḷakāram ṭakāramāyē tiriyātu) āytamāy(t tirintu) niṟṟalum varaiyum nilaimai iṉṟu, takaram varum kālai - takara mutalmoḻi varum kālattu.
u-m: muḵṭītu, muṭṭītu eṉa varum. 	(104)
(ēkāram - īṟṟacai. ukara nīṭṭam ceyyuḷ vikāram. ‘āṉ’ iṭaiccol.‘akaram’ cāriyai. varainilaimai - kaṭiyum nilaimai)
401.	neṭiyata ṉiṟuti yiyalpā kunavum
	vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi vēṟṟumai nilaiyalum
	pōṟṟal vēṇṭum moḻiyumā ruḷavē.
itu, mēlataṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭiyataṉ iṟuti iyalpākunavum - (ḷakāram) neṭiyataṉ iṟuti tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavaṟṟaiyum, vēṟṟumai alvaḻi vēṟṟumai nilaiyalum - vēṟṟumai allāta alvaḻiyiṭattu vēṟṟumai(yiṉ iyalpuṭaiyaṉa vāyt tirintu) muṭitalaiyum, pōṟṟal vēṇṭum moḻiyumār uḷa - pōṟṟutal vēṇṭum moḻikaḷum uḷa.
u-m: vāḷkaṭitu, kōḷkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; tōṭkaṭitu; nāṭkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum varum.
‘pōṟṟal vēṇṭum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, utaḷaṅkāy, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa ammuppeṟṟu muṭivaṉa koḷka.
(`ār’, `ē’ acaikaḷ. ammu - am cāriyai.)	(105)
402.	toḻiṟpeya rellān toḻiṟ peyariyala.
iḵtu, iv vīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyarkku vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum alvaḻikkaṇṇum eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) toḻiṟpeyar ellām toḻiṟpeyar iyala - (ḷakāra īṟṟut) toḻiṟpeyarellām vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum alvaḻik kaṇṇum ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar iyalpiṉa vāy (297, 298) vaṉkaṇam vantavaḻi valleḻuttum ukaramum peṟṟum, meṉkaṇamum iṭaikkaṇattu vakaramum vantavaḻi ukaram peṟṟum muṭiyum.
u-m: tuḷḷuk kaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu eṉavum; tuḷḷukkaṭumai; ciṟumai, tīmai, perumai; ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, valimai eṉavum varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, toḻiṟpeyarkaḷ iruvaḻiyum ivvā ṟaṉṟip piṟavāṟu muṭivaṉavum koḷka.
u-m: kōḷkaṭitu, kōṭkaṭitu; vāḷkaṭitu, vāṭkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum; kōḷ kaṭumai, kōṭkaṭumai; vāḷ kaṭumai, vāṭkaṭumai; ciṟumai, tīmai, perumai eṉavum varum. 
īṇṭu, vāḷ eṉṟatu - collutalai.	(106)
403.	iruḷeṉ kiḷavi veyiliya ṉilaiyum.
ituvum, av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip 
piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iruḷ eṉ kiḷavi veyil iyal nilaiyum - iruḷ eṉṉum col (vēṟṟumaipporuṭ puṇarccikkaṇ) veyil eṉṉum colliṉ iyalpilē (niṉṟu, attum iṉṉum peṟṟu muṭiyum).
u-m:	iruḷattuk koṇṭāṉ, iruḷiṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
404. 	puḷḷum vaḷḷun toḻiṟpeya riyala.
ituvum, av vīṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) puḷḷum vaḷḷum toḻiṟpeyar iyala - puḷ eṉṉum collum vaḷ eṉṉum collum (vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum alvaḻik kaṇṇum) ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar iyalpiṟṟavāy (vaṉ kaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum, meṉkaṇattum iṭaik kaṇattu vakarattum ukaram peṟṟum) muṭiyum.
u-m:	puḷḷukkaṭitu, vaḷḷukkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu eṉavum; puḷḷuk kaṭumai, vaḷḷukkaṭumai; ciṟumai, tīmai, perumai, ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, valimai eṉavum varum.
itaṉait ‘toḻiṟpeyarellām’ eṉṟataṉpiṉ vaiyāta muṟaiyaṉṟiya kūṟṟiṉāṉ, iruvaḻiyum vēṟṟumait tiripu eyti muṭivaṉavum koḷka.
u-m:	puṭkaṭitu, vaṭkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu; ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu eṉavum; puṭkaṭumai, vaṭkaṭumai; ciṟumai, tīmai, perumai, ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, valimai eṉavum varum.
iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, paḷ eṉpataṉkaṇṇum, kaḷ eṉpataṉ kaṇṇum iruvaḻiyum ivviru muṭipu peṟṟamaiyum koḷka.
paḷḷukkaṭitu, kaḷḷukkaṭitu; paḷḷukkaṭumai, kaḷḷuk kaṭumai eṉavum; paṭkaṭitu, kaṭkaṭitu; paṭkaṭumai, kaṭkaṭumai eṉavum oṭṭuka.	(108)
405. 	makka ḷeṉṉum peyarnilaik kiḷavi
	takkavaḻi yaṟintu valittalu murittē.
itu, makkaḷ eṉṉum uyartiṇaip peyarukku ‘uyirīṟākiya vuyartiṇaip peyarum’ (tokai. 11) eṉpataṉuḷ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) makkaḷ eṉṉum peyarnilai kiḷaviyum - makkaḷ eṉṉum peyarccolliṉ iṟuti, takkavaḻi aṟintu valittalum urittu - (iyalpēyaṉṟit) takka iṭam aṟintu valloṟṟāy(t tirintu) muṭitalum ām.
‘takkavaḻi’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, am makkaḷ uṭampu, uyir nīṅkiya kālattu im muṭipu eṉak koḷka.
u-m: makkaṭkai; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.	(109)
(‘kiḷavi’ ākupeyar. ‘ēkāram’ īṟṟacai)
406. 	uṇarak kūṟiya puṇariyaṉ maruṅkiṉ
	kaṇṭuceyaṟ kuriyavai kaṇṇiṉar koḷalē.
iḵtu ivvōttiṟkup puṟanaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uṇarak kūṟiya - uṇarak kūṟappaṭṭa puḷḷiyīṟu(kaḷ), puṇar iyal maruṅkiṉ - (varumoḻiyoṭu) puṇarum iyal piṭattu, kaṇṭu ceyaṟku uriyavai kaṇṇiṉar koḷal - (mēl muṭitta muṭipukaḷēyaṉṟi vaḻakkiṉuḷ) kaṇṭu muṭittaṟkuriya piṟa muṭipukaḷaik karutikkoṇṭu muṭikka.
u-m:  viḻaṉ kuḷam; cēṟu, taṟai, paḻaṉam eṉa ivai, ṉakāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭintaṉa. poṉṉappattam eṉpatu avvīṟu akkuppeṟṟu muṭintatu.
nīr kuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu (eṉpatu) rakāra īṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu.
vēr kuṟitu; vērkkuṟitu (eṉpatu) av vīṟṟu alvaḻi uṟaḻcci.
amparkkoṇṭāṉ, imparkkoṇṭāṉ, umpark koṇṭāṉ, empark koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉpaṉa av vīṟṟuḷ urupu vārātu urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantavaṟṟiṉ muṭipu.
takarkkuṭṭi eṉpatu av vīṟṟu irupeyaroṭṭup paṇput tokaiyākiya alvaḻi muṭipu.
vaṭacārkkūrai, teṉcārkkūrai eṉpaṉa avvīṟṟu ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiya marūu muṭipu.
ucilaṅkōṭu, eliyālaṅkōṭu eṉpaṉa lakāra īṟṟu ammup pēṟu.
kallampāṟai eṉpatu av vīṟṟu alvaḻi ammuppēṟu.
aḻalattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu avvīṟṟu attuppēṟu.
aḻukkaṟpōr eṉpatu av vīṟṟu alvaḻit tiripu.
yāḻkuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉpaṉa ḻakāra īṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu.
vīḻkuṟitu, vīḻkkuṟitu eṉpaṉa av vīṟṟu alvaḻi uṟaḻcci.
tāḻappāvai eṉpatu av vīṟṟu alvaḻi akkuppēṟu.
iṉi ‘uṇarak kūṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, kuḷattiṉpuṟam, marat  tiṉpuṟam eṉa urupiṟku eytiya attōṭu iṉpeṟutalum koḷka.
iṉik ‘kaṇṇiṉar’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, orunāḷaik kuḻavi eṉa ḷakara īṟu ai eṉṉum cāriyaiyum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭintaṉa koḷka.
piṟavum, iv vōttiṉ vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavellām itaṉ akattu muṭittuk koḷka.	(110)
eṭṭāvatu - puḷḷimayaṅkiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.
kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal
kuṟṟiyalukaravīṟu niṉṟu varumoḻiyōṭu puṇarum iyal piṉai yuṇarttuvatu ivviyalākum. neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉum toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟum kuṟṟiyalukaram varum eṉa moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiṉār. toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟuk kuṟṟukarattai ayaliluḷḷa eḻuttu vakaiyāl uyirttoṭar, iṭaittoṭar, āytat toṭar, vaṉṟoṭar, meṉṟoṭar eṉa aintākappakuttu, avaṟṟoṭu neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉvaruṅ kuṟṟukarattaiyuṅ kūṭṭi aṟuvakaippaṭuttu viḷakkukiṉṟār.
yaraḻamuṉṉark kacatapaṅañanama īroṟṟākivaruṅ kuṟṟukaraṅka ḷ oruvāṟṟāṉ iṭaiyoṟṟukkaḷōṭu toṭarntuvariṉum kuṟṟiya lukarattaiyaṭuttu nērē toṭarntaṉa allavātaliṉ avai iṭait toṭarākak koḷḷappaṭāveṉṟum, āṟīṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum iruvaḻiyilum keṭātu niṟaintē niṟkumeṉṟum, vaṉṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻi varumiṭattu muṉkūṟiya (araimāttiraiyiṉuṅ kuṟukum) iyalpil niṟṟalumuritteṉṟum, kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟiṉmuṉ yakara mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu īṟṟiṟ kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkeṭa aṅku ōr ikaram vantu kuṟuki niṟkumeṉṟum ivviyaliṉ mutal aintu cūttirattāṉum kuṟṟiyalukarattiyal piṉai āciriyar potuvakaiyāṟ kūṟippōntār. 6-mutal 17-varaiyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷāl kuṟṟukaravīṟṟu vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarcci kūṟiṉār. 18-mutal kuṟṟukaravīṟṟu alvaḻippuṇarcciyiṉait toṭaṅkik kūṟukiṉṟār.
uṇmait taṉmaiyai yuṇarttum uṇṭu eṉṉuñcol valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻivariṉ iṟutiniṉṟa ukaram mey yoṭuṅkeṭa ṇakaram ḷakaramākat tiriyumeṉṟum, kuṟṟukara vīṟṟut ticaippeyarkaḷuḷ iraṇṭu perunticaiyum tammiṟ puṇarumiṭattu ēyeṉṉuñcāriyai iṭaiyē vantumuṭiyu meṉṟum, kuṟṟukaravīṟṟup perunticaikaḷōṭu kōṇat ticaikaḷ puṇarumiṭattu iṟutiyukaram meyyoṭuṅ koṭumeṉṟum avvaḻi teṟku eṉpataṉ ṟakaram ṉakaramākat tiriyumeṉṟum kūṟuvar āciriyar.
kuṟṟukara vīṟṟu eṇṇup peyariyalpiṉai 28-ām cūttira mutalākat toṭaṅkik kūṟukiṉṟār. pattu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉ muṉ oṉṟumutal eṭṭuvaraiyuḷḷa eṇkaḷum āyirameṉṉum eṇṇum niṟaippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum vantu puṇaru muṟaiyiṉai 28-mutal 30-varaiyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ uṇarttuvaṉa oṉṟu mutal eṭṭuvaraiyuḷḷa kuṟṟukara vīṟṟu eṇṇum peyarkaḷiṉmuṉ pattu vantu puṇartalai 31-mutal 38-varaiyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭuvaṉa. oṉpatu eṉṉum colliṉmuṉ pattu vantu puṇaruṅkāl nilaimoḻiyākiya oṉpatil okarattiṟku mēlākat takara meytōṉṟit toṉpatu eṉṟākip patu keṭṭu ṉakaram ṇakaramākat tirintu iraṭṭittut toṇṇ eṉa niṟkumeṉṟum, varumoḻiyākiya paḵteṉpatil pakaravuyirmeyyum āytamumkeṭṭu ūkāram tōṉṟi iṟutiyiluḷḷa ‘tu’ eṉpataṉ takaram ṟakaramākat tirintu ūṟu eṉṟākit toṇṇuhṟu eṉa muṭiyumeṉṟum, ivviyal 39-ām cūttirattāl āciriyar viritturaippar. mēṟkūṟiya oṉṟumutal oṉpatu varaiyuḷḷa eṇṇuppeyarkaḷōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟaip peyarum vantu puṇaru muṟaiyiṉai 40-mutal 53-varaiyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷālum, nūṟeṉṉuñcol varumoḻiyāy vantu puṇarvataṉai 54, 55, 56, 57-ām cūttiraṅkaḷālum, āyiram eṉṉum eṇ taṉittum nūṟeṉṉum aṭaiyaṭuttum puṇartalai 58-mutal 65-varaiyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷālum kūṟuvar āciriyar. oṉpatu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉmuṉ nūṟu eṉṉum eṇ vantupuṇariṉ muṟkūṟiyavāṟu nilaimoḻiyāy niṉṟa oṉpatu eṉṉum colliṉ okarattiṉ muṉṉē takaramey oṉṟu tōṉṟit toṉpatu eṉṟākip patukeṭṭu ṉakaramey ḷakara meyyāka iraṭṭittut toḷḷ eṉa niṟkumeṉṟum, varumoḻiyākiya nūṟeṉpataṉ mutalil niṉṟa nakaramey keṭa ammeyyiṉ mēlēṟiya ūkāram ākāramākattirintu ataṉai yaṭuttu ikaravuyirum rakaravuyir meyyum iṭaiyētōṉṟi īṟṟiluḷḷa ṟakara vukaraṅkeṭṭu makarameytōṉṟi āyiram eṉṟākit toḷḷāyiram eṉa muṭiyumeṉṟum 57-ām cūttirattāl tolkāppiyaṉār virittukkūṟiyuḷḷār. toṇṇuhṟu, toḷḷāyiram eṉṉum puṇarmoḻikaḷukku āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟum puṇarcci muṟaikaḷ valintu kūṟuvaṉavākavē amaintuḷḷaṉa veṉṟum oṉpatu eṉṉum poruḷpaṭat tolkāppiyarē toṇṭu eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyarait tam nūlil āṇṭiruttalāl toṇṭu+pattu = toṇṇuhṟu eṉṟum, toṇṭu+āyiram = toḷḷāyiram eṉṟum nilaimoḻi varumoḻi ceytu puṇarcciviti kūṟuvatē moḻiyiyalpukku ottatākumeṉṟum paritimāṟkalaiñar kūṟum koḷkai iṅku cintikkat takkatākum.
